--============================================================================
-- LOUIS HUB // ILLEGAL SOCCER SUITE v3.2.0 (ULTRA PRECISION & ANTI-LAG EDITION)
-- PART 1/10: CORE FOUNDATION & RUNTIME ENGINE
-- Modules: Virtual Registry, Janitor, Signal, Scheduler, HookGuard
--============================================================================

do
    if not game:IsLoaded() then
        pcall(function()
            game.Loaded:Wait()
        end)
    end

    local GlobalEnv = (type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or _G

    -- Bersihkan instance LouisHub lama jika masih berjalan
    if type(GlobalEnv.LouisHub) == "table" and type(GlobalEnv.LouisHub.Destroy) == "function" then
        pcall(GlobalEnv.LouisHub.Destroy)
    end

    -- Service resolver yang aman dari hook eksternal
    local function safeService(name, skipClone)
        local ok, service = pcall(function()
            return game:GetService(name)
        end)
        if not ok or not service then
            return nil
        end
        if not skipClone and type(cloneref) == "function" then
            local cOk, cloned = pcall(cloneref, service)
            if cOk and cloned then
                return cloned
            end
        end
        return service
    end

    local Players = safeService("Players")
    local RunService = safeService("RunService")
    local HttpService = safeService("HttpService")
    local UserInputService = safeService("UserInputService")
    local GuiService = safeService("GuiService")
    local Lighting = safeService("Lighting")

    if Players and not Players.LocalPlayer then
        local tWait = os.clock()
        while not Players.LocalPlayer and (os.clock() - tWait < 10) do
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
    end

    local function cloneTable(tbl)
        if type(table.clone) == "function" then
            return table.clone(tbl)
        end
        local copy = {}
        for k, v in pairs(tbl) do
            copy[k] = v
        end
        return copy
    end

    local function clearTable(tbl)
        if type(table.clear) == "function" then
            table.clear(tbl)
            return
        end
        for k in pairs(tbl) do
            tbl[k] = nil
        end
    end

    --========================================================================
    -- VIRTUAL MODULE REGISTRY
    --========================================================================
    local LouisHub = {
        Name = "Louis Hub",
        GameName = "Illegal Soccer",
        Version = "3.2.0",
        PartLoaded = { [1] = true },
        _factories = {},
        _cache = {},
        _cleanupCallbacks = {},
        _destroyed = false,
    }

    GlobalEnv.LouisHub = LouisHub

    function LouisHub.SafeService(name, skipClone)
        return safeService(name, skipClone)
    end

    function LouisHub.Define(path, factory)
        LouisHub._factories[path] = factory
        LouisHub._cache[path] = nil
    end

    function LouisHub.Require(path)
        local cached = LouisHub._cache[path]
        if cached ~= nil then
            return cached
        end
        local factory = LouisHub._factories[path]
        if not factory then
            error("[LouisHub] Virtual module not registered: " .. tostring(path), 2)
        end
        local moduleResult = factory(LouisHub.Require, LouisHub)
        if moduleResult == nil then
            moduleResult = true
        end
        LouisHub._cache[path] = moduleResult
        return moduleResult
    end

    function LouisHub.HasModule(path)
        return LouisHub._factories[path] ~= nil
    end

    function LouisHub.OnDestroy(callback)
        if type(callback) == "function" then
            table.insert(LouisHub._cleanupCallbacks, callback)
        end
    end

    function LouisHub.Destroy()
        if LouisHub._destroyed then
            return
        end
        LouisHub._destroyed = true
        for i = #LouisHub._cleanupCallbacks, 1, -1 do
            pcall(LouisHub._cleanupCallbacks[i])
        end
        clearTable(LouisHub._cleanupCallbacks)
        clearTable(LouisHub._cache)
        if GlobalEnv.LouisHub == LouisHub then
            GlobalEnv.LouisHub = nil
        end
    end

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Core/Janitor (Memory Management & Leak Prevention)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Core/Janitor", function()
        local Janitor = {}
        Janitor.__index = Janitor

        local function cleanupItem(item, methodName)
            if item == nil then
                return
            end
            local t = typeof(item)
            if t == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                if item.Connected then
                    item:Disconnect()
                end
                return
            elseif t == "Instance" then
                item:Destroy()
                return
            elseif t == "thread" then
                pcall(task.cancel, item)
                return
            elseif t == "function" then
                item()
                return
            elseif type(item) == "table" then
                if methodName and type(item[methodName]) == "function" then
                    item[methodName](item)
                elseif type(item.Disconnect) == "function" then
                    item:Disconnect()
                elseif type(item.Destroy) == "function" then
                    item:Destroy()
                elseif type(item.Cleanup) == "function" then
                    item:Cleanup()
                end
            end
        end

        function Janitor.new()
            return setmetatable({
                _tasks = {},
                _named = {},
                _disposed = false,
            }, Janitor)
        end

        function Janitor:Add(item, methodName, key)
            if self._disposed then
                pcall(cleanupItem, item, methodName)
                return item
            end
            if key ~= nil then
                self:Remove(key)
                self._named[key] = { item = item, method = methodName }
            else
                table.insert(self._tasks, { item = item, method = methodName })
            end
            return item
        end

        function Janitor:Connect(rbxSignal, fn, key)
            local conn = rbxSignal:Connect(fn)
            return self:Add(conn, "Disconnect", key)
        end

        function Janitor:BindRenderStep(name, priority, fn)
            RunService:BindToRenderStep(name, priority, fn)
            return self:Add(function()
                pcall(function()
                    RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(name)
                end)
            end, nil, "RenderStep:" .. name)
        end

        function Janitor:Remove(key)
            local entry = self._named[key]
            if entry then
                self._named[key] = nil
                pcall(cleanupItem, entry.item, entry.method)
            end
        end

        function Janitor:Cleanup()
            for key, entry in pairs(self._named) do
                self._named[key] = nil
                pcall(cleanupItem, entry.item, entry.method)
            end
            for i = #self._tasks, 1, -1 do
                local entry = self._tasks[i]
                self._tasks[i] = nil
                pcall(cleanupItem, entry.item, entry.method)
            end
        end

        function Janitor:Destroy()
            if self._disposed then
                return
            end
            self._disposed = true
            self:Cleanup()
        end

        return Janitor
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Core/Signal (Fast Event Dispatcher)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Core/Signal", function()
        local Signal = {}
        Signal.__index = Signal

        local Connection = {}
        Connection.__index = Connection

        function Connection.new(signal, fn)
            return setmetatable({
                _signal = signal,
                _fn = fn,
                Connected = true,
            }, Connection)
        end

        function Connection:Disconnect()
            if not self.Connected then
                return
            end
            self.Connected = false
            local listeners = self._signal._listeners
            for i = #listeners, 1, -1 do
                if listeners[i] == self then
                    table.remove(listeners, i)
                    break
                end
            end
        end

        function Signal.new()
            return setmetatable({
                _listeners = {},
                _destroyed = false,
            }, Signal)
        end

        function Signal:Connect(fn)
            assert(type(fn) == "function", "[Signal] Connect expects a function")
            if self._destroyed then
                local dummy = Connection.new(self, fn)
                dummy.Connected = false
                return dummy
            end
            local conn = Connection.new(self, fn)
            table.insert(self._listeners, conn)
            return conn
        end

        function Signal:Once(fn)
            local conn
            conn = self:Connect(function(...)
                if conn and conn.Connected then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
                fn(...)
            end)
            return conn
        end

        function Signal:Fire(...)
            if self._destroyed or #self._listeners == 0 then
                return
            end
            local snapshot = cloneTable(self._listeners)
            for i = 1, #snapshot do
                local conn = snapshot[i]
                if conn.Connected then
                    task.spawn(conn._fn, ...)
                end
            end
        end

        function Signal:FireSync(...)
            if self._destroyed or #self._listeners == 0 then
                return
            end
            local snapshot = cloneTable(self._listeners)
            for i = 1, #snapshot do
                local conn = snapshot[i]
                if conn.Connected then
                    local ok, err = pcall(conn._fn, ...)
                    if not ok then
                        warn("[LouisHub/Signal] Listener error:", err)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        function Signal:Destroy()
            if self._destroyed then
                return
            end
            self._destroyed = true
            for i = #self._listeners, 1, -1 do
                self._listeners[i].Connected = false
                self._listeners[i] = nil
            end
        end

        return Signal
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Core/Scheduler (Multi-Tier Anti-Lag Throttling)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Core/Scheduler", function(Require)
        local Janitor = Require("Core/Janitor")
        local Scheduler = {}
        Scheduler.__index = Scheduler

        function Scheduler.new(hubName)
            return setmetatable({
                _name = hubName or "LouisHub",
                _janitor = Janitor.new(),
                _heartbeatTasks = {},
                _renderTasks = {},
                _started = false,
                _renderBindingName = (hubName or "LouisHub") .. "_RenderLoop",
                _stats = {
                    LastHeartbeatMs = 0,
                    LastRenderMs = 0,
                    ActiveTasks = 0,
                },
            }, Scheduler)
        end

        function Scheduler:AddHeartbeat(id, intervalSeconds, callback)
            self._heartbeatTasks[id] = {
                id = id,
                interval = intervalSeconds or 0,
                elapsed = 0,
                callback = callback,
                enabled = true,
            }
            return function()
                self._heartbeatTasks[id] = nil
            end
        end

        function Scheduler:AddRenderStep(id, intervalSeconds, callback)
            self._renderTasks[id] = {
                id = id,
                interval = intervalSeconds or 0,
                elapsed = 0,
                callback = callback,
                enabled = true,
            }
            return function()
                self._renderTasks[id] = nil
            end
        end

        function Scheduler:SetTaskInterval(id, intervalSeconds)
            local interval = math.max(0, tonumber(intervalSeconds) or 0)
            if self._heartbeatTasks[id] then
                self._heartbeatTasks[id].interval = interval
                self._heartbeatTasks[id].elapsed = 0
            end
            if self._renderTasks[id] then
                self._renderTasks[id].interval = interval
                self._renderTasks[id].elapsed = 0
            end
        end

        function Scheduler:SetTaskEnabled(id, enabled)
            if self._heartbeatTasks[id] then
                self._heartbeatTasks[id].enabled = enabled == true
            end
            if self._renderTasks[id] then
                self._renderTasks[id].enabled = enabled == true
            end
        end

        function Scheduler:RemoveTask(id)
            self._heartbeatTasks[id] = nil
            self._renderTasks[id] = nil
        end

        function Scheduler:GetStats()
            return self._stats
        end

        function Scheduler:Start()
            if self._started then
                return
            end
            self._started = true

            self._janitor:Connect(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
                local t0 = os.clock()
                local count = 0
                for _, entry in pairs(self._heartbeatTasks) do
                    if entry.enabled then
                        count = count + 1
                        if entry.interval <= 0 then
                            local ok, err = pcall(entry.callback, dt)
                            if not ok then
                                warn("[LouisHub/Scheduler] Heartbeat '" .. tostring(entry.id) .. "' error:", err)
                            end
                        else
                            entry.elapsed = entry.elapsed + dt
                            if entry.elapsed >= entry.interval then
                                local stepDt = entry.elapsed
                                entry.elapsed = 0
                                local ok, err = pcall(entry.callback, stepDt)
                                if not ok then
                                    warn("[LouisHub/Scheduler] Heartbeat '" .. tostring(entry.id) .. "' error:", err)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                self._stats.LastHeartbeatMs = (os.clock() - t0) * 1000
                self._stats.ActiveTasks = count
            end, "Heartbeat")

            local priority = Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 5
            self._janitor:BindRenderStep(self._renderBindingName, priority, function(dt)
                local t0 = os.clock()
                for _, entry in pairs(self._renderTasks) do
                    if entry.enabled then
                        if entry.interval <= 0 then
                            local ok, err = pcall(entry.callback, dt)
                            if not ok then
                                warn("[LouisHub/Scheduler] RenderStep '" .. tostring(entry.id) .. "' error:", err)
                            end
                        else
                            entry.elapsed = entry.elapsed + dt
                            if entry.elapsed >= entry.interval then
                                local stepDt = entry.elapsed
                                entry.elapsed = 0
                                local ok, err = pcall(entry.callback, stepDt)
                                if not ok then
                                    warn("[LouisHub/Scheduler] RenderStep '" .. tostring(entry.id) .. "' error:", err)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                self._stats.LastRenderMs = (os.clock() - t0) * 1000
            end)
        end

        function Scheduler:Destroy()
            self._started = false
            clearTable(self._heartbeatTasks)
            clearTable(self._renderTasks)
            self._janitor:Destroy()
        end

        return Scheduler
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Core/HookGuard (Scoped Function Wrapper & Restore Engine)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Core/HookGuard", function()
        local HookGuard = {}
        HookGuard.__index = HookGuard

        if type(GlobalEnv._LouisHubOriginalHooks) ~= "table" then
            GlobalEnv._LouisHubOriginalHooks = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        end
        local GlobalOriginals = GlobalEnv._LouisHubOriginalHooks

        function HookGuard.new(store)
            return setmetatable({
                _store = store,
                _wrapped = {},
                _disposed = false,
            }, HookGuard)
        end

        function HookGuard:IsBypassEnabled()
            if self._disposed or not self._store then
                return false
            end
            return self._store:Get("Meta.BypassMode") == true
        end

        function HookGuard:IsMonkeyPatchEnabled()
            if self._disposed or not self._store then
                return false
            end
            return self._store:Get("Meta.MonkeyPatchMode") == true
        end

        function HookGuard:GetTrueOriginal(targetTable, methodName)
            if type(targetTable) ~= "table" or type(methodName) ~= "string" then
                return nil
            end
            local modOriginals = GlobalOriginals[targetTable]
            if modOriginals and type(modOriginals[methodName]) == "function" then
                return modOriginals[methodName]
            end
            local cur = targetTable[methodName]
            if type(cur) == "function" then
                return cur
            end
            return nil
        end

        function HookGuard:WrapTagged(tag, targetTable, methodName, wrapperFactory)
            if self._disposed or type(targetTable) ~= "table" or type(methodName) ~= "string" then
                return false
            end
            local currentFn = targetTable[methodName]
            if type(currentFn) ~= "function" then
                return false
            end

            local modOriginals = GlobalOriginals[targetTable]
            if not modOriginals then
                modOriginals = {}
                GlobalOriginals[targetTable] = modOriginals
            end

            local trueOriginal = modOriginals[methodName]
            if type(trueOriginal) ~= "function" then
                trueOriginal = currentFn
                modOriginals[methodName] = trueOriginal
            end

            for _, existing in ipairs(self._wrapped) do
                if existing.tag == tag and existing.target == targetTable and existing.key == methodName then
                    return true
                end
            end

            local wrappedFn = wrapperFactory(trueOriginal, self)
            if type(wrappedFn) ~= "function" then
                return false
            end

            targetTable[methodName] = wrappedFn
            table.insert(self._wrapped, {
                tag = tag or "Scoped",
                target = targetTable,
                key = methodName,
                original = trueOriginal,
                wrapped = wrappedFn,
            })
            return true
        end

        function HookGuard:Wrap(targetTable, methodName, wrapperFactory)
            return self:WrapTagged("Scoped", targetTable, methodName, wrapperFactory)
        end

        function HookGuard:RestoreTag(tag)
            for i = #self._wrapped, 1, -1 do
                local entry = self._wrapped[i]
                if entry.tag == tag then
                    pcall(function()
                        if type(entry.target) == "table" and type(entry.original) == "function" then
                            if entry.target[entry.key] == entry.wrapped then
                                entry.target[entry.key] = entry.original
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                    table.remove(self._wrapped, i)
                end
            end
        end

        function HookGuard:Restore(targetTable, methodName)
            for i = #self._wrapped, 1, -1 do
                local entry = self._wrapped[i]
                if entry.target == targetTable and entry.key == methodName then
                    pcall(function()
                        if entry.target[entry.key] == entry.wrapped then
                            entry.target[entry.key] = entry.original
                        end
                    end)
                    table.remove(self._wrapped, i)
                end
            end
        end

        function HookGuard:RestoreAll()
            for i = #self._wrapped, 1, -1 do
                local entry = self._wrapped[i]
                self._wrapped[i] = nil
                pcall(function()
                    if type(entry.target) == "table" and type(entry.original) == "function" then
                        entry.target[entry.key] = entry.original
                    end
                end)
            end
        end

        function HookGuard:GetWrappedCount(tag)
            if not tag then
                return #self._wrapped
            end
            local count = 0
            for _, entry in ipairs(self._wrapped) do
                if entry.tag == tag then
                    count = count + 1
                end
            end
            return count
        end

        function HookGuard:Destroy()
            if self._disposed then
                return
            end
            self._disposed = true
            self:RestoreAll()
        end

        return HookGuard
    end)

    -- Inisialisasi awal Context Core
    local SchedulerClass = LouisHub.Require("Core/Scheduler")
    local scheduler = SchedulerClass.new("LouisHub")
    scheduler:Start()

    LouisHub.Context = {
        Scheduler = scheduler,
        Players = Players,
        RunService = RunService,
        UserInputService = UserInputService,
        HttpService = HttpService,
        GuiService = GuiService,
        Lighting = Lighting,
    }

    LouisHub.OnDestroy(function()
        pcall(function() scheduler:Destroy() end)
    end)

    print(string.format("[LouisHub v%s] Part 1/10 initialized (Core Foundation & Virtual Engine Ready)", LouisHub.Version))
end

do
    local GlobalEnv = (type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or _G
    local LouisHub = GlobalEnv.LouisHub

    if type(LouisHub) ~= "table" or not (LouisHub.PartLoaded and LouisHub.PartLoaded[1]) then
        error("[LouisHub] Part 1/10 must be executed before Part 2/10!", 2)
    end

    local HttpService = LouisHub.SafeService("HttpService")
    local Lighting = LouisHub.SafeService("Lighting")

    local function deepCopy(val)
        if type(val) ~= "table" then
            return val
        end
        local copy = {}
        for k, v in pairs(val) do
            copy[k] = deepCopy(v)
        end
        return copy
    end

    local function clearTable(tbl)
        if type(table.clear) == "function" then
            table.clear(tbl)
            return
        end
        for k in pairs(tbl) do
            tbl[k] = nil
        end
    end

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Core/Store (Reactive Config State & JSON Persistence)

            Goalkeeper = {

        local function hasFileApi()
            return type(writefile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function" and type(isfile) == "function"
        end

        local function ensureDirectories()
            if type(makefolder) ~= "function" or type(isfolder) ~= "function" then
                return
            end
            pcall(function()
                if not isfolder(BASE_FOLDER) then
                    makefolder(BASE_FOLDER)
                end
                if not isfolder(GAME_FOLDER) then
                    makefolder(GAME_FOLDER)
                end
            end)
        end

        local function sanitizeProfileName(name)
            local str = tostring(name or "Default"):gsub("[^%w_%-%s]", ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
            if str == "" then
                str = "Default"
            end
            return str
        end

        local function mergeDefaults(defaults, loaded)
            if type(loaded) ~= "table" then
                return deepCopy(defaults)
            end
            local result = {}
            for k, defVal in pairs(defaults) do
                local loadedVal = loaded[k]
                if type(defVal) == "table" then
                    result[k] = mergeDefaults(defVal, loadedVal)
                elseif loadedVal ~= nil and type(loadedVal) == type(defVal) then
                    result[k] = loadedVal
                else
                    result[k] = defVal
                end
            end
            return result
        end

        local function splitPath(path)
            local parts = {}
            for segment in string.gmatch(path, "[^%.]+") do
                table.insert(parts, segment)
            end
            return parts
        end

        local Store = {}
        Store.__index = Store

        function Store.new()
            local self = setmetatable({
                _config = deepCopy(DEFAULT_CONFIG),
                _memoryProfiles = {},
                _changedSignal = Signal.new(),
                _pathSignals = {},
                _saveScheduled = false,
                _destroyed = false,
                _fileSupported = hasFileApi(),
            }, Store)

            if self._fileSupported then
                ensureDirectories()
                local meta = self:_readJsonFile(META_FILE)
                if type(meta) == "table" and type(meta.LastProfile) == "string" then
                    self._config.Meta.ActiveProfile = sanitizeProfileName(meta.LastProfile)
                end
                if self._config.Meta.AutoLoad then
                    self:LoadConfig(self._config.Meta.ActiveProfile, true)
                end
            end

            self._config.UI.DashboardOpen = true
            return self
        end

        function Store:IsFilePersistenceAvailable()
            return self._fileSupported
        end

        function Store:_readJsonFile(filePath)
            if not self._fileSupported or not HttpService then
                return nil
            end
            local okExists, exists = pcall(isfile, filePath)
            if not okExists or not exists then
                return nil
            end
            local okRead, content = pcall(readfile, filePath)
            if not okRead or type(content) ~= "string" or content == "" then
                return nil
            end
            local okDecode, decoded = pcall(function()
                return HttpService:JSONDecode(content)
            end)
            if okDecode and type(decoded) == "table" then
                return decoded
            end
            return nil
        end

        function Store:_writeJsonFile(filePath, dataTable)
            if not self._fileSupported or not HttpService then
                return false
            end
            ensureDirectories()
            local okEncode, json = pcall(function()
                return HttpService:JSONEncode(dataTable)
            end)
            if not okEncode or type(json) ~= "string" then
                return false
            end
            return pcall(writefile, filePath, json) == true
        end

        function Store:GetProfilePath(profileName)
            return GAME_FOLDER .. "/" .. sanitizeProfileName(profileName) .. ".json"
        end

        function Store:GetDefaultConfig()
            return deepCopy(DEFAULT_CONFIG)
        end

        function Store:GetAll()
            return self._config
        end

        function Store:Get(path)
            local parts = splitPath(path)
            local cur = self._config
            for i = 1, #parts do
                if type(cur) ~= "table" then
                    return nil
                end
                cur = cur[parts[i]]
            end
            return cur
        end

        function Store:_scheduleAutoSave()
            if self._destroyed or not self._config.Meta.AutoSave or self._saveScheduled then
                return
            end
            self._saveScheduled = true
            task.delay(0.5, function()
                if self._destroyed then
                    return
                end
                self._saveScheduled = false
                self:SaveConfig(self._config.Meta.ActiveProfile)
            end)
        end

        function Store:Set(path, value, silent)
            if self._destroyed then
                return false
            end
            local parts = splitPath(path)
            if #parts == 0 then
                return false
            end
            local cur = self._config
            for i = 1, #parts - 1 do
                local key = parts[i]
                if type(cur[key]) ~= "table" then
                    cur[key] = {}
                end
                cur = cur[key]
            end
            local leaf = parts[#parts]
            local oldValue = cur[leaf]
            if oldValue == value then
                return false
            end
            cur[leaf] = value

            if not silent then
                self._changedSignal:Fire(path, value, oldValue)
                local pathSig = self._pathSignals[path]
                if pathSig then
                    pathSig:Fire(value, oldValue, path)
                end
            end

            if path ~= "UI.DashboardOpen" and path ~= "UI.DashboardMinimized" then
                self:_scheduleAutoSave()
            end
            return true
        end

        function Store:OnChanged(path, callback)
            if type(path) == "function" then
                return self._changedSignal:Connect(path)
            end
            local sig = self._pathSignals[path]
            if not sig then
                sig = Signal.new()
                self._pathSignals[path] = sig
            end
            return sig:Connect(callback)
        end

        function Store:SaveConfig(profileName)
            local safeName = sanitizeProfileName(profileName or self._config.Meta.ActiveProfile)
            self._config.Meta.ActiveProfile = safeName
            local snapshot = deepCopy(self._config)
            self._memoryProfiles[safeName] = snapshot

            if self._fileSupported then
                local savedMain = self:_writeJsonFile(self:GetProfilePath(safeName), snapshot)
                self:_writeJsonFile(META_FILE, { LastProfile = safeName })
                return savedMain, safeName
            end
            return true, safeName
        end

        function Store:LoadConfig(profileName, silentMissing)
            local safeName = sanitizeProfileName(profileName or self._config.Meta.ActiveProfile)
            local loaded = nil
            if self._fileSupported then
                loaded = self:_readJsonFile(self:GetProfilePath(safeName))
            end
            if not loaded then
                loaded = self._memoryProfiles[safeName]
            end
            if not loaded then
                if not silentMissing then
                    return false, "Profile not found: " .. safeName
                end
                return false, "Not found"
            end

            local merged = mergeDefaults(DEFAULT_CONFIG, loaded)
            merged.Meta.ActiveProfile = safeName
            self._config = merged
            self._memoryProfiles[safeName] = deepCopy(merged)

            if self._fileSupported then
                self:_writeJsonFile(META_FILE, { LastProfile = safeName })
            end

            self._changedSignal:Fire("*", merged, nil)
            for path, sig in pairs(self._pathSignals) do
                sig:Fire(self:Get(path), nil, path)
            end
            return true, safeName
        end

        function Store:ListProfiles()
            local found = { Default = true }
            for name in pairs(self._memoryProfiles) do
                found[name] = true
            end
            if self._fileSupported and type(listfiles) == "function" then
                ensureDirectories()
                local ok, files = pcall(listfiles, GAME_FOLDER)
                if ok and type(files) == "table" then
                    for _, filePath in ipairs(files) do
                        local fileName = tostring(filePath):match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
                        if fileName and fileName ~= "_meta" then
                            found[fileName] = true
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            local list = {}
            for name in pairs(found) do
                table.insert(list, name)
            end
            table.sort(list)
            return list
        end

        function Store:DeleteProfile(profileName)
            local safeName = sanitizeProfileName(profileName)
            if safeName == "Default" then
                return false, "Cannot delete Default profile"
            end
            self._memoryProfiles[safeName] = nil
            if self._fileSupported and type(delfile) == "function" then
                local filePath = self:GetProfilePath(safeName)
                local okExists, exists = pcall(isfile, filePath)
                if okExists and exists then
                    pcall(delfile, filePath)
                end
            end
            if self._config.Meta.ActiveProfile == safeName then
                self._config.Meta.ActiveProfile = "Default"
                self:SaveConfig("Default")
            end
            return true
        end

        function Store:ResetToDefaults()
            local activeProfile = self._config.Meta.ActiveProfile or "Default"
            local keepLang = self._config.Meta.Language or "ID"
            self._config = deepCopy(DEFAULT_CONFIG)
            self._config.Meta.ActiveProfile = activeProfile
            self._config.Meta.Language = keepLang
            self._changedSignal:Fire("*", self._config, nil)
            for path, sig in pairs(self._pathSignals) do
                sig:Fire(self:Get(path), nil, path)
            end
            self:SaveConfig(activeProfile)
        end

        function Store:Destroy()
            if self._destroyed then
                return
            end
            self._destroyed = true
            self._saveScheduled = false
            self._changedSignal:Destroy()
            for _, sig in pairs(self._pathSignals) do
                sig:Destroy()
            end
            clearTable(self._pathSignals)
        end

        return Store
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Core/PerformanceOptimizer (Zero-Lag & Potato-Phone Friendly)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Core/PerformanceOptimizer", function(Require)
        local Janitor = Require("Core/Janitor")

        local PerformanceOptimizer = {}
        PerformanceOptimizer.__index = PerformanceOptimizer

        function PerformanceOptimizer.new(store, scheduler)
            local self = setmetatable({
                _janitor = Janitor.new(),
                _store = store,
                _scheduler = scheduler,
                _ball = nil,
                _gfxApplied = false,
                _savedLighting = nil,
                _savedEffects = {},
                _lastIsHolding = nil,
            }, PerformanceOptimizer)

            self._janitor:Add(store:OnChanged("Meta.PerformanceMode", function()
                self:Apply(true)
            end))
            self._janitor:Add(store:OnChanged("Meta.LowGraphicsBoost", function()
                self:Apply(true)
            end))
            self._janitor:Add(store:OnChanged("*", function()
                self:Apply(true)
            end))

            self:Apply(true)
            return self
        end

        function PerformanceOptimizer:SetBallBridge(ballBridge)
            self._ball = ballBridge
            self:Apply(true)
        end

        function PerformanceOptimizer:IsPerformanceMode()
            return self._store and self._store:Get("Meta.PerformanceMode") == true
        end

        function PerformanceOptimizer:GetAdaptiveIntervals()
            local perfOn = self:IsPerformanceMode()
            local isHolding = (self._ball and type(self._ball.IsLocalOwner) == "function") and self._ball:IsLocalOwner() or false

            if perfOn then
                return {
                    IsHolding = isHolding,
                    ActorScanInterval = isHolding and 0.09 or 0.08,
                    DefenderScanInterval = isHolding and 0.08 or 0.12,
                    VisualsInterval = isHolding and 0.06 or 0.033,
                    UIInterval = isHolding and 0.25 or 0.18,
                    SolverMaxAge = isHolding and 0.12 or 0.16,
                }
            end

            return {
                IsHolding = isHolding,
                ActorScanInterval = 0.035,
                DefenderScanInterval = 0.04,
                VisualsInterval = 0.016,
                UIInterval = 0.05,
                SolverMaxAge = 0.08,
            }
        end

        function PerformanceOptimizer:_applyGraphicsBoost(enable)
            if not Lighting then
                return
            end
            if enable and not self._gfxApplied then
                self._gfxApplied = true
                self._savedLighting = {
                    GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
                    ShadowSoftness = Lighting.ShadowSoftness,
                    EnvironmentDiffuseScale = Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale,
                    EnvironmentSpecularScale = Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale,
                }
                pcall(function()
                    Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
                    Lighting.ShadowSoftness = 0
                    Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0.2
                    Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0.2
                end)

                clearTable(self._savedEffects)
                for _, child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("PostEffect") then
                        self._savedEffects[child] = child.Enabled
                        pcall(function()
                            child.Enabled = false
                        end)
                    end
                end
            elseif not enable and self._gfxApplied then
                self._gfxApplied = false
                if self._savedLighting then
                    pcall(function()
                        Lighting.GlobalShadows = self._savedLighting.GlobalShadows
                        Lighting.ShadowSoftness = self._savedLighting.ShadowSoftness
                        Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = self._savedLighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale
                        Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = self._savedLighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale
                    end)
                    self._savedLighting = nil
                end
                for eff, wasEnabled in pairs(self._savedEffects) do
                    if eff and eff.Parent then
                        pcall(function()
                            eff.Enabled = wasEnabled
                        end)
                    end
                end
                clearTable(self._savedEffects)
            end
        end

        function PerformanceOptimizer:Apply(forceUpdate)
            local perfOn = self:IsPerformanceMode()
            local gfxBoost = perfOn and (self._store:Get("Meta.LowGraphicsBoost") ~= false)
            self:_applyGraphicsBoost(gfxBoost)

            local intervals = self:GetAdaptiveIntervals()
            if not forceUpdate and self._lastIsHolding == intervals.IsHolding then
                return
            end
            self._lastIsHolding = intervals.IsHolding

            if self._scheduler then
                self._scheduler:SetTaskInterval("LouisHub_Visuals", intervals.VisualsInterval)
                self._scheduler:SetTaskInterval("LouisHub_UI", intervals.UIInterval)
            end
        end

        function PerformanceOptimizer:Destroy()
            self:_applyGraphicsBoost(false)
            self._ball = nil
            self._janitor:Destroy()
        end

        return PerformanceOptimizer
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- BOOTSTRAP PART 2
    --========================================================================
    local ctx = LouisHub.Context
    local StoreClass = LouisHub.Require("Core/Store")
    local HookGuardClass = LouisHub.Require("Core/HookGuard")
    local PerfOptClass = LouisHub.Require("Core/PerformanceOptimizer")

    local store = StoreClass.new()
    local hookGuard = HookGuardClass.new(store)
    local perfOpt = PerfOptClass.new(store, ctx.Scheduler)

    ctx.Store = store
    ctx.HookGuard = hookGuard
    ctx.Performance = perfOpt

    LouisHub.OnDestroy(function()
        pcall(function() perfOpt:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() hookGuard:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() store:Destroy() end)
    end)

    LouisHub.PartLoaded[2] = true
    print(string.format("[LouisHub v%s] Part 2/10 initialized (Store, HookGuard & Anti-Lag Optimizer Ready | Anti-Lag: %s)",
        LouisHub.Version,
        tostring(store:Get("Meta.PerformanceMode"))
    ))
end

                TOAST_LOADED = "Profil dimuat: %s",

        local function humanizeFallbackKey(rawKey)
            local cleaned = tostring(rawKey)
                :gsub("^LBL_", "")
                :gsub("^SUB_", "")
                :gsub("^SEC_", "")
                :gsub("^BTN_", "")
                :gsub("^TAB_", "")
                :gsub("^Q_", "")
                :gsub("_", " ")
            cleaned = cleaned:gsub("(%a)([%w']*)", function(first, rest)
                return string.upper(first) .. string.lower(rest)
            end)
            return cleaned
        end

        local LocaleController = {}
        LocaleController.__index = LocaleController

        function LocaleController.new(store)
            return setmetatable({ _store = store }, LocaleController)
        end

        function LocaleController:GetLang()
            local lang = self._store and self._store:Get("Meta.Language") or "ID"
            if lang ~= "EN" and lang ~= "ID" then
                return "ID"
            end
            return lang
        end

        function LocaleController:T(key, ...)
            local lang = self:GetLang()
            local dict = STRINGS[lang] or STRINGS.EN
            local template = dict[key] or STRINGS.EN[key]
            if not template then
                template = humanizeFallbackKey(key)
            end
            if select("#", ...) > 0 then
                local ok, formatted = pcall(string.format, template, ...)
                if ok then
                    return formatted
                end
            end
            return template
        end

        return LocaleController
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- BOOTSTRAP PART 3
    --========================================================================
    local ctx = LouisHub.Context
    local LocaleClass = LouisHub.Require("Core/Locale")
    local locale = LocaleClass.new(ctx.Store)
    ctx.Locale = locale

    LouisHub.PartLoaded[3] = true
    print(string.format("[LouisHub v%s] Part 3/10 initialized (Clean Bilingual Locale Ready | Lang: %s)",
        LouisHub.Version,
        locale:GetLang()
    ))
end

do
    local GlobalEnv = (type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or _G
    local LouisHub = GlobalEnv.LouisHub

    if type(LouisHub) ~= "table"
        or not (LouisHub.PartLoaded and LouisHub.PartLoaded[1] and LouisHub.PartLoaded[2] and LouisHub.PartLoaded[3])
    then
        error("[LouisHub] Parts 1/10, 2/10, and 3/10 must be executed before Part 4/10!", 2)
    end

    local Players = LouisHub.SafeService("Players")
    local ReplicatedStorage = LouisHub.SafeService("ReplicatedStorage")

    local function clearTable(tbl)
        if type(table.clear) == "function" then
            table.clear(tbl)
            return
        end
        for k in pairs(tbl) do
            tbl[k] = nil
        end
    end

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Bridge/GameModules
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Bridge/GameModules", function()
        local GameModules = {}
        local cache = {}
        local failedAt = {}

        local function findByPath(root, dottedPath)
            local cur = root
            for segment in string.gmatch(dottedPath, "[^%.]+") do
                if not cur then
                    return nil
                end
                cur = cur:FindFirstChild(segment)
            end
            return cur
        end

        function GameModules.Get(dottedPath, forceRetry)
            if cache[dottedPath] ~= nil then
                return cache[dottedPath]
            end
            local now = os.clock()
            if not forceRetry and failedAt[dottedPath] and (now - failedAt[dottedPath] < 1.5) then
                return nil
            end
            if not ReplicatedStorage then
                return nil
            end
            local inst = findByPath(ReplicatedStorage, dottedPath)
            if not inst or not inst:IsA("ModuleScript") then
                failedAt[dottedPath] = now
                return nil
            end
            local ok, result = pcall(require, inst)
            if ok and result ~= nil then
                cache[dottedPath] = result
                failedAt[dottedPath] = nil
                return result
            end
            failedAt[dottedPath] = now
            return nil
        end

        function GameModules.GetRemote(groupName, remoteName)
            local remotesMod = GameModules.Get("Modules.Remotes")
            if remotesMod and type(remotesMod.Get) == "function" then
                local ok, remote = pcall(remotesMod.Get, groupName, remoteName)
                if ok and remote then
                    return remote
                end
            end
            if ReplicatedStorage then
                local remFolder = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                local grp = remFolder and remFolder:FindFirstChild(groupName)
                local rem = grp and grp:FindFirstChild(remoteName)
                if rem then
                    return rem
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        function GameModules.GetRemoteGroup(groupName)
            local remotesMod = GameModules.Get("Modules.Remotes")
            if remotesMod and type(remotesMod.GetGroup) == "function" then
                local ok, group = pcall(remotesMod.GetGroup, groupName)
                if ok and type(group) == "table" then
                    return group
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        function GameModules.GetBallCommandRemotes()
            local list = {}
            if not ReplicatedStorage then
                return list
            end
            local remFolder = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            local ballRemotes = remFolder and remFolder:FindFirstChild("Ball")
            if not ballRemotes then
                return list
            end
            for _, child in ipairs(ballRemotes:GetChildren()) do
                if child.Name ~= "State" and (child:IsA("RemoteEvent") or child:IsA("UnreliableRemoteEvent")) then
                    table.insert(list, child)
                end
            end
            return list
        end

        function GameModules.ClearCache()
            clearTable(cache)
            clearTable(failedAt)
        end

        GameModules.Paths = {
            ActionChargeState = "Modules.Actions.ActionChargeState",
            ActionCommands = "Modules.Actions.ActionCommands",
            ActionLocks = "Modules.Actions.ActionLocks",
            ActionMotion = "Modules.Actions.ActionMotion",
            ActionMovement = "Modules.Actions.ActionMovement",
            ActionRemoteProtocol = "Modules.Actions.ActionRemoteProtocol",
            AimFacing = "Modules.Actions.AimFacing",
            AimSolver = "Modules.Actions.AimSolver",
            AssistedPass = "Modules.Actions.AssistedPass",
            BallHitStamina = "Modules.Actions.BallHitStamina",
            BicycleKick = "Modules.Actions.BicycleKick",
            ChargePathVelocity = "Modules.Actions.ChargePathVelocity",
            Dodge = "Modules.Actions.Dodge",
            EndpointAim = "Modules.Actions.EndpointAim",
            GoalkeeperActions = "Modules.Actions.GoalkeeperActions",
            GoalkeeperDive = "Modules.Actions.GoalkeeperDive",
            GoalkeeperDiveAssist = "Modules.Actions.GoalkeeperDiveAssist",
            Headbutt = "Modules.Actions.Headbutt",
            Kick = "Modules.Actions.Kick",
            KickCore = "Modules.Actions.KickCore",
            KickPowerups = "Modules.Actions.KickPowerups",
            Pass = "Modules.Actions.Pass",
            RainbowFlick = "Modules.Actions.RainbowFlick",
            ServerCooldownGrace = "Modules.Actions.ServerCooldownGrace",
            Shoot = "Modules.Actions.Shoot",
            ShotAimAssist = "Modules.Actions.ShotAimAssist",
            SlideTackle = "Modules.Actions.SlideTackle",
            Sprint = "Modules.Actions.Sprint",
            Throw = "Modules.Actions.Throw",

            BallIds = "Modules.Ball.BallIds",
            BallCarry = "Modules.Ball.Carry",
            FlightSampling = "Modules.Ball.FlightSampling",
            LandingIndicator = "Modules.Ball.LandingIndicator",
            BallOwnership = "Modules.Ball.Ownership",

            BallReceiving = "Modules.Ball.Receiving",
            VolleyLockOn = "Modules.Ball.VolleyLockOn",

            Actors = "Modules.Characters.Actors",
            CharacterControllers = "Modules.Characters.CharacterControllers",
            CharacterMotion = "Modules.Characters.CharacterMotion",
            CombatHitboxes = "Modules.Characters.CombatHitboxes",

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Bridge/MatchBridge
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Bridge/MatchBridge", function(Require)
        local Janitor = Require("Core/Janitor")
        local Signal = Require("Core/Signal")
        local GameModules = Require("Bridge/GameModules")

        local MatchBridge = {}
        MatchBridge.__index = MatchBridge

        function MatchBridge.new()
            return setmetatable({
                _janitor = Janitor.new(),
                _localPlayer = Players and Players.LocalPlayer,
                _matchState = nil,
                _isGoalkeeper = false,
                _localTeamName = nil,
                _playerTeams = {},
                _playerPositions = {},
                _isRainbowFlickUnlocked = false,
                RoleChanged = Signal.new(),
                MatchStateChanged = Signal.new(),
                GoalAnnounced = Signal.new(),
            }, MatchBridge)
        end

        function MatchBridge:_updateGoalkeeperFlag()
            local isGk = false
            local gkRole = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientGoalkeeperRole)
            if gkRole and type(gkRole.IsGoalkeeper) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(gkRole.IsGoalkeeper)
                if ok and res == true then
                    isGk = true
                end
            end
            if not isGk and self._localPlayer and self._localPlayer.Character then
                local ms = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.MatchSettings)
                local attrName = (ms and ms.Constants and ms.Constants.GoalkeeperRoleAttributeName) or "ActiveGoalkeeper"
                isGk = self._localPlayer.Character:GetAttribute(attrName) == true
            end
            if self._isGoalkeeper ~= isGk then
                self._isGoalkeeper = isGk
                self.RoleChanged:Fire(isGk)
            end
        end

        function MatchBridge:_bindCharacter(char)
            self._janitor:Remove("CharRoleAttr")
            self:_updateGoalkeeperFlag()
            if not char then
                return
            end
            local ms = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.MatchSettings)
            local attrName = (ms and ms.Constants and ms.Constants.GoalkeeperRoleAttributeName) or "ActiveGoalkeeper"
            self._janitor:Connect(char:GetAttributeChangedSignal(attrName), function()
                self:_updateGoalkeeperFlag()
            end, "CharRoleAttr")
        end

        function MatchBridge:Start()
            if not self._localPlayer and Players then
                self._localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
            end
            if self._localPlayer then
                if self._localPlayer.Character then
                    self:_bindCharacter(self._localPlayer.Character)
                end
                self._janitor:Connect(self._localPlayer.CharacterAdded, function(char)
                    self:_bindCharacter(char)
                end, "CharAdded")
                self._janitor:Connect(self._localPlayer.CharacterRemoving, function()
                    self:_bindCharacter(nil)
                end, "CharRemoving")
            end

            local matchStateRemote = GameModules.GetRemote("Match", "State")
            if matchStateRemote and matchStateRemote.OnClientEvent then
                self._janitor:Connect(matchStateRemote.OnClientEvent, function(payload)
                    if type(payload) ~= "table" then
                        return
                    end
                    if payload.LocalTeamName ~= nil or payload.PlayerTeams ~= nil or payload.PlayerPositions ~= nil then
                        self._matchState = payload
                        if payload.LocalTeamName ~= nil then
                            self._localTeamName = payload.LocalTeamName
                        end
                        if type(payload.PlayerTeams) == "table" then
                            self._playerTeams = payload.PlayerTeams
                        end
                        if type(payload.PlayerPositions) == "table" then
                            self._playerPositions = payload.PlayerPositions
                        end
                        if payload.IsRainbowFlickUnlocked ~= nil then
                            self._isRainbowFlickUnlocked = payload.IsRainbowFlickUnlocked == true
                        end
                        self.MatchStateChanged:Fire(payload)
                    elseif payload.Kind == nil then
                        self._matchState = payload
                        self.MatchStateChanged:Fire(payload)
                    end
                end, "MatchStateRemote")
            end

            local goalRemote = GameModules.GetRemote("Ball", "GoalAnnouncement")
            if goalRemote and goalRemote.OnClientEvent then
                self._janitor:Connect(goalRemote.OnClientEvent, function(payload)
                    self.GoalAnnounced:Fire(payload)
                end, "GoalAnnouncementRemote")
            end
        end

        function MatchBridge:Refresh()
            self:_updateGoalkeeperFlag()
        end

        function MatchBridge:IsGoalkeeper()
            self:_updateGoalkeeperFlag()
            return self._isGoalkeeper
        end

        function MatchBridge:GetRoleName()
            return self:IsGoalkeeper() and "Goalkeeper" or "Striker"
        end

        function MatchBridge:GetLocalTeamName()
            local actorTeams = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientActorTeams)
            if actorTeams and type(actorTeams.GetActorTeamName) == "function" and self._localPlayer then
                local ok, team = pcall(actorTeams.GetActorTeamName, self._localPlayer)
                if ok and team then
                    return team
                end
            end
            if self._localTeamName then
                return self._localTeamName
            end
            if self._localPlayer then
                return self._playerTeams[tostring(self._localPlayer.UserId)]
            end
            return nil
        end

        function MatchBridge:GetActorTeamName(actor)
            local actorTeams = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientActorTeams)
            if actorTeams and type(actorTeams.GetActorTeamName) == "function" and actor then
                local ok, team = pcall(actorTeams.GetActorTeamName, actor)
                if ok and team then
                    return team
                end
            end
            local actors = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Actors)
            local uid = actors and actors.GetUserId and actors.GetUserId(actor) or (actor and actor.UserId)
            if uid then
                return self._playerTeams[tostring(uid)]
            end
            return nil
        end

        function MatchBridge:GetActorPositionName(userId)
            if userId == nil then
                return nil
            end
            return self._playerPositions[tostring(userId)]
        end

        function MatchBridge:GetOpposingTeamName(teamName)
            local team = teamName or self:GetLocalTeamName()
            if not team then
                return nil
            end
            local ms = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.MatchSettings)
            if ms and type(ms.GetOpposingTeamName) == "function" then
                return ms.GetOpposingTeamName(team)
            end
            return (team == "Team1") and "Team2" or "Team1"
        end

        function MatchBridge:GetOpposingGoalParts()
            local tutorial = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientTutorialSession)
            if tutorial and type(tutorial.GetGoalPart) == "function" then
                local ok, tutGoal = pcall(tutorial.GetGoalPart)
                if ok and tutGoal then
                    return { tutGoal }
                end
            end
            local practice = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientPracticeSession)
            if practice and type(practice.GetGoalParts) == "function" then
                local ok, pracGoals = pcall(practice.GetGoalParts)
                if ok and pracGoals and #pracGoals > 0 then
                    return pracGoals
                end
            end
            local myTeam = self:GetLocalTeamName()
            if not myTeam then
                return {}
            end
            local oppTeam = self:GetOpposingTeamName(myTeam)
            local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
            local data = map and map:FindFirstChild("Data")
            local teamFolder = data and oppTeam and data:FindFirstChild(oppTeam)
            local goal = teamFolder and teamFolder:FindFirstChild("Goal")
            if goal and goal:IsA("BasePart") then
                return { goal }
            end
            return {}
        end

        function MatchBridge:GetDefendedGoalPart()
            local practice = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientPracticeSession)
            if practice and type(practice.GetDefendedGoalPart) == "function" then
                local ok, pGoal = pcall(practice.GetDefendedGoalPart)
                if ok and pGoal then
                    return pGoal
                end
            end
            local myTeam = self:GetLocalTeamName()
            if not myTeam then
                return nil
            end
            local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
            local data = map and map:FindFirstChild("Data")
            local teamFolder = data and myTeam and data:FindFirstChild(myTeam)
            local goal = teamFolder and teamFolder:FindFirstChild("Goal")
            if goal and goal:IsA("BasePart") then
                return goal
            end
            return nil
        end

        function MatchBridge:IsKickoffFrozen()
            local kf = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientKickoffFreeze)
            if kf and type(kf.IsFrozen) == "function" then
                local ok, frozen = pcall(kf.IsFrozen)
                if ok then
                    return frozen == true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        function MatchBridge:IsRainbowFlickUnlocked()
            if self._isRainbowFlickUnlocked then
                return true
            end
            local controls = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Controls)
            if controls and type(controls.IsRainbowFlickUnlocked) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(controls.IsRainbowFlickUnlocked)
                if ok and res == true then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        function MatchBridge:AreItemsEnabled()
            local ms = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.MatchSettings)
            if ms and type(ms.AreItemsEnabled) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(ms.AreItemsEnabled)
                if ok then
                    return res == true
                end
            end
            return true
        end

        function MatchBridge:AreOwnGoalsEnabled()
            local ms = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.MatchSettings)
            if ms and type(ms.AreOwnGoalsEnabled) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(ms.AreOwnGoalsEnabled)
                if ok then
                    return res == true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        function MatchBridge:GetMatchState()
            return self._matchState
        end

        function MatchBridge:Destroy()
            self.RoleChanged:Destroy()
            self.MatchStateChanged:Destroy()
            self.GoalAnnounced:Destroy()
            self._janitor:Destroy()
        end

        return MatchBridge
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Bridge/BallBridge
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Bridge/BallBridge", function(Require)
        local Janitor = Require("Core/Janitor")
        local Signal = Require("Core/Signal")
        local GameModules = Require("Bridge/GameModules")

        local BallBridge = {}
        BallBridge.__index = BallBridge

        function BallBridge.new(perfOpt)
            return setmetatable({
                _janitor = Janitor.new(),
                _localPlayer = Players and Players.LocalPlayer,
                _perf = perfOpt,
                _lastGoalCrossing = nil,
                _cachedSnap = nil,
                _cachedSnapAt = 0,
                PresentationEvent = Signal.new(),
                BallStateReceived = Signal.new(),
            }, BallBridge)
        end

        function BallBridge:Start()
            local function onPresentation(eventType, userId, a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6, a7, a8, a9, a10, a11, a12, a13, a14, a15, a16, a17, a18, a19, a20, a21)
                if type(eventType) ~= "string" then
                    return
                end
                if eventType == "KickChargeStarted" or eventType == "KickChargeUpdated" or eventType == "KickReleased" or eventType == "KickChargeCanceled" then
                    self.PresentationEvent:Fire({
                        EventType = eventType,
                        UserId = userId,
                        PassType = a1,
                        AnimDirection = a2,
                        ChargeSeconds = a3,
                        AimDirection = a4,
                        ReportedVelocity = a6,
                        StartedAt = a7,
                        IsActive = a8 == true,
                        ActionId = a9,
                        TargetPosition = a10,
                        PresentationId = a11,
                        TargetUserId = a12,
                        Sequence = a13,
                        IsBicycleKick = a14 == true,
                        KickDirection = a15,
                        Curve = a16,
                        BallId = a19,
                        BlockedGoalTeamName = a20,
                        ExtraData = a21,
                        ReceivedAt = os.clock(),
                    })
                elseif eventType == "TackleStarted" then
                    self.PresentationEvent:Fire({
                        EventType = eventType,
                        UserId = userId,
                        DurationSeconds = a1,
                        AimDirection = a2,
                        StartedAt = a7,
                        ReceivedAt = os.clock(),
                    })
                elseif eventType == "GoalkeeperDiveStarted" or eventType == "RainbowFlickStarted" then
                    self.PresentationEvent:Fire({
                        EventType = eventType,
                        UserId = userId,
                        Arg1 = a1,
                        StartedAt = a7 or a1,
                        ReceivedAt = os.clock(),
                    })
                end
            end

            local teRemote = GameModules.GetRemote("Ball", "TackleEffect")
            if teRemote and teRemote.OnClientEvent then
                self._janitor:Connect(teRemote.OnClientEvent, onPresentation, "BallTackleEffect")
            end

            local apRemote = GameModules.GetRemote("Ball", "ActionPresentationUpdate")
            if apRemote and apRemote.OnClientEvent then
                self._janitor:Connect(apRemote.OnClientEvent, onPresentation, "BallActionPres")
            end

            local stateRemote = GameModules.GetRemote("Ball", "State")
            if stateRemote and stateRemote.OnClientEvent then
                self._janitor:Connect(stateRemote.OnClientEvent, function(payload)
                    self._cachedSnap = nil
                    if type(payload) ~= "table" then
                        return
                    end
                    if payload.GoalCrossingEvaluated and payload.GoalCrossingPosition then
                        self._lastGoalCrossing = {
                            BallId = payload.BallId,
                            Position = payload.GoalCrossingPosition,
                            Time = payload.GoalCrossingTime,
                            GoalPart = payload.GoalCrossingGoalPart,
                            ReceivedAt = os.clock(),
                        }
                    elseif payload.Kind == "Owned" or payload.Kind == "Removed" then
                        self._lastGoalCrossing = nil
                    end
                    self.BallStateReceived:Fire(payload)
                end, "BallStateEvent")
            end
        end

        function BallBridge:GetLastServerGoalCrossing()
            return self._lastGoalCrossing
        end

        function BallBridge:GetServerTime()
            local smoothing = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientMotionSmoothing)
            if smoothing and type(smoothing.GetSmoothedServerTime) == "function" then
                local ok, t = pcall(smoothing.GetSmoothedServerTime)
                if ok and type(t) == "number" then
                    return t
                end
            end
            local ok, srvTime = pcall(function()
                return workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
            end)
            return (ok and srvTime) or os.clock()
        end

        function BallBridge:GetRenderer()
            return GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientBallRenderer)
        end

        function BallBridge:GetBallInstance()
            local renderer = self:GetRenderer()
            if renderer and type(renderer.GetBall) == "function" then
                local ok, b = pcall(renderer.GetBall)
                if ok then
                    return b
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        function BallBridge:GetMatchBallId()
            local renderer = self:GetRenderer()
            if renderer and type(renderer.GetMatchBallId) == "function" then
                local ok, id = pcall(renderer.GetMatchBallId)
                if ok and id then
                    return id
                end
            end
            return "MainMatch"
        end

        function BallBridge:GetOwnerUserId()
            local renderer = self:GetRenderer()
            if renderer and type(renderer.GetOwnedUserId) == "function" then
                local ok, uid = pcall(renderer.GetOwnedUserId)
                if ok then
                    return uid
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        function BallBridge:IsLocalOwner()
            if not self._localPlayer then
                self._localPlayer = Players and Players.LocalPlayer
            end
            if not self._localPlayer then
                return false
            end
            local renderer = self:GetRenderer()
            if renderer and type(renderer.IsBoundBallOwnedBy) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(renderer.IsBoundBallOwnedBy, self._localPlayer.UserId)
                if ok then
                    return res == true
                end
            end
            return self:GetOwnerUserId() == self._localPlayer.UserId
        end

        function BallBridge:GetPosition()
            local renderer = self:GetRenderer()
            if renderer and type(renderer.GetPosition) == "function" then
                local ok, pos = pcall(renderer.GetPosition)
                if ok and pos then
                    return pos
                end
            end
            local ball = self:GetBallInstance()
            return ball and ball.Position or nil
        end

        function BallBridge:GetVelocity()
            local renderer = self:GetRenderer()
            if renderer and type(renderer.GetVelocity) == "function" then
                local ok, vel = pcall(renderer.GetVelocity)
                if ok and vel then
                    return vel
                end
            end
            return Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end

        function BallBridge:GetAuthoritativeState()
            local renderer = self:GetRenderer()
            if renderer and type(renderer.GetAuthoritativeMovementState) == "function" then
                local ok, state, boundaryWorld = pcall(renderer.GetAuthoritativeMovementState)
                if ok and state then
                    return state, boundaryWorld
                end
            end
            if renderer and type(renderer.GetMovementState) == "function" then
                local ok, state, boundaryWorld = pcall(renderer.GetMovementState)
                if ok then
                    return state, boundaryWorld
                end
            end
            return nil, nil
        end

        function BallBridge:GetChargeBoundaryWorld(teamOrPlayer, ballId)
            local preview = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientChargePathPreview)
            local targetId = ballId or self:GetMatchBallId()
            if preview and type(preview.GetChargeBoundaryWorld) == "function" then
                local ok, world = pcall(preview.GetChargeBoundaryWorld, teamOrPlayer or self._localPlayer, targetId)
                if ok and world then
                    return world
                end
            end
            local _, authWorld = self:GetAuthoritativeState()
            if authWorld then
                return authWorld
            end
            local fbw = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.FieldBoundaryWorld)
            if fbw and type(fbw.GetBoundaryWorld) == "function" then
                local ok, world = pcall(fbw.GetBoundaryWorld)
                if ok and world then
                    return world
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        function BallBridge:GetVolleyLockUserId()
            local renderer = self:GetRenderer()
            if renderer and type(renderer.GetVolleyLockUserId) == "function" then
                local ok, uid = pcall(renderer.GetVolleyLockUserId)
                if ok then
                    return uid
                end
            end
            local state = self:GetAuthoritativeState()
            return state and state.VolleyUserId or nil
        end

        function BallBridge:GetBallRadius()
            local physics = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.BallPhysics)
            return (physics and physics.BallRadius) or 1
        end

        function BallBridge:GetSnapshot(maxAge)
            local now = os.clock()
            local isHolding = self:IsLocalOwner()
            local perfOn = self._perf and self._perf:IsPerformanceMode()
            local ttl = maxAge or (isHolding and (perfOn and 0.045 or 0.025) or 0.01)

            if self._cachedSnap and (now - self._cachedSnapAt) < ttl then
                return self._cachedSnap
            end

            local state, boundary = self:GetAuthoritativeState()
            local pos = self:GetPosition()
            local vel = self:GetVelocity()
            local ownerId = self:GetOwnerUserId()
            local snap = {
                BallId = self:GetMatchBallId(),
                BallPart = self:GetBallInstance(),
                Position = pos,
                Velocity = vel,
                Speed = vel and vel.Magnitude or 0,
                OwnerUserId = ownerId,
                IsLocalOwner = isHolding,
                VolleyUserId = self:GetVolleyLockUserId(),
                Mode = state and state.Mode or (ownerId and "Owned" or "Unknown"),
                Spin = state and state.Spin or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
                State = state,
                BoundaryWorld = boundary,
                ServerTime = self:GetServerTime(),
            }
            self._cachedSnap = snap
            self._cachedSnapAt = now
            return snap
        end

        function BallBridge:Destroy()
            self.PresentationEvent:Destroy()
            self.BallStateReceived:Destroy()
            self._janitor:Destroy()
        end

        return BallBridge
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Bridge/PlayerBridge (Ultra-Light Actor & Multi-Defender Scanner)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Bridge/PlayerBridge", function(Require)
        local GameModules = Require("Bridge/GameModules")

        local PlayerBridge = {}
        PlayerBridge.__index = PlayerBridge

        function PlayerBridge.new(matchBridge, store, perfOpt)
            return setmetatable({
                _localPlayer = Players and Players.LocalPlayer,
                _matchBridge = matchBridge,
                _store = store,
                _perf = perfOpt,
                _cachedActors = nil,
                _cachedActorsAt = 0,
                _cachedDefenders = {},
                _cachedDefendersAt = 0,
            }, PlayerBridge)
        end

        function PlayerBridge:InvalidateActorsCache()
            self._cachedActors = nil
            self._cachedActorsAt = 0
            clearTable(self._cachedDefenders)
            self._cachedDefendersAt = 0
        end

        function PlayerBridge:GetLocalPlayer()
            if not self._localPlayer and Players then
                self._localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
            end
            return self._localPlayer
        end

        function PlayerBridge:GetCharacter()
            local lp = self:GetLocalPlayer()
            return lp and lp.Character or nil
        end

        function PlayerBridge:GetRoot()
            local char = self:GetCharacter()
            if not char then
                return nil
            end
            return char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end

        function PlayerBridge:GetHumanoid()
            local char = self:GetCharacter()
            if not char then
                return nil
            end
            return char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        end

        function PlayerBridge:GetFlatVelocity()
            local root = self:GetRoot()
            if not root then
                return Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end
            local v = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            return Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
        end

        function PlayerBridge:GetMoveCommand()
            local char = self:GetCharacter()
            local cc = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.CharacterControllers)
            if char and cc and type(cc.GetMoveCommand) == "function" then
                local ok, vec = pcall(cc.GetMoveCommand, char)
                if ok and typeof(vec) == "Vector3" then
                    return vec
                end
            end
            local hum = self:GetHumanoid()
            return hum and hum.MoveDirection or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end

        function PlayerBridge:GetReticleAimRay()
            local reticle = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientReticle)
            if reticle and type(reticle.GetCameraAimRayWithoutHit) == "function" then
                local ok, ray = pcall(reticle.GetCameraAimRayWithoutHit)
                if ok and type(ray) == "table" then
                    return ray
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        function PlayerBridge:IsLanded()
            local char = self:GetCharacter()
            local hum = self:GetHumanoid()
            if not char or not hum then
                return false
            end
            local cc = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.CharacterControllers)
            if cc and type(cc.IsLanded) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(cc.IsLanded, char, hum)
                if ok then
                    return res == true
                end
            end
            local state = hum:GetState()
            return state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall
        end

        function PlayerBridge:IsRagdolled()
            local char = self:GetCharacter()
            local lp = self:GetLocalPlayer()
            local ragdoll = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Ragdoll)
            if ragdoll and type(ragdoll.IsActorRagdolled) == "function" and lp then
                local ok, res = pcall(ragdoll.IsActorRagdolled, lp, char)
                if ok then
                    return res == true
                end
            end
