            return false
        end

        function PlayerBridge:IsStunned()
            local char = self:GetCharacter()
            local lp = self:GetLocalPlayer()
            local stun = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Stun)
            if stun and type(stun.IsActorStunned) == "function" and lp then
                local ok, res = pcall(stun.IsActorStunned, lp, char)
                if ok then
                    return res == true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        function PlayerBridge:IsDribbling()
            local clientDodge = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientDodge)
            if clientDodge and type(clientDodge.IsDribbling) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(clientDodge.IsDribbling)
                if ok and res == true then
                    return true
                end
            end
            local char = self:GetCharacter()
            local modDodge = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Dodge)
            if char and modDodge and type(modDodge.IsDribbling) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(modDodge.IsDribbling, char)
                if ok then
                    return res == true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        function PlayerBridge:IsSliding()
            local clientTackle = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientSlideTackle)
            if clientTackle and type(clientTackle.IsSlideTackling) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(clientTackle.IsSlideTackling)
                if ok and res == true then
                    return true
                end
            end
            local char = self:GetCharacter()
            local modTackle = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.SlideTackle)
            if char and modTackle and type(modTackle.GetSlidingUntil) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(modTackle.GetSlidingUntil, char)
                if ok then
                    return res ~= nil
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        function PlayerBridge:IsChargingKickOrPass()
            local shoot = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientShoot)
            if shoot and type(shoot.IsCharging) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(shoot.IsCharging)
                if ok and res == true then
                    return true
                end
            end
            local pass = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientPass)
            if pass and type(pass.IsCharging) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(pass.IsCharging)
                if ok and res == true then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        function PlayerBridge:HasKickBlock()
            local lp = self:GetLocalPlayer()
            local locks = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ActionLocks)
            if locks and type(locks.HasKickBlock) == "function" and lp then
                local ok, res = pcall(locks.HasKickBlock, lp)
                if ok then
                    return res == true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        function PlayerBridge:HasTackleBlock()
            local lp = self:GetLocalPlayer()
            local locks = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ActionLocks)
            if locks and type(locks.HasTackleBlock) == "function" and lp then
                local ok, res = pcall(locks.HasTackleBlock, lp)
                if ok then
                    return res == true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        function PlayerBridge:HasDodgeBlock()
            local lp = self:GetLocalPlayer()
            local locks = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ActionLocks)
            if locks and type(locks.HasDodgeBlock) == "function" and lp then
                local ok, res = pcall(locks.HasDodgeBlock, lp)
                if ok then
                    return res == true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        function PlayerBridge:HasGoalkeeperDiveBlock(volleyUserId)
            local lp = self:GetLocalPlayer()
            local locks = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ActionLocks)
            if locks and type(locks.HasGoalkeeperDiveBlock) == "function" and lp then
                local ok, res = pcall(locks.HasGoalkeeperDiveBlock, lp, volleyUserId)
                if ok then
                    return res == true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        function PlayerBridge:GetStamina()
            local clientSprint = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientSprint)
            if clientSprint and type(clientSprint.GetStamina) == "function" then
                local ok, val = pcall(clientSprint.GetStamina)
                if ok and type(val) == "number" then
                    return val
                end
            end
            return 1
        end

        function PlayerBridge:IsSprinting()
            local clientSprint = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientSprint)
            if clientSprint and type(clientSprint.IsSprinting) == "function" then
                local ok, val = pcall(clientSprint.IsSprinting)
                if ok then
                    return val == true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        function PlayerBridge:GetDodgeBoostRemaining()
            local char = self:GetCharacter()
            local modSprint = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Sprint)
            if char and modSprint and type(modSprint.GetDodgeBoostUntil) == "function" then
                local ok, untilTime = pcall(modSprint.GetDodgeBoostUntil, char)
                if ok and type(untilTime) == "number" then
                    local now = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
                    return math.max(0, untilTime - now)
                end
            end
            return 0
        end

        function PlayerBridge:GetPowerupMultiplier()
            local lp = self:GetLocalPlayer()
            local kp = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.KickPowerups)
            if kp and type(kp.GetMultiplier) == "function" and lp then
                local ok, mul = pcall(kp.GetMultiplier, lp)
                if ok and type(mul) == "number" then
                    return mul
                end
            end
            return 1
        end

        function PlayerBridge:CanUseRainbowFlick()
            local lp = self:GetLocalPlayer()
            if not lp then
                return false
            end
            if self._matchBridge and (self._matchBridge:IsGoalkeeper() or not self._matchBridge:IsRainbowFlickUnlocked()) then
                return false
            end
            if self:IsRagdolled() or self:HasKickBlock() then
                return false
            end
            local rf = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.RainbowFlick)
            if rf and type(rf.IsWindingUp) == "function" then
                local ok, winding = pcall(rf.IsWindingUp, lp)
                if ok and winding then
                    return false
                end
            end
            local controls = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Controls)
            if controls and type(controls.IsAvailable) == "function" then
                local ok, avail = pcall(controls.IsAvailable, "RainbowFlick")
                if ok and avail == false then
                    return false
                end
            end
            return true
        end

        function PlayerBridge:GetAllActorsSnapshot(maxAge, forceRefresh)
            local now = os.clock()
            local adaptiveTtl = self._perf and self._perf:GetAdaptiveIntervals().ActorScanInterval or 0.045
            local ttl = maxAge or adaptiveTtl

            if not forceRefresh and self._cachedActors and (now - self._cachedActorsAt) < ttl then
                return self._cachedActors
            end

            local actorsMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Actors)
            local charMotion = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.CharacterMotion)
            local ms = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.MatchSettings)
            local slideTackle = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.SlideTackle)
            local ragdoll = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Ragdoll)
            local stun = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Stun)

            local myTeam = self._matchBridge and self._matchBridge:GetLocalTeamName() or nil
            local models = {}
            if actorsMod and type(actorsMod.GetAllCharacterModels) == "function" then
                local ok, list = pcall(actorsMod.GetAllCharacterModels)
                if ok and type(list) == "table" then
                    models = list
                end
            end

            local teammates = {}
            local enemies = {}
            local enemyGoalkeeper = nil
            local fallbackGk = nil
            local myChar = self:GetCharacter()

            for _, char in ipairs(models) do
                if char and char.Parent and char ~= myChar then
                    local actor = (actorsMod and actorsMod.GetFromCharacter) and actorsMod.GetFromCharacter(char) or char
                    local userId = (actorsMod and actorsMod.GetUserId) and actorsMod.GetUserId(actor) or nil
                    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                    local pos = (charMotion and charMotion.GetPosition and charMotion.GetPosition(char))
                        or (root and root.Position)
                    local vel = (charMotion and charMotion.GetVelocity and charMotion.GetVelocity(char))
                        or (root and root.AssemblyLinearVelocity)
                        or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    local cf = (charMotion and charMotion.GetCFrame and charMotion.GetCFrame(char))
                        or (root and root.CFrame)

                    if pos and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                        local teamName = self._matchBridge and self._matchBridge:GetActorTeamName(actor) or nil
                        local posName = self._matchBridge and self._matchBridge:GetActorPositionName(userId) or nil
                        local hasGkAttr = (ms and ms.IsGoalkeeperCharacter and ms.IsGoalkeeperCharacter(char)) == true
                        local isGk = hasGkAttr or (posName == "Goalkeeper")
                        local slidingUntil = (slideTackle and slideTackle.GetSlidingUntil) and slideTackle.GetSlidingUntil(char) or nil
                        local isSliding = slidingUntil ~= nil
                        local isRagdolled = (ragdoll and ragdoll.IsActorRagdolled and ragdoll.IsActorRagdolled(actor, char)) == true
                        local isStunned = (stun and stun.IsActorStunned and stun.IsActorStunned(actor, char)) == true

                        local info = {
                            Actor = actor,
                            Character = char,
                            UserId = userId,
                            DisplayName = hum.DisplayName ~= "" and hum.DisplayName or char.Name,
                            Root = root,
                            Humanoid = hum,
                            Position = pos,
                            CFrame = cf,
                            Velocity = vel,
                            TeamName = teamName,
                            PositionName = posName,
                            HasActiveGkRole = hasGkAttr,
                            IsGoalkeeper = isGk,
                            IsSliding = isSliding,
                            SlidingUntil = slidingUntil,
                            IsRagdolled = isRagdolled,
                            IsStunned = isStunned,
                        }

                        if myTeam and teamName == myTeam then
                            table.insert(teammates, info)
                        elseif myTeam and teamName and teamName ~= myTeam then
                            table.insert(enemies, info)
                            if hasGkAttr then
                                enemyGoalkeeper = info
                            elseif isGk and not fallbackGk then
                                fallbackGk = info
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            if not enemyGoalkeeper then
                enemyGoalkeeper = fallbackGk
            end

            local snapshot = {
                Teammates = teammates,
                Enemies = enemies,
                EnemyGoalkeeper = enemyGoalkeeper,
            }
            self._cachedActors = snapshot
            self._cachedActorsAt = now
            return snapshot
        end

        -- Fast Spatial & Cone Culling untuk deteksi bek berlapis (Potato-Phone Safe)
        function PlayerBridge:GetNearbyDefenders(originPos, forwardDir, maxRadius, maxConeDeg, actorsSnapshot)
            if not originPos then
                return {}
            end

            local now = os.clock()
            local scanInterval = self._perf and self._perf:GetAdaptiveIntervals().DefenderScanInterval or 0.08
            if not actorsSnapshot and (now - self._cachedDefendersAt) < scanInterval and #self._cachedDefenders > 0 then
                return self._cachedDefenders
            end

            local snap = actorsSnapshot or self:GetAllActorsSnapshot()
            local radius = maxRadius or 20
            local minDot = maxConeDeg and math.cos(math.rad(maxConeDeg)) or -1
            local fwdFlat = nil
            if typeof(forwardDir) == "Vector3" then
                local f = Vector3.new(forwardDir.X, 0, forwardDir.Z)
                if f.Magnitude > 1e-4 then
                    fwdFlat = f.Unit
                end
            end

            clearTable(self._cachedDefenders)
            for _, enemy in ipairs(snap.Enemies or {}) do
                if not enemy.IsRagdolled and not enemy.IsStunned and not enemy.IsGoalkeeper then
                    local rel = Vector3.new(enemy.Position.X - originPos.X, 0, enemy.Position.Z - originPos.Z)
                    local dist = rel.Magnitude
                    if dist > 0.1 and dist <= radius then
                        local inCone = true
                        local dotVal = 1
                        if fwdFlat then
                            dotVal = (rel / dist):Dot(fwdFlat)
                            inCone = dotVal >= minDot
                        end
                        if inCone then
                            table.insert(self._cachedDefenders, {
                                Enemy = enemy,
                                Distance = dist,
                                Dot = dotVal,
                                PredictedPos = enemy.Position + Vector3.new(enemy.Velocity.X, 0, enemy.Velocity.Z) * 0.14,
                            })
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            table.sort(self._cachedDefenders, function(a, b)
                return a.Distance < b.Distance
            end)

            self._cachedDefendersAt = now
            return self._cachedDefenders
        end

        function PlayerBridge:Destroy()
            self._cachedActors = nil
            clearTable(self._cachedDefenders)
        end

        return PlayerBridge
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- BOOTSTRAP PART 4
    --========================================================================
    local ctx = LouisHub.Context
    local MatchBridgeClass = LouisHub.Require("Bridge/MatchBridge")
    local BallBridgeClass = LouisHub.Require("Bridge/BallBridge")
    local PlayerBridgeClass = LouisHub.Require("Bridge/PlayerBridge")

    local matchBridge = MatchBridgeClass.new()
    local ballBridge = BallBridgeClass.new(ctx.Performance)
    local playerBridge = PlayerBridgeClass.new(matchBridge, ctx.Store, ctx.Performance)

    matchBridge:Start()
    ballBridge:Start()

    if ctx.Performance and type(ctx.Performance.SetBallBridge) == "function" then
        ctx.Performance:SetBallBridge(ballBridge)
    end

    ctx.Match = matchBridge
    ctx.Ball = ballBridge
    ctx.Player = playerBridge

    LouisHub.OnDestroy(function()
        pcall(function() matchBridge:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() ballBridge:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() playerBridge:Destroy() end)
    end)

    LouisHub.PartLoaded[4] = true
    print(string.format("[LouisHub v%s] Part 4/10 initialized (GameModules & Adaptive Bridges Ready)", LouisHub.Version))
end

--============================================================================
-- LOUIS HUB // ILLEGAL SOCCER SUITE v3.2.0 (ULTRA PRECISION & ANTI-LAG EDITION)
-- PART 5/10: PHYSICS PREDICTOR & ADVANCED BALLISTICS SOLVER (ANTI-SKYWARD)
-- Requires Parts 1/10 - 4/10 loaded in getgenv().LouisHub
--============================================================================

do
    local GlobalEnv = (type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or _G
    local LouisHub = GlobalEnv.LouisHub

    if type(LouisHub) ~= "table"
        or not (LouisHub.PartLoaded and LouisHub.PartLoaded[1] and LouisHub.PartLoaded[2] and LouisHub.PartLoaded[3] and LouisHub.PartLoaded[4])
    then
        error("[LouisHub] Parts 1/10 through 4/10 must be executed before Part 5/10!", 2)
    end

    local function flatVec(v)
        return Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
    end

    local function flatUnit(v, fallback)
        if typeof(v) ~= "Vector3" then
            return fallback
        end
        local fv = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
        if fv.Magnitude > 1e-4 then
            return fv.Unit
        end
        return fallback
    end

    local function finite(n)
        return type(n) == "number" and n == n and math.abs(n) < math.huge
    end

    local function finiteVector(v)
        return typeof(v) == "Vector3" and finite(v.X) and finite(v.Y) and finite(v.Z)
    end

    local function segmentPointDistance(a, b, p)
        local ab = b - a
        local lenSq = ab:Dot(ab)
        if lenSq <= 1e-6 then
            return (p - a).Magnitude, 0
        end
        local t = math.clamp((p - a):Dot(ab) / lenSq, 0, 1)
        local closest = a + ab * t
        return (p - closest).Magnitude, t
    end

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Engine/PhysicsPredictor (Zero-Spike Ring Buffer & Quick Sim)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Engine/PhysicsPredictor", function(Require)
        local GameModules = Require("Bridge/GameModules")

        local PhysicsPredictor = {}
        PhysicsPredictor.__index = PhysicsPredictor

        function PhysicsPredictor.new(ballBridge, matchBridge, store, perfOpt)
            return setmetatable({
                _ball = ballBridge,
                _match = matchBridge,
                _store = store,
                _perf = perfOpt,
                _trajCache = {},
                _quickPointsScratch = {},
            }, PhysicsPredictor)
        end

        function PhysicsPredictor:_isPerfMode()
            if self._perf then
                return self._perf:IsPerformanceMode()
            end
            return self._store and self._store:Get("Meta.PerformanceMode") == true
        end

        function PhysicsPredictor:SampleTrajectory(state, boundaryWorld, maxSeconds, stepSeconds, maxBounces)
            if not state then
                return nil
            end
            local physics = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.BallPhysics)
            if not physics then
                return nil
            end

            local bw = boundaryWorld or self._ball:GetChargeBoundaryWorld()
            if not bw then
                return nil
            end

            local perfOn = self:_isPerfMode()
            local duration = maxSeconds or 2.0
            local step = stepSeconds or 0.05
            if perfOn then
                step = math.max(step, 0.08)
            end
            local bounceCap = maxBounces or 2
            if perfOn then
                bounceCap = math.min(bounceCap, 1)
            end

            local startTime = state.UpdatedAt or state.StartedAt or self._ball:GetServerTime()
            local now = os.clock()
            local pos = state.Position
            local vel = state.Velocity

            if not (finiteVector(pos) and finiteVector(vel)) then
                return nil
            end

            for i = #self._trajCache, 1, -1 do
                local entry = self._trajCache[i]
                if (now - entry.CachedAt) > 0.30 then
                    table.remove(self._trajCache, i)
                elseif entry.StartTime == startTime
                    and entry.Boundary == bw
                    and entry.Duration == duration
                    and entry.Step == step
                    and entry.BounceCap == bounceCap
                    and (entry.Pos - pos).Magnitude < 0.03
                    and (entry.Vel - vel).Magnitude < 0.06 then
                    return entry.Result
                end
            end

            local points = { pos }
            local bounces = {}
            local bounceTimesSeen = {}

            local curState = state
            local steps = math.max(1, math.floor(duration / step))
            local scratch = {}

            for i = 1, steps do
                local t = startTime + i * step
                local ok, nextState = pcall(physics.GetStateAtTime, curState, t, bw, scratch)
                if not ok or not nextState then
                    break
                end

                if nextState.BoundaryHits then
                    for _, hit in ipairs(nextState.BoundaryHits) do
                        local hitTime = hit.Time or t
                        local key = math.floor(hitTime * 1000)
                        if not bounceTimesSeen[key] then
                            bounceTimesSeen[key] = true
                            table.insert(bounces, {
                                Position = hit.Position,
                                Normal = hit.Normal,
                                FaceNormal = hit.FaceNormal,
                                Time = hitTime,
                                Velocity = hit.Velocity,
                                Part = hit.Part,
                            })
                        end
                    end
                end

                table.insert(points, nextState.Position)
                curState = nextState

                if #bounces > bounceCap then
                    break
                end
                if nextState.Mode == "Resting" and physics.IsStateSettled then
                    local sOk, settled = pcall(physics.IsStateSettled, nextState, bw)
                    if sOk and settled then
                        break
                    end
                end
            end

            local landing = nil
            local landingMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.LandingIndicator)
            if landingMod and type(landingMod.GetLanding) == "function" then
                local lOk, hit = pcall(landingMod.GetLanding, state, bw, startTime)
                if lOk and hit then
                    local effPos = hit.Position
                    if type(landingMod.GetEffectPosition) == "function" then
                        local eOk, ep = pcall(landingMod.GetEffectPosition, state, hit)
                        if eOk and ep then
                            effPos = ep
                        end
                    end
                    landing = {
                        Position = hit.Position,
                        EffectPosition = effPos,
                        Normal = hit.Normal,
                        Time = hit.Time,
                    }
                end
            end

            local result = {
                Points = points,
                Bounces = bounces,
                Landing = landing,
                StartTime = startTime,
            }

            table.insert(self._trajCache, 1, {
                CachedAt = now,
                StartTime = startTime,
                Boundary = bw,
                Duration = duration,
                Step = step,
                BounceCap = bounceCap,
                Pos = pos,
                Vel = vel,
                Result = result,
            })
            if #self._trajCache > 4 then
                table.remove(self._trajCache)
            end

            return result
        end

        -- Simulasi jalur cepat tanpa alokasi array berat (ramah HP kentang)
        function PhysicsPredictor:SampleQuickPoints(virtualState, boundaryWorld, endTime, stepsCount)
            local physics = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.BallPhysics)
            local startT = virtualState.StartedAt or self._ball:GetServerTime()
            local span = math.clamp((endTime or (startT + 1.2)) - startT, 0.06, 1.8)
            local steps = stepsCount or (self:_isPerfMode() and 4 or 7)
            local pts = self._quickPointsScratch
            pts[1] = virtualState.Position
            local count = 1
            local scratch = {}

            for i = 1, steps do
                local t = startT + (span * i / steps)
                local nextPos = nil
                if physics and type(physics.GetStateAtTime) == "function" then
                    local ok, st = pcall(physics.GetStateAtTime, virtualState, t, boundaryWorld, scratch)
                    if ok and st and st.Position then
                        nextPos = st.Position
                    end
                end
                if not nextPos then
                    nextPos = virtualState.Position + virtualState.Velocity * (t - startT)
                end
                count = count + 1
                pts[count] = nextPos
            end

            return pts, count
        end

        function PhysicsPredictor:GetGoalCrossing(state, boundaryWorld, goalPart, fromTime, horizonSeconds)
            if not (state and boundaryWorld and goalPart) then
                return nil, nil
            end
            local gkPred = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.GoalkeeperPrediction)
            if gkPred and type(gkPred.GetGoalCrossing) == "function" then
                local ok, pos, hitTime = pcall(gkPred.GetGoalCrossing, state, boundaryWorld, goalPart, fromTime, horizonSeconds or 2.8)
                if ok and pos then
                    return pos, hitTime
                end
            end
            local physics = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.BallPhysics)
            if physics and type(physics.GetFirstBoxEntry) == "function" then
                local ok, pos, hitTime = pcall(physics.GetFirstBoxEntry, state, boundaryWorld, goalPart, fromTime, horizonSeconds or 2.8)
                if ok and pos then
                    return pos, hitTime
                end
            end
            return nil, nil
        end

        function PhysicsPredictor:CreateVirtualState(position, velocity, spin, ownerUserId, teamName)
            local physics = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.BallPhysics)
            local now = self._ball:GetServerTime()
            local radius = (physics and physics.BallRadius) or 1
            local state
            if physics and type(physics.NewState) == "function" then
                state = physics.NewState(position, velocity, radius, now, spin or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
            else
                state = {
                    InitialPosition = position,
                    InitialVelocity = velocity,
                    InitialSpin = spin or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
                    StartedAt = now,
                    Position = position,
                    Velocity = velocity,
                    Spin = spin or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
                    UpdatedAt = now,
                    Radius = radius,
                    Mode = "Airborne",
                }
            end
            state.LastKickerUserId = ownerUserId
            state.LastKickerTeamName = teamName
            return state
        end

        return PhysicsPredictor
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Engine/BallisticsSolver (Anti-Skyward & Magnus Silent Aim Engine)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Engine/BallisticsSolver", function(Require)
        local GameModules = Require("Bridge/GameModules")

        local BallisticsSolver = {}
        BallisticsSolver.__index = BallisticsSolver

        function BallisticsSolver.new(matchBridge, ballBridge, playerBridge, physicsPredictor, store)
            return setmetatable({
                _match = matchBridge,
                _ball = ballBridge,
                _player = playerBridge,
                _predictor = physicsPredictor,
                _store = store,
            }, BallisticsSolver)
        end

        function BallisticsSolver:BuildSyntheticAimRay(originPos, lookDir, baseRay)
            local dir = lookDir.Magnitude > 1e-4 and lookDir.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
            local camCF = CFrame.lookAt(originPos, originPos + dir * 25)
            local fov = (baseRay and baseRay.CameraFieldOfView) or (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView) or 70
            local vp = (baseRay and baseRay.CameraViewportSize) or (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize) or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
            return {
                Origin = originPos,
                Direction = dir,
                CameraCFrame = camCF,
                CameraFieldOfView = fov,
                CameraViewportSize = vp,
                ScreenPosition = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                IsAirborne = baseRay and baseRay.IsAirborne or nil,
            }
        end

        -- Membatasi sudut elevasi vertikal agar tidak menembak ke langit (Anti-Skyward)
        function BallisticsSolver:ClampDirectionPitch(dir, maxPitchDeg)
            if typeof(dir) ~= "Vector3" or dir.Magnitude <= 1e-4 then
                return Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
            end
            local unitDir = dir.Unit
            local horiz = flatUnit(unitDir, Vector3.new(0, 0, -1))
            local pitch = math.asin(math.clamp(unitDir.Y, -0.95, 0.95))
            local maxRad = math.rad(math.clamp(maxPitchDeg or 22, 6, 38))
            local clampedPitch = math.clamp(pitch, -0.35, maxRad)
            return (horiz * math.cos(clampedPitch) + Vector3.new(0, math.sin(clampedPitch), 0)).Unit
        end

        -- Deteksi hambatan segmen cepat untuk bek berlapis & barikade
        function BallisticsSolver:CheckPathObstruction(startPos, virtualState, boundaryWorld, crossingTime, nearbyDefenders, barricades)
            local pts, count = self._predictor:SampleQuickPoints(virtualState, boundaryWorld, crossingTime)
            if count < 2 then
                return false, nil, nil
            end

            for idx = 2, count do
                local prevPos = pts[idx - 1]
                local curPos = pts[idx]

                for _, part in ipairs(barricades or {}) do
                    if part and part.Parent and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local localStart = part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(prevPos)
                        local localEnd = part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(curPos)
                        local d = segmentPointDistance(localStart, localEnd, Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
                        if d <= part.Size.Magnitude * 0.5 + 0.85 then
                            return true, "Barricade", part.Position
                        end
                    end
                end

                for _, defInfo in ipairs(nearbyDefenders or {}) do
                    local defPos = defInfo.PredictedPos or (defInfo.Enemy and defInfo.Enemy.Position)
                    if defPos then
                        local d = segmentPointDistance(prevPos, curPos, defPos)
                        if d <= 3.05 then
                            return true, "Defender", defPos
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            return false, nil, nil
        end

        -- Kalibrasi iteratif AimRay agar titik crossing bola mendarat tepat di targetPos
        function BallisticsSolver:CalibrateAimRay(char, chargeSeconds, startPos, reportedVel, boundaryWorld, goalPart, targetPos, curveVal, baseAimRay, opts)
            local cpv = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ChargePathVelocity)
            local cmds = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ActionCommands)
            local shootMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Shoot)
            local lp = self._player:GetLocalPlayer()
            local userId = lp and lp.UserId or nil
            local myTeam = self._match:GetLocalTeamName()
            local shotPassType = (cmds and cmds.PassTypes and cmds.PassTypes.Shot) or "Shot"

            local rawDelta = targetPos - startPos
            if rawDelta.Magnitude <= 1e-4 then
                local fallbackDir = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
                return self:BuildSyntheticAimRay(startPos + Vector3.new(0, 0, 12), fallbackDir, baseAimRay), fallbackDir * 95, nil
            end

            local curCam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local camDist = curCam and math.clamp((startPos - curCam.CFrame.Position).Magnitude, 10, 24) or 15

            local arcComp = opts and opts.ArcCompensation ~= false
            local maxPitchDeg = (opts and opts.MaxPitchDeg) or (self._store and self._store:Get("Striker.SilentAimMaxPitchDeg")) or 22
            local maxPitchRad = math.rad(math.clamp(maxPitchDeg, 8, 36))

            local baseLook = arcComp and self:ClampDirectionPitch(rawDelta.Unit, maxPitchDeg) or rawDelta.Unit
            local baseFlat = flatUnit(baseLook, Vector3.new(0, 0, -1))
            local basePitch = math.asin(math.clamp(baseLook.Y, -0.9, 0.9))

            local yawOffset = 0
            -- Slight downward compensation (-0.035 rad) counters Shoot.GetVelocity vertical camera-lift
            local pitchOffset = arcComp and -0.035 or -0.015

            local bestRay = nil
            local bestVel = nil
            local bestCross = nil
            local bestErr = math.huge

            local perfOn = self._store and self._store:Get("Meta.PerformanceMode") == true
            local maxIter = (opts and opts.MaxIterations) or (perfOn and 2 or 3)

            for iter = 1, maxIter do
                local yawCF = CFrame.fromAxisAngle(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), yawOffset)
                local horiz = (yawCF * baseFlat).Unit
                local p = math.clamp(basePitch + pitchOffset, -0.35, maxPitchRad)
                local testDir = (horiz * math.cos(p) + Vector3.new(0, math.sin(p), 0)).Unit
                local camOrigin = startPos - testDir * camDist
                local testRay = self:BuildSyntheticAimRay(camOrigin, testDir, baseAimRay)

                local launchVel = nil
                if cpv and type(cpv.GetLaunchVelocity) == "function" then
                    local ok, vel = pcall(
                        cpv.GetLaunchVelocity,
                        char,
                        chargeSeconds,
                        shotPassType,
                        testRay,
                        startPos,
                        reportedVel,
                        horiz,
                        nil,
                        nil,
                        boundaryWorld,
                        0.05,
                        curveVal
                    )
                    if ok and typeof(vel) == "Vector3" then
                        launchVel = vel
                    end
                end

                if not launchVel then
                    break
                end

                local spin = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                if shootMod and type(shootMod.GetSpin) == "function" then
                    local sOk, sp = pcall(shootMod.GetSpin, char, launchVel, reportedVel, curveVal, chargeSeconds)
                    if sOk and typeof(sp) == "Vector3" then
                        spin = sp
                    end
                end

                local vState = self._predictor:CreateVirtualState(startPos, launchVel, spin, userId, myTeam)
                local crossPos, crossTime = self._predictor:GetGoalCrossing(vState, boundaryWorld, goalPart, vState.StartedAt, 2.5)

                if crossPos then
                    local err = (crossPos - targetPos).Magnitude
                    if err < bestErr then
                        bestErr = err
                        bestRay = testRay
                        bestVel = launchVel
                        bestCross = { Position = crossPos, Time = crossTime, Spin = spin, VirtualState = vState }
                    end
                    if err <= 0.45 then
                        break
                    end

                    local toGoal = targetPos - startPos
                    local toCross = crossPos - startPos
                    local goalFlat = flatUnit(toGoal, horiz)
                    local crossFlat = flatUnit(toCross, horiz)
                    local dYaw = math.atan2(crossFlat:Cross(goalFlat).Y, math.clamp(crossFlat:Dot(goalFlat), -1, 1))
                    local horizDist = math.max(flatVec(toGoal).Magnitude, 1)
                    local dPitch = math.atan2(targetPos.Y - crossPos.Y, horizDist)

                    yawOffset = math.clamp(yawOffset + dYaw * 0.85, -0.58, 0.58)
                    pitchOffset = math.clamp(pitchOffset + dPitch * 0.85, -0.40, 0.32)
                else
                    -- Bola melambung melewati mistar/tiang: turunkan pitch secara bertahap
                    if iter == 1 then
                        pitchOffset = pitchOffset - 0.08
                    else
                        pitchOffset = pitchOffset - 0.05
                        yawOffset = yawOffset * 0.5
                    end
                end
            end

            if not bestRay then
                bestRay = self:BuildSyntheticAimRay(startPos - baseLook * camDist, baseLook, baseAimRay)
            end

            return bestRay, bestVel, bestCross
        end

        return BallisticsSolver
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- BOOTSTRAP PART 5
    --========================================================================
    local ctx = LouisHub.Context
    ctx.Engines = ctx.Engines or {}

    local PhysicsPredictorClass = LouisHub.Require("Engine/PhysicsPredictor")
    local BallisticsSolverClass = LouisHub.Require("Engine/BallisticsSolver")

    local physicsPredictor = PhysicsPredictorClass.new(ctx.Ball, ctx.Match, ctx.Store, ctx.Performance)
    local ballisticsSolver = BallisticsSolverClass.new(ctx.Match, ctx.Ball, ctx.Player, physicsPredictor, ctx.Store)

    ctx.Engines.Physics = physicsPredictor
    ctx.Engines.Ballistics = ballisticsSolver

    LouisHub.PartLoaded[5] = true
    print(string.format("[LouisHub v%s] Part 5/10 initialized (Ballistics Anti-Skyward & Physics Predictor Ready)", LouisHub.Version))
end

--============================================================================
-- LOUIS HUB // ILLEGAL SOCCER SUITE v3.2.0 (ULTRA PRECISION & ANTI-LAG EDITION)
-- PART 6/10: ADVANCED GOAL TARGET SOLVER (MULTI-DEFENDER & SILENT AIM PRO)
-- Requires Parts 1/10 - 5/10 loaded in getgenv().LouisHub
--============================================================================

do
    local GlobalEnv = (type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or _G
    local LouisHub = GlobalEnv.LouisHub

    if type(LouisHub) ~= "table"
        or not (LouisHub.PartLoaded
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[1]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[2]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[3]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[4]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[5])
    then
        error("[LouisHub] Parts 1/10 through 5/10 must be executed before Part 6/10!", 2)
    end

    local function clamp01(v)
        return math.clamp(v, 0, 1)
    end

    local function flatVec(v)
        return Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
    end

    local function flatUnit(v, fallback)
        if typeof(v) ~= "Vector3" then
            return fallback
        end
        local fv = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
        if fv.Magnitude > 1e-4 then
            return fv.Unit
        end
        return fallback
    end

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Engine/GoalTargetSolver
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Engine/GoalTargetSolver", function(Require)
        local GameModules = Require("Bridge/GameModules")

        local GoalTargetSolver = {}
        GoalTargetSolver.__index = GoalTargetSolver

        function GoalTargetSolver.new(matchBridge, ballBridge, playerBridge, physicsPredictor, ballisticsSolver, store, perfOpt)
            return setmetatable({
                _match = matchBridge,
                _ball = ballBridge,
                _player = playerBridge,
                _predictor = physicsPredictor,
                _ballistics = ballisticsSolver,
                _store = store,
                _perf = perfOpt,
                _lastSolution = nil,
                _lastSolvedAt = 0,
                _lastSolvedPos = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
                _lastSolvedLook = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1),
                _lastDefenderSig = 0,
                _lastSilentSol = nil,
                _lastSilentAt = 0,
                _cachedBarricades = {},
                _barricadesAt = 0,
            }, GoalTargetSolver)
        end

        function GoalTargetSolver:_isPerfMode()
            if self._perf then
                return self._perf:IsPerformanceMode()
            end
            return self._store and self._store:Get("Meta.PerformanceMode") == true
        end

        function GoalTargetSolver:GetGoalMouth(goalPart, fromPos)
            if not (goalPart and fromPos) then
                return nil
            end
            local assist = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ShotAimAssist)
            if assist and type(assist.GetGoalMouth) == "function" then
                local ok, mouth = pcall(assist.GetGoalMouth, goalPart, fromPos)
                if ok and mouth then
                    return mouth
                end
            end

            local pos = goalPart.Position
            local flat = Vector3.new(fromPos.X - pos.X, 0, fromPos.Z - pos.Z)
            if flat.Magnitude <= 1e-4 then
                return nil
            end
            local forward = flat.Unit
            local lateral = forward:Cross(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
            local cf = goalPart.CFrame
            local size = goalPart.Size
            local halfDepth = math.abs(forward:Dot(cf.RightVector)) * size.X * 0.5
                + math.abs(forward:Dot(cf.LookVector)) * size.Z * 0.5
            local halfWidth = math.abs(lateral:Dot(cf.RightVector)) * size.X * 0.5
                + math.abs(lateral:Dot(cf.LookVector)) * size.Z * 0.5

            return {
                Center = pos,
                PlanePosition = pos + forward * halfDepth,
                Forward = forward,
                Lateral = lateral,
                HalfWidth = halfWidth,
                BottomY = pos.Y - size.Y * 0.5,
                TopY = pos.Y + size.Y * 0.5,
            }
        end

        function GoalTargetSolver:GetActiveBarricadeParts()
            local now = os.clock()
            if (now - self._barricadesAt) < 0.45 then
                return self._cachedBarricades
            end
            local strikes = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.BarricadeStrikes)
            if strikes and type(strikes.GetReplicatedBoundaryParts) == "function" then
                local ok, parts = pcall(strikes.GetReplicatedBoundaryParts)
                if ok and type(parts) == "table" then
                    self._cachedBarricades = parts
                    self._barricadesAt = now
                    return parts
                end
            end
            self._cachedBarricades = {}
            self._barricadesAt = now
            return self._cachedBarricades
        end

        function GoalTargetSolver:_findEffectiveEnemyGoalkeeper(mouth, actorsSnapshot)
            if actorsSnapshot and actorsSnapshot.EnemyGoalkeeper then
                return actorsSnapshot.EnemyGoalkeeper
            end
            local bestGk = nil
            local bestDist = 26
            for _, enemy in ipairs((actorsSnapshot and actorsSnapshot.Enemies) or {}) do
                if not enemy.IsRagdolled then
                    local d = (enemy.Position - mouth.Center).Magnitude
                    if d < bestDist then
                        bestDist = d
                        bestGk = enemy
                    end
                end
            end
            return bestGk
        end

        function GoalTargetSolver:_buildTargetCandidates(mouth, placement, enemyGk, startPos)
            local radius = self._ball:GetBallRadius()
            local latMargin = radius + 1.25
            local topMargin = radius + 1.05
            local usableHalfW = math.max(0.7, mouth.HalfWidth - latMargin)
            local minY = mouth.BottomY + radius + 0.55
            local maxY = math.max(minY + 0.5, mouth.TopY - topMargin)
            local spanY = maxY - minY
            -- Target ketinggian aman (70% max span) agar tidak membentur mistar / melambung
            local highY = minY + spanY * 0.70
            local midY = minY + spanY * 0.42
            local dist = (mouth.Center - startPos).Magnitude

            local function makePoint(latFrac, yVal, label)
                local wp = mouth.PlanePosition + mouth.Lateral * (usableHalfW * latFrac)
                return {
                    Position = Vector3.new(wp.X, math.clamp(yVal, minY, maxY), wp.Z),
                    LatFrac = latFrac,
                    Label = label,
                }
            end

            if placement == "TopLeft" then
                return { makePoint(-0.88, highY, "TopLeft") }
            elseif placement == "TopRight" then
                return { makePoint(0.88, highY, "TopRight") }
            elseif placement == "BottomLeft" then
                return { makePoint(-0.88, minY, "BottomLeft") }
            elseif placement == "BottomRight" then
                return { makePoint(0.88, minY, "BottomRight") }
            elseif placement == "CenterLow" or placement == "Bottom" then
                return { makePoint(0, minY, "CenterLow") }
            elseif placement == "CenterHigh" or placement == "Top" then
                return { makePoint(0, highY, "CenterHigh") }
            elseif placement == "Center" then
                return { makePoint(0, midY, "Center") }
            elseif placement == "SmartCorner" or placement == "Smart" then
                local sideSign = 1
                if enemyGk and enemyGk.Position then
                    local gkLat = (enemyGk.Position - mouth.Center):Dot(mouth.Lateral)
                    if math.abs(gkLat) < 0.75 then
                        local latVel = flatVec(enemyGk.Velocity):Dot(mouth.Lateral)
                        sideSign = (latVel >= 0) and -1 or 1
                    else
                        sideSign = (gkLat >= 0) and -1 or 1
                    end
                end
                local ty = (dist <= 24) and minY or highY
                return {
                    makePoint(0.88 * sideSign, ty, "SmartCorner"),
                    makePoint(-0.88 * sideSign, highY, "AltCorner"),
                    makePoint(0.88 * sideSign, minY, "SmartLowCorner"),
                }
            end

            -- AntiGK full scan
            return {
                makePoint(-0.88, highY, "TopLeft"),
                makePoint(0.88, highY, "TopRight"),
                makePoint(-0.88, minY, "BottomLeft"),
                makePoint(0.88, minY, "BottomRight"),
                makePoint(0, highY, "CenterTop"),
            }
        end

        function GoalTargetSolver:_evaluateGKSaveMargin(crossPos, crossTime, now, mouth, enemyGk)
            if not enemyGk or not enemyGk.Position or enemyGk.IsRagdolled or enemyGk.IsStunned then
                return 30.0
            end
            local flightSec = math.max(0.05, (crossTime or (now + 0.4)) - now)
            local gkVel = flatVec(enemyGk.Velocity)
            local predGkPos = enemyGk.Position + gkVel * math.min(flightSec * 0.55, 0.42)

            local gkLat = (predGkPos - mouth.PlanePosition):Dot(mouth.Lateral)
            local crossLat = (crossPos - mouth.PlanePosition):Dot(mouth.Lateral)
            local latGap = math.abs(crossLat - gkLat)

            local gkDepth = (predGkPos - mouth.PlanePosition):Dot(mouth.Forward)
            local crossHeight = crossPos.Y - predGkPos.Y

            local diveMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.GoalkeeperDive)
            local maxSaveHeight = (diveMod and diveMod.Constants and diveMod.Constants.MaximumSaveHeight) or 9.5

            local gkMoveReach = math.min(flightSec, 0.65) * 22
            local effectiveGap = latGap - (gkMoveReach * 0.45)

            local heightBonus = 0
            if crossHeight > 5.2 then
                heightBonus = (crossHeight - 5.2) * 1.15
            end
            if crossHeight > maxSaveHeight + 1.8 then
                heightBonus = heightBonus + 5.0
            end

            local depthBonus = 0
            if gkDepth > 12 then
                depthBonus = (gkDepth - 12) * 0.35
            end

            local bodyDist = (flatVec(crossPos) - flatVec(predGkPos)).Magnitude
            if bodyDist < 4.2 and crossHeight < 6.8 then
                effectiveGap = effectiveGap - 7.0
            end

            return effectiveGap + heightBonus + depthBonus
        end

        function GoalTargetSolver:GetShotChargeConstants()
            local char = self._player:GetCharacter()
            local shootMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Shoot)
            local powerups = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.KickPowerups)
            local baseKick = (shootMod and shootMod.Constants and shootMod.Constants.Kick) or nil
            if powerups and type(powerups.GetChargeConstants) == "function" and char and baseKick then
                local ok, consts = pcall(powerups.GetChargeConstants, char, baseKick)
                if ok and type(consts) == "table" and consts.MaximumChargeSeconds then
                    return consts
                end
            end
            if type(baseKick) == "table" and baseKick.MaximumChargeSeconds then
                return baseKick
            end
            return {
                MinimumChargeSeconds = 0.08,
                MaximumChargeSeconds = 0.65,
                MinimumReleaseSeconds = 0.08,
            }
        end

        function GoalTargetSolver:EvaluateShotZone(startPos, goalMouth, actorsSnapshot, config)
            local dist = (goalMouth.Center - startPos).Magnitude
            local hardCap = math.min(config.HardCapDistance or 100, 100)
            local minDist = config.MinShootDistance or 16
            local maxDist = math.min(config.MaxShootDistance or 72, hardCap)
            local quickSnapDist = config.QuickSnapDistance or 18
            local powerMul = self._player:GetPowerupMultiplier()

            -- Slider jarak KHUSUS Auto-Shoot Bicycle Kick
            local bikeMin = config.BicycleMinDistance or 12
            local bikeMax = math.max(bikeMin + 2, config.BicycleMaxDistance or 42)
            local inBikeRange = (dist >= bikeMin) and (dist <= bikeMax)

            if dist > hardCap then
                return {
                    InRange = false,
                    InBicycleRange = false,
                    Zone = "OutOfRange",
                    Distance = dist,
                    RecommendedMode = "None",
                    Reason = "Beyond 100st cap",
                }
            end

            if config.RangeMode == "Custom" then
                local okRange = dist >= minDist and dist <= maxDist
                return {
                    InRange = okRange,
                    InBicycleRange = inBikeRange,
                    Zone = okRange and "CustomRange" or (dist < minDist and "BelowMin" or "AboveMax"),
                    Distance = dist,
                    RecommendedMode = dist <= quickSnapDist and "QuickSnap" or "Standard",
                    Reason = okRange and "Custom range OK" or "Outside custom range",
                }
            end

            local gk = self:_findEffectiveEnemyGoalkeeper(goalMouth, actorsSnapshot)
            local gkOutOfPosition = false
            if gk and gk.Position then
                local gkLat = math.abs((gk.Position - goalMouth.Center):Dot(goalMouth.Lateral))
                local gkDepth = (gk.Position - goalMouth.PlanePosition):Dot(goalMouth.Forward)
                gkOutOfPosition = (gkLat > goalMouth.HalfWidth * 0.42) or (gkDepth > 13)
            else
                gkOutOfPosition = true
            end

            if dist <= quickSnapDist then
                return {
                    InRange = true,
                    InBicycleRange = inBikeRange,
                    Zone = "QuickSnap",
                    Distance = dist,
                    RecommendedMode = "QuickSnap",
                    Reason = "QuickSnap range",
                }
            elseif dist <= maxDist then
                return {
                    InRange = true,
                    InBicycleRange = inBikeRange,
                    Zone = "SweetSpot",
                    Distance = dist,
                    RecommendedMode = "Optimal",
                    Reason = "Optimal range",
                }
            elseif (powerMul > 1 or gkOutOfPosition) and dist <= hardCap then
                return {
                    InRange = true,
                    InBicycleRange = inBikeRange,
                    Zone = "LongShot",
                    Distance = dist,
                    RecommendedMode = "FullPower",
                    Reason = powerMul > 1 and "Supercharged long shot" or "GK off-line",
                }
            end

            return {
                InRange = false,
                InBicycleRange = inBikeRange,
                Zone = "TooFarForSmart",
                Distance = dist,
                RecommendedMode = "DribbleCloser",
                Reason = "Move < " .. math.floor(maxDist) .. "st",
            }
        end

        function GoalTargetSolver:ComputeLaunchVelocity(char, chargeSeconds, targetPos, startPos, reportedVel, boundaryWorld, curve)
            local cpv = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ChargePathVelocity)
            local cmds = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ActionCommands)
            local shotPassType = (cmds and cmds.PassTypes and cmds.PassTypes.Shot) or "Shot"
            local aimDir = (targetPos - startPos)
            if aimDir.Magnitude > 1e-4 then
                aimDir = aimDir.Unit
            else
                aimDir = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
            end

            if cpv and type(cpv.GetLaunchVelocity) == "function" then
                local ok, vel = pcall(
                    cpv.GetLaunchVelocity,
                    char,
                    chargeSeconds,
                    shotPassType,
                    aimDir,
                    startPos,
                    reportedVel,
                    flatUnit(aimDir),
                    targetPos,
                    nil,
                    boundaryWorld,
                    0.05,
                    curve
                )
                if ok and typeof(vel) == "Vector3" then
                    return vel, aimDir
                end
            end

            return aimDir * 95, aimDir
        end

        -- Untuk Aimbot Goal (Target Tracking Correction)
        function GoalTargetSolver:SolveCustomTargetPlacement(placementName)
            local char = self._player:GetCharacter()
            local root = self._player:GetRoot()
            if not (char and root) then
                return nil
            end

            local goalParts = self._match:GetOpposingGoalParts()
            local goalPart = goalParts[1]
            if not goalPart then
                return nil
            end

            local startPos = self._ball:GetPosition() or (root.Position + root.CFrame.LookVector * 2)
            local mouth = self:GetGoalMouth(goalPart, startPos)
            if not mouth then
                return nil
            end

            local snapshot = self._player:GetAllActorsSnapshot(0.1)
            local enemyGk = self:_findEffectiveEnemyGoalkeeper(mouth, snapshot)
            local cands = self:_buildTargetCandidates(mouth, placementName or "Smart", enemyGk, startPos)
            local targetCand = cands[1]

            if not targetCand then
                return nil
            end

            local rawDelta = targetCand.Position - startPos
            local rawDir = rawDelta.Magnitude > 1e-4 and rawDelta.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)

            return {
                TargetPosition = targetCand.Position,
                TargetLabel = targetCand.Label,
                GoalMouth = mouth,
                GoalPart = goalPart,
                StartPos = startPos,
                Direction = rawDir,
                Distance = rawDelta.Magnitude,
            }
        end

        -- Upgraded Silent Aim Solver dengan Anti-Skyward, Magnus Curve & Bek Berlapis
        function GoalTargetSolver:SolveSilentAimSolution(config, actorsSnapshot)
            local nowClock = os.clock()
            local perfOn = self:_isPerfMode()
            local ttl = perfOn and 0.08 or 0.045
            if self._lastSilentSol and (nowClock - self._lastSilentAt) < ttl then
                return self._lastSilentSol
            end

            local char = self._player:GetCharacter()
            local root = self._player:GetRoot()
            if not (char and root) then
                self._lastSilentSol = nil
                return nil
            end

            local goalParts = self._match:GetOpposingGoalParts()
            local goalPart = goalParts[1]
            if not goalPart then
                self._lastSilentSol = nil
                return nil
            end

            local carry = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.BallCarry)
            local startPos = self._ball:GetPosition()
            if carry and type(carry.GetLaunchPosition) == "function" then
                local ok, lpPos = pcall(carry.GetLaunchPosition, char, "Shot")
                if ok and typeof(lpPos) == "Vector3" then
                    startPos = lpPos
                end
            end
            startPos = startPos or (root.Position + root.CFrame.LookVector * 2)

            local mouth = self:GetGoalMouth(goalPart, startPos)
            if not mouth then
                self._lastSilentSol = nil
                return nil
            end

            local snap = actorsSnapshot or self._player:GetAllActorsSnapshot()
            local enemyGk = self:_findEffectiveEnemyGoalkeeper(mouth, snap)
            local placement = config.TrajectoryPlacement or "Smart"
            local candidates = self:_buildTargetCandidates(mouth, placement, enemyGk, startPos)
            local targetCand = candidates[1]
            if not targetCand then
                return nil
            end

            local toGoalFlat = flatUnit(mouth.Center - startPos, Vector3.new(0, 0, -1))
            local nearbyDefenders = config.MultiDefenderCheck ~= false
                and self._player:GetNearbyDefenders(startPos, toGoalFlat, 26, 65, snap)
                or {}
            local barricades = self:GetActiveBarricadeParts()

            local curvedEnabled = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.CurvedKicks)
            local canCurve = curvedEnabled and type(curvedEnabled.IsEnabled) == "function" and curvedEnabled.IsEnabled()
            local curveStr = math.clamp(config.SilentAimCurveStrength or 0.85, 0.1, 1.0)
            local curveOptions = { 0 }
            if canCurve and (#nearbyDefenders > 0 or config.AutoCurveBypass) then
                curveOptions = { 0, -curveStr, curveStr }
            end

            local chargeConsts = self:GetShotChargeConstants()
            local chargeSec = chargeConsts.MaximumChargeSeconds or 0.65
            local boundaryWorld = self._ball:GetChargeBoundaryWorld()
            local reportedVel = self._player:GetFlatVelocity()
            local shootMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Shoot)
            local lp = self._player:GetLocalPlayer()
            local userId = lp and lp.UserId or nil
            local myTeam = self._match:GetLocalTeamName()
            local baseAimRay = self._player:GetReticleAimRay()

            local chosenCurve = 0
            local chosenTarget = targetCand.Position
            local bestScore = -math.huge

            for _, cand in ipairs(candidates) do
                for _, cVal in ipairs(curveOptions) do
                    local launchVel = self:ComputeLaunchVelocity(char, chargeSec, cand.Position, startPos, reportedVel, boundaryWorld, cVal)
                    local spin = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    if shootMod and type(shootMod.GetSpin) == "function" then
                        local sOk, sp = pcall(shootMod.GetSpin, char, launchVel, reportedVel, cVal, chargeSec)
                        if sOk and typeof(sp) == "Vector3" then
                            spin = sp
                        end
                    end
                    local vState = self._predictor:CreateVirtualState(startPos, launchVel, spin, userId, myTeam)
                    local crossPos, crossTime = self._predictor:GetGoalCrossing(vState, boundaryWorld, goalPart, vState.StartedAt, 2.4)
                    if crossPos then
                        local blocked = self._ballistics:CheckPathObstruction(startPos, vState, boundaryWorld, crossTime, nearbyDefenders, barricades)
                        local gkMargin = self:_evaluateGKSaveMargin(crossPos, crossTime, vState.StartedAt, mouth, enemyGk)
                        local score = gkMargin + (blocked and -55 or 10) + (cVal == 0 and 1.2 or 0)
                        if score > bestScore then
                            bestScore = score
                            chosenCurve = cVal
                            chosenTarget = cand.Position
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            local calRay, calVel, calCross = self._ballistics:CalibrateAimRay(
                char,
                chargeSec,
                startPos,
                reportedVel,
                boundaryWorld,
                goalPart,
                chosenTarget,
                chosenCurve,
                baseAimRay,
                {
                    ArcCompensation = config.SilentAimArcCompensation ~= false,
                    MaxPitchDeg = config.SilentAimMaxPitchDeg or 22,
                    MaxIterations = perfOn and 2 or 3,
                }
            )

            local result = {
                GoalPart = goalPart,
                GoalMouth = mouth,
                TargetPosition = chosenTarget,
                CrossingPosition = calCross and calCross.Position or chosenTarget,
                StartPos = startPos,
                Direction = calRay and calRay.Direction or self._ballistics:ClampDirectionPitch((chosenTarget - startPos).Unit, config.SilentAimMaxPitchDeg or 22),
                CalibratedAimRay = calRay,
                LaunchVelocity = calVel,
                Curve = chosenCurve,
                NearbyDefenderCount = #nearbyDefenders,
            }
            self._lastSilentSol = result
            self._lastSilentAt = nowClock
            return result
        end

        -- Full Shot Solver dengan Multi-Defender Check & Delta-Threshold Memoization
        function GoalTargetSolver:SolveBestShot(config, actorsSnapshot, opts)
            local nowClock = os.clock()
            local perfOn = self:_isPerfMode()
            local maxAge = (opts and opts.MaxAge) or (perfOn and 0.14 or 0.08)
            local force = (opts and opts.Force) == true
            local skipCal = (opts and opts.SkipCalibration) == true

            local root = self._player:GetRoot()
            local char = self._player:GetCharacter()
            if not (char and root) then
                self._lastSolution = nil
                return nil
            end

            local curPos = root.Position
            local curLook = root.CFrame.LookVector
            local posMoved = (curPos - self._lastSolvedPos).Magnitude > 0.45
            local lookChanged = (curLook - self._lastSolvedLook).Magnitude > 0.16

            local goalParts = self._match:GetOpposingGoalParts()
            local goalPart = goalParts[1]
            if not goalPart then
                self._lastSolution = nil
                return nil
            end

            local carry = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.BallCarry)
            local startPos = self._ball:GetPosition()
            if carry and type(carry.GetLaunchPosition) == "function" then
                local ok, lpPos = pcall(carry.GetLaunchPosition, char, "Shot")
                if ok and typeof(lpPos) == "Vector3" then
                    startPos = lpPos
                end
            end
            if not startPos then
                startPos = curPos + curLook * 2
            end

            local mouth = self:GetGoalMouth(goalPart, startPos)
            if not mouth then
                self._lastSolution = nil
                return nil
            end

            local toGoalFlat = flatUnit(mouth.Center - startPos, Vector3.new(0, 0, -1))
            local nearbyDefenders = (config.MultiDefenderCheck ~= false)
                and self._player:GetNearbyDefenders(startPos, toGoalFlat, 28, 65, actorsSnapshot)
                or {}
            local defSig = #nearbyDefenders * 100 + (nearbyDefenders[1] and math.floor(nearbyDefenders[1].Distance) or 0)

            -- Memoization ultra-ringan untuk pembawa bola (anti-stutter)
            if not force
                and self._lastSolution
                and not posMoved
                and not lookChanged
                and defSig == self._lastDefenderSig
                and (nowClock - self._lastSolvedAt < maxAge)
            then
                if skipCal or self._lastSolution.CalibratedAimRay ~= nil then
                    return self._lastSolution
                end
            end

            local zoneInfo = self:EvaluateShotZone(startPos, mouth, actorsSnapshot, config)
            local enemyGk = self:_findEffectiveEnemyGoalkeeper(mouth, actorsSnapshot)
            local candidates = self:_buildTargetCandidates(mouth, config.ShotPlacement or "AntiGK", enemyGk, startPos)
            local barricades = self:GetActiveBarricadeParts()

            local chargeConsts = self:GetShotChargeConstants()
            local minC = chargeConsts.MinimumReleaseSeconds or chargeConsts.MinimumChargeSeconds or 0.08
            local maxC = chargeConsts.MaximumChargeSeconds or 0.65

            local chargeSeconds = maxC
            if config.PowerMode == "Manual" then
                local alpha = math.clamp((config.ManualPowerPercent or 95) / 100, 0.05, 1)
                chargeSeconds = minC + (maxC - minC) * alpha
            elseif config.PowerMode == "Adaptive" then
                if zoneInfo.RecommendedMode == "QuickSnap" then
                    chargeSeconds = minC + (maxC - minC) * 0.44
                elseif zoneInfo.Distance <= 32 then
                    chargeSeconds = minC + (maxC - minC) * 0.80
                else
                    chargeSeconds = maxC
                end
            end

            local boundaryWorld = self._ball:GetChargeBoundaryWorld()
            local reportedVel = self._player:GetFlatVelocity()
            local curvedEnabled = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.CurvedKicks)
            local canCurve = curvedEnabled and type(curvedEnabled.IsEnabled) == "function" and curvedEnabled.IsEnabled()

            local curveList = { 0 }
            if canCurve and config.AutoCurveBypass then
                if perfOn and not force then
                    curveList = { 0, -0.68, 0.68 }
                else
                    curveList = { 0, -0.58, 0.58, -0.95, 0.95 }
                end
            end

            local shootMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Shoot)
            local lp = self._player:GetLocalPlayer()
            local userId = lp and lp.UserId or nil
            local myTeam = self._match:GetLocalTeamName()
            local now = self._ball:GetServerTime()
            local baseAimRay = self._player:GetReticleAimRay()

            local closestDefDist = nearbyDefenders[1] and nearbyDefenders[1].Distance or math.huge
            local bestSolution = nil
            local bestScore = -math.huge

            for _, cand in ipairs(candidates) do
                for _, curveVal in ipairs(curveList) do
                    local launchVel, aimDir = self:ComputeLaunchVelocity(char, chargeSeconds, cand.Position, startPos, reportedVel, boundaryWorld, curveVal)
                    local spin = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    if shootMod and type(shootMod.GetSpin) == "function" then
                        local sOk, sp = pcall(shootMod.GetSpin, char, launchVel, reportedVel, curveVal, chargeSeconds)
                        if sOk and typeof(sp) == "Vector3" then
                            spin = sp
                        end
                    end

                    local virtualState = self._predictor:CreateVirtualState(startPos, launchVel, spin, userId, myTeam)
                    local crossPos, crossTime = self._predictor:GetGoalCrossing(virtualState, boundaryWorld, goalPart, virtualState.StartedAt, 2.5)

                    if crossPos then
                        local blocked, blockKind = self._ballistics:CheckPathObstruction(
                            startPos,
                            virtualState,
                            boundaryWorld,
                            crossTime,
                            nearbyDefenders,
                            barricades
                        )

                        -- Cek jarak ke GK jika sangat dekat
                        if not blocked and enemyGk and not enemyGk.IsRagdolled then
                            if (enemyGk.Position - startPos).Magnitude <= 16 then
                                local gkBlocked = self._ballistics:CheckPathObstruction(
                                    startPos,
                                    virtualState,
                                    boundaryWorld,
                                    crossTime,
                                    { { PredictedPos = enemyGk.Position } },
                                    nil
                                )
                                if gkBlocked then
                                    blocked = true
                                    blockKind = "Goalkeeper"
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        local gkMargin = self:_evaluateGKSaveMargin(crossPos, crossTime, now, mouth, enemyGk)
                        local score = gkMargin
                        if blocked then
                            score = score - (blockKind == "Barricade" and 60 or 48)
                        end
                        if curveVal == 0 then
                            score = score + 0.6
                        end

                        if score > bestScore then
                            bestScore = score
                            bestSolution = {
                                GoalPart = goalPart,
                                GoalMouth = mouth,
                                TargetPosition = cand.Position,
                                TargetLabel = cand.Label,
                                LaunchPosition = startPos,
                                AimDirection = aimDir,
                                LaunchVelocity = launchVel,
                                Spin = spin,
                                Curve = curveVal,
                                ChargeSeconds = chargeSeconds,
                                MaxChargeSeconds = maxC,
                                PowerAlpha = clamp01((chargeSeconds - minC) / math.max(maxC - minC, 0.01)),
                                CrossingPosition = crossPos,
                                CrossingTime = crossTime,
                                Zone = zoneInfo,
                                Blocked = blocked,
                                BlockKind = blockKind,
                                NearbyDefenderCount = #nearbyDefenders,
                                ClosestDefenderDist = closestDefDist,
                                GKSaveMargin = gkMargin,
                                IsGKReachable = (enemyGk ~= nil) and (gkMargin < (config.AntiGKMinMarginStuds or 5.0)),
                                VirtualState = virtualState,
                                CalibratedAimRay = nil,
                            }
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            if bestSolution then
                if not skipCal then
                    local calRay, calVel, calCross = self._ballistics:CalibrateAimRay(
                        char,
                        bestSolution.ChargeSeconds,
                        startPos,
                        reportedVel,
                        boundaryWorld,
                        goalPart,
                        bestSolution.TargetPosition,
                        bestSolution.Curve,
                        baseAimRay,
                        {
                            ArcCompensation = config.SilentAimArcCompensation ~= false,
                            MaxPitchDeg = config.SilentAimMaxPitchDeg or 22,
                            MaxIterations = perfOn and 2 or 3,
                        }
                    )
                    if calRay then
                        bestSolution.CalibratedAimRay = calRay
                        if calVel then
                            bestSolution.LaunchVelocity = calVel
                        end
                        if calCross then
                            bestSolution.CrossingPosition = calCross.Position
                            bestSolution.CrossingTime = calCross.Time
                            bestSolution.Spin = calCross.Spin
                            bestSolution.VirtualState = calCross.VirtualState
                        end
                    end
                end
            else
                local fallbackPos = candidates[1] and candidates[1].Position or mouth.Center
                local fallbackVel, fallbackAim = self:ComputeLaunchVelocity(char, chargeSeconds, fallbackPos, startPos, reportedVel, boundaryWorld, 0)
                local safeDir = self._ballistics:ClampDirectionPitch(fallbackAim, config.SilentAimMaxPitchDeg or 22)
                bestSolution = {
                    GoalPart = goalPart,
                    GoalMouth = mouth,
                    TargetPosition = fallbackPos,
                    TargetLabel = "Fallback",
                    LaunchPosition = startPos,
                    AimDirection = safeDir,
                    LaunchVelocity = fallbackVel,
                    Spin = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
                    Curve = 0,
                    ChargeSeconds = chargeSeconds,
                    MaxChargeSeconds = maxC,
                    PowerAlpha = clamp01((chargeSeconds - minC) / math.max(maxC - minC, 0.01)),
                    CrossingPosition = nil,
                    CrossingTime = nil,
                    Zone = zoneInfo,
                    Blocked = true,
                    BlockKind = "NoPath",
                    NearbyDefenderCount = #nearbyDefenders,
                    ClosestDefenderDist = closestDefDist,
                    GKSaveMargin = 0,
                    IsGKReachable = true,
                    VirtualState = nil,
                    CalibratedAimRay = skipCal and nil or self._ballistics:BuildSyntheticAimRay(startPos - safeDir * 14, safeDir, baseAimRay),
                }
            end

            self._lastSolution = bestSolution
            self._lastSolvedAt = nowClock
            self._lastSolvedPos = curPos
            self._lastSolvedLook = curLook
            self._lastDefenderSig = defSig
            return bestSolution
        end

        function GoalTargetSolver:GetLastSolution()
            return self._lastSolution
        end

        function GoalTargetSolver:GetLastSilentSolution()
            return self._lastSilentSol
        end

        return GoalTargetSolver
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- BOOTSTRAP PART 6
    --========================================================================
    local ctx = LouisHub.Context
    local GoalTargetSolverClass = LouisHub.Require("Engine/GoalTargetSolver")

    local goalTargetSolver = GoalTargetSolverClass.new(
        ctx.Match,
        ctx.Ball,
        ctx.Player,
        ctx.Engines.Physics,
        ctx.Engines.Ballistics,
        ctx.Store,
        ctx.Performance
    )

    ctx.Engines.GoalSolver = goalTargetSolver

    LouisHub.PartLoaded[6] = true
    print(string.format("[LouisHub v%s] Part 6/10 initialized (Multi-Defender & Calibrated GoalTargetSolver Ready)", LouisHub.Version))
end

--============================================================================
-- LOUIS HUB // ILLEGAL SOCCER SUITE v3.2.0 (ULTRA PRECISION & ANTI-LAG EDITION)
-- PART 7/10: VOLLEY, PASS LANE, TACKLE, GK TELEMETRY & FAKE TRICK ENGINE
-- Requires Parts 1/10 - 6/10 loaded in getgenv().LouisHub
--============================================================================

do
    local GlobalEnv = (type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or _G
    local LouisHub = GlobalEnv.LouisHub

    if type(LouisHub) ~= "table"
        or not (LouisHub.PartLoaded
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[1]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[2]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[3]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[4]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[5]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[6])
    then
        error("[LouisHub] Parts 1/10 through 6/10 must be executed before Part 7/10!", 2)
    end

    local function clearTable(tbl)
        if type(table.clear) == "function" then
            table.clear(tbl)
            return
        end
        for k in pairs(tbl) do
            tbl[k] = nil
        end
    end

    local function flatVec(v)
        return Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
    end

    local function flatUnit(v, fallback)
        if typeof(v) ~= "Vector3" then
            return fallback
        end
        local fv = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
        if fv.Magnitude > 1e-4 then
            return fv.Unit
        end
        return fallback
    end

    local function segmentPointDistance(a, b, p)
        local ab = b - a
        local lenSq = ab:Dot(ab)
        if lenSq <= 1e-6 then
            return (p - a).Magnitude, 0
        end
        local t = math.clamp((p - a):Dot(ab) / lenSq, 0, 1)
