        local closest = a + ab * t
        return (p - closest).Magnitude, t
    end

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Engine/VolleyAnalyzer
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Engine/VolleyAnalyzer", function(Require)
        local GameModules = Require("Bridge/GameModules")

        local VolleyAnalyzer = {}
        VolleyAnalyzer.__index = VolleyAnalyzer

        function VolleyAnalyzer.new(ballBridge, playerBridge, matchBridge)
            return setmetatable({
                _ball = ballBridge,
                _player = playerBridge,
                _match = matchBridge,
                _lastEval = nil,
            }, VolleyAnalyzer)
        end

        function VolleyAnalyzer:Evaluate(config)
            local char = self._player:GetCharacter()
            local hum = self._player:GetHumanoid()
            local lp = self._player:GetLocalPlayer()
            if not (char and hum and lp) then
                self._lastEval = nil
                return nil
            end

            if self._match:IsGoalkeeper() then
                self._lastEval = nil
                return nil
            end

            local ballSnap = self._ball:GetSnapshot()
            if ballSnap.IsLocalOwner then
                self._lastEval = nil
                return nil
            end

            local vo = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientVolleyOpportunity)
            local bicycleMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.BicycleKick)
            local headbuttMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Headbutt)

            local eval = nil
            if vo and type(vo.GetEvaluation) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(vo.GetEvaluation, char)
                if ok then
                    eval = res
                end
            end

            local isLockedByMe = false
            if vo and type(vo.IsLockedBy) == "function" then
                local ok, locked = pcall(vo.IsLockedBy, lp)
                if ok then
                    isLockedByMe = locked == true
                end
            end

            if not eval and not isLockedByMe then
                self._lastEval = nil
                return nil
            end

            local ballGrounded = false
            if eval then
                ballGrounded = eval.BallGrounded == true
            elseif vo and type(vo.IsVolleyBallGrounded) == "function" then
                local ok, g = pcall(vo.IsVolleyBallGrounded, char)
                if ok then
                    ballGrounded = g == true
                end
            end

            local isLanded = self._player:IsLanded()
            local canBicycle = false
            if bicycleMod and type(bicycleMod.CanBicycleKick) == "function" then
                local ok, cb = pcall(bicycleMod.CanBicycleKick, char, hum)
                if ok then
                    canBicycle = cb == true
                end
            end

            local canHeadbutt = false
            if headbuttMod and headbuttMod.Enabled ~= false and type(headbuttMod.CanHeadbutt) == "function" then
                local ok, ch = pcall(headbuttMod.CanHeadbutt, char, not isLanded)
                if ok then
                    canHeadbutt = ch == true
                end
            end

            local isOwnFlickReady = false
            if ballSnap.State then
                local cmds = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ActionCommands)
                local rfKind = cmds and cmds.KickKinds and cmds.KickKinds.RainbowFlick or "RainbowFlick"
                if ballSnap.State.LastKickerUserId == lp.UserId and ballSnap.State.LastKickKind == rfKind then
                    isOwnFlickReady = ballSnap.Velocity.Y < 0
                end
            end

            local approachSec = eval and eval.ApproachSeconds or 0
            local carryApproachSec = eval and eval.CarryApproachSeconds or approachSec
            local predictedArrival = nil
            if vo and type(vo.GetPredictedArrivalTime) == "function" and eval then
                local ok, t = pcall(vo.GetPredictedArrivalTime, eval)
                if ok then
                    predictedArrival = t
                end
            end

            local claim = nil
            if vo and type(vo.GetClaim) == "function" then
                local ok, c = pcall(vo.GetClaim, char)
                if ok then
                    claim = c
                end
            end

            local recommended = "GroundVolley"
            if not ballGrounded then
                if canBicycle and config.AllowBicycleShot then
                    recommended = "BicycleShot"
                elseif canHeadbutt and config.AllowHeadbutt then
                    recommended = "Headbutt"
                elseif isLanded and config.AllowBicycleShot and config.AutoJumpForBicycle and approachSec >= 0.08 and approachSec <= 0.45 then
                    recommended = "JumpForBicycle"
                else
                    recommended = "AirVolley"
                end
            else
                recommended = "GroundVolley"
            end

            local result = {
                IsAvailable = true,
                IsLockedByMe = isLockedByMe,
                BallGrounded = ballGrounded,
                IsLanded = isLanded,
                CanBicycle = canBicycle,
                CanHeadbutt = canHeadbutt,
                IsOwnFlickReady = isOwnFlickReady,
                ApproachSeconds = approachSec,
                CarryApproachSeconds = carryApproachSec,
                MissAngleDegrees = eval and eval.MissAngleDegrees or 0,
                PredictedArrivalTime = predictedArrival,
                Claim = claim,
                RecommendedAction = recommended,
            }
            self._lastEval = result
            return result
        end

        function VolleyAnalyzer:GetLastEvaluation()
            return self._lastEval
        end

        return VolleyAnalyzer
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Engine/PassLaneAnalyzer
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Engine/PassLaneAnalyzer", function(Require)
        local GameModules = Require("Bridge/GameModules")

        local PassLaneAnalyzer = {}
        PassLaneAnalyzer.__index = PassLaneAnalyzer

        function PassLaneAnalyzer.new(playerBridge, matchBridge, goalTargetSolver)
            return setmetatable({
                _player = playerBridge,
                _match = matchBridge,
                _goalSolver = goalTargetSolver,
                _lastCandidates = {},
            }, PassLaneAnalyzer)
        end

        function PassLaneAnalyzer:Evaluate(actorsSnapshot)
            local root = self._player:GetRoot()
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not (root and cam) then
                clearTable(self._lastCandidates)
                return nil, self._lastCandidates
            end

            local myPos = root.Position
            local camLook = flatUnit(cam.CFrame.LookVector, Vector3.new(0, 0, -1))
            local oppGoals = self._match:GetOpposingGoalParts()
            local oppGoalPos = oppGoals[1] and oppGoals[1].Position or nil
            local barricades = self._goalSolver:GetActiveBarricadeParts()

            local assistedPass = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.AssistedPass)
            local maxDist = (assistedPass and assistedPass.Constants and assistedPass.Constants.MaximumTargetDistance) or 140

            local scored = {}
            for _, mate in ipairs(actorsSnapshot.Teammates or {}) do
                if not mate.IsRagdolled then
                    local toMate = mate.Position - myPos
                    local dist = toMate.Magnitude
                    if dist > 2 and dist <= maxDist then
                        local dir = toMate.Unit
                        local aimAlign = math.clamp((camLook:Dot(flatUnit(dir, camLook)) + 1) * 0.5, 0, 1)

                        local blocked = false
                        local blockKind = nil
                        for _, part in ipairs(barricades) do
                            if part and part.Parent and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                                local d = segmentPointDistance(
                                    part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(myPos),
                                    part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(mate.Position),
                                    Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                                )
                                if d <= part.Size.Magnitude * 0.5 + 0.8 then
                                    blocked = true
                                    blockKind = "Barricade"
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        if not blocked then
                            for _, enemy in ipairs(actorsSnapshot.Enemies or {}) do
                                if not enemy.IsRagdolled then
                                    local d, t = segmentPointDistance(myPos, mate.Position, enemy.Position)
                                    if d <= 2.9 and t > 0.04 and t < 0.96 then
                                        blocked = true
                                        blockKind = "Defender"
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        local progressScore = 0.5
                        if oppGoalPos then
                            local myGoalDist = (oppGoalPos - myPos).Magnitude
                            local mateGoalDist = (oppGoalPos - mate.Position).Magnitude
                            progressScore = math.clamp((myGoalDist - mateGoalDist) / 60 + 0.5, 0, 1)
                        end

                        local distScore = 1 - math.clamp(dist / maxDist, 0, 1)
                        local laneScore = blocked and 0.15 or 1.0
                        local totalScore = (aimAlign * 0.35 + progressScore * 0.25 + distScore * 0.15 + laneScore * 0.25)

                        table.insert(scored, {
                            Actor = mate.Actor,
                            Character = mate.Character,
                            UserId = mate.UserId,
                            DisplayName = mate.DisplayName,
                            Position = mate.Position,
                            Distance = dist,
                            IsBlocked = blocked,
                            BlockKind = blockKind,
                            RecommendedPassType = (blocked and dist > 22) and "LobPass" or "LowPass",
                            Score = totalScore,
                        })
                    end
                end
            end

            table.sort(scored, function(a, b)
                return a.Score > b.Score
            end)

            self._lastCandidates = scored
            return scored[1], scored
        end

        function PassLaneAnalyzer:GetLastCandidates()
            return self._lastCandidates
        end

        return PassLaneAnalyzer
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Engine/TackleAnalyzer
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Engine/TackleAnalyzer", function(Require)
        local GameModules = Require("Bridge/GameModules")

        local TackleAnalyzer = {}
        TackleAnalyzer.__index = TackleAnalyzer

        function TackleAnalyzer.new(playerBridge, ballBridge, matchBridge)
            return setmetatable({
                _player = playerBridge,
                _ball = ballBridge,
                _match = matchBridge,
            }, TackleAnalyzer)
        end

        function TackleAnalyzer:GetReachLimits()
            local st = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.SlideTackle)
            local minR = (st and st.Constants and st.Constants.TargetReachMinimumStuds) or 4
            local maxR = (st and st.Constants and st.Constants.TargetReachMaximumStuds) or 16
            return minR, maxR
        end

        function TackleAnalyzer:EvaluateOffBallTackleTarget(actorsSnapshot)
            if self._ball:IsLocalOwner() then
                return nil
            end
            local root = self._player:GetRoot()
            if not root then
                return nil
            end

            local minReach, maxReach = self:GetReachLimits()
            local ownerId = self._ball:GetOwnerUserId()
            local targetPos = nil
            local targetKind = nil

            if ownerId then
                for _, enemy in ipairs((actorsSnapshot and actorsSnapshot.Enemies) or {}) do
                    if enemy.UserId == ownerId and not enemy.IsRagdolled then
                        targetPos = enemy.Position + flatVec(enemy.Velocity) * 0.15
                        targetKind = "EnemyCarrier"
                        break
                    end
                end
            else
                local ballPos = self._ball:GetPosition()
                if ballPos then
                    targetPos = ballPos
                    targetKind = "LooseBall"
                end
            end

            if not targetPos then
                return {
                    InReach = false,
                    MinReach = minReach,
                    MaxReach = maxReach,
                    Distance = math.huge,
                }
            end

            local dist = flatVec(targetPos - root.Position).Magnitude
            return {
                InReach = dist <= maxReach,
                IsOptimalZone = dist >= minReach and dist <= maxReach,
                Distance = dist,
                MinReach = minReach,
                MaxReach = maxReach,
                TargetPosition = targetPos,
                TargetKind = targetKind,
            }
        end

        return TackleAnalyzer
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Engine/GoalkeeperAnalyzer
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Engine/GoalkeeperAnalyzer", function(Require)
        local Janitor = Require("Core/Janitor")
        local GameModules = Require("Bridge/GameModules")

        local GoalkeeperAnalyzer = {}
        GoalkeeperAnalyzer.__index = GoalkeeperAnalyzer

        function GoalkeeperAnalyzer.new(matchBridge, ballBridge, playerBridge, physicsPredictor, store)
            return setmetatable({
                _janitor = Janitor.new(),
                _match = matchBridge,
                _ball = ballBridge,
                _player = playerBridge,
                _predictor = physicsPredictor,
                _store = store,
                _activeCharges = {},
                _ownedSince = nil,
                _lastAnalysis = nil,
                _lastPreShotCheckAt = 0,
                _cachedPreShotThreat = nil,
            }, GoalkeeperAnalyzer)
        end

        function GoalkeeperAnalyzer:Start()
            self._janitor:Add(self._ball.PresentationEvent:Connect(function(evt)
                local et = evt.EventType
                local uid = evt.UserId
                if type(uid) ~= "number" then
                    return
                end
                if et == "KickChargeStarted" or et == "KickChargeUpdated" then
                    self._activeCharges[uid] = {
                        UserId = uid,
                        PassType = evt.PassType,
                        AimDirection = evt.AimDirection,
                        ChargeSeconds = evt.ChargeSeconds or 0,
                        KickDirection = evt.KickDirection,
                        Curve = evt.Curve,
                        TargetPosition = evt.TargetPosition,
                        IsBicycleKick = evt.IsBicycleKick,
                        ExtraData = evt.ExtraData,
                        StartedAt = evt.StartedAt or self._ball:GetServerTime(),
                        UpdatedAt = os.clock(),
                    }
                elseif et == "KickChargeCanceled" or et == "KickReleased" then
                    self._activeCharges[uid] = nil
                end
            end))
        end

        function GoalkeeperAnalyzer:_evaluatePreShotThreat(defendedGoal, boundaryWorld)
            local now = os.clock()
            local perfOn = self._store and self._store:Get("Meta.PerformanceMode") == true
            if next(self._activeCharges) == nil then
                self._cachedPreShotThreat = nil
                return nil
            end
            if perfOn and (now - self._lastPreShotCheckAt) < 0.10 then
                return self._cachedPreShotThreat
            end
            self._lastPreShotCheckAt = now

            local myTeam = self._match:GetLocalTeamName()
            local gkPred = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.GoalkeeperPrediction)
            local actorsMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Actors)
            local serverNow = self._ball:GetServerTime()

            local bestThreat = nil
            for uid, charge in pairs(self._activeCharges) do
                if now - charge.UpdatedAt > 2.4 then
                    self._activeCharges[uid] = nil
                else
                    local actor = actorsMod and actorsMod.GetByUserId and actorsMod.GetByUserId(uid) or nil
                    local actorTeam = actor and self._match:GetActorTeamName(actor) or nil
                    if actor and (not myTeam or actorTeam ~= myTeam) then
                        local shotState = nil
                        if gkPred and type(gkPred.GetActorShotState) == "function" then
                            local liveCharge = charge.ChargeSeconds or 0
                            if charge.StartedAt then
                                liveCharge = math.max(liveCharge, serverNow - charge.StartedAt)
                            end
                            local cmd = {
                                Kind = "Kick",
                                PassType = charge.PassType,
                                AimDirection = charge.AimDirection,
                                KickDirection = charge.KickDirection,
                                TargetPosition = charge.TargetPosition,
                                ChargeSeconds = liveCharge,
                                Curve = charge.Curve,
                                IsBicycleKick = charge.IsBicycleKick,
                            }
                            local ok, st = pcall(gkPred.GetActorShotState, actor, cmd, nil, boundaryWorld)
                            if ok and st then
                                shotState = st
                            end
                        end

                        if shotState and shotState.Position and shotState.Velocity then
                            local vState = self._predictor:CreateVirtualState(shotState.Position, shotState.Velocity, shotState.Spin, uid, actorTeam)
                            local crossPos, crossTime = self._predictor:GetGoalCrossing(vState, boundaryWorld, defendedGoal, serverNow, 2.2)
                            if crossPos then
                                local eta = math.max(0, (crossTime or serverNow) - serverNow)
                                if not bestThreat or eta < bestThreat.ETA then
                                    bestThreat = {
                                        UserId = uid,
                                        CrossingPosition = crossPos,
                                        ETA = eta,
                                        ShotSpeed = shotState.Velocity.Magnitude,
                                    }
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            self._cachedPreShotThreat = bestThreat
            return bestThreat
        end

        function GoalkeeperAnalyzer:EvaluateTelemetry(config, dmcInfo)
            if not self._match:IsGoalkeeper() then
                self._ownedSince = nil
                self._lastAnalysis = nil
                return nil
            end

            local root = self._player:GetRoot()
            local defendedGoal = self._match:GetDefendedGoalPart()
            if not (root and defendedGoal) then
                return nil
            end

            local ballSnap = self._ball:GetSnapshot()
            local boundaryWorld = ballSnap.BoundaryWorld or self._ball:GetChargeBoundaryWorld()
            local now = os.clock()

            if ballSnap.IsLocalOwner then
                if not self._ownedSince then
                    self._ownedSince = now
                end
            else
                self._ownedSince = nil
            end

            local gkActions = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.GoalkeeperActions)
            local forcedClearMax = (gkActions and gkActions.Constants and gkActions.Constants.ForcedClearHoldSeconds) or 5.0
            local ownedElapsed = self._ownedSince and (now - self._ownedSince) or 0

            local bestPassOut = nil
            if ballSnap.IsLocalOwner then
                local actorsSnapshot = self._player:GetAllActorsSnapshot(0.08)
                local bestScore = -math.huge
                for _, mate in ipairs(actorsSnapshot.Teammates or {}) do
                    if not mate.IsRagdolled then
                        local d = (mate.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                        if d > 6 and d <= 125 then
                            local score = d * 0.4
                            for _, enemy in ipairs(actorsSnapshot.Enemies or {}) do
                                local ed = (enemy.Position - mate.Position).Magnitude
                                if ed < 14 then
                                    score = score - (14 - ed) * 3
                                end
                            end
                            if score > bestScore then
                                bestScore = score
                                bestPassOut = mate
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            local preShotThreat = nil
            if config and config.PreShotThreatAlert and boundaryWorld then
                preShotThreat = self:_evaluatePreShotThreat(defendedGoal, boundaryWorld)
            end

            local incomingThreat = nil
            if dmcInfo and dmcInfo.GoalPosition and dmcInfo.ETA then
                incomingThreat = {
                    CrossingPosition = dmcInfo.GoalPosition,
                    ETA = dmcInfo.ETA,
                    Speed = ballSnap.Speed,
                }
            end

            local result = {
                DefendedGoal = defendedGoal,
                OwnedElapsed = ownedElapsed,
                ForcedClearMax = forcedClearMax,
                BestPassOut = bestPassOut,
                PreShotThreat = preShotThreat,
                IncomingThreat = incomingThreat,
                DiveAdvice = dmcInfo and dmcInfo.DiveAdvice or nil,
            }
            self._lastAnalysis = result
            return result
        end

        function GoalkeeperAnalyzer:GetLastAnalysis()
            return self._lastAnalysis
        end

        function GoalkeeperAnalyzer:Destroy()
            clearTable(self._activeCharges)
            self._janitor:Destroy()
        end

        return GoalkeeperAnalyzer
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Engine/FakeTrickEngine (State Machine: Bait -> Feint/Flick -> Shoot)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Engine/FakeTrickEngine", function()
        local FakeTrickEngine = {}
        FakeTrickEngine.__index = FakeTrickEngine

        function FakeTrickEngine.new(store, playerBridge, ballBridge)
            return setmetatable({
                _store = store,
                _player = playerBridge,
                _ball = ballBridge,
                _phase = "Idle", -- "Idle", "Baiting", "Feinting", "ReadyToShoot"
                _startedAt = 0,
                _phaseEndsAt = 0,
                _cooldownUntil = 0,
                _feintKind = nil,
                _feintDir = nil,
            }, FakeTrickEngine)
        end

        function FakeTrickEngine:IsActive()
            return self._phase ~= "Idle"
        end

        function FakeTrickEngine:GetPhase()
            return self._phase
        end

        function FakeTrickEngine:Reset()
            self._phase = "Idle"
            self._feintKind = nil
            self._feintDir = nil
        end

        function FakeTrickEngine:CanTrigger(shotSolution)
            if self._phase ~= "Idle" then
                return false
            end
            if os.clock() < self._cooldownUntil then
                return false
            end
            local cfg = self._store:Get("Striker")
            if not (cfg and cfg.FakeTrickBeforeShoot) then
                return false
            end
            if not self._ball:IsLocalOwner() then
                return false
            end
            if self._player:IsRagdolled() or self._player:IsStunned() or self._player:IsDribbling() then
                return false
            end

            local trigDist = cfg.FakeTrickTriggerDistance or 14
            local defDist = (shotSolution and shotSolution.ClosestDefenderDist) or math.huge
            local isBlocked = shotSolution and shotSolution.Blocked == true

            -- Picu jika ada bek dekat dalam jarak pemicu atau jalur tembak terblokir bek
            return (defDist <= trigDist) or (isBlocked and defDist <= (trigDist + 4))
        end

        function FakeTrickEngine:ComputeLateralFeintDirection(shotSolution)
            local root = self._player:GetRoot()
            if not root then
                return Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
            end
            local toGoal = shotSolution and shotSolution.GoalMouth
                and flatUnit(shotSolution.GoalMouth.Center - root.Position, root.CFrame.LookVector)
                or flatUnit(root.CFrame.LookVector, Vector3.new(0, 0, -1))
            local right = Vector3.new(-toGoal.Z, 0, toGoal.X)

            local nearby = self._player:GetNearbyDefenders(root.Position, toGoal, 16, 85)
            local latBias = 0
            for _, dInfo in ipairs(nearby) do
                local rel = flatVec(dInfo.Enemy.Position - root.Position)
                latBias = latBias + rel:Dot(right)
            end

            -- Menghindar berlawanan arah dengan posisi bek dominan
            local dodgeSign = (latBias >= 0) and -1 or 1
            return (right * dodgeSign + toGoal * 0.45).Unit
        end

        function FakeTrickEngine:BeginSequence(shotSolution, dispatcher)
            if not self:CanTrigger(shotSolution) then
                return false
            end

            local now = os.clock()
            local cfg = self._store:Get("Striker")
            local style = cfg.FakeTrickStyle or "SmartFeint" -- "SmartFeint", "FakeShotDodge", "FlickFake"

            self._startedAt = now
            self._feintDir = self:ComputeLateralFeintDirection(shotSolution)

            -- Langkah 1: Jika mendukung Fake Shot Bait (start charge 0.09s -> cancel), lakukan bait dulu
            local didBait = false
            if style ~= "FlickFake" and dispatcher and type(dispatcher.TriggerFakeShotBait) == "function" then
                didBait = dispatcher:TriggerFakeShotBait(0.09)
            end

            if didBait then
                self._phase = "Baiting"
                self._phaseEndsAt = now + 0.11
                self._feintKind = style
                return true
            end

            -- Langsung masuk fase Feint jika bait tidak diperlukan
            return self:_enterFeintPhase(shotSolution, dispatcher, style)
        end

        function FakeTrickEngine:_enterFeintPhase(shotSolution, dispatcher, style)
            local now = os.clock()
            local useFlick = (style == "FlickFake" or (style == "SmartFeint" and shotSolution and shotSolution.Zone and shotSolution.Zone.Distance > 20))
                and self._player:CanUseRainbowFlick()

            if useFlick and dispatcher and dispatcher:TriggerRainbowFlick() then
                self._phase = "Feinting"
                self._feintKind = "RainbowFlick"
                self._phaseEndsAt = now + 0.24
                self._cooldownUntil = now + 1.8
                return true
            end

            if dispatcher and type(dispatcher.TriggerLateralDodgeFeint) == "function" and dispatcher:TriggerLateralDodgeFeint(self._feintDir) then
                self._phase = "Feinting"
                self._feintKind = "DodgeFeint"
                self._phaseEndsAt = now + 0.22
                self._cooldownUntil = now + 1.4
                return true
            end

            self:Reset()
            self._cooldownUntil = now + 0.8
            return false
        end

        -- Mengembalikan status: "Idle", "Busy", "ExecuteShotNow"
        function FakeTrickEngine:Step(shotSolution, dispatcher)
            if self._phase == "Idle" then
                return "Idle"
            end

            local now = os.clock()
            -- Safety timeout agar tidak pernah nyangkut lebih dari 0.85 detik
            if (now - self._startedAt) > 0.85 or self._player:IsRagdolled() or self._player:IsStunned() then
                self:Reset()
                return "Idle"
            end

            if self._phase == "Baiting" then
                if now >= self._phaseEndsAt then
                    local ok = self:_enterFeintPhase(shotSolution, dispatcher, self._feintKind or "SmartFeint")
                    if not ok then
                        return "ExecuteShotNow"
                    end
                end
                return "Busy"
            end

            if self._phase == "Feinting" then
                if self._feintKind == "RainbowFlick" then
                    if not self._ball:IsLocalOwner() and now >= self._phaseEndsAt then
                        self:Reset()
                        return "ExecuteShotNow"
                    end
                elseif now >= self._phaseEndsAt and not self._player:IsDribbling() then
                    self:Reset()
                    return "ExecuteShotNow"
                end
                return "Busy"
            end

            self:Reset()
            return "Idle"
        end

        return FakeTrickEngine
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- BOOTSTRAP PART 7
    --========================================================================
    local ctx = LouisHub.Context
    local VolleyAnalyzerClass = LouisHub.Require("Engine/VolleyAnalyzer")
    local PassLaneAnalyzerClass = LouisHub.Require("Engine/PassLaneAnalyzer")
    local TackleAnalyzerClass = LouisHub.Require("Engine/TackleAnalyzer")
    local GoalkeeperAnalyzerClass = LouisHub.Require("Engine/GoalkeeperAnalyzer")
    local FakeTrickEngineClass = LouisHub.Require("Engine/FakeTrickEngine")

    local volleyAnalyzer = VolleyAnalyzerClass.new(ctx.Ball, ctx.Player, ctx.Match)
    local passLaneAnalyzer = PassLaneAnalyzerClass.new(ctx.Player, ctx.Match, ctx.Engines.GoalSolver)
    local tackleAnalyzer = TackleAnalyzerClass.new(ctx.Player, ctx.Ball, ctx.Match)
    local goalkeeperAnalyzer = GoalkeeperAnalyzerClass.new(ctx.Match, ctx.Ball, ctx.Player, ctx.Engines.Physics, ctx.Store)
    local fakeTrickEngine = FakeTrickEngineClass.new(ctx.Store, ctx.Player, ctx.Ball)

    goalkeeperAnalyzer:Start()

    ctx.Engines.Volley = volleyAnalyzer
    ctx.Engines.PassLane = passLaneAnalyzer
    ctx.Engines.Tackle = tackleAnalyzer
    ctx.Engines.Goalkeeper = goalkeeperAnalyzer
    ctx.Engines.FakeTrick = fakeTrickEngine

    LouisHub.OnDestroy(function()
        pcall(function() goalkeeperAnalyzer:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() fakeTrickEngine:Reset() end)
    end)

    LouisHub.PartLoaded[7] = true
    print(string.format("[LouisHub v%s] Part 7/10 initialized (Analyzers & Fake Trick State Machine Ready)", LouisHub.Version))
end

do

    local LouisHub = GlobalEnv.LouisHub

    if type(LouisHub) ~= "table"
        or not (LouisHub.PartLoaded
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[1]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[2]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[3]

            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[7])
    then
        error("[LouisHub] Parts 1/10 through 7/10 must be executed before Part 8/10!", 2)
    end

    local Players = LouisHub.SafeService("Players")
    local RunService = LouisHub.SafeService("RunService")
    local UserInputService = LouisHub.SafeService("UserInputService")

    local function clearTable(tbl)
        if type(table.clear) == "function" then
            table.clear(tbl)
            return
        end
        for k in pairs(tbl) do
            tbl[k] = nil
        end
    end

    local function flatVec(v)
        return Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
    end

    local function flatUnit(v, fallback)
        if typeof(v) ~= "Vector3" then
            return fallback
        end
        local fv = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
        if fv.Magnitude > 1e-4 then
            return fv.Unit
        end
        return fallback
    end

    local function finite(n)
        return type(n) == "number" and n == n and math.abs(n) < math.huge
    end

    local function finiteVector(v)
        return typeof(v) == "Vector3" and finite(v.X) and finite(v.Y) and finite(v.Z)
    end

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Engine/DmcGoalkeeperEngine (Unabridged + Missing Helpers Fixed)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Engine/DmcGoalkeeperEngine", function(Require)
        local Janitor = Require("Core/Janitor")
        local GameModules = Require("Bridge/GameModules")

        local DmcGoalkeeperEngine = {}
        DmcGoalkeeperEngine.__index = DmcGoalkeeperEngine

        local function unitVec(v, fallback)
            v = flatVec(v)
            return v.Magnitude > 0.0001 and v.Unit or (fallback or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1))
        end

        local function paddedBox(box, padding)
            local pad = math.max(0, tonumber(padding) or 0)
            return {
                Size = box.Size + Vector3.new(pad, pad, pad),
                CFrameOffset = box.CFrameOffset,
                Shape = box.Shape,
            }
        end

        local function choosePlan(candidates)
            local best = nil
            for i = 1, #candidates do
                local cand = candidates[i]
                if not best or cand.score < best.score then
                    best = cand
                end
            end
            return best
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine.new(store, playerBridge, matchBridge)
            local lp = Players and Players.LocalPlayer or nil

                    lastPlanReason = "NONE",
                    loopBusy = false,
                    runtimeFailures = 0,

                    runtimeError = nil,
                    countedFlights = {},

                    manualActionUntil = 0,
                    motionLease = nil,

                    ownLockAcquires = 0,
                    ownLockReleases = 0,
                    goalBasis = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
                    stick = Vector2.zero,
                    touches = {},

        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:SetUiBusyChecker(fn)
            self._isUiBusyFn = fn
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_isUiBusy()
            if type(self._isUiBusyFn) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(self._isUiBusyFn)
                if ok and res == true then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_resolveModules()
            if self._M then
                return self._M
            end
            local P = GameModules.Paths
            local M = {
                Dive = GameModules.Get(P.GoalkeeperDive),
                Assist = GameModules.Get(P.GoalkeeperDiveAssist),
                Actions = GameModules.Get(P.ActionCommands),
                Protocol = GameModules.Get(P.ActionRemoteProtocol),
                Locks = GameModules.Get(P.ActionLocks),
                Contacts = GameModules.Get(P.GoalkeeperActions),
                Physics = GameModules.Get(P.BallPhysics),
                Hitboxes = GameModules.Get(P.HitboxSettings),
                Prediction = GameModules.Get(P.GoalkeeperPrediction),
                Match = GameModules.Get(P.MatchSettings),
                Controllers = GameModules.Get(P.CharacterControllers),
                Grounding = GameModules.Get(P.Grounding),
                Ragdoll = GameModules.Get(P.Ragdoll),
                Renderer = GameModules.Get(P.ClientBallRenderer),
                Teams = GameModules.Get(P.ClientActorTeams),
                Practice = GameModules.Get(P.ClientPracticeSession),
                Movement = GameModules.Get(P.ClientMovement),
                Presentation = GameModules.Get(P.ClientGoalkeeperDivePresentation),
                Slide = GameModules.Get(P.ClientSlideTackle),
                Dodge = GameModules.Get(P.ClientDodge),
                Freeze = GameModules.Get(P.ClientKickoffFreeze),
                Replay = GameModules.Get(P.ClientGoalReplay),
                Clock = GameModules.Get(P.ClientMotionSmoothing),
                Keybinds = GameModules.Get(P.Keybinds),
                Role = GameModules.Get(P.ClientGoalkeeperRole),
                Actors = GameModules.Get(P.Actors),
                Motion = GameModules.Get(P.CharacterMotion),
            }

            if M.Dive and M.Assist and M.Actions and M.Protocol and M.Locks and M.Contacts
                and M.Physics and M.Hitboxes and M.Prediction and M.Match and M.Controllers
                and M.Grounding and M.Ragdoll and M.Renderer and M.Teams and M.Movement
                and M.Clock and M.Role and M.Actors and M.Motion then
                self._M = M
                return M
            end
            return nil
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_bindInputsAndStore()
            self._janitor:Connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
                if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick1 then
                    self._S.stick = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
                end
            end, "DmcGK_Thumb")

            self._janitor:Connect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, processed)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    self._S.touches[input] = true
                end
                if not self._S.alive or processed or not self._S.wasKeeper then
                    return
                end
                local M = self._M
                if not (M and M.Keybinds and type(M.Keybinds.Matches) == "function") then
                    return
                end
                for _, name in ipairs({ "Dive", "Tackle", "Dribble", "Jump", "RainbowFlick" }) do
                    local ok, matched = pcall(M.Keybinds.Matches, name, input)
                    if ok and matched then
                        self._S.manualActionUntil = os.clock() + 0.30
                        self._S.allowPositioning = false
                        self:_releaseMove(false)
                        return
                    end
                end
            end, "DmcGK_InputBegan")

            self._janitor:Connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
                self._S.touches[input] = nil
                if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick1 then
                    self._S.stick = Vector2.zero
                end
            end, "DmcGK_InputEnded")

            self._janitor:Connect(UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased, function()
                self._S.focused = false
                self._S.stick = Vector2.zero
                clearTable(self._S.touches)
            end, "DmcGK_FocusLost")

            self._janitor:Connect(UserInputService.WindowFocused, function()
                self._S.focused = true
                self._S.manualUntil = os.clock() + self._Config.ManualReleaseGrace
                self._S.nextPlanAt = 0
            end, "DmcGK_FocusGain")

            local keys = {
                "Goalkeeper.AutoDive",
                "Goalkeeper.CloseRangeRush",
                "Goalkeeper.HighBallJumps",
                "Goalkeeper.JumpThenDive",
                "Goalkeeper.DiveFilter",
                "Goalkeeper.BackwardRecovery",
                "Goalkeeper.PositionAssist",
                "Goalkeeper.PreShotCoverage",
                "Goalkeeper.PositionMode",
                "Goalkeeper.RespectManualMovement",
            }
            for _, k in ipairs(keys) do
                self._janitor:Add(self._store:OnChanged(k, function()
                    self:SyncFromStore()
                end))
            end
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:SyncFromStore()
            local gk = self._store:Get("Goalkeeper") or {}
            local prevEnabled = self._Config.Enabled
            self._Config.Enabled = gk.AutoDive == true
            self._Config.CloseRangeRush = gk.CloseRangeRush ~= false
            self._Config.HighBallJumps = gk.HighBallJumps ~= false
            self._Config.JumpThenDive = gk.JumpThenDive ~= false
            self._Config.DiveFilter = (gk.DiveFilter == "ALL" and "SMART") or (gk.DiveFilter or "SMART")
            self._Config.BackwardRecovery = gk.BackwardRecovery ~= false
            self._Config.PositionAssist = gk.PositionAssist ~= false
            self._Config.PreShotCoverage = gk.PreShotCoverage ~= false
            self._Config.PositionMode = gk.PositionMode or "THREATS"
            self._Config.RespectManualMovement = gk.RespectManualMovement ~= false

            if self._Config.Enabled and not prevEnabled then
                self._S.runtimeFailures = 0
                self._S.runtimeRetryAt = 0
                self._S.runtimeError = nil
                self._S.nextPlanAt = 0
            elseif not self._Config.Enabled and prevEnabled then
                self:ReleaseOwnedState(true)
                self:_status("OFF", "Manual controls untouched")
            end

            self._Rush.candidate = nil
            if self._M then
                self:_invalidateFrame(true)
            end
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_bindRemotes()
            if self._remotesReady then
                return
            end
            local stateRemote = GameModules.GetRemote("Ball", "State")
            local tackleRemote = GameModules.GetRemote("Ball", "Tackle")
            if not (stateRemote and tackleRemote) then
                return
            end
            self._remotesReady = true

            self._janitor:Connect(stateRemote.OnClientEvent, function(payload)
                if not self._S.alive or typeof(payload) ~= "table" or not self._M then
                    return
                end
                local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
                self._localPlayer = lp
                local key = tostring(payload.BallId)

                if payload.Kind == "Movement" or payload.Kind == "Removed" then
                    self._S.catches[key] = nil
                    if payload.BallId == self._M.Renderer.GetMatchBallId() then
                        self._S.nextPlanAt = 0
                        local revision = payload.Kind == "Movement" and self:_trajectoryStamp(payload.State) or nil
                        local identified = revision and (revision.movementSequence ~= nil
                            or revision.actionId ~= nil or revision.flightStartedAt ~= nil)
                        if not (identified and self._S.keeperWait and self:_sameTrajectory(self._S.keeperWait.trajectory, revision)) then
                            self._S.keeperWait = nil
                            self._S.keeperContact = nil
                        end
                    end
                    return
                end

                if not lp or payload.Kind ~= "Owned" or payload.OwnerUserId ~= lp.UserId then
                    return
                end
                if not (payload.IsDiveCatch or payload.IsGoalkeeperJumpCatch) then
                    return
                end
                if self._S.catches[key] == payload.OwnerUserId then
                    return
                end

                self._S.catches[key] = payload.OwnerUserId
                self._Stats.CatchesObserved = self._Stats.CatchesObserved + 1

                local expectedId = self:_pendingActionId()
                local matched = expectedId ~= nil
                    and payload.ActionId == expectedId
                    and self._S.pending ~= nil
                    and payload.BallId == self._S.pending.ballId

                if matched then
                    self._Stats.ActionMatches = self._Stats.ActionMatches + 1
                end

                self:_status("CATCH OBSERVED", matched and "Matching action ID" or "Catch confirmed")

                if self._S.pending and payload.BallId == self._S.pending.ballId then
                    self._S.pending = nil
                end
            end, "DmcGK_StateRemote")

            self._janitor:Connect(tackleRemote.OnClientEvent, function(packet)
                if not self._S.alive or typeof(packet) ~= "table" or typeof(packet.Command) ~= "table" then
                    return
                end
                if packet.Protocol ~= "ActionCommand" or packet.Phase ~= "ExecuteRejected" then
                    return
                end

                local expectedId = self:_pendingActionId()
                if expectedId == nil or packet.Command.ActionId ~= expectedId then
                    return
                end

                self._Stats.Rejected = self._Stats.Rejected + 1
                local delay = math.max(0.25, tonumber(packet.CooldownRemainingSeconds) or 0.25)
                if finite(packet.CooldownEndsAt) then
                    delay = math.max(delay, packet.CooldownEndsAt - workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
                end

                self._S.nextActionAt = math.max(self._S.nextActionAt, os.clock() + delay)
                local ownedMotion = self._S.controller ~= nil
                self:_finishMotion(true)

                local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
                if ownedMotion and lp and lp.Character and self._M and self._M.Presentation and type(self._M.Presentation.Stop) == "function" then
                    pcall(self._M.Presentation.Stop, lp.Character)
                end

                self._S.pending = nil
                self:_status("SERVER REJECTED", string.format("Wait %.2fs", delay))
            end, "DmcGK_TackleRemote")
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_report(key, message)
            if os.clock() >= (self._S.lastWarning[key] or 0) then
                self._S.lastWarning[key] = os.clock() + 4
                if self._Config.Debug then
                    warn("[LouisHub/DmcGK] " .. key .. ": " .. tostring(message))
                end
            end
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_flushStatusDisplay()
            if not self._S.displayCandidate then
                return
            end
            local clock = os.clock()
            if clock >= self._S.displayUntil and clock - self._S.displayCandidateSince >= self._Config.UIStatusDebounce then
                self._S.displayName = self._S.status
                self._S.displayDetail = self._S.detail
                self._S.displayCandidate = nil
            end
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_status(name, detail)
            self._S.status = name
            self._S.detail = detail or ""

            if self._S.displayName == name then
                self._S.displayDetail = self._S.detail
                self._S.displayCandidate = nil
                return
            end

            local event = name == "CATCH OBSERVED"
                or name == "SERVER REJECTED"
                or name == "JUMP QUEUED"
                or name == "CLOSE-RANGE RUSH"
                or string.sub(name, 1, 9) == "DIVE SENT"

            local immediate = event
                or name == "OFF"
                or name == "NO CHARACTER"
                or name == "WAITING FOR GK"
                or name == "RUNTIME ERROR"
                or name == "MANUAL SLIDE"
                or name == "MANUAL DODGE"
                or name == "RAGDOLL"
                or name == "ROUND TRANSITION"
                or name == "WINDOW UNFOCUSED"

            if immediate then
                self._S.displayName = name
                self._S.displayDetail = self._S.detail
                self._S.displayCandidate = nil
                self._S.displayUntil = os.clock() + (event and self._Config.UIEventHoldSeconds or 0)
                return
            end

            if self._S.displayCandidate ~= name then
                self._S.displayCandidate = name
                self._S.displayCandidateSince = os.clock()
            end
            self:_flushStatusDisplay()
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_now()
            return self._M.Clock.GetSmoothedServerTime()
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_inputActive()
            if not self._S.focused or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
                return true
            end
            for _, key in ipairs({
                Enum.KeyCode.W, Enum.KeyCode.A, Enum.KeyCode.S, Enum.KeyCode.D,
                Enum.KeyCode.Up, Enum.KeyCode.Down, Enum.KeyCode.Left, Enum.KeyCode.Right,
            }) do
                if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(key) then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return self._S.stick.Magnitude > 0.12 or next(self._S.touches) ~= nil
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_recordRequest(kind, frame)
            self._Stats.Requests = self._Stats.Requests + 1
            if kind == "JUMP" then
                self._Stats.JumpRequests = self._Stats.JumpRequests + 1
            elseif kind == "DIVE" then
                self._Stats.DiveRequests = self._Stats.DiveRequests + 1
            end
            local key = frame and frame.flightKey
            if key and not self._S.countedFlights[key] then
                self._S.countedFlights[key] = true
                self._Stats.FlightsActedOn = self._Stats.FlightsActedOn + 1
            end
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_resetPositionTarget()
            self._S.positionActive = false
            self._S.positionTarget = nil
            self._S.positionKey = nil
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_noteMoveRelease(reason)
            self._S.lastReleaseReason = reason
            self._S.releaseReasons[reason] = (self._S.releaseReasons[reason] or 0) + 1
            return reason
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_releaseMove(allowWrite)
            local S = self._S
            local M = self._M
            local character, owned = S.moveCharacter, S.ownedMove
            self:_resetPositionTarget()

            local function released(reason)
                if S.moveCharacter == character and S.ownedMove == owned then
                    S.ownedMove = nil
                    S.moveCharacter = nil
                end
                return reason == "NONE" and reason or self:_noteMoveRelease(reason)
            end

            if not owned or owned.Magnitude == 0 then return released("NONE") end
            if not character or not character.Parent or not M then return released("CHARACTER_GONE") end
            if not allowWrite then return released("YIELD_WITHOUT_WRITE") end
            if self:_inputActive() and S.focused and not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
                return released("MANUAL_INPUT")
            end

            local current = M.Controllers.GetMoveCommand(character)
            if current.Magnitude == 0 then return released("ALREADY_ZERO") end
            if current.Unit:Dot(owned.Unit) <= 0.999 then return released("COMMAND_CHANGED") end

            assert(M.Controllers.SetMoveCommand(character, Vector3.zero) ~= false, "Automatic movement stop was refused")
            S.releaseWrites = S.releaseWrites + 1
            return released("STOPPED")
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_invalidateFrame(allowWrite)
            self._S.lastFrame = nil
            self._S.keeperWait = nil
            self._S.keeperContact = nil
            self._S.allowPositioning = false
            self._S.coverageFrame = nil
            self._S.coverageNextAt = 0
            self._S.nextPlanAt = 0
            self:_releaseMove(allowWrite)
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_releaseMotionLock(lease)
            if not lease or not lease.lockHeld then
                return true
            end
            local ok, err = pcall(self._M.Movement.UnlockMovement, lease.lockName)
            ok = ok and err ~= false
            if ok then
                lease.lockHeld = false
                self._S.ownLockReleases = self._S.ownLockReleases + 1
                self._S.retiredLocks[lease.lockName] = nil
            else
                self._S.retiredLocks[lease.lockName] = lease
                self:_report("OWN LOCK RELEASE", err)
            end
            return ok
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_releaseMotionController(lease)
            if not lease or not lease.cancelPending then
                return true
            end
            local ok, result = pcall(lease.controller.Cancel)
            if ok and result ~= false then
                lease.cancelPending = false
                self._S.retiredMotions[lease] = nil
                return true
            end
            self._S.retiredMotions[lease] = true
            self:_report("OWN MOTION CANCEL", result)
            return false
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_finishMotion(cancel, expected)
            local lease = self._S.motionLease
            if not lease or (expected and lease ~= expected) then
                return false
            end
            self._S.motionLease = nil
            local active = lease.controller
            if self._S.controller == active then
                self._S.controller = nil
            end
            lease.closed = true
            local canceled = true
            if active and cancel then
                lease.cancelPending = true
                canceled = self:_releaseMotionController(lease)
            end
            local unlocked = self:_releaseMotionLock(lease)
            return canceled and unlocked
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_serviceOwnedMotion()
            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
            for lease in pairs(self._S.retiredMotions) do
                if os.clock() >= (lease.cancelRetryAt or 0) then
                    lease.cancelRetryAt = os.clock() + 1
                    self:_releaseMotionController(lease)
                end
            end
            for _, lease in pairs(self._S.retiredLocks) do
                if os.clock() >= (lease.retryAt or 0) then
                    lease.retryAt = os.clock() + 1
                    self:_releaseMotionLock(lease)
                end
            end
            local lease = self._S.motionLease
            if not lease then return end
            if not lease.root.Parent or (lp and lp.Character ~= lease.character) then
                self:_finishMotion(true, lease)
                return
            end
            if lease.controller and type(lease.controller.IsActive) == "function" then
                local ok, active = pcall(lease.controller.IsActive)
                if ok and active == false then
                    self:_finishMotion(false, lease)
                    return
                end
            end
            if os.clock() >= lease.deadline then
                self:_report("OWN MOTION TIMEOUT", "Releasing unfinished dive")
                self:_finishMotion(true, lease)
            end
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_manualJumpHeld()
            local M = self._M
            if not (M and M.Keybinds and type(M.Keybinds.Get) == "function") then
                return false
            end
            local ok, held = pcall(function()
                local binding = M.Keybinds.Get("Jump")
                for _, key in ipairs(binding.KeyCodes or {}) do
                    for _, linked in ipairs(M.Keybinds.GetLinkedKeyCodes(key)) do
                        if M.Keybinds.IsGamepadKeyCode(linked) then
                            if UserInputService:IsGamepadButtonDown(Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1, linked) then
                                return true
                            end
                        elseif UserInputService:IsKeyDown(linked) then
                            return true
                        end
                    end
                end
                for _, inputType in ipairs(binding.InputTypes or {}) do
                    if inputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                        or inputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2
                        or inputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3 then
                        if UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(inputType) then
                            return true
                        end
                    end
                end
                return false
            end)
            if not ok then
                return true
            end
            return held == true
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_releaseJumpLatch()
            local jump = self._S.jump
            if jump and jump.latch then
                if not self:_manualJumpHeld() and self._M and self._M.Movement then
                    assert(self._M.Movement.ReleaseJump() ~= false, "Jump latch release was refused")
                end
                jump.latch = false
            end
            return true
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_cancelOwnedJumpQueue()
            local queued = self._S.jump and self._S.jump.phase == "QUEUED"
            if not queued then
                return false
            end
            if not self:_manualJumpHeld() and self._M and self._M.Movement then
                self._S.jumpBlockOwned = true
                local ok, err = pcall(self._M.Movement.SetJumpStateBlocked, self._S.moveLock .. ".Queue", true)
                local released, releaseError = pcall(self._M.Movement.SetJumpStateBlocked, self._S.moveLock .. ".Queue", false)
                ok = ok and err ~= false
                released = released and releaseError ~= false
                self._S.jumpBlockOwned = not released
                assert(ok and released, "Queued jump cancellation was not confirmed")
            end
            self:_releaseJumpLatch()
            self._S.jump = nil
            if self._S.pending and self._S.pending.kind == "JUMP" then
                self._S.pending = nil
            end
            return true
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_authorizedFrame(frame)
            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
            return self._S.alive
                and self._Config.Enabled
                and self._S.focused
                and not self:_isUiBusy()
                and frame
                and lp and lp.Character == frame.character
                and frame.humanoid.Health > 0
                and self._M.Match.IsGoalkeeperCharacter(frame.character)
                and os.clock() >= self._S.manualActionUntil
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_blocked(character, root)
            local M = self._M
            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
            if next(self._S.retiredMotions) or next(self._S.retiredLocks) then
                return "OWN CLEANUP PENDING"
            end
            if M.Controllers.IsSuspended(character) then
                return "CONTROLLER SUSPENDED"
            end
            local isRagdolled
            if type(M.Ragdoll.IsLocallyRagdolled) == "function" then
                isRagdolled = M.Ragdoll.IsLocallyRagdolled(lp)
            elseif type(M.Ragdoll.IsEnabled) == "function" then
                isRagdolled = M.Ragdoll.IsEnabled(character)
            else
                return "RAGDOLL CHECK UNAVAILABLE"
            end
            if isRagdolled then
                return "RAGDOLL"
            end
            if (M.Freeze and M.Freeze.IsFrozen and M.Freeze.IsFrozen())
                or (M.Replay and M.Replay.IsActive and M.Replay.IsActive()) then
                return "ROUND TRANSITION"
            end
            if M.Slide and M.Slide.IsSlideTackling and M.Slide.IsSlideTackling() then
                return "MANUAL SLIDE"
            end
            if M.Dodge and M.Dodge.IsDribbling and M.Dodge.IsDribbling() then
                return "MANUAL DODGE"
            end
            if M.Dive.IsDiveConstraintActive(root) then
                return "DIVE IN PROGRESS"
            end
            if lp and M.Locks.HasGoalkeeperDiveBlock(lp, M.Renderer.GetVolleyLockUserId()) then
                return "ACTION LOCK"
            end
            if #M.Movement.GetZeroWalkSpeedSources() > 0 then
                return "MOVEMENT LOCK"
            end
            return nil
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_contextFor(root, state)
            local M = self._M
            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
            local goal = M.Practice and M.Practice.GetDefendedGoalPart and M.Practice.GetDefendedGoalPart()
            local mode, team, attack = "PRACTICE", nil, nil

            if not goal then
                team = lp and M.Teams.GetActorTeamName(lp)
                if not team then return nil end
                local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
                local data = map and map:FindFirstChild("Data")
                local home = data and data:FindFirstChild(team)
                local opponent = data and data:FindFirstChild(M.Match.GetOpposingTeamName(team))
                goal = home and home:FindFirstChild("Goal")
                attack = opponent and opponent:FindFirstChild("Goal")
                mode = "MATCH"
            end

            if not (goal and goal:IsA("BasePart")) then
                return nil
            end

            local forward
            if attack and attack:IsA("BasePart") then
                forward = unitVec(attack.Position - goal.Position)
            else
                local cached = self._S.goalBasis[goal]
                if cached and cached.frame == goal.CFrame then
                    forward = cached.forward
                else
                    local axis = goal.Size.X < goal.Size.Z * 0.5 and goal.CFrame.RightVector or goal.CFrame.LookVector
                    forward = unitVec(axis)
                    local reference = root.Position - goal.Position
                    if math.abs(flatVec(reference):Dot(forward)) < 0.3 and state and state.Position then
                        reference = state.Position - goal.Position
                    end
                    if reference:Dot(forward) < 0 then
                        forward = -forward
                    end
                    if cached and cached.forward:Dot(forward) < 0 then
                        forward = -forward
                    end
                    self._S.goalBasis[goal] = { frame = goal.CFrame, forward = forward }
                end
            end

            return {
                goal = goal,
                team = team,
                mode = mode,
                forward = forward,
                right = Vector3.new(-forward.Z, 0, forward.X),
            }
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_goalHalfWidth(context)
            local goal = context.goal
            local frame, size, axis = goal.CFrame, goal.Size, context.right
            return math.max(0.5, (
                math.abs(frame.RightVector:Dot(axis)) * size.X
                + math.abs(frame.UpVector:Dot(axis)) * size.Y
                + math.abs(frame.LookVector:Dot(axis)) * size.Z
            ) * 0.5)
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_readBall()
            local M = self._M
            local ballId = M.Renderer.GetMatchBallId()
            local owner = M.Renderer.GetOwnedUserId()
            if owner ~= nil then
                return { phase = "OWNED", ballId = ballId, owner = owner }
            end
            local state, world = M.Renderer.GetAuthoritativeMovementState()
            if not state or not world then
                local source = M.Renderer.GetTerminalVisualSourceKind and M.Renderer.GetTerminalVisualSourceKind() or nil
                return { phase = "UNAVAILABLE", ballId = ballId, source = source }
            end
            if not finiteVector(state.Position) or not finiteVector(state.Velocity) then
                return { phase = "INVALID", ballId = ballId }
            end
            return {
                phase = state.VolleyUserId and "VOLLEY" or "MOVEMENT",
                state = state,
                world = world,
                ballId = ballId,
                flightKey = tostring(ballId) .. ":" .. (
                    finite(state.FlightStartedAt) and ("flight:" .. tostring(state.FlightStartedAt))
                    or (finite(state.ActionId) and ("action:" .. tostring(state.ActionId))
                    or ("legacy:" .. tostring(state.StartedAt)))
                ),
            }
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_unavailableStatus(ball, context)
            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
            local mode = context and context.mode or "CLIENT"
            if ball.phase == "OWNED" then
                self._S.missingSince = nil
                self._S.ballInfo = (lp and ball.owner == lp.UserId) and "Held by you" or "Held by another actor"
                self:_status("READY / " .. mode, "Ball: " .. self._S.ballInfo .. "; positioning paused")
            elseif ball.phase == "VOLLEY" then
                self._S.missingSince = nil
                self._S.ballInfo = "Volley locked"
                self:_status("VOLLEY LOCK / " .. mode, "No automatic action")
            else
                self._S.missingSince = self._S.missingSince or os.clock()
                self._S.ballInfo = ball.phase == "INVALID" and "Invalid trajectory" or "No movement trajectory"
                if os.clock() - self._S.missingSince >= self._Config.MissingStateDisplayDelay then
                    self:_status("NO BALL STATE / " .. mode, self._S.ballInfo .. "; actions paused")
                else
                    self:_status("READY / " .. mode, "Ball state transition; actions paused")
                end
            end
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_trajectoryStamp(state)
            if not state or not finiteVector(state.Position) or not finiteVector(state.Velocity) then
                return nil
            end
            return {
                movementSequence = finite(state.MovementSequence) and state.MovementSequence or nil,
                actionId = finite(state.ActionId) and state.ActionId or nil,
                flightStartedAt = finite(state.FlightStartedAt) and state.FlightStartedAt or nil,
                radius = finite(state.Radius) and state.Radius or nil,
                blockedGoalTeam = state.BlockedGoalTeamName,
            }
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_sameTrajectory(a, b)
            if a == b then return true end
            if not a or not b then return false end
            return a.movementSequence == b.movementSequence
                and a.actionId == b.actionId
                and a.flightStartedAt == b.flightStartedAt
                and a.radius == b.radius
                and a.blockedGoalTeam == b.blockedGoalTeam
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_trajectoryChanged(frame, ball)
            local before, state = frame.sourceTrajectory, ball.state
            if not before or not state then return false end
            if not finiteVector(state.Position) or not finiteVector(state.Velocity) then return true end
            local sequence = finite(state.MovementSequence) and state.MovementSequence or nil
            local actionId = finite(state.ActionId) and state.ActionId or nil
            local flightStartedAt = finite(state.FlightStartedAt) and state.FlightStartedAt or nil
            local radius = finite(state.Radius) and state.Radius or nil
            return before.movementSequence ~= sequence or before.actionId ~= actionId
                or before.flightStartedAt ~= flightStartedAt
                or before.radius ~= radius or before.blockedGoalTeam ~= state.BlockedGoalTeamName
        end

        local function plannerSetting(value, fallback, low, high)
            return math.clamp(finite(value) and value or fallback, low, high)
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_plannerDeadline(frame)
            local anchor = frame.plannerAnchor or frame.readClock
            local normalHz = plannerSetting(self._Config.PlannerHz, 12, 1, 120)
            local urgentHz = math.max(normalHz, plannerSetting(self._Config.CloseShotPlannerHz, 30, 1, 120))
            local urgentWindow = plannerSetting(self._Config.UrgentShotSeconds, 0.55, 0.01, 2)
            local hz = finite(frame.eta) and frame.eta <= urgentWindow and urgentHz or normalHz
            local deadline = anchor + 1 / hz
            if finite(frame.launchAt) then
                deadline = math.min(deadline, frame.launchAt)
            end
            return deadline
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_trackPlanWindow(frame, plan)
            frame.launchAt = plan and plan.kind == "DIVE" and finite(plan.delay)
                and plan.delay > 0 and (frame.readClock + plan.delay) or nil
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_snapshot(character, root, humanoid)
            local M = self._M
            local readClock = os.clock()
            local ball = self:_readBall()
            if ball.phase ~= "MOVEMENT" then
                return nil, ball.phase, ball
            end

            local state, world = ball.state, ball.world
            local sourceTrajectory = self:_trajectoryStamp(state)
            local context = self:_contextFor(root, state)
            if not context then
                return nil, "WAITING FOR TEAM / GOAL"
            end

            local timestamp = self:_now()
            if not finite(timestamp) then
                return nil, "CLOCK UNAVAILABLE"
            end
            if finite(state.UpdatedAt) and state.UpdatedAt > timestamp + 0.10 then
                return nil, "WAITING FOR STATE TIME"
            end
            if finite(state.UpdatedAt) then
                timestamp = math.max(timestamp, state.UpdatedAt)
            end

            local current = M.Physics.GetStateAtTime(state, timestamp, world, {})
            if typeof(current) ~= "table" or not finiteVector(current.Position) or not finiteVector(current.Velocity) then
                return nil, "INVALID PREDICTED STATE"
            end

            local radius = current.Radius or M.Physics.BallRadius
            if not finite(radius) or radius <= 0 then
                return nil, "INVALID BALL RADIUS"
            end

            local position, crossingTime
            if not (context.team and state.BlockedGoalTeamName == context.team
                and type(M.Match.AreOwnGoalsEnabled) == "function" and not M.Match.AreOwnGoalsEnabled()) then
                position, crossingTime = M.Prediction.GetGoalCrossing(
                    current,
                    world,
                    context.goal,
                    timestamp,
                    M.Prediction.Constants.GoalCrossingMaximumPredictionSeconds
                )
            end

            if position ~= nil and (not finiteVector(position) or not finite(crossingTime)) then
                return nil, "INVALID GOAL CROSSING"
            end

            local landed = M.Controllers.IsLanded(character, humanoid)
            local humanoidState = M.Controllers.GetHumanoidState(character, humanoid)
            local receiveAirborne = humanoidState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping
                or humanoidState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall
            local floorY = M.Grounding.GetStandingY(humanoid, root)
            if not finite(floorY) then
                floorY = root.Position.Y
            end

            local jumpSpeed = humanoid.UseJumpPower
                and humanoid.JumpPower
                or math.sqrt(math.max(0, 2 * workspace.Gravity * humanoid.JumpHeight))

            local movementVelocity = flatVec(M.Controllers.GetLocomotionVelocity(character, humanoid))

            return {
                character = character,
                root = root,
                humanoid = humanoid,
                rootCF = root.CFrame,
                velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity,
                movementVelocity = movementVelocity,
                floorY = floorY,
                landed = landed,
                receiveAirborne = receiveAirborne,
                jumpSpeed = jumpSpeed,
                gravity = workspace.Gravity,
                state = current,
                world = world,
                at = timestamp,
                readClock = readClock,
                sourceTrajectory = sourceTrajectory,
                lastKickerUserId = state.LastKickerUserId,
                launchAt = nil,
                context = context,
                ballId = ball.ballId,
                flightKey = ball.flightKey,
                goalPosition = position,
                goalTime = crossingTime,
                eta = position and finite(crossingTime) and (crossingTime - timestamp) or nil,
                radius = radius,
            }
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_naturalRoot(f, t)
            local dy = 0
            if not f.landed then
                dy = math.max(f.floorY - f.rootCF.Position.Y, f.velocity.Y * t - 0.5 * f.gravity * t * t)
            end
            return f.rootCF + f.movementVelocity * t + Vector3.new(0, dy, 0)
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_holdLandingTime(f)
            if not f.receiveAirborne then return 0 end
            local height = math.max(0, f.rootCF.Position.Y - f.floorY)
            local velocity = f.velocity.Y
            if f.gravity > 0 then
                local speed = math.sqrt(velocity * velocity + 2 * f.gravity * height)
                if velocity < 0 then
                    return 2 * height / (speed - velocity)
                end
                return (velocity + speed) / f.gravity
            end
            return velocity < 0 and height / -velocity or math.huge
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_jumpedRoot(f, t, queueDelay)
            local elapsed = math.max(0, t - queueDelay)
            local rise = math.max(0, f.jumpSpeed * elapsed - 0.5 * f.gravity * elapsed * elapsed)
            return f.rootCF + f.movementVelocity * t + Vector3.new(0, rise, 0)
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_diveRoot(f, plan, t)
            local M = self._M
            local elapsed = math.max(0, t - plan.delay)
            local assist = plan.assist
            local distance = M.Dive.Constants.Distance + (assist and assist.ExtraDistance or 0)
            local scale = assist and assist.TravelScale or 1
            local vertical = assist and assist.InitialVerticalVelocity or M.Dive.Constants.InitialVerticalVelocity
            local gravity = assist and assist.Gravity or M.Dive.Constants.GravityStudsPerSecondSquared
            local direction = plan.direction
            if assist and assist.YawRadians ~= 0 then
                direction = M.Dive.RotateFlatDirection(direction, assist.YawRadians)
            end
            local travel = M.Dive.GetTravel(elapsed * scale, distance)
            local rise = math.max(f.floorY - plan.origin.Position.Y, vertical * elapsed + 0.5 * gravity * elapsed * elapsed)
            return plan.origin + direction * travel + Vector3.new(0, rise, 0)
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_rootAt(f, plan, t)
            if plan.kind == "HOLD" then
                return self:_naturalRoot(f, t)
            elseif plan.kind == "JUMP" then
                return self:_jumpedRoot(f, t, plan.queueDelay)
            end
            return self:_diveRoot(f, plan, t)
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_sampleFlight(f, horizon)
            local M = self._M
            local points = { { t = 0, p = f.state.Position } }
            if not finite(horizon) or horizon <= 0 then
                return points
            end
            local step = math.clamp(self._Config.SampleStep, 0.0025, 0.025)
            if f.eta and f.eta <= self._Config.UrgentShotSeconds then
                step = math.min(step, 0.005)
            end
            local a, b, state = {}, {}, f.state
            local settled = false
            local t = 0
            while t < horizon - 0.000001 do
                t = math.min(horizon, t + step)
                if not settled then
                    local output = state == a and b or a
                    state = M.Physics.GetStateAtTime(state, f.at + t, f.world, output)
                    settled = state.Mode == "Resting"
                        and type(M.Physics.IsStateSettled) == "function"
                        and M.Physics.IsStateSettled(state, f.world)
                end
                points[#points + 1] = { t = t, p = state.Position }
            end
            return points
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_receiveBox(f, plan, airborne)
            local M = self._M
            if plan.kind == "HOLD" then
                return M.Hitboxes.ExtendToFeet(airborne and M.Hitboxes.AirReceive or M.Hitboxes.Receive, f.humanoid, f.root)
            elseif plan.kind == "JUMP" then
                return paddedBox(M.Hitboxes.AirReceive, f.radius * 2)
            end
            local padding = math.max(M.Contacts.Constants.ContactPadding or 1.25, f.radius * 2)
            return paddedBox(M.Hitboxes.AirReceive, padding)
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_diveContactBounds(f, plan)
            local M = self._M
            local box = M.Hitboxes.AirReceive
            local padding = M.Contacts.Constants.ContactPadding or 1.25
            if not box or not finiteVector(box.Size) or box.Size.X <= 0 or box.Size.Y <= 0 or box.Size.Z <= 0
                or not finite(padding) or padding < 0 or not finite(f.radius) or f.radius <= 0
                or typeof(box.CFrameOffset) ~= "CFrame" or not finiteVector(box.CFrameOffset.Position)
