                or typeof(plan.origin) ~= "CFrame" or not finiteVector(plan.origin.Position) then
                return nil, nil
            end
            padding = math.max(1.25, padding, f.radius * 2)
            local half = (box.Size + Vector3.new(padding, padding, padding)) * 0.5
            local basis = plan.origin * box.CFrameOffset
            local right, up, look = basis.RightVector, basis.UpVector, basis.LookVector
            local offset = plan.origin:VectorToWorldSpace(box.CFrameOffset.Position)
            if not finiteVector(right) or not finiteVector(up) or not finiteVector(look) or not finiteVector(offset) then
                return nil, nil
            end
            local p = plan.origin.Position
            local margin = 0.01 + math.max(math.abs(p.X), math.abs(p.Y), math.abs(p.Z)) * 0.000001
            local extent = Vector3.new(
                math.abs(right.X) * half.X + math.abs(up.X) * half.Y + math.abs(look.X) * half.Z + margin,
                math.abs(right.Y) * half.X + math.abs(up.Y) * half.Y + math.abs(look.Y) * half.Z + margin,
                math.abs(right.Z) * half.X + math.abs(up.Z) * half.Y + math.abs(look.Z) * half.Z + margin
            )
            if not finiteVector(extent) then return nil, nil end
            return offset, extent
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_inspectPath(f, plan, points, requireContact)
            local M = self._M
            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
            local first = (plan.kind == "DIVE" or plan.kind == "JUMP_DIVE") and plan.delay
                or (plan.kind == "JUMP" and (plan.queueDelay + self._Config.SampleStep) or 0)

            local last = math.min(
                f.eta - self._Config.GoalMarginSeconds,
                (plan.kind == "DIVE" or plan.kind == "JUMP_DIVE")
                    and (plan.delay + M.Dive.Constants.HitboxCurveSeconds)
                    or self._Config.PredictionHorizon
            )

            if plan.kind == "JUMP" and f.gravity > 0 then
                last = math.min(last, plan.queueDelay + 2 * f.jumpSpeed / f.gravity)
            end
            if last <= first then return nil end

            local groundBox = self:_receiveBox(f, plan)
            local landingAt = plan.kind == "HOLD" and self:_holdLandingTime(f) or 0
            local airBox = landingAt > 0 and self:_receiveBox(f, plan, true) or nil
            local contact, nearest, deepest = nil, math.huge, 0
            local previousRoot, previousTime
            local isDive = plan.kind == "DIVE" or plan.kind == "JUMP_DIVE"
            local boundOffset, boundExtent, contactOptions
            if isDive then
                boundOffset, boundExtent = self:_diveContactBounds(f, plan)
                contactOptions = { BallRadius = f.radius }
            end

            for i = 2, #points do
                local left, right = points[i - 1], points[i]
                if right.t > first and left.t < last then
                    local t0 = math.max(first, left.t)
                    local stop = math.min(last, right.t)
                    while t0 < stop do
                        local airborne = airBox ~= nil and t0 < landingAt
                        local t1 = airborne and math.min(stop, landingAt) or stop
                        local box = airborne and airBox or groundBox
                        local width = right.t - left.t
                        local p0 = left.p:Lerp(right.p, (t0 - left.t) / width)
                        local p1 = left.p:Lerp(right.p, (t1 - left.t) / width)
                        local r0 = previousTime == t0 and previousRoot or self:_rootAt(f, plan, t0)
                        local r1 = self:_rootAt(f, plan, t1)
                        previousRoot, previousTime = r1, t1

                        local relativeHeight = p1.Y - r1.Position.Y
                        local relativeEnd
                        local measureMiss = true
                        if boundExtent and boundOffset then
                            relativeEnd = p1 - r1.Position - boundOffset
                            local dx = math.max(math.abs(relativeEnd.X) - boundExtent.X, 0)
                            local dy = math.max(math.abs(relativeEnd.Y) - boundExtent.Y, 0)
                            local dz = math.max(math.abs(relativeEnd.Z) - boundExtent.Z, 0)
                            local threshold = math.max(nearest, 0.0001)
                            measureMiss = not (dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz > threshold * threshold)
                        end

                        if measureMiss and (not isDive or relativeHeight <= M.Dive.Constants.MaximumSaveHeight) then
                            local miss, localPoint = M.Hitboxes.GetMissVector(r1 * box.CFrameOffset, box, p1)
                            nearest = math.min(nearest, miss.Magnitude)
                            if miss.Magnitude < 0.0001 then
                                local h = box.Size * 0.5
                                local radial = math.sqrt((localPoint.X / h.X) ^ 2 + (localPoint.Z / h.Z) ^ 2)
                                deepest = math.max(deepest, 1 - math.max(radial, math.abs(localPoint.Y / h.Y)))
                            end
                        end

                        local alpha
                        if isDive then
                            local possible = not contact or t0 < contact
                            if possible and boundExtent and boundOffset and relativeEnd then
                                local a = p0 - r0.Position - boundOffset
                                local b, h = relativeEnd, boundExtent
                                possible = not (
                                    (a.X > h.X and b.X > h.X) or (a.X < -h.X and b.X < -h.X)
                                    or (a.Y > h.Y and b.Y > h.Y) or (a.Y < -h.Y and b.Y < -h.Y)
                                    or (a.Z > h.Z and b.Z > h.Z) or (a.Z < -h.Z and b.Z < -h.Z)
                                )
                            end
                            if possible and contactOptions and lp then
                                contactOptions.StartRootCFrame = r0
                                contactOptions.EndRootCFrame = r1
                                local hit = M.Contacts.FindDiveSaveContact(lp, p0, p1, contactOptions)
                                alpha = hit and hit.Alpha
                            end
                        else
                            alpha = M.Hitboxes.GetSegmentEnterAlpha({ CFrame = r1 }, box, p0 + r1.Position - r0.Position, p1)
                        end

                        if finite(alpha) and alpha >= 0 and alpha <= 1 then
                            local time = t0 + (t1 - t0) * alpha
                            if (not airborne or time < landingAt) and time < f.eta - self._Config.GoalMarginSeconds - 0.000001 then
                                contact = contact and math.min(contact, time) or time
                                nearest = 0
                            end
                        end
                        t0 = t1
                    end
                end
                if right.t >= last then break end
            end

            if requireContact and not contact then return nil end
            if nearest == math.huge then return nil end
            return { time = contact, miss = nearest, clearance = math.max(0, deepest) }
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_allowedDirections(f)
            local filter = self._Config.DiveFilter
            local result
            if filter == "FORWARD" then
                result = { "F" }
            elseif filter == "LEFT" then
                result = { "L", "LF" }
            elseif filter == "RIGHT" then
                result = { "R", "RF" }
            elseif filter == "SIDES" then
                result = { "L", "R", "LF", "RF" }
            else
                result = { "F", "L", "R", "LF", "RF" }
            end

            if self._Config.BackwardRecovery and (filter == "SMART" or filter == "ALL") then
                local passed = (f.state.Position - f.rootCF.Position):Dot(f.context.forward) < -1
                local high = f.state.Position.Y - f.rootCF.Position.Y > self._M.Hitboxes.AirReceive.Size.Y * 0.5
                if passed or high or (f.eta and f.eta <= self._Config.UrgentShotSeconds) then
                    table.insert(result, "B")
                    table.insert(result, "LB")
                    table.insert(result, "RB")
                end
            end
            return result
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_makeDive(f, kind, delay, name)
            local M = self._M
            local basis = CFrame.lookAt(f.rootCF.Position, f.rootCF.Position + f.context.forward)
            local choice = M.Dive.GetDirectionChoiceByName(name)
            local origin = kind == "JUMP_DIVE"
                and self:_jumpedRoot(f, delay, self._S.queueEstimate)
                or self:_naturalRoot(f, delay)

            return {
                kind = kind,
                delay = delay,
                name = name,
                choice = choice,
                direction = M.Dive.GetWorldDirection(choice, basis, f.context.forward),
                origin = origin,
                basis = basis,
                queueDelay = self._S.queueEstimate,
            }
        end

        local function actionCost(plan, hit)
            if plan.kind == "HOLD" then
                return -10
            end
            local cost = plan.kind == "JUMP_DIVE" and 2 or (plan.kind == "JUMP" and 0.9 or 1)
            if plan.name == "B" or plan.name == "LB" or plan.name == "RB" then
                cost = cost + 0.18
            end
            return cost + plan.delay * 0.8 - hit.clearance * 0.7 + hit.time * 0.05
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_prepareAssist(f, plan)
            local M = self._M
            local proxy = { CFrame = plan.origin, Position = plan.origin.Position }
            local assist = M.Assist.GetAssist(f.state, f.world, proxy, plan.direction, f.at + plan.delay)
            if not assist then return nil end
            assist = table.clone(assist)
            if plan.kind == "DIVE" and plan.delay == 0 then
                M.Assist.DropBlockedLaunchYaw(f.root, plan.direction, assist)
            end
            return assist
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_evaluateDive(f, kind, delay, name, points, prepared)
            local base = prepared or self:_makeDive(f, kind, delay, name)
            base.useAssist = false

            local hit
            if prepared then
                hit = base.rawContact
                base.rawContact = nil
                if hit and not hit.time then hit = nil end
            else
                hit = self:_inspectPath(f, base, points, true)
            end

            local best
            if hit then
                base.contact = hit
                base.score = actionCost(base, hit)
                best = base
            end

            local assist = self:_prepareAssist(f, base)
            if assist then
                local corrected = table.clone(base)
                corrected.assist = assist
                corrected.useAssist = true
                local correctedHit = self:_inspectPath(f, corrected, points, true)
                if correctedHit then
                    corrected.contact = correctedHit
                    corrected.score = actionCost(corrected, correctedHit)
                    if not best or corrected.score < best.score - 0.001 then
                        best = corrected
                    end
                end
            end
            return best
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_divePathMayReach(f, points, horizon)
            local M = self._M
            local assist = M.Assist and M.Assist.Constants
            local dive = M.Dive.Constants
            local box = M.Hitboxes.AirReceive
            local extra = assist and assist.MaximumExtraReachStuds
            local distance = dive and dive.Distance
            local padding = M.Contacts.Constants.ContactPadding or 1.25

            if not finite(extra) or extra < 0 or not finite(distance) or distance < 0
                or not finite(padding) or padding < 0 or not finite(f.radius) or f.radius <= 0
                or not finite(horizon) or horizon <= 0 or not finiteVector(f.rootCF.Position)
                or not finiteVector(f.movementVelocity) or not box or not finiteVector(box.Size)
                or box.Size.X <= 0 or box.Size.Y <= 0 or box.Size.Z <= 0
                or typeof(box.CFrameOffset) ~= "CFrame" or not finiteVector(box.CFrameOffset.Position) or #points < 2 then
                return true
            end

            padding = math.max(1.25, padding, f.radius * 2)
            local contactSize = box.Size + Vector3.new(padding, padding, padding)
            local reach = distance + extra + contactSize.Magnitude * 0.5 + box.CFrameOffset.Position.Magnitude + 0.05
            local start = f.rootCF.Position
            local finish = start + f.movementVelocity * horizon
            if not finite(reach) or not finiteVector(finish) then return true end

            local minX, maxX = math.huge, -math.huge
            local minZ, maxZ = math.huge, -math.huge
            for _, point in ipairs(points) do
                local p = point.p
                if not finiteVector(p) then return true end
                minX, maxX = math.min(minX, p.X), math.max(maxX, p.X)
                minZ, maxZ = math.min(minZ, p.Z), math.max(maxZ, p.Z)
            end

            return not (maxX < math.min(start.X, finish.X) - reach
                or minX > math.max(start.X, finish.X) + reach
                or maxZ < math.min(start.Z, finish.Z) - reach
                or minZ > math.max(start.Z, finish.Z) + reach)
        end

        local KEEPER_FORECAST_SECONDS = 0.35
        local KEEPER_LAUNCH_RESERVE = 0.10
        local KEEPER_ORDER_MARGIN = 0.035
        local KEEPER_MIN_WAIT = 0.015
        local KEEPER_URGENT_GOAL = 0.25
        local KEEPER_DELAYS = { 0.05, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20, 0.25, 0.30, 0.40, 0.50 }

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_timingRootAt(body, t)
            local dy = body.airborne and math.max(
                body.floor - body.cf.Position.Y,
                body.velocity.Y * t - 0.5 * body.gravity * t * t
            ) or 0
            return body.cf + body.horizontal * t + Vector3.new(0, dy, 0)
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_timingPlayerContact(f, character, actor, points, limit)
            local M = self._M
            if character == f.character or not character.Parent
                or M.Motion.GetContext(character) ~= M.Motion.GetContext(f.character) then
                return nil
            end
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root = type(M.Motion.GetQueryRoot) == "function" and M.Motion.GetQueryRoot(character)
                or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not root or not root.Parent then
                return nil
            end
            local cf = M.Motion.GetCFrame(character)
            local velocity = M.Motion.GetVelocity(character) or root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            if typeof(cf) ~= "CFrame" or not finiteVector(cf.Position) or not finiteVector(velocity) then
                return nil
            end

            local state = M.Controllers.GetHumanoidState(character, humanoid)
            local airborne = state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall
            local floor = M.Grounding.GetStandingY(humanoid, root)
            if not finite(floor) then floor = cf.Position.Y end

            local body = {
                cf = cf,
                velocity = velocity,
                horizontal = flatVec(velocity),
                floor = floor,
                airborne = airborne,
                gravity = math.max(0, f.gravity),
            }
            local landing = airborne and self:_holdLandingTime({
                receiveAirborne = true,
                rootCF = cf,
                velocity = velocity,
                floorY = floor,
                gravity = body.gravity,
            }) or 0

            local groundBox = M.Hitboxes.ExtendToFeet(M.Hitboxes.Receive, humanoid, root)
            local airBox = airborne and M.Hitboxes.ExtendToFeet(M.Hitboxes.AirReceive, humanoid, root) or nil
            local initialBox = airBox or groundBox
            actor = actor or M.Actors.GetFromCharacter(character)
            local kicker = actor and finite(actor.UserId) and actor.UserId == f.lastKickerUserId
            local leavingKicker = kicker and M.Hitboxes.ContainsPoint({ CFrame = cf }, initialBox, points[1].p)

            local reach = initialBox.Size.Magnitude * 0.5 + initialBox.CFrameOffset.Position.Magnitude
            local endRoot = self:_timingRootAt(body, limit).Position
            local lowX, highX = math.min(cf.Position.X, endRoot.X) - reach, math.max(cf.Position.X, endRoot.X) + reach
            local lowZ, highZ = math.min(cf.Position.Z, endRoot.Z) - reach, math.max(cf.Position.Z, endRoot.Z) + reach
            local possible = false
            for i = 2, #points do
                local a, b = points[i - 1], points[i]
                if a.t >= limit then break end
                if math.max(a.p.X, b.p.X) >= lowX and math.min(a.p.X, b.p.X) <= highX
                    and math.max(a.p.Z, b.p.Z) >= lowZ and math.min(a.p.Z, b.p.Z) <= highZ then
                    possible = true
                    break
                end
            end
            if not possible then return nil end

            for i = 2, #points do
                local left, right = points[i - 1], points[i]
                if left.t >= limit then break end
                local start, stop = left.t, math.min(limit, right.t)
                while start < stop do
                    local inAir = airBox ~= nil and start < landing
                    local finish = inAir and math.min(stop, landing) or stop
                    local box = inAir and airBox or groundBox
                    local width = right.t - left.t
                    local a = left.p:Lerp(right.p, (start - left.t) / width)
                    local b = left.p:Lerp(right.p, (finish - left.t) / width)
                    local r0, r1 = self:_timingRootAt(body, start), self:_timingRootAt(body, finish)
                    if leavingKicker then
                        leavingKicker = M.Hitboxes.ContainsPoint({ CFrame = r1 }, box, b)
                    else
                        local alpha = M.Hitboxes.GetSegmentEnterAlpha({ CFrame = r1 }, box, a + r1.Position - r0.Position, b)
                        if finite(alpha) and alpha >= 0 and alpha <= 1 then
                            local time = start + (finish - start) * alpha
                            if time < limit and (not inAir or time < landing) then
                                return { character = character, time = time }
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    start = finish
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_timingFindContact(f, plan, points)
            local M = self._M
            local limit = math.min(
                KEEPER_FORECAST_SECONDS,
                plan.contact.time - KEEPER_ORDER_MARGIN,
                f.eta - self._Config.GoalMarginSeconds
            )
            if limit <= 0 or M.Motion.GetContext(f.character) == nil then return nil end
            local seen, best = {}, nil
            local function add(character, actor)
                if not character or character == f.character or seen[character] then return end
                seen[character] = true
                local ok, hit = pcall(self._timingPlayerContact, self, f, character, actor, points,
                    best and math.min(limit, best.time) or limit)
                if ok and hit then
                    best = hit
                elseif not ok then
                    self._S.keeperTimingErrors = (self._S.keeperTimingErrors or 0) + 1
                    self._S.keeperTimingError = tostring(hit)
                end
            end
            if Players then
                for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do add(player.Character, player) end
            end
            local characters = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
            local function addChildren(container)
                if not container then return end
                for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("Model") then add(child) end
                end
            end
            addChildren(characters)
            addChildren(characters and characters:FindFirstChild("Players"))
            addChildren(characters and characters:FindFirstChild("NPCs"))
            return best
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_timingSameWindow(f, window)
            return window and window.character == f.character and window.root == f.root
                and window.ballId == f.ballId and window.flightKey == f.flightKey and window.world == f.world
                and window.goal == f.context.goal and window.team == f.context.team
                and self:_sameTrajectory(window.trajectory, f.sourceTrajectory)
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_timingRefine(f, immediate, points)
            if not self:_timingSameWindow(f, self._S.keeperWait) then self._S.keeperWait = nil end
            self._S.keeperContact = nil
            if not immediate or immediate.kind ~= "DIVE" or immediate.delay ~= 0 then return immediate end

            local reserve = KEEPER_LAUNCH_RESERVE
            local clock = math.max(f.readClock, os.clock())
            if f.eta <= KEEPER_URGENT_GOAL or immediate.contact.time <= reserve
                or (self._S.keeperWait and clock >= self._S.keeperWait.deadline - KEEPER_MIN_WAIT) then
                return immediate
            end

            local hit = self:_timingFindContact(f, immediate, points)
            self._S.keeperContact = hit
            if not hit then return immediate end

            local latest, candidates = nil, {}
            local minimumClearance = math.min(0.12, immediate.contact.clearance)
            for _, delay in ipairs(KEEPER_DELAYS) do
                if delay < f.eta - self._Config.GoalMarginSeconds - reserve then
                    self._S.delayedPlansChecked = (self._S.delayedPlansChecked or 0) + 1
                    local candidate = self:_evaluateDive(f, "DIVE", delay, immediate.name, points)
                    if candidate and candidate.contact.clearance >= minimumClearance then
                        candidates[#candidates + 1] = candidate
                        latest = candidate
                    end
                end
            end
            if not latest then return immediate end

            local safeDelay = latest.delay - reserve
            if self._S.keeperWait then
                safeDelay = math.min(safeDelay, self._S.keeperWait.deadline - f.readClock)
            end
            if safeDelay <= KEEPER_MIN_WAIT or safeDelay <= clock - f.readClock then
                return immediate
            end

            self._S.delayedPlansChecked = (self._S.delayedPlansChecked or 0) + 1
            local selected = self:_evaluateDive(f, "DIVE", safeDelay, immediate.name, points)
            if not selected or selected.contact.clearance < minimumClearance then
                selected = nil
                for i = #candidates, 1, -1 do
                    if candidates[i].delay <= safeDelay + 0.000001 then
                        selected = candidates[i]
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            if not selected or selected.delay <= KEEPER_MIN_WAIT or selected.delay <= clock - f.readClock then
                return immediate
            end

            local deadline = f.readClock + selected.delay
            if deadline <= os.clock() + KEEPER_MIN_WAIT then return immediate end
            if not self._S.keeperWait then
                self._S.keeperWait = {
                    character = f.character,
                    root = f.root,
                    ballId = f.ballId,
                    flightKey = f.flightKey,
                    world = f.world,
                    goal = f.context.goal,
                    team = f.context.team,
                    trajectory = f.sourceTrajectory,
                    deadline = deadline,
                }
                self._S.keeperWaits = (self._S.keeperWaits or 0) + 1
            else
                self._S.keeperWait.deadline = math.min(self._S.keeperWait.deadline, deadline)
            end
            selected.waitForContact = true
            return selected
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_timingProtect(f, plan, points)
            local ok, result = pcall(self._timingRefine, self, f, plan, points)
            if ok then return result end
            self._S.keeperTimingErrors = (self._S.keeperTimingErrors or 0) + 1
            self._S.keeperTimingError = tostring(result)
            return plan
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_planSave(f)
            local began = os.clock()
            self._S.immediatePlansChecked = 0
            self._S.delayedPlansChecked = 0

            local function finish(plan, reason)
                self._S.plannerMilliseconds = (os.clock() - began) * 1000
                self._S.lastPlanReason = reason or (plan and plan.kind) or "NO PRE-GOAL CONTACT"
                return plan, reason
            end

            local horizon = math.min(self._Config.PredictionHorizon, f.eta - self._Config.GoalMarginSeconds)
            if horizon <= 0.0001 then
                return finish(nil, "TOO LATE")
            end

            local points = self:_sampleFlight(f, horizon)
            local hold = { kind = "HOLD", delay = 0 }
            local holdFrame = f
            if self._S.ownedMove then
                holdFrame = table.clone(f)
                holdFrame.movementVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end

            local holdContact = self:_inspectPath(holdFrame, hold, points, true)
            if holdContact then
                hold.contact = holdContact
                hold.score = actionCost(hold, holdContact)
                return finish(hold)
            end

            if self._S.jump and not self._Config.JumpThenDive then
                return finish(nil, "JUMP ONLY / RECOVERY")
            end

            local candidates = {}
            local jumpAvailable = self._Config.HighBallJumps
                and not self._S.jump
                and f.landed
                and os.clock() >= self._S.nextJumpAt
                and f.jumpSpeed > 0

            if jumpAvailable then
                local jump = { kind = "JUMP", delay = 0, queueDelay = self._S.queueEstimate }
                local hit = self:_inspectPath(f, jump, points, true)
                if hit then
                    jump.contact = hit
                    jump.score = actionCost(jump, hit)
                    candidates[#candidates + 1] = jump
                end
            end

            if not self:_divePathMayReach(f, points, horizon) then
                local plan = choosePlan(candidates)
                return finish(plan, not plan and "OUTSIDE DIVE REACH" or nil)
            end

            local directions = self:_allowedDirections(f)
            for _, name in ipairs(directions) do
                self._S.immediatePlansChecked = self._S.immediatePlansChecked + 1
                local plan = self:_evaluateDive(f, "DIVE", 0, name, points, nil)
                if plan then
                    candidates[#candidates + 1] = plan
                end
            end

            if #candidates > 0 then
                return finish(self:_timingProtect(f, choosePlan(candidates), points))
            end

            local possible = {}
            local function consider(kind, delay, name)
                if delay >= horizon - 0.005 then return end
                local plan = self:_makeDive(f, kind, delay, name)
                local raw = self:_inspectPath(f, plan, points, false)
                if raw then
                    plan.rawMiss = raw.miss
                    plan.rawContact = raw
                    possible[#possible + 1] = plan
                end
            end

            for _, name in ipairs(directions) do
                for _, delay in ipairs({ 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30 }) do
                    consider("DIVE", delay, name)
                end
                if jumpAvailable and self._Config.JumpThenDive then
                    for _, age in ipairs({ 0.10, 0.18, 0.26 }) do
                        consider("JUMP_DIVE", self._S.queueEstimate + age, name)
                    end
                end
            end

            table.sort(possible, function(a, b)
                local av = a.rawMiss + (a.kind == "JUMP_DIVE" and 0.10 or 0) + a.delay * 0.2
                local bv = b.rawMiss + (b.kind == "JUMP_DIVE" and 0.10 or 0) + b.delay * 0.2
                if av ~= bv then return av < bv end
                return a.name < b.name
            end)

            local budgetHit = false
            for _, candidate in ipairs(possible) do
                if self._S.delayedPlansChecked >= self._Config.MaxFinalCandidates then
                    budgetHit = true
                    break
                end
                self._S.delayedPlansChecked = self._S.delayedPlansChecked + 1
                local plan = self:_evaluateDive(f, candidate.kind, candidate.delay, candidate.name, points, candidate)
                if plan then
                    candidates[#candidates + 1] = plan
                end
            end

            if #candidates > 0 then
                return finish(choosePlan(candidates))
            end

            return finish(nil, budgetHit and "NO CONTACT / SEARCH LIMIT" or "NO PRE-GOAL CONTACT")
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_requestJump(f, plan)
            local M = self._M
            if not self._Config.HighBallJumps then return false end
            if plan.kind == "JUMP_DIVE" and not self._Config.JumpThenDive then return false end
            if not self:_authorizedFrame(f) or self:_manualJumpHeld() then return false end

            if self._S.ownedMove then
                self:_releaseMove(true)
                self._S.nextPlanAt = 0
                self:_status("SETTLING FOR JUMP", "Rechecking position after walking")
                return false
            end

            local fresh = self:_snapshot(f.character, f.root, f.humanoid)
            if fresh and not self:_sameTrajectory(f.sourceTrajectory, fresh.sourceTrajectory) then
                self._S.lastFrame = nil
                self._S.nextPlanAt = 0
                self:_status("REPLAN JUMP", "Trajectory changed before launch")
                return false
            end

            if not fresh or not fresh.eta or fresh.eta <= 0 or fresh.flightKey ~= f.flightKey
                or fresh.context.goal ~= f.context.goal or fresh.context.team ~= f.context.team
                or not fresh.landed or fresh.jumpSpeed <= 0 or self._S.jump or os.clock() < self._S.nextJumpAt then
                return false
            end

            local recheck
            if plan.kind == "JUMP_DIVE" then
                recheck = self:_makeDive(fresh, "JUMP_DIVE", plan.delay, plan.name)
                if plan.useAssist then
                    recheck.assist = self:_prepareAssist(fresh, recheck)
                end
            else
                recheck = { kind = "JUMP", delay = 0, queueDelay = self._S.queueEstimate }
            end

            local horizon = math.min(self._Config.PredictionHorizon, fresh.eta - self._Config.GoalMarginSeconds)
            if horizon <= 0.0001 then return false end
            local hit = self:_inspectPath(fresh, recheck, self:_sampleFlight(fresh, horizon), true)
            if not hit then
                self:_status("REPLAN JUMP", "No modeled pre-goal contact")
                return false
            end

            f, plan = fresh, recheck
            plan.contact = hit

            local reader = M.Controllers.CreateNativeJumpImpulseReader
                and M.Controllers.CreateNativeJumpImpulseReader(f.character) or nil

            if not self._Config.HighBallJumps or (plan.kind == "JUMP_DIVE" and not self._Config.JumpThenDive) then
                return false
            end
            if not self:_authorizedFrame(f) or self:_blocked(f.character, f.root) or self:_manualJumpHeld()
                or self._S.jump or os.clock() < self._S.nextJumpAt or not M.Controllers.IsLanded(f.character, f.humanoid) then
                return false
            end

            local jumpOK, requested = pcall(M.Movement.Jump)
            self._S.nextJumpAt = os.clock() + self._Config.JumpRetrySeconds

            if not jumpOK or not requested then
                if not self:_manualJumpHeld() then
                    pcall(M.Movement.ReleaseJump)
                end
                self:_status("JUMP REFUSED", "Existing jump state or input block")
                return false
            end

            self._S.jump = {
                phase = "QUEUED",
                requested = os.clock(),
                reader = reader,
                character = f.character,
                root = f.root,
                initialY = f.root.Position.Y,
                flightKey = f.flightKey,
                latch = true,
                planned = plan.kind,
            }
            self:_recordRequest("JUMP", f)
            self._S.pending = { kind = "JUMP", at = os.clock(), ballId = f.ballId, flight = f.flightKey }
            self:_status("JUMP QUEUED", "Waiting for actual takeoff")
            return true
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_updateJump(character, root, humanoid)
            local j = self._S.jump
            if not j then return false end
            if j.character ~= character or not root.Parent then
                self:_releaseJumpLatch()
                self._S.jump = nil
                return false
            end

            if j.phase == "QUEUED" then
                local impulse
                if j.reader then
                    impulse = j.reader()
                else
                    impulse = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y > 1
                        and root.Position.Y > j.initialY + 0.035
                        and not self._M.Controllers.IsLanded(character, humanoid)
                end
                if impulse then
                    j.phase = "AIRBORNE"
                    j.launched = os.clock()
                    local observedDelay = math.max(0, j.launched - j.requested)
                    local boundedDelay = math.clamp(observedDelay, 0.015, 0.20)
                    self._S.queueEstimate = self._S.queueEstimate * 0.7 + boundedDelay * 0.3
                    self:_releaseJumpLatch()
                elseif (j.reader and impulse == nil) or (os.clock() - j.requested > self._Config.JumpQueueTimeout) then
                    self:_cancelOwnedJumpQueue()
                    self:_status("JUMP NOT LAUNCHED", "No dive started")
                end
                return true
            end

            if self._M.Controllers.IsLanded(character, humanoid) then
                self:_releaseJumpLatch()
                self._S.jump = nil
            end
            return false
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_confirmHold(f)
            if not self:_authorizedFrame(f) then return false end
            self._S.allowPositioning = false
            if self._S.ownedMove then
                local released = self:_releaseMove(true)
                self._S.lastFrame = nil
                self._S.nextPlanAt = 0
                if released == "STOPPED" then
                    self._S.holdStops = self._S.holdStops + 1
                    self:_status("RECHECK HOLD", "Own movement stopped; waiting for fresh update")
                    return false
                end
            end

            local fresh = self:_snapshot(f.character, f.root, f.humanoid)
            if not fresh or not finite(fresh.eta) or fresh.eta <= 0 or fresh.flightKey ~= f.flightKey
                or fresh.root ~= f.root or fresh.context.goal ~= f.context.goal then
                self._S.lastFrame = nil
                self._S.nextPlanAt = 0
                self:_status("REPLAN HOLD", "Ball, role, or goal state changed")
                return false
            end
            if not self:_authorizedFrame(fresh) or self:_blocked(fresh.character, fresh.root) then
                return false
            end

            self._S.holdRechecks = self._S.holdRechecks + 1
            local horizon = math.min(self._Config.PredictionHorizon, fresh.eta - self._Config.GoalMarginSeconds)
            local hit
            if horizon > 0.0001 then
                hit = self:_inspectPath(fresh, { kind = "HOLD", delay = 0 }, self:_sampleFlight(fresh, horizon), true)
            end
            if not hit then
                self._S.holdRecheckMisses = self._S.holdRecheckMisses + 1
                self._S.lastFrame = nil
                self._S.nextPlanAt = 0
                self:_status("REPLAN HOLD", "Retained movement no longer predicts contact")
                return false
            end

            self:_trackPlanWindow(fresh, { kind = "HOLD", contact = hit })
            fresh.plannerAnchor = os.clock()
            self._S.lastFrame = fresh
            self._S.nextPlanAt = math.min(self._S.nextPlanAt, self:_plannerDeadline(fresh))
            self:_status("HOLD", string.format("Receive in %.3fs", hit.time))
            return true
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_queuedJumpMustCancel(ball)
            local jump = self._S.jump
            if not jump or jump.phase ~= "QUEUED" then return false end
            return not self._Config.HighBallJumps
                or (jump.planned == "JUMP_DIVE" and not self._Config.JumpThenDive)
                or not self._S.focused
                or self:_isUiBusy()
                or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() ~= nil
                or os.clock() < self._S.manualActionUntil
                or (ball ~= nil and (ball.phase ~= "MOVEMENT" or jump.flightKey ~= ball.flightKey))
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:ReleaseOwnedState(allowWrite)
            local S = self._S
            local M = self._M
            local failures = {}

            local function attempt(label, fn, ...)
                local ok, result = pcall(fn, ...)
                if not ok or result == false then
                    failures[#failures + 1] = label .. ": " .. tostring(result)
                    self:_report(label, result)
                end
            end

            if M then
                attempt("OWN POSITION RELEASE", self._invalidateFrame, self, allowWrite)
            else
                S.lastFrame = nil
                S.allowPositioning = false
                S.ownedMove = nil
                S.moveCharacter = nil
            end

            if S.jump and S.jump.phase == "QUEUED" then
                attempt("OWN JUMP CANCEL", function()
                    local j = S.jump
                    if not j or j.phase ~= "QUEUED" then return true end
                    local impulse
                    if j.reader then
                        impulse = j.reader()
                    else
                        local root, character = j.root, j.character
                        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        impulse = root and root.Parent and humanoid and finite(j.initialY)
                            and root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y > 1 and root.Position.Y > j.initialY + 0.035
                            and M and not M.Controllers.IsLanded(character, humanoid)
                    end
                    if S.jump ~= j then return true end
                    if impulse then
                        j.phase = "AIRBORNE"
                        j.launched = os.clock()
                        return true
                    end
                    return self:_cancelOwnedJumpQueue()
                end)
            end
            attempt("OWN JUMP RELEASE", self._releaseJumpLatch, self)

            if S.jump and S.jump.phase ~= "QUEUED" and not S.jump.latch then
                S.jump = nil
            end

            for lease in pairs(S.retiredMotions) do
                attempt("OWN MOTION RETRY", self._releaseMotionController, self, lease)
            end
            for _, lease in pairs(S.retiredLocks) do
                attempt("OWN LOCK RETRY", self._releaseMotionLock, self, lease)
            end
            if S.motionLease then
                attempt("OWN MOTION RELEASE", self._finishMotion, self, true)
            end

            if S.jumpBlockOwned and M then
                local ok, result = pcall(M.Movement.SetJumpStateBlocked, S.moveLock .. ".Queue", false)
                S.jumpBlockOwned = not (ok and result ~= false)
                if S.jumpBlockOwned then
                    failures[#failures + 1] = "OWN JUMP BLOCK RELEASE: " .. tostring(result)
                end
            end

            S.currentContext = nil
            S.pending = nil
            S.lastFollowupBlock = nil
            S.wasKeeper = false
            return #failures == 0, table.concat(failures, "; ")
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_hasOwnedState()
            local S = self._S
            return S.wasKeeper or S.motionLease ~= nil or S.jump ~= nil or S.ownedMove ~= nil
                or S.jumpBlockOwned or next(S.retiredLocks) ~= nil or next(S.retiredMotions) ~= nil
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_requestDive(f, plan, ignoreManualCheck)
            local M = self._M
            if not ignoreManualCheck and not self:_authorizedFrame(f) then return false end
            if plan.kind ~= "DIVE" or plan.delay ~= 0 then return false end

            if self._S.jump and not self._Config.JumpThenDive then
                if self._S.lastFollowupBlock ~= self._S.jump then
                    self._S.lastFollowupBlock = self._S.jump
                    self._S.followupBlocks = self._S.followupBlocks + 1
                end
                self:_status("JUMP ONLY / RECOVERY", "Automatic follow-up dives disabled")
                return false
            end

            if os.clock() - f.readClock > 0.09 then
                self:_status("REPLAN", "Planner sample expired")
                return false
            end

            if self._S.jump and (self._S.jump.phase ~= "AIRBORNE"
                or os.clock() - self._S.jump.launched < self._Config.JumpDiveMinimumAge) then
                return false
            end

            local reason = self:_blocked(f.character, f.root)
            if reason or os.clock() < self._S.nextActionAt then return false end

            self:_releaseMove(true)
            local fresh = self:_snapshot(f.character, f.root, f.humanoid)
            if fresh and not self:_sameTrajectory(f.sourceTrajectory, fresh.sourceTrajectory) then
                self._S.lastFrame = nil
                self._S.nextPlanAt = 0
                self:_status("REPLAN", "Trajectory changed before launch")
                return false
            end
            if not fresh or not fresh.eta or fresh.flightKey ~= f.flightKey
                or fresh.context.goal ~= f.context.goal or fresh.context.team ~= f.context.team then
                return false
            end

            local commit = self:_makeDive(fresh, "DIVE", 0, plan.name)
            if plan.useAssist then
                commit.assist = self:_prepareAssist(fresh, commit)
            end

            local horizon = math.min(M.Dive.Constants.HitboxCurveSeconds, fresh.eta - self._Config.GoalMarginSeconds)
            if horizon <= 0.0001 then return false end
            local flight = self:_sampleFlight(fresh, horizon)
            local hit = self:_inspectPath(fresh, commit, flight, true)
            if not hit and commit.assist then
                commit.assist = nil
                hit = self:_inspectPath(fresh, commit, flight, true)
            end
            if not hit then
                self:_status("REPLAN", "Final assisted motion has no contact")
                return false
            end

            commit.contact = hit
            if not ignoreManualCheck then
                local timed = self:_timingProtect(fresh, commit, flight)
                if timed.delay > 0 then
                    self._S.lastFrame = fresh
                    self:_trackPlanWindow(fresh, timed)
                    self._S.nextPlanAt = self:_plannerDeadline(fresh)
                    self._S.allowPositioning = true
                    self:_status("TIMING DIVE " .. timed.name, "Rechecking receiver/blocker")
                    return false
                end
            end

            if os.clock() - fresh.readClock > 0.09 then
                self._S.nextPlanAt = 0
                self:_status("REPLAN", "Final contact sample expired")
                return false
            end

            local exclusions = { fresh.character }
            local renderedBall = M.Renderer.GetBall()
            if renderedBall then table.insert(exclusions, renderedBall) end

            local params = RaycastParams.new()
            params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            params.FilterDescendantsInstances = exclusions
            params.RespectCanCollide = true

            local targetCF = self:_diveRoot(fresh, commit, hit.time)
            local travel = targetCF.Position - fresh.root.Position
            if travel.Magnitude > 0.05 and workspace:Raycast(fresh.root.Position, travel, params) then
                self:_status("PATH BLOCKED", "No forced dive")
                return false
            end

            if (not ignoreManualCheck and not self:_authorizedFrame(fresh)) or self._S.motionLease then
                return false
            end
            if self._S.jump and not self._Config.JumpThenDive then return false end

            self._S.nextLeaseId = self._S.nextLeaseId + 1
            local lease = {
                id = self._S.nextLeaseId,
                character = fresh.character,
                root = fresh.root,
                lockName = self._S.moveLock .. "." .. tostring(self._S.nextLeaseId),
                lockHeld = false,
                controller = nil,
                closed = false,
                deadline = os.clock() + M.Dive.Constants.DurationSeconds + 2,
            }
            self._S.motionLease = lease

            local controller, sentActionId, request
            local sendFailed = false
            self._S.nextActionAt = os.clock() + M.Dive.Constants.RepeatDelaySeconds

            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                lease.lockHeld = true
                M.Movement.LockMovement(lease.lockName)
                self._S.ownLockAcquires = self._S.ownLockAcquires + 1

                controller = M.Dive.Run(
                    fresh.root,
                    fresh.humanoid,
                    commit.choice,
                    function() return commit.basis end,
                    {
                        Assist = commit.assist,
                        StartedAt = fresh.at,
                        Finish = function(_, finished)
                            if self._S.motionLease == lease and (not lease.controller or lease.controller == finished) then
                                self:_finishMotion(false, lease)
                            end
                        end,
                    }
                )
                assert(controller, "Dive controller refused to start")
                assert(self._S.motionLease == lease and not lease.closed, "Dive completed before registration")
                lease.controller = controller
                self._S.controller = controller

                if not ignoreManualCheck then
                    assert(self:_authorizedFrame(fresh), "Action canceled before send")
                end

                local motion = assert(M.Dive.GetMotionSample(fresh.root), "Missing motion sample")
                local command = M.Actions.GoalkeeperDive({
                    AimDirection = commit.direction,
                    DirectionName = commit.name,
                    DiveMotion = motion,
                    ShotTime = fresh.at,
                })

                request = { kind = "DIVE", command = command, at = os.clock(), ballId = fresh.ballId, flight = fresh.flightKey }
                self._S.pending = request

                local sent, result = pcall(M.Protocol.Send, command)
                if not sent then
                    sendFailed = true
                    error("Native action sender failed: " .. tostring(result), 0)
                end
                if not finite(result) or command.ActionId ~= result then
                    sendFailed = true
                    error("Native action sender returned unexpected action ID", 0)
                end

                sentActionId = result
                request.id = result
                self:_recordRequest("DIVE", fresh)

                if self._S.motionLease == lease and not lease.closed and M.Presentation and type(M.Presentation.Play) == "function" then
                    pcall(M.Presentation.Play, fresh.character, commit.name, fresh.at)
                end
            end)

            self._S.nextActionAt = math.max(self._S.nextActionAt, os.clock() + (ok and M.Dive.Constants.RepeatDelaySeconds or 0.4))

            if not ok then
                local registered = lease.controller ~= nil
                self:_finishMotion(true, lease)
                if controller and not registered then pcall(controller.Cancel) end
                if self._S.pending == request then self._S.pending = nil end
                if sendFailed then
                    self._S.transportError = tostring(err)
                    self._store:Set("Goalkeeper.AutoDive", false)
                    self:ReleaseOwnedState(true)
                    self:_status("OFF", "Native sender failed; auto save disabled")
                else
                    self:_status("DIVE ERROR", tostring(err))
                end
                return false
            end

            self:_releaseJumpLatch()
            self._S.jump = nil
            self._S.lastAdvice = {
                DirectionName = commit.name,
                WorldDirection = commit.direction,
                OffsetSeconds = hit.time,
                AssistActive = commit.assist ~= nil,
                IsLegalHeight = true,
                MissDistance = 0,
                NeedsJump = false,
            }

            if self._S.pending == request then
                self:_status("DIVE SENT " .. commit.name, string.format("contact %.3fs / goal %.3fs (id %.0f)", hit.time, fresh.eta, sentActionId or 0))
            end
            return true
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_position(f, dt)
            local M = self._M
            if not self._S.allowPositioning or not self._Config.PositionAssist then
                self:_releaseMove(true)
                return
            end
            if self._S.jump or self._S.controller then
                self:_releaseMove(false)
                return
            end
            if self._Config.RespectManualMovement and self:_inputActive() then
                self._S.manualUntil = os.clock() + self._Config.ManualReleaseGrace
                self:_releaseMove(false)
                return
            end
            if os.clock() < self._S.manualUntil then
                self:_releaseMove(true)
                return
            end
            if os.clock() - f.readClock >= self._Config.CachedFrameMaxAge then
                self:_releaseMove(true)
                return
            end
            if self:_blocked(f.character, f.root) then
                self:_releaseMove(false)
                return
            end
            if not M.Controllers.IsLanded(f.character, f.humanoid) then
                self:_releaseMove(false)
                return
            end

            local remaining = f.eta and (f.eta - math.max(0, os.clock() - f.readClock)) or nil
            local threat = remaining ~= nil and remaining > 0 and f.goalPosition ~= nil
            local coverage = f.isCoverage == true

            if coverage and not self._Config.PreShotCoverage then
                self:_releaseMove(true)
                return
            end
            if not coverage and not threat and self._Config.PositionMode == "THREATS" then
                self:_releaseMove(true)
                return
            end
            if f.eta and not threat then
                self:_releaseMove(true)
                return
            end

            local c = f.context
            local origin = c.goal.Position
            local depth = self._Config.HomeDepth
            local ballOffset = f.state.Position - origin
            local halfWidth = math.max(0.5, self:_goalHalfWidth(c) - 2)
            local lateral = ballOffset:Dot(c.right) * depth / math.max(depth, ballOffset:Dot(c.forward))

            if threat then
                local crossing = (f.goalPosition - origin):Dot(c.right)
                local current = (f.root.Position - origin):Dot(c.right)
                lateral = math.clamp(current, crossing - self._Config.SafeLateralReach, crossing + self._Config.SafeLateralReach)
                if f.state.Position.Y - f.root.Position.Y > 5 then
                    depth = math.max(3.5, depth - 2.5)
                end
            end

            lateral = math.clamp(lateral, -halfWidth, halfWidth)
            local goalTarget = coverage and f.coverageTarget or (origin + c.forward * depth + c.right * lateral)
            local key = (coverage and "COVER:" or "SHOT:") .. tostring(c.goal) .. ":" .. f.flightKey .. ":" .. self._Config.PositionMode

            if self._S.positionKey ~= key then
                self:_resetPositionTarget()
                self._S.positionKey = key
                self._S.positionTarget = goalTarget
            elseif flatVec(goalTarget - self._S.positionTarget).Magnitude > self._Config.TargetChangeDeadzone then
                local response = math.max(0.01, self._Config.TargetResponseSeconds)
                local alpha = (threat and remaining < 0.35) and 1 or (1 - math.exp(-math.clamp(dt, 0, 0.10) / response))
                self._S.positionTarget = self._S.positionTarget:Lerp(goalTarget, alpha)
            end

            local errorVector = flatVec(self._S.positionTarget - f.root.Position)
            local startRadius = coverage and self._Config.CoverageStartRadius
                or (threat and self._Config.PositionStartDeadzone or self._Config.HomeRadius)
            local stopRadius = coverage and self._Config.CoverageStopRadius
                or (threat and self._Config.PositionDeadzone or self._Config.HomeStopRadius)
            startRadius = math.max(startRadius, stopRadius + 0.05)

            local distance = errorVector.Magnitude
            local settleRadius = coverage
                and (stopRadius + math.min(0.15, (startRadius - stopRadius) * 0.5))
                or stopRadius

            if self._S.positionActive then
                if distance <= settleRadius then
                    self:_releaseMove(true)
                    return
                end
            elseif distance < startRadius then
                if self._S.ownedMove then
                    self:_releaseMove(true)
                end
                return
            else
                self._S.positionActive = true
                self._S.positionChangedAt = os.clock()
            end

            local depthError = errorVector:Dot(c.forward)
            local sideError = errorVector:Dot(c.right)
            local vector = errorVector

            if not coverage and (not threat or remaining > 1.3) then
                if math.abs(depthError) > 1.4 then
                    vector = c.forward * depthError + c.right * sideError * 0.2
                elseif math.abs(sideError) > stopRadius then
                    vector = c.right * sideError
                else
                    vector = c.forward * depthError
                end
            elseif not coverage and remaining > 0.65 then
                vector = c.forward * depthError * 0.6 + c.right * sideError
            end

            if vector.Magnitude < 0.05 then
                self:_releaseMove(true)
                return
            end

            local scale = math.clamp((distance - stopRadius) / 3, 0, 1)
            local desired = vector.Unit * scale
            if self._S.ownedMove then
                local old = self._S.ownedMove
                if old.Magnitude > 0.001 and old:Dot(desired) < 0 then
                    old = Vector3.zero
                end
                desired = old:Lerp(desired, 1 - math.exp(-math.clamp(dt, 0, 0.10) / math.max(0.01, self._Config.PositionResponseSeconds)))
            end

            if desired.Magnitude < 0.005 then
                self:_releaseMove(true)
                return
            end

            if coverage then
                local params = RaycastParams.new()
                params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { f.character }
                params.RespectCanCollide = true
                local probe = desired.Unit * math.min(distance, 3)
                local shoulder = c.right * math.max(0.5, f.root.Size.X * 0.4)
                for _, shift in ipairs({ Vector3.zero, shoulder, -shoulder }) do
                    if workspace:Raycast(f.root.Position + shift, probe, params) then
                        self:_releaseMove(true)
                        self._S.allowPositioning = false
                        self:_status("COVERAGE BLOCKED", "Walking path obstructed")
                        return
                    end
                end
                self._S.coverageWrites = self._S.coverageWrites + 1
            end

            M.Controllers.SetMoveCommand(f.character, desired)
            self._S.ownedMove = desired
            self._S.moveCharacter = f.character
            self._S.positionWrites = self._S.positionWrites + 1
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_pendingActionId()
            local pending = self._S.pending
            if not pending then return nil end
            return pending.id or (pending.command and pending.command.ActionId)
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_rushSnapshot(character, root, humanoid)
            local M = self._M
            local Rush = self._Rush
            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
            if not self._Config.CloseRangeRush or self._S.jump or self._S.motionLease or self._S.controller then
                return nil
            end
            if not self:_authorizedFrame({ character = character, humanoid = humanoid }) then
                return nil
            end
            if self:_inputActive() or self:_manualJumpHeld() or os.clock() < self._S.manualUntil then
                return nil
            end
            if os.clock() < self._S.nextActionAt or self:_blocked(character, root) then
                return nil
            end
            if not M.Role.IsGoalkeeper() or not M.Controllers.IsLanded(character, humanoid) then
                return nil
            end

            local ball = self:_readBall()
            if ball.phase ~= "OWNED" or not finite(ball.owner) or (lp and ball.owner == lp.UserId) then
                return nil
            end

            local actor = M.Actors.GetByUserId(ball.owner)
            local target = actor and M.Actors.GetCharacter(actor)
            local targetRoot = target and target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local targetHumanoid = target and target:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not targetRoot or not targetHumanoid or targetHumanoid.Health <= 0 then
                return nil
            end

            local ownTeam = lp and M.Teams.GetActorTeamName(lp)
            local otherTeam = M.Teams.GetActorTeamName(actor)
            if ownTeam == nil or otherTeam == nil or ownTeam == otherTeam then
                return nil
            end
            if M.Match.IsGoalkeeperCharacter(target) or not M.Controllers.IsLanded(target, targetHumanoid) then
                return nil
            end

            local context = self:_contextFor(root)
            if not context then return nil end

            local targetPosition = M.Motion.GetPosition(target)
            local velocity = M.Motion.GetVelocity(target)
            local ballPosition = M.Renderer.GetPosition()
            if not finiteVector(targetPosition) or not finiteVector(velocity) or not finiteVector(ballPosition) then
                return nil
            end
            if (ballPosition - targetPosition).Magnitude > 7 or math.abs(velocity.Y) > 6 then
                return nil
            end

            velocity = flatVec(velocity)
            local offset = flatVec(targetPosition - root.Position)
            local distance = offset.Magnitude
            if distance < 0.25 or distance > Rush.Range then
                return nil
            end

            local goalPosition = context.goal.Position
            local keeperDepth = (root.Position - goalPosition):Dot(context.forward)
            local targetDepth = (targetPosition - goalPosition):Dot(context.forward)
            local halfWidth = self:_goalHalfWidth(context)

            if keeperDepth < 0 or keeperDepth > Rush.GoalDepth then return nil end
            if targetDepth < keeperDepth - 1 or targetDepth > Rush.GoalDepth or targetDepth < 0 then return nil end
            if math.abs((targetPosition - goalPosition):Dot(context.right)) > halfWidth + 2 then return nil end
            if velocity:Dot(context.forward) > 2 then return nil end

            local closing = -velocity:Dot(offset.Unit)
            local goalDirection = unitVec(goalPosition - targetPosition)
            local facingGoal = unitVec(targetRoot.CFrame.LookVector):Dot(goalDirection) > 0.40
            if distance > 6 and closing < 1 and not facingGoal then return nil end

            local floorY = M.Grounding.GetStandingY(humanoid, root)
            if not finite(floorY) then return nil end
            local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local cameraCF = camera and camera.CFrame or root.CFrame

            return {
                character = character,
                root = root,
                humanoid = humanoid,
                rootCF = root.CFrame,
                floorY = floorY,
                context = context,
                target = target,
                owner = ball.owner,
                ballId = ball.ballId,
                ballPosition = ballPosition,
                targetPosition = targetPosition,
                targetVelocity = velocity,
                cameraCF = cameraCF,
                readClock = os.clock(),
                halfWidth = halfWidth,
            }
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_rushDirectionAllowed(direction, context)
            local forward = direction:Dot(context.forward)
            local right = direction:Dot(context.right)
            if forward < -0.20 then return false end
            local filter = self._Config.DiveFilter
            if filter == "FORWARD" then return forward > 0.90
            elseif filter == "LEFT" then return right < -0.35
            elseif filter == "RIGHT" then return right > 0.35
            elseif filter == "SIDES" then return math.abs(right) > 0.35
            end
            return true
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_rushContact(f, plan)
            local M = self._M
            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
            local horizon = math.min(self._Rush.ContactHorizon, M.Dive.Constants.HitboxCurveSeconds)
            local box = M.Hitboxes.AirReceive
            local previousTime, previousBall, previousRoot = 0, f.ballPosition, f.rootCF
            local time = 0

            while time < horizon - 0.000001 do
                time = math.min(horizon, time + 0.01)
                local ballPosition = f.ballPosition + f.targetVelocity * time
                local rootCF = self:_diveRoot(f, plan, time)
                local hit = lp and M.Contacts.FindDiveSaveContact(lp, previousBall, ballPosition, {
                    StartRootCFrame = previousRoot,
                    EndRootCFrame = rootCF,
                    BallRadius = M.Physics.BallRadius,
                })
                local coreAlpha = M.Hitboxes.GetSegmentEnterAlpha(
                    { CFrame = rootCF },
                    box,
                    previousBall + rootCF.Position - previousRoot.Position,
                    ballPosition
                )
                if hit and coreAlpha then
                    local contactTime = previousTime + (time - previousTime) * math.max(hit.Alpha, coreAlpha)
                    if contactTime >= 0.03 then return contactTime end
                end
                previousTime, previousBall, previousRoot = time, ballPosition, rootCF
            end
            return nil
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_rushPathClear(f, plan)
            local M = self._M
            local exclusions = { f.character, f.target }
            local renderedBall = M.Renderer.GetBall()
            if renderedBall then exclusions[#exclusions + 1] = renderedBall end

            local params = RaycastParams.new()
            params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            params.FilterDescendantsInstances = exclusions
            params.RespectCanCollide = true

            local side = Vector3.new(-plan.direction.Z, 0, plan.direction.X)
            local shoulder = side * math.max(0.5, f.root.Size.X * 0.4)
            local origin = f.context.goal.Position
            local previous = f.root.Position
            local duration = M.Dive.Constants.DurationSeconds
            local time = 0

            while time < duration - 0.000001 do
                time = math.min(duration, time + 0.05)
                local position = self:_diveRoot(f, plan, time).Position
                local offset = position - origin
                local depth = offset:Dot(f.context.forward)
                if depth < -0.5 or depth > self._Rush.GoalDepth then return false end
                if math.abs(offset:Dot(f.context.right)) > f.halfWidth + 2 then return false end
                local travel = position - previous
                if travel.Magnitude > 0.001 then
                    for _, shift in ipairs({ Vector3.zero, shoulder, -shoulder }) do
                        if workspace:Raycast(previous + shift, travel, params) then return false end
                    end
                end
                previous = position
            end
            return true
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_rushPlan(f)
            local M = self._M
            local best
            local toward = unitVec(f.targetPosition - f.root.Position, f.context.forward)
            for _, name in ipairs({ "F", "LF", "L", "LB", "RF", "R", "RB", "B" }) do
                local choice = M.Dive.GetDirectionChoiceByName(name)
                local direction = M.Dive.GetWorldDirection(choice, f.cameraCF, unitVec(f.rootCF.LookVector))
                if self:_rushDirectionAllowed(direction, f.context) and direction:Dot(toward) > 0.50 then
                    local plan = {
                        kind = "DIVE",
                        name = name,
                        choice = choice,
                        direction = direction,
                        origin = f.rootCF,
                        delay = 0,
                        assist = nil,
                    }
                    local contact = self:_rushContact(f, plan)
                    if contact then
                        plan.contact = contact
                        plan.score = contact + 0.08 * (1 - direction:Dot(toward))
                        if (not best or plan.score < best.score) and self:_rushPathClear(f, plan) then
                            best = plan
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return best
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_tryCloseRush(character, root, humanoid)
            local M = self._M
            local Rush = self._Rush
            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
            if os.clock() < Rush.nextScan then return false end
            Rush.nextScan = os.clock() + Rush.ScanInterval

            if os.clock() - Rush.lastSeenAt > 0.15 then
                Rush.candidate = nil
            end

            local f = self:_rushSnapshot(character, root, humanoid)
            if not f then
                Rush.candidate = nil
                return false
            end

            Rush.lastSeenAt = os.clock()
            local key = tostring(f.ballId) .. ":" .. tostring(f.owner)
            if Rush.candidate ~= key then
                Rush.candidate = key
                Rush.candidateSince = os.clock()
                return false
            end
            if os.clock() - Rush.candidateSince < Rush.ConfirmSeconds then
                return false
            end

            local plan = self:_rushPlan(f)
            if not plan or os.clock() - f.readClock > 0.05 then
                return false
            end

            local current = self:_readBall()
            if current.phase ~= "OWNED" or current.owner ~= f.owner or current.ballId ~= f.ballId then
                return false
            end
            if not lp or lp.Character ~= character or not self._Config.CloseRangeRush then return false end
            if not self:_authorizedFrame(f) or self:_blocked(character, root) or self:_inputActive() then return false end
            if M.Actors.GetCharacter(M.Actors.GetByUserId(f.owner)) ~= f.target then return false end

            local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local cameraCF = camera and camera.CFrame or root.CFrame
            if unitVec(cameraCF.LookVector):Dot(unitVec(f.cameraCF.LookVector)) < 0.999 then
                return false
            end

            local actualChoice = M.Dive.GetDirectionChoice(plan.direction, cameraCF, unitVec(root.CFrame.LookVector))
            if actualChoice.Name ~= plan.choice.Name then return false end

            if self._S.ownedMove then self:_releaseMove(true) end
            local previousMove = M.Controllers.GetMoveCommand(character)
            if not finiteVector(previousMove) then return false end

            local wrote = false
            local ok, started = pcall(function()
                wrote = true
                M.Controllers.SetMoveCommand(character, plan.direction)
                local selected = M.Dive.GetDirectionChoice(M.Controllers.GetMoveCommand(character), cameraCF, unitVec(root.CFrame.LookVector))
                if selected.Name ~= plan.choice.Name then return false end
                local finalBall = self:_readBall()
                if finalBall.phase ~= "OWNED" or finalBall.owner ~= f.owner or finalBall.ballId ~= f.ballId or not self._Config.CloseRangeRush then
                    return false
                end
                return M.Role.Dive()
            end)

            local restored, restoreError = pcall(function()
                if not wrote or not lp or lp.Character ~= character or not character.Parent then return end
                local move = M.Controllers.GetMoveCommand(character)
                if not finiteVector(move) then error("Cannot read movement after rush") end
                if move.Magnitude > 0.01 and move.Unit:Dot(plan.direction) > 0.999 then
                    M.Controllers.SetMoveCommand(character, previousMove)
                end
            end)

            Rush.candidate = nil
            if not restored then
                self._store:Set("Goalkeeper.AutoDive", false)
                self:_report("CLOSE RUSH MOVEMENT", restoreError)
            end

            if not ok then
                self._store:Set("Goalkeeper.CloseRangeRush", false)
                pcall(M.Role.Cleanup)
                self:_report("CLOSE RUSH", started)
                self:_status("RUSH DISABLED", "Native dive failed; shot saves active")
                return false
            end

            if started ~= true then return false end

            self._S.nextActionAt = math.max(self._S.nextActionAt, os.clock() + M.Dive.Constants.RepeatDelaySeconds)
            self._S.nextPlanAt = 0
            self._S.allowPositioning = false
            self._S.pending = { kind = "RUSH", at = os.clock(), ballId = f.ballId }
            self:_recordRequest("DIVE", nil)
            self._Stats.RushRequests = self._Stats.RushRequests + 1
            self:_status("CLOSE-RANGE RUSH", string.format("%s rush intercept (%.3fs)", plan.name, plan.contact))
            return true
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_coverageTarget(context, ballPosition, ballVelocity)
            local lead = flatVec(ballVelocity) * self._Config.CoverageLeadSeconds
            if lead.Magnitude > self._Config.CoverageMaximumLead then
                lead = lead.Unit * self._Config.CoverageMaximumLead
            end
            local offset = ballPosition + lead - context.goal.Position
            local x = offset:Dot(context.right)
            local z = offset:Dot(context.forward)
            local width = self:_goalHalfWidth(context)

            if z <= 2 or z > self._Config.CoverageRange or math.abs(x) > width + 22 then
                return nil
            end

            local depth = math.min(
                math.clamp(z * 0.30, self._Config.CoverageMinimumDepth, math.max(self._Config.CoverageMinimumDepth, self._Config.HomeDepth)),
                z - 1.25
            )
            local left = Vector3.new(-width - x, 0, -z)
            local right = Vector3.new(width - x, 0, -z)
            local bisector = left.Unit + right.Unit
            if math.abs(bisector.Z) < 0.0001 then return nil end

            local lateral = x + (depth - z) * bisector.X / bisector.Z
            local limit = math.max(0.5, width - 1.5)
            lateral = math.clamp(lateral, -limit, limit)

            return context.goal.Position + context.forward * depth + context.right * lateral
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_coverageSnapshot(character, root, humanoid, ball, context)
            local M = self._M
            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
            if ball.phase ~= "OWNED" or not finite(ball.owner) or (lp and ball.owner == lp.UserId) then
                return nil
            end

            local actor = M.Actors.GetByUserId(ball.owner)
            local target = actor and M.Actors.GetCharacter(actor)
            local targetHumanoid = target and target:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not targetHumanoid or targetHumanoid.Health <= 0 then return nil end

            local ownTeam = lp and M.Teams.GetActorTeamName(lp)
            local otherTeam = M.Teams.GetActorTeamName(actor)
            if ownTeam == nil or otherTeam == nil or ownTeam == otherTeam then return nil end
            if M.Match.IsGoalkeeperCharacter(target) then return nil end

            local ballPosition = M.Renderer.GetPosition()
            local actorPosition = M.Motion.GetPosition(target)
            local velocity = M.Motion.GetVelocity(target)
            if not finiteVector(ballPosition) or not finiteVector(actorPosition) or not finiteVector(velocity) then
                return nil
            end
            if (ballPosition - actorPosition).Magnitude > 7 then return nil end

            local rootOffset = root.Position - context.goal.Position
            local depth = rootOffset:Dot(context.forward)
            if depth < -1 or depth > self._Rush.GoalDepth or math.abs(rootOffset:Dot(context.right)) > self:_goalHalfWidth(context) + 2 then
                return nil
            end

            local destination = self:_coverageTarget(context, ballPosition, velocity)
            if not destination then return nil end

            return {
                isCoverage = true,
                character = character,
                root = root,
                humanoid = humanoid,
                context = context,
                target = target,
                targetHumanoid = targetHumanoid,
                owner = ball.owner,
                ballId = ball.ballId,
                coverageTarget = destination,
                state = { Position = ballPosition },
                flightKey = "OWNER:" .. tostring(ball.owner),
                readClock = os.clock(),
                goalFrame = context.goal.CFrame,
            }
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_coverCarrier(character, root, humanoid, ball, context, dt)
            local M = self._M
            if not self._Config.PreShotCoverage or not self._Config.PositionAssist
                or self._S.jump or self._S.controller or not M.Controllers.IsLanded(character, humanoid) then
                self._S.coverageFrame = nil
                self._S.allowPositioning = false
                self:_releaseMove(true)
                return false
            end

            if self._Config.RespectManualMovement and self:_inputActive() then
                self._S.manualUntil = os.clock() + self._Config.ManualReleaseGrace
                self._S.coverageFrame = nil
                self._S.allowPositioning = false
                self:_releaseMove(false)
                return false
            end

            local frame = self._S.coverageFrame
            local clock = os.clock()
            if not frame or clock >= self._S.coverageNextAt or frame.owner ~= ball.owner
                or frame.root ~= root or frame.context.goal ~= context.goal or frame.goalFrame ~= context.goal.CFrame then
                frame = self:_coverageSnapshot(character, root, humanoid, ball, context)
                self._S.coverageFrame = frame
                self._S.coverageNextAt = clock + 1 / self._Config.CoverageUpdateHz
            end

            if not frame or clock - frame.readClock > self._Config.CachedFrameMaxAge
                or not frame.target.Parent or frame.targetHumanoid.Health <= 0 then
                self._S.allowPositioning = false
                self:_releaseMove(true)
                return false
            end

            local current = self:_readBall()
            if current.phase ~= "OWNED" or current.owner ~= frame.owner or current.ballId ~= frame.ballId then
                self:_invalidateFrame(true)
                return false
            end

            self._S.allowPositioning = true
            self:_position(frame, dt)
            if self._S.allowPositioning then
                self:_status("COVERING ANGLE", self._S.positionActive and "Adjusting before shot" or "Holding covered angle")
