            end
            return true
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:TriggerManualSmartDive()
            local M = self:_resolveModules()
            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
            local character = lp and lp.Character
            local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not (M and character and humanoid and root and humanoid.Health > 0) then
                return false
            end
            if not M.Match.IsGoalkeeperCharacter(character) then
                return false
            end

            local f = self:_snapshot(character, root, humanoid)
            if f and f.eta and f.eta > 0 and f.eta <= self._Config.PredictionHorizon then
                local plan = self:_planSave(f)
                if plan and plan.kind == "JUMP" then
                    if pcall(M.Movement.Jump) then
                        self:_status("SMART DIVE: JUMP", "High ball intercept")
                        return true
                    end
                elseif plan and (plan.kind == "DIVE" or plan.kind == "JUMP_DIVE") then
                    local zeroPlan = table.clone(plan)
                    zeroPlan.kind = "DIVE"
                    zeroPlan.delay = 0
                    if self:_requestDive(f, zeroPlan, true) then
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end

            local ok, res = pcall(M.Role.Dive)
            return ok and res == true
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:_tick(dt)
            local S = self._S
            if not S.alive then return end

            local M = self:_resolveModules()
            if not M then
                self:_status("WAITING MODULES", "Loading game modules...")
                return
            end
            self:_bindRemotes()
            self:_serviceOwnedMotion()

            if not self._Config.Enabled then
                if self:_hasOwnedState() then
                    self:ReleaseOwnedState(true)
                end
                self:_status("OFF", S.runtimeError and "Runtime error paused" or "Manual controls untouched")
                return
            end

            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
            self._localPlayer = lp
            local character = lp and lp.Character
            local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if not root or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                if self:_hasOwnedState() then
                    self:ReleaseOwnedState(false)
                end
                self:_status("NO CHARACTER")
                return
            end

            if character ~= S.lastChar then
                if self:_hasOwnedState() then
                    self:ReleaseOwnedState(false)
                else
                    self:_invalidateFrame(false)
                end
                S.jump = nil
                S.pending = nil
                S.lastChar = character
                S.ballSignature = nil
                S.currentContext = nil
                S.missingSince = nil
            end

            if not M.Match.IsGoalkeeperCharacter(character) then
                if self:_hasOwnedState() then
                    self:ReleaseOwnedState(true)
                end
                self:_status("WAITING FOR GK", "Manual controls untouched")
                return
            end

            S.wasKeeper = true
            local jumpWasQueued = self:_updateJump(character, root, humanoid)
            if self:_queuedJumpMustCancel(nil) then
                self:_cancelOwnedJumpQueue()
            end

            if not S.focused then
                self:_invalidateFrame(true)
                self:_cancelOwnedJumpQueue()
                self:_status("WINDOW UNFOCUSED")
                return
            end

            if self:_isUiBusy() or os.clock() < S.manualActionUntil then
                self:_invalidateFrame(false)
                self:_status(self:_isUiBusy() and "INTERFACE OPEN" or "MANUAL ACTION", "Automatic requests paused")
                return
            end

            local ball = self:_readBall()
            S.ballPhase = ball.phase
            local signature = tostring(ball.ballId) .. ":" .. ball.phase .. ":" .. tostring(ball.flightKey or ball.owner)
            if signature ~= S.ballSignature then
                self:_invalidateFrame(true)
                S.ballSignature = signature
                S.observedBallId = ball.ballId
            end

            local context = self:_contextFor(root, ball.state)
            if not context then
                self:_invalidateFrame(true)
                self:_cancelOwnedJumpQueue()
                S.currentContext = nil
                self:_status("WAITING FOR TEAM / GOAL")
                return
            end

            if S.currentContext and (S.currentContext.goal ~= context.goal or S.currentContext.team ~= context.team) then
                self:_invalidateFrame(true)
                self:_cancelOwnedJumpQueue()
            end
            S.currentContext = context

            if self:_queuedJumpMustCancel(ball) then
                self:_cancelOwnedJumpQueue()
            end

            if jumpWasQueued and S.jump then
                S.allowPositioning = false
                if S.jump and S.jump.phase == "QUEUED" then
                    self:_status("JUMP QUEUED", "Waiting for takeoff")
                end
                return
            end

            if S.controller then
                S.allowPositioning = false
                self:_releaseMove(false)
                return
            end

            local why = self:_blocked(character, root)
            if why then
                self:_invalidateFrame(false)
                self:_status(why)
                return
            end

            if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
                self:_invalidateFrame(true)
                self:_status("TEXT INPUT")
                return
            end

            if ball.phase ~= "MOVEMENT" then
                S.lastFrame = nil
                S.lastAdvice = nil

                if ball.phase == "OWNED" then
                    S.ballInfo = (lp and ball.owner == lp.UserId) and "Held by you" or "Held by another actor"
                    S.missingSince = nil

                    local rushOK, rushed = pcall(self._tryCloseRush, self, character, root, humanoid)
                    if not rushOK then
                        self._store:Set("Goalkeeper.CloseRangeRush", false)
                        self._Rush.candidate = nil
                        self:_report("CLOSE RUSH DISABLED", rushed)
                    elseif rushed then
                        S.coverageFrame = nil
                        return
                    end

                    local coverageOK, covered = pcall(self._coverCarrier, self, character, root, humanoid, ball, context, dt)
                    if not coverageOK then
                        self._store:Set("Goalkeeper.PreShotCoverage", false)
                        self:_invalidateFrame(true)
                        self:_report("PRE-SHOT COVERAGE DISABLED", covered)
                    elseif covered then
                        return
                    end
                else
                    self._Rush.candidate = nil
                    self:_invalidateFrame(true)
                end

                S.allowPositioning = false
                self:_releaseMove(true)
                self:_unavailableStatus(ball, context)
                return
            end

            self._Rush.candidate = nil
            S.missingSince = nil
            S.ballInfo = "Movement trajectory available"

            if S.lastFrame and (S.lastFrame.flightKey ~= ball.flightKey or S.lastFrame.world ~= ball.world or S.lastFrame.root ~= root) then
                self:_invalidateFrame(true)
            end
            if S.lastFrame and self:_trajectoryChanged(S.lastFrame, ball) then
                S.nextPlanAt = 0
            end

            if os.clock() < S.nextPlanAt then
                if S.allowPositioning and S.lastFrame then
                    self:_position(S.lastFrame, dt)
                end
                return
            end

            local f, reason, unavailable = self:_snapshot(character, root, humanoid)
            S.lastFrame = f
            if not f then
                self:_invalidateFrame(true)
                if unavailable then
                    self:_unavailableStatus(unavailable, context)
                else
                    self:_status(reason or "NO STATE")
                end
                return
            end

            S.currentContext = f.context
            f.plannerAnchor = os.clock()
            S.nextPlanAt = self:_plannerDeadline(f)

            if not f.eta or f.eta <= 0 then
                S.lastAdvice = nil
                S.allowPositioning = self._Config.PositionMode ~= "THREATS"
                self:_position(f, dt)
                self:_status(
                    "READY / " .. f.context.mode,
                    S.allowPositioning and "Home positioning active" or "Idle movement off"
                )
                return
            end

            if f.eta > self._Config.PredictionHorizon then
                S.lastAdvice = nil
                S.allowPositioning = true
                self:_position(f, dt)
                self:_status("TRACKING", string.format("Goal in %.2fs", f.eta))
                return
            end

            S.allowPositioning = false
            local plan, noPlan = self:_planSave(f)
            self:_trackPlanWindow(f, plan)
            S.nextPlanAt = self:_plannerDeadline(f)

            if plan and (plan.kind == "DIVE" or plan.kind == "JUMP_DIVE" or plan.kind == "JUMP") then
                S.lastAdvice = {
                    DirectionName = plan.name or plan.kind,
                    WorldDirection = plan.direction,
                    OffsetSeconds = plan.contact and plan.contact.time or f.eta,
                    AssistActive = plan.useAssist == true,
                    IsLegalHeight = true,
                    MissDistance = plan.contact and plan.contact.miss or 0,
                    NeedsJump = plan.kind == "JUMP" or plan.kind == "JUMP_DIVE",
                }
            else
                S.lastAdvice = nil
            end

            if plan and plan.kind == "HOLD" then
                self:_confirmHold(f)
                return
            end

            if not plan then
                S.allowPositioning = true
                self:_position(f, dt)
                self:_status(noPlan or "NO VERIFIED CONTACT", "No blind dive")
                return
            end

            if os.clock() < S.nextActionAt then
                S.allowPositioning = true
                self:_position(f, dt)
                self:_status("ACTION COOLDOWN")
                return
            end

            if plan.kind == "JUMP" or plan.kind == "JUMP_DIVE" then
                self:_requestJump(f, plan)
            elseif plan.delay > 0 then
                S.nextPlanAt = math.min(S.nextPlanAt, f.readClock + plan.delay)
                S.allowPositioning = true
                self:_position(f, dt)
                self:_status("TIMING DIVE " .. tostring(plan.name), string.format("Replan in %.2fs", math.max(0, S.nextPlanAt - os.clock())))
            else
                self:_requestDive(f, plan, false)
            end
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:Step(dt)
            local S = self._S
            if not S.alive or S.loopBusy or os.clock() < S.runtimeRetryAt then
                return
            end
            S.loopBusy = true
            local ok, err = xpcall(function()
                self:_tick(dt)
            end, debug.traceback)
            S.loopBusy = false

            if not ok then
                S.runtimeFailures = S.runtimeFailures + 1
                S.runtimeError = tostring(err)
                S.runtimeRetryAt = os.clock() + self._Config.RuntimeErrorRetrySeconds
                pcall(self.ReleaseOwnedState, self, true)
                if S.runtimeFailures >= self._Config.RuntimeErrorLimit then
                    self._store:Set("Goalkeeper.AutoDive", false)
                end
                self:_status(self._Config.Enabled and "RUNTIME ERROR" or "OFF", S.runtimeError:match("[^\n]+"))
                self:_report("RUNTIME", err)
            elseif self._Config.Enabled then
                S.runtimeFailures = 0
                S.runtimeRetryAt = 0
                S.runtimeError = nil
            end
            self:_flushStatusDisplay()
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:GetDisplayStatus()
            self:_flushStatusDisplay()
            return self._S.displayName, self._S.displayDetail
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:GetTelemetryInfo()
            local f = self._S.lastFrame
            return {
                GoalPosition = f and f.goalPosition or nil,
                ETA = f and f.eta or nil,
                DiveAdvice = self._S.lastAdvice,
                DisplayStatus = self._S.displayName,
                DisplayDetail = self._S.displayDetail,
                PlannerMs = self._S.plannerMilliseconds,
                Stats = self._Stats,
            }
        end

        function DmcGoalkeeperEngine:Destroy()
            if not self._S.alive then
                return
            end
            self._S.alive = false
            pcall(self.ReleaseOwnedState, self, true)
            self._janitor:Destroy()
        end

        return DmcGoalkeeperEngine
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Automation/GoalkeeperController
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Automation/GoalkeeperController", function()
        local GoalkeeperController = {}
        GoalkeeperController.__index = GoalkeeperController

        function GoalkeeperController.new(store, matchBridge, ballBridge, engines)
            return setmetatable({
                _store = store,
                _match = matchBridge,
                _ball = ballBridge,
                _engines = engines,
                _lastStatus = "Idle",
            }, GoalkeeperController)
        end

        function GoalkeeperController:TriggerSmartDive()
            if not self._match:IsGoalkeeper() then
                return false
            end
            return self._engines.DmcGK:TriggerManualSmartDive()
        end

        function GoalkeeperController:GetStatus()
            return self._lastStatus
        end

        function GoalkeeperController:Step(dt)
            self._engines.DmcGK:Step(dt)

            if not self._match:IsGoalkeeper() then
                self._lastStatus = "Outfield"
                self._engines.Goalkeeper:EvaluateTelemetry(self._store:Get("Goalkeeper"), nil)
                return
            end

            local cfg = self._store:Get("Goalkeeper")
            local dmcInfo = self._engines.DmcGK:GetTelemetryInfo()
            local analysis = self._engines.Goalkeeper:EvaluateTelemetry(cfg, dmcInfo)

            if self._ball:IsLocalOwner() and cfg.ClearGuard and analysis then
                local rem = math.max(0, (analysis.ForcedClearMax or 5) - (analysis.OwnedElapsed or 0))
                self._lastStatus = string.format("ClearTimer: %.1fs", rem)
                return
            end

            if dmcInfo and dmcInfo.DisplayStatus and dmcInfo.DisplayStatus ~= "OFF" then
                self._lastStatus = dmcInfo.DisplayStatus
            elseif analysis and analysis.PreShotThreat then
                self._lastStatus = string.format("PreShot Alert (%.1fs)", analysis.PreShotThreat.ETA)
            else
                self._lastStatus = "Guarding Goal"
            end
        end

        return GoalkeeperController
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Automation/SprintController (Unlimited Stamina + Anti-Exhaust)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Automation/SprintController", function(Require)
        local GameModules = Require("Bridge/GameModules")

        local SprintController = {}
        SprintController.__index = SprintController

        local GUARD_SOURCE = "LouisHubStaminaGuard"
        local UNLIM_SOURCE = "LouisHubUnlimited"

        function SprintController.new(store, playerBridge, matchBridge)
            return setmetatable({
                _store = store,
                _player = playerBridge,
                _match = matchBridge,
                _isGuarding = false,
                _unlimitedApplied = false,
            }, SprintController)
        end

        function SprintController:SetBlocked(blocked)
            local clientSprint = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientSprint)
            if clientSprint and type(clientSprint.SetBlocked) == "function" then
                pcall(clientSprint.SetBlocked, GUARD_SOURCE, blocked)
            end
            self._isGuarding = blocked
        end

        function SprintController:_applyUnlimited(enable)
            if self._unlimitedApplied == enable then
                return
            end
            self._unlimitedApplied = enable
            local clientSprint = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientSprint)
            if clientSprint and type(clientSprint.SetUnlimitedStamina) == "function" then
                pcall(clientSprint.SetUnlimitedStamina, UNLIM_SOURCE, enable)
            end
        end

        function SprintController:Step()
            local staminaGuardOn = self._store:Get("Striker.StaminaGuard") == true
            local unlimitedStaminaOn = self._store:Get("Striker.UnlimitedStamina") == true

            if self._match:IsGoalkeeper() then
                if self._isGuarding then
                    self:SetBlocked(false)
                end
                self:_applyUnlimited(false)
                return
            end

            if unlimitedStaminaOn then
                if self._isGuarding then
                    self:SetBlocked(false)
                end
                self:_applyUnlimited(true)
                return
            else
                self:_applyUnlimited(false)
            end

            if not staminaGuardOn then
                if self._isGuarding then
                    self:SetBlocked(false)
                end
                return
            end

            local sprintMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Sprint)
            local lowThresh = (sprintMod and sprintMod.Constants and sprintMod.Constants.LowStaminaThreshold) or 0.08
            local stamina = self._player:GetStamina()
            local userFloor = math.clamp((self._store:Get("Striker.StaminaFloorPercent") or 10) / 100, 0.03, 0.45)
            local floorAlpha = math.max(userFloor, lowThresh * 0.6)
            local releaseAlpha = math.min(0.95, floorAlpha + 0.12)

            if not self._isGuarding and stamina <= floorAlpha then
                self:SetBlocked(true)
            elseif self._isGuarding and stamina >= releaseAlpha then
                self:SetBlocked(false)
            end
        end

        function SprintController:IsGuarding()
            return self._isGuarding
        end

        function SprintController:Destroy()
            if self._isGuarding then
                self:SetBlocked(false)
            end
            self:_applyUnlimited(false)
        end

        return SprintController
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Automation/ActionDispatcher (Silent Aim Pro + Fake Trick Actions)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Automation/ActionDispatcher", function(Require)
        local GameModules = Require("Bridge/GameModules")

        local ActionDispatcher = {}
        ActionDispatcher.__index = ActionDispatcher

        function ActionDispatcher.new(store, hookGuard, playerBridge, ballBridge, matchBridge, engines)
            local self = setmetatable({
                _store = store,
                _hookGuard = hookGuard,
                _player = playerBridge,
                _ball = ballBridge,
                _match = matchBridge,
                _engines = engines,
                _isShootingSequence = false,
                _isFakeBaiting = false,
                _lastActionAt = {},

                _activeShotAimRay = nil,
                _activeShotCurve = nil,
                _activeSilentCurve = nil,
                _suppressShotAimAssist = false,
                _activeDodgeDir = nil,
                _activeDodgeUntil = 0,
                _activeGkDiveWorldDir = nil,
                _shotCamLockConn = nil,
                _hooksInstalled = false,
                _monkeyPatchConn = nil,
            }, ActionDispatcher)

            self:InstallScopedHooks()

            if self._store:Get("Meta.MonkeyPatchMode") == true then
                self:ApplyMonkeyPatches()
            end

            self._monkeyPatchConn = self._store:OnChanged("Meta.MonkeyPatchMode", function(enabled)
                if enabled == true then
                    self:ApplyMonkeyPatches()
                else
                    self:RemoveMonkeyPatches()
                end
            end)

            return self
        end

        function ActionDispatcher:_resolveManualSilentAim()
            local strikerCfg = self._store:Get("Striker")
            if not (strikerCfg and strikerCfg.TargetTrajectoryCorrection) then
                self._activeSilentCurve = nil
                return nil
            end
            local silentSol = self._engines.GoalSolver:SolveSilentAimSolution(strikerCfg)
            if silentSol then
                self._activeSilentCurve = (silentSol.Curve and silentSol.Curve ~= 0) and silentSol.Curve or nil
                return silentSol
            end
            self._activeSilentCurve = nil
            return nil
        end

        function ActionDispatcher:InstallScopedHooks()
            if self._hooksInstalled or not self._hookGuard then
                return
            end
            self._hooksInstalled = true

            local reticleMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientReticle)
            if reticleMod then
                self._hookGuard:WrapTagged("Scoped", reticleMod, "GetCameraAimRayWithoutHit", function(origFn, guard)
                    return function(...)
                        if guard:IsBypassEnabled() then
                            -- Override untuk tembakan otomatis
                            if self._activeShotAimRay then
                                local src = self._activeShotAimRay
                                return {
                                    Origin = src.Origin,
                                    Direction = src.Direction,
                                    CameraCFrame = src.CameraCFrame,
                                    CameraFieldOfView = src.CameraFieldOfView,
                                    CameraViewportSize = src.CameraViewportSize,
                                    ScreenPosition = src.ScreenPosition,
                                    IsAirborne = src.IsAirborne,
                                }
                            end

                            -- Upgraded Silent Aim Goal saat charge manual (dengan Anti-Skyward & Magnus Curve)
                            if self._store:Get("Striker.TargetTrajectoryCorrection")
                                and not self._isFakeBaiting
                                and self._player:IsChargingKickOrPass()
                                and not self._match:IsGoalkeeper()
                            then
                                local silentSol = self:_resolveManualSilentAim()
                                if silentSol and silentSol.CalibratedAimRay then
                                    local src = silentSol.CalibratedAimRay
                                    return {
                                        Origin = src.Origin,
                                        Direction = src.Direction,
                                        CameraCFrame = src.CameraCFrame,
                                        CameraFieldOfView = src.CameraFieldOfView,
                                        CameraViewportSize = src.CameraViewportSize,
                                        ScreenPosition = src.ScreenPosition,
                                        IsAirborne = src.IsAirborne,
                                    }
                                elseif silentSol and silentSol.Direction then
                                    local baseRay = origFn(...)
                                    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                                    local camCF = cam and cam.CFrame or CFrame.identity
                                    return {
                                        Origin = silentSol.StartPos,
                                        Direction = silentSol.Direction,
                                        CameraCFrame = camCF,
                                        CameraFieldOfView = baseRay and baseRay.CameraFieldOfView or 70,
                                        CameraViewportSize = baseRay and baseRay.CameraViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720),
                                        ScreenPosition = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                                        IsAirborne = baseRay and baseRay.IsAirborne or nil,
                                    }
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        return origFn(...)
                    end
                end)
            end

            local shotAssistMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ShotAimAssist)
            if shotAssistMod then
                self._hookGuard:WrapTagged("Scoped", shotAssistMod, "GetAssistedDirection", function(origFn, guard)
                    return function(...)
                        if guard:IsBypassEnabled() then
                            if self._suppressShotAimAssist then
                                return nil
                            end
                            if self._store:Get("Striker.TargetTrajectoryCorrection") and not self._match:IsGoalkeeper() then
                                return nil
                            end
                        end
                        return origFn(...)
                    end
                end)
            end

            local cpvMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ChargePathVelocity)
            if cpvMod then
                self._hookGuard:WrapTagged("Scoped", cpvMod, "GetLaunchVelocity", function(origFn, guard)
                    return function(p1, p2, p3, p4, p5, p6, p7, p8, p9, p10, p11, p12)
                        if guard:IsBypassEnabled() then
                            if self._activeShotCurve ~= nil and self._isShootingSequence then
                                p12 = self._activeShotCurve
                            elseif self._store:Get("Striker.TargetTrajectoryCorrection") and self._player:IsChargingKickOrPass() and not self._match:IsGoalkeeper() then
                                if self._activeSilentCurve ~= nil then
                                    p12 = self._activeSilentCurve
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        return origFn(p1, p2, p3, p4, p5, p6, p7, p8, p9, p10, p11, p12)
                    end
                end)
                self._hookGuard:WrapTagged("Scoped", cpvMod, "Get", function(origFn, guard)
                    return function(p1, p2, p3, p4, p5, p6, p7, p8, p9, p10)
                        if guard:IsBypassEnabled() then
                            if self._activeShotCurve ~= nil and self._isShootingSequence then
                                p10 = self._activeShotCurve
                            elseif self._store:Get("Striker.TargetTrajectoryCorrection") and self._player:IsChargingKickOrPass() and not self._match:IsGoalkeeper() then
                                if self._activeSilentCurve ~= nil then
                                    p10 = self._activeSilentCurve
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        return origFn(p1, p2, p3, p4, p5, p6, p7, p8, p9, p10)
                    end
                end)
            end

            local shootSharedMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Shoot)
            if shootSharedMod then
                self._hookGuard:WrapTagged("Scoped", shootSharedMod, "GetSpin", function(origFn, guard)
                    return function(char, vel, repVel, curve, chargeSec)
                        if guard:IsBypassEnabled() and (char == nil or char == self._player:GetCharacter()) then
                            if self._activeShotCurve ~= nil and self._isShootingSequence then
                                curve = self._activeShotCurve
                            elseif self._store:Get("Striker.TargetTrajectoryCorrection") and not self._match:IsGoalkeeper() then
                                if self._activeSilentCurve ~= nil then
                                    curve = self._activeSilentCurve
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        return origFn(char, vel, repVel, curve, chargeSec)
                    end
                end)
            end

            local protoMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ActionRemoteProtocol)
            if protoMod then
                for _, methodName in ipairs({ "Start", "Update", "Release" }) do
                    self._hookGuard:WrapTagged("Scoped", protoMod, methodName, function(origFn, guard)
                        return function(cmd)
                            if guard:IsBypassEnabled() and type(cmd) == "table" then
                                local cmds = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ActionCommands)
                                local kickKind = cmds and cmds.Kinds and cmds.Kinds.Kick or "Kick"
                                if cmd.Kind == kickKind or (cmds and cmds.IsVolley and cmds.IsVolley(cmd)) then
                                    if self._activeShotCurve ~= nil and self._isShootingSequence then
                                        cmd.Curve = self._activeShotCurve
                                    elseif self._store:Get("Striker.TargetTrajectoryCorrection") and not self._match:IsGoalkeeper() then
                                        if self._activeSilentCurve ~= nil then
                                            cmd.Curve = self._activeSilentCurve
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            return origFn(cmd)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            local dodgeMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Dodge)
            if dodgeMod then
                self._hookGuard:WrapTagged("Scoped", dodgeMod, "GetDashDirection", function(origFn, guard)
                    return function(char, fallback)
                        if self._activeDodgeDir and os.clock() <= self._activeDodgeUntil and guard:IsBypassEnabled() then
                            local lpChar = self._player:GetCharacter()
                            if char == nil or char == lpChar then
                                return self._activeDodgeDir
                            end
                        end
                        return origFn(char, fallback)
                    end
                end)
            end

            local gkDiveMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.GoalkeeperDive)
            local actionMotion = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ActionMotion)
            if gkDiveMod then
                self._hookGuard:WrapTagged("Scoped", gkDiveMod, "GetDirectionChoice", function(origFn, guard)
                    return function(moveCmd, basisCF, fallbackLook)
                        if self._activeGkDiveWorldDir and guard:IsBypassEnabled() then
                            local desired = flatUnit(self._activeGkDiveWorldDir, nil)
                            if desired and basisCF then
                                local choices = (actionMotion and actionMotion.DirectionChoices) or {}
                                local bestChoice = nil
                                local bestDot = -math.huge
                                for _, choice in pairs(choices) do
                                    local wDir = gkDiveMod.GetWorldDirection(choice, basisCF, fallbackLook)
                                    local d = wDir:Dot(desired)
                                    if d > bestDot then
                                        bestDot = d
                                        bestChoice = choice
                                    end
                                end
                                if bestChoice then
                                    return bestChoice
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        return origFn(moveCmd, basisCF, fallbackLook)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end

        function ActionDispatcher:ApplyMonkeyPatches()
            if not self._hookGuard then
                return
            end

            local reticleMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientReticle)
            if reticleMod then
                self._hookGuard:WrapTagged("MonkeyPatch", reticleMod, "GetCameraAimRayWithoutHit", function(origFn, guard)
                    return function(...)
                        if guard:IsMonkeyPatchEnabled() and not self._isShootingSequence and not self._match:IsGoalkeeper() then
                            if self._store:Get("Striker.TargetTrajectoryCorrection") and not self._isFakeBaiting and self._player:IsChargingKickOrPass() then
                                local silentSol = self:_resolveManualSilentAim()
                                if silentSol and silentSol.CalibratedAimRay then
                                    local src = silentSol.CalibratedAimRay
                                    return {
                                        Origin = src.Origin,
                                        Direction = src.Direction,
                                        CameraCFrame = src.CameraCFrame,
                                        CameraFieldOfView = src.CameraFieldOfView,
                                        CameraViewportSize = src.CameraViewportSize,
                                        ScreenPosition = src.ScreenPosition,
                                        IsAirborne = src.IsAirborne,
                                    }
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        return origFn(...)
                    end
                end)
            end

            local shotAssistMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ShotAimAssist)
            if shotAssistMod then
                self._hookGuard:WrapTagged("MonkeyPatch", shotAssistMod, "GetAssistedDirection", function(origFn, guard)
                    return function(aimRay, startPos, launchVel, spin, boundaryWorld, goalParts, strengthScale, userId)
                        if guard:IsMonkeyPatchEnabled() and not self._isShootingSequence then
                            local lp = self._player:GetLocalPlayer()
                            if lp and userId == lp.UserId and not self._match:IsGoalkeeper() then
                                if self._store:Get("Striker.TargetTrajectoryCorrection") then
                                    return nil
                                end
                                local sol = self._engines and self._engines.GoalSolver and self._engines.GoalSolver:GetLastSolution()
                                if sol and sol.TargetPosition and sol.Zone and sol.Zone.InRange and not sol.Blocked then
                                    local aimDir = (type(aimRay) == "table" and aimRay.Direction) or flatUnit(launchVel, Vector3.new(0, 0, -1))
                                    local desiredDir = (sol.TargetPosition - startPos)
                                    if desiredDir.Magnitude > 1e-4 and aimDir and aimDir.Magnitude > 1e-4 then
                                        local a = aimDir.Unit
                                        local b = desiredDir.Unit
                                        local cross = a:Cross(b)
                                        if cross.Magnitude > 1e-4 then
                                            local angle = math.acos(math.clamp(a:Dot(b), -1, 1))
                                            local maxBend = math.rad(14)
                                            local rot = CFrame.fromAxisAngle(cross.Unit, math.min(angle, maxBend))
                                            return rot * a, rot
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        return origFn(aimRay, startPos, launchVel, spin, boundaryWorld, goalParts, strengthScale, userId)
                    end
                end)
            end

            local gkDiveMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.GoalkeeperDive)
            local actionMotion = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ActionMotion)
            if gkDiveMod then
                self._hookGuard:WrapTagged("MonkeyPatch", gkDiveMod, "GetDirectionChoice", function(origFn, guard)
                    return function(moveCmd, basisCF, fallbackLook)
                        if guard:IsMonkeyPatchEnabled() and not self._activeGkDiveWorldDir then
                            local info = self._engines and self._engines.DmcGK and self._engines.DmcGK:GetTelemetryInfo()
                            local advice = info and info.DiveAdvice
                            if advice and advice.WorldDirection and basisCF then
                                local desired = flatUnit(advice.WorldDirection, nil)
                                if desired then
                                    local choices = (actionMotion and actionMotion.DirectionChoices) or {}
                                    local bestChoice = nil
                                    local bestDot = -math.huge
                                    for _, choice in pairs(choices) do
                                        local wDir = gkDiveMod.GetWorldDirection(choice, basisCF, fallbackLook)
                                        local d = wDir:Dot(desired)
                                        if d > bestDot then
                                            bestDot = d
                                            bestChoice = choice
                                        end
                                    end
                                    if bestChoice then
                                        return bestChoice
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        return origFn(moveCmd, basisCF, fallbackLook)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end

        function ActionDispatcher:RemoveMonkeyPatches()
            if self._hookGuard then
                self._hookGuard:RestoreTag("MonkeyPatch")
            end
        end

        function ActionDispatcher:CanAttempt(actionKey, minInterval)
            local now = os.clock()
            local last = self._lastActionAt[actionKey] or 0
            if now - last < (minInterval or 0.12) then
                return false
            end
            self._lastActionAt[actionKey] = now
            return true
        end

        function ActionDispatcher:CreateActionInput(actionName)
            local controls = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Controls)
            if controls and type(controls.CreateInput) == "function" then
                local ok, inp = pcall(controls.CreateInput, actionName)
                if ok and inp then
                    return inp
                end
            end
            return {
                KeyCode = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown,
                UserInputType = Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1,
                UserInputState = Enum.UserInputState.Begin,
            }
        end

        function ActionDispatcher:AimCameraAtPoint(worldPoint, smoothFactor)
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not (cam and typeof(worldPoint) == "Vector3") then
                return false
            end
            local pos = cam.CFrame.Position
            local delta = worldPoint - pos
            if delta.Magnitude <= 1e-4 then
                return false
            end
            -- Clamp pitch agar fallback camera aim tidak mendongak ekstrem
            local maxPitch = self._store:Get("Striker.SilentAimMaxPitchDeg") or 22
            local safeDir = self._engines and self._engines.Ballistics
                and self._engines.Ballistics:ClampDirectionPitch(delta.Unit, maxPitch)
                or delta.Unit
            local targetCF = CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos + safeDir * delta.Magnitude)
            if smoothFactor and smoothFactor > 0 then
                cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame:Lerp(targetCF, math.clamp(smoothFactor, 0.05, 1))
            else
                cam.CFrame = targetCF
            end
            return true
        end

        function ActionDispatcher:TriggerJump()
            if not self:CanAttempt("Jump", 0.18) then
                return false
            end
            local movement = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientMovement)
            if movement and type(movement.Jump) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(movement.Jump)
                if ok and res then
                    task.delay(0.08, function()
                        if type(movement.ReleaseJump) == "function" then
                            pcall(movement.ReleaseJump)
                        end
                    end)
                    return true
                end
            end
            local hum = self._player:GetHumanoid()
            if hum then
                hum.Jump = true
                return true
            end
            return false
        end

        function ActionDispatcher:TriggerHeadbutt()
            if not self:CanAttempt("Headbutt", 0.25) then
                return false
            end
            local clientShoot = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientShoot)
            if clientShoot and type(clientShoot.TryHeadbutt) == "function" then
                local inp = self:CreateActionInput("Jump")
                local ok, res = pcall(clientShoot.TryHeadbutt, inp)
                return ok and res == true
            end
            return false
        end

        -- Fake Shot Bait: memulai animasi charge sesaat lalu CancelCharge() (zero cooldown)
        function ActionDispatcher:TriggerFakeShotBait(durationSec)
            if self._isShootingSequence or self._isFakeBaiting then
                return false
            end
            if not self:CanAttempt("FakeBait", 0.55) then
                return false
            end
            if self._player:IsRagdolled() or self._player:HasKickBlock() or not self._ball:IsLocalOwner() then
                return false
            end
            local clientShoot = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientShoot)
            if not (clientShoot and type(clientShoot.HandleInputBegan) == "function" and type(clientShoot.CancelCharge) == "function") then
                return false
            end

            self._isFakeBaiting = true
            local kickInput = self:CreateActionInput("Kick")
            local okBegin, began = pcall(clientShoot.HandleInputBegan, kickInput, false)
            if not (okBegin and began) then
                self._isFakeBaiting = false
                return false
            end

            task.delay(math.clamp(durationSec or 0.09, 0.05, 0.15), function()
                if not LouisHub._destroyed then
                    pcall(clientShoot.CancelCharge)
                end
                self._isFakeBaiting = false
            end)
            return true
        end

        -- Lateral Dodge Feint: mengarahkan Dribble dash ke sisi bek yang terbuka
        function ActionDispatcher:TriggerLateralDodgeFeint(worldDir)
            if not self:CanAttempt("DodgeFeint", 0.45) then
                return false
            end
            if self._player:IsRagdolled() or self._player:HasDodgeBlock() or self._player:IsDribbling() then
                return false
            end
            if not self._ball:IsLocalOwner() then
                return false
            end
            local clientDodge = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientDodge)
            if not (clientDodge and type(clientDodge.HandleInputBegan) == "function") then
                return false
            end

            local dir = flatUnit(worldDir, nil)
            if dir then
                self._activeDodgeDir = dir
                self._activeDodgeUntil = os.clock() + 0.35
            end

            local dribbleInput = self:CreateActionInput("Dribble")
            local ok, handled = pcall(clientDodge.HandleInputBegan, dribbleInput, false)
            return ok and handled == true and clientDodge.IsDribbling() == true
        end

        function ActionDispatcher:TriggerRainbowFlick()
            if not self:CanAttempt("RainbowFlick", 0.45) then
                return false
            end
            if not self._player:CanUseRainbowFlick() then
                return false
            end

            local controls = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.Controls)
            local vim = LouisHub.SafeService("VirtualInputManager")

            if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled and controls and type(controls.GetActionBindInputs) == "function" and vim then
                local ok, inputs = pcall(function()
                    return { controls.GetActionBindInputs("RainbowFlick") }
                end)
                if ok and type(inputs) == "table" then
                    for _, item in ipairs(inputs) do
                        if typeof(item) == "EnumItem" and item.EnumType == Enum.KeyCode then
                            local pressOk = pcall(function()
                                vim:SendKeyEvent(true, item, false, game)
                                task.delay(0.04, function()
                                    pcall(vim.SendKeyEvent, vim, false, item, false, game)
                                end)
                            end)
                            if pressOk then
                                return true
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            local proto = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ActionRemoteProtocol)
            local cmds = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ActionCommands)
            local rfMod = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.RainbowFlick)
            local tackleFx = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientTackleEffects)
            local clientDodge = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientDodge)
            local clientAim = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientAim)
            local vo = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientVolleyOpportunity)
            local renderer = self._ball:GetRenderer()
            local physics = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.BallPhysics)

            local lp = self._player:GetLocalPlayer()
            local char = self._player:GetCharacter()
            local root = self._player:GetRoot()
            if not (proto and cmds and rfMod and lp and char and root) then
                return false
            end

            local isOwner = self._ball:IsLocalOwner()
            local isVolley = false
            if not isOwner and vo and type(vo.GetEvaluation) == "function" then
                local okEv, ev = pcall(vo.GetEvaluation, char)
                if okEv and ev ~= nil then
                    isVolley = true
                end
            end
            if not (isOwner or isVolley) then
                return false
            end

            if clientDodge and type(clientDodge.EndDribbleForStrike) == "function" then
                pcall(clientDodge.EndDribbleForStrike)
            end
            if clientAim and type(clientAim.Clear) == "function" then
                pcall(clientAim.Clear)
            end

            local shotTime = self._ball:GetServerTime()
            local detachDelay = (rfMod.Constants and rfMod.Constants.BallDetachSeconds) or 0.25
            local graceDelay = (rfMod.Constants and rfMod.Constants.DetachCommandGraceSeconds) or 0.15
            local fallbackActionId = nil

            if isVolley then
                local claim = vo and type(vo.GetClaim) == "function" and vo.GetClaim(char) or nil
                if vo and type(vo.PredictLock) == "function" then
                    pcall(vo.PredictLock, lp.UserId, shotTime + detachDelay, cmds.VolleyTargetKinds and cmds.VolleyTargetKinds.RainbowFlick)
                end
                local vCmd = cmds.RainbowFlick({
                    Mode = cmds.Modes and cmds.Modes.Volley or "Volley",
                    ShotTime = shotTime,
                })
                vCmd.VolleyOpportunity = claim
                proto.Start(vCmd)
                proto.Release(vCmd)
                fallbackActionId = vCmd.SessionId
            else
                local cCmd = cmds.RainbowFlick({ ShotTime = shotTime })
                fallbackActionId = proto.Send(cCmd)
            end

            if type(rfMod.SetWindingUpUntil) == "function" then
                pcall(rfMod.SetWindingUpUntil, lp, shotTime + detachDelay + graceDelay)
            end
            if tackleFx and type(tackleFx.PlayLocalRainbowFlick) == "function" then
                pcall(tackleFx.PlayLocalRainbowFlick)
            end

            task.delay(detachDelay, function()
                if LouisHub._destroyed then
                    return
                end
                local curChar = self._player:GetCharacter()
                if not curChar or self._player:IsRagdolled() then
                    return
                end
                if not (self._ball:IsLocalOwner() or (vo and type(vo.IsLockedBy) == "function" and vo.IsLockedBy(lp))) then
                    return
                end
                if type(rfMod.GetCharacterLaunch) == "function" then
                    local okL, launch = pcall(rfMod.GetCharacterLaunch, curChar)
                    if okL and launch and launch.Position and launch.Velocity then
                        local detachTime = shotTime + detachDelay
                        local dCmd = cmds.RainbowFlick({
                            Mode = cmds.Modes and cmds.Modes.RainbowFlickDetach or "RainbowFlickDetach",
                            AimDirection = launch.Direction,
                            ShotTime = detachTime,
                        })
                        local actionId = proto.Send(dCmd)
                        if renderer and type(renderer.ApplyPredictedState) == "function" and physics and type(physics.NewState) == "function" then
                            local myTeam = self._match:GetLocalTeamName()
                            local predState = physics.NewState(launch.Position, launch.Velocity, physics.BallRadius or 1, detachTime)
                            predState.ActionId = actionId or dCmd.ActionId
                            predState.FallbackActionId = fallbackActionId
                            predState.BlockedGoalTeamName = myTeam
                            predState.LastKickerTeamName = myTeam
                            predState.LastKickerUserId = lp.UserId
                            predState.LastKickKind = cmds.Kinds and cmds.Kinds.RainbowFlick or "RainbowFlick"
                            predState.FlightStartedAt = detachTime
                            pcall(renderer.ApplyPredictedState, predState, true)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)

            return true
        end

        function ActionDispatcher:_clearShotOverrides()
            if self._shotCamLockConn and self._shotCamLockConn.Connected then
                self._shotCamLockConn:Disconnect()
            end
            self._shotCamLockConn = nil
            self._activeShotAimRay = nil
            self._activeShotCurve = nil
            self._suppressShotAimAssist = false
            self._isShootingSequence = false
        end

        function ActionDispatcher:ExecuteShot(solution, isVolley)
            if self._isShootingSequence or self._isFakeBaiting then
                return false
            end
            if not self:CanAttempt("Shoot", 0.22) then
                return false
            end
            if self._player:IsRagdolled() or self._player:HasKickBlock() then
                return false
            end

            local clientShoot = GameModules.Get(GameModules.Paths.ClientShoot)
            if not (clientShoot and type(clientShoot.HandleInputBegan) == "function" and type(clientShoot.HandleInputEnded) == "function") then
                return false
            end

            self._isShootingSequence = true
            local bypassOn = self._hookGuard and self._hookGuard:IsBypassEnabled()
            local kickInput = self:CreateActionInput("Kick")

            if bypassOn and solution and solution.CalibratedAimRay then
                self._activeShotAimRay = solution.CalibratedAimRay
                self._suppressShotAimAssist = true
                self._activeShotCurve = (solution.Curve and solution.Curve ~= 0) and solution.Curve or nil
            elseif solution and solution.TargetPosition then
                local aimPoint = solution.TargetPosition
                if solution.CalibratedAimRay and solution.CalibratedAimRay.Direction and solution.LaunchPosition then
                    aimPoint = solution.LaunchPosition + solution.CalibratedAimRay.Direction * 30
                end
                self:AimCameraAtPoint(aimPoint, 0)
                if self._store:Get("Striker.LockAimDuringShot") ~= false then
                    self._shotCamLockConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                        if self._isShootingSequence and not LouisHub._destroyed then
                            self:AimCameraAtPoint(aimPoint, 0)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            local okBegin, began = pcall(clientShoot.HandleInputBegan, kickInput, false)
            if not (okBegin and began) then
                self:_clearShotOverrides()
                return false
            end

            local holdTime = math.clamp((solution and solution.ChargeSeconds) or 0.28, 0.05, 0.78)
            if isVolley then
                holdTime = math.min(holdTime, 0.12)
            end

            task.delay(holdTime, function()
                if LouisHub._destroyed then
                    self:_clearShotOverrides()
                    return
                end
                if not bypassOn and solution and solution.TargetPosition then
                    local aimPoint = solution.TargetPosition
                    if solution.CalibratedAimRay and solution.CalibratedAimRay.Direction and solution.LaunchPosition then
                        aimPoint = solution.LaunchPosition + solution.CalibratedAimRay.Direction * 30
                    end
                    self:AimCameraAtPoint(aimPoint, 0)
                end
                pcall(clientShoot.HandleInputEnded, kickInput)
                task.delay(0.14, function()
                    self:_clearShotOverrides()
                end)
            end)

            return true
        end

        function ActionDispatcher:IsShooting()
            return self._isShootingSequence or self._isFakeBaiting
        end

        function ActionDispatcher:Destroy()
            if self._monkeyPatchConn then
                self._monkeyPatchConn:Disconnect()
                self._monkeyPatchConn = nil
            end
            self:RemoveMonkeyPatches()
            self:_clearShotOverrides()
            self._isFakeBaiting = false
            self._activeDodgeDir = nil
            self._activeGkDiveWorldDir = nil
            self._activeSilentCurve = nil
        end

        return ActionDispatcher
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- BOOTSTRAP PART 8
    --========================================================================
    local ctx = LouisHub.Context
    local DmcGoalkeeperEngineClass = LouisHub.Require("Engine/DmcGoalkeeperEngine")
    local GoalkeeperControllerClass = LouisHub.Require("Automation/GoalkeeperController")
    local SprintControllerClass = LouisHub.Require("Automation/SprintController")
    local ActionDispatcherClass = LouisHub.Require("Automation/ActionDispatcher")

    local dmcGoalkeeper = DmcGoalkeeperEngineClass.new(ctx.Store, ctx.Player, ctx.Match)
    ctx.Engines.DmcGK = dmcGoalkeeper

    local goalkeeperController = GoalkeeperControllerClass.new(ctx.Store, ctx.Match, ctx.Ball, ctx.Engines)
    local sprintController = SprintControllerClass.new(ctx.Store, ctx.Player, ctx.Match)
    local actionDispatcher = ActionDispatcherClass.new(ctx.Store, ctx.HookGuard, ctx.Player, ctx.Ball, ctx.Match, ctx.Engines)

    ctx.Automation = ctx.Automation or {}
    ctx.Automation.Goalkeeper = goalkeeperController
    ctx.Automation.Sprint = sprintController
    ctx.Automation.Dispatcher = actionDispatcher

    LouisHub.OnDestroy(function()
        pcall(function() dmcGoalkeeper:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() actionDispatcher:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() sprintController:Destroy() end)
    end)

    LouisHub.PartLoaded[8] = true
    print(string.format("[LouisHub v%s] Part 8/10 initialized (DmcGK Fixed, SprintController & ActionDispatcher Ready)", LouisHub.Version))
end

--============================================================================
-- LOUIS HUB // ILLEGAL SOCCER SUITE v3.2.0 (ULTRA PRECISION & ANTI-LAG EDITION)
-- PART 9/10: DMC AUTO-DRIBBLE, UPGRADED STRIKER CONTROLLER & OPTIMIZED VISUALS
-- Requires Parts 1/10 - 8/10 loaded in getgenv().LouisHub
--============================================================================

do
    local GlobalEnv = (type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or _G
    local LouisHub = GlobalEnv.LouisHub

    if type(LouisHub) ~= "table"
        or not (LouisHub.PartLoaded
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[1]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[2]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[3]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[4]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[5]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[6]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[7]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[8])
    then
        error("[LouisHub] Parts 1/10 through 8/10 must be executed before Part 9/10!", 2)
    end

    local Players = LouisHub.SafeService("Players")
    local RunService = LouisHub.SafeService("RunService")
    local UserInputService = LouisHub.SafeService("UserInputService")
    local GuiService = LouisHub.SafeService("GuiService")
    local CoreGuiDirect = LouisHub.SafeService("CoreGui", true)
    local CoreGuiCloned = LouisHub.SafeService("CoreGui")

    local function flatVec(v)
        return Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
    end

    local function flatUnit(v, fallback)
        if typeof(v) ~= "Vector3" then
            return fallback
        end
        local fv = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
        if fv.Magnitude > 1e-4 then
            return fv.Unit
        end
        return fallback
    end

    local function finite(n)
        return type(n) == "number" and n == n and math.abs(n) < math.huge
    end

    local function finiteVector(v)
        return typeof(v) == "Vector3" and finite(v.X) and finite(v.Y) and finite(v.Z)
    end

    local function createExecutorGui(name)
        local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        gui.Name = name
        gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        gui.DisplayOrder = 999999

        local parented = false

        local function tryParent(target)
            if not target or typeof(target) ~= "Instance" then
                return false
            end
            local ok = pcall(function()
                gui.Parent = target
            end)
            return ok and gui.Parent ~= nil
        end

        if type(gethui) == "function" then
            local ok, hui = pcall(gethui)
            if ok and tryParent(hui) then
                parented = true
            end
        end

        if not parented and tryParent(CoreGuiDirect) then
            parented = true
        end

        if not parented and tryParent(CoreGuiCloned) then
            parented = true
        end

        if not parented and Players then
            local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
            if lp then
                local pg = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
                if tryParent(pg) then
                    parented = true
                end
            end
        end

        if not parented then
            warn("[LouisHub] Failed to parent ScreenGui to gethui/CoreGui/PlayerGui!")
        end

        return gui
    end

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Automation/DmcAutoDribble (100% Unabridged Native Engine)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Automation/DmcAutoDribble", function(Require)
        local GameModules = Require("Bridge/GameModules")

        local DmcAutoDribble = {}
        DmcAutoDribble.__index = DmcAutoDribble

        local C = {
            Interval = 1 / 60,
            DiscoverySeconds = 0.15,
            Range = 40,
            Lookahead = 0.22,
            KickLookahead = 0.12,
            RetrySeconds = 0.25,
            Step = 0.025,
            Padding = 1.0,
            ManualGrace = 0.15,
            MaximumDisplayLead = 0.20,
            ChargeStateGrace = 0.10,
            MaximumNetworkLead = 0.12,
            PingSampleSeconds = 0.5,
            PickupReadySeconds = 0.35,
        }

        local function attackDirection(value)
            if typeof(value) == "table" or typeof(value) == "Ray" then
                value = value.Direction
            end
            if not finiteVector(value) then
                return nil
            end
            local direction = flatVec(value)
            return direction.Magnitude > 1e-5 and direction.Unit or nil
        end

        local function segmentBox(a, b, half)
            local low, high = 0, 1
            for _, axis in ipairs({ "X", "Y", "Z" }) do
                local origin = a[axis]
                local delta = b[axis] - origin
                local extent = half[axis]
                if math.abs(delta) < 1e-8 then
                    if math.abs(origin) > extent then
                        return false
                    end
                else
                    local enter, leave = (-extent - origin) / delta, (extent - origin) / delta
                    if enter > leave then
                        enter, leave = leave, enter
                    end
                    low, high = math.max(low, enter), math.min(high, leave)
                    if low > high then
                        return false
                    end
                end
            end
            return true
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble.new(store, hookGuard)
            local lp = Players and Players.LocalPlayer or nil
            local self = setmetatable({
                _store = store,
                _hookGuard = hookGuard,
                _localPlayer = lp,
                _alive = true,
                _enabled = false,
                _status = "OFF",
                _lastError = nil,
                _generation = 0,
                _loading = false,
                _N = nil,
                _focused = true,
                _ignoreInputFn = nil,

                _heartbeat = nil,
                _inputConnection = nil,
                _controlConnection = nil,
                _focusLostConn = nil,
                _focusGainConn = nil,
                _storeConn = nil,
                _pickupWindow = nil,
                _scanning = false,
                _manualHadBall = true,
                _attackConnections = {},
                _characterConnections = {},
                _characterIndex = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
                _characterIndexReady = false,
                _attacks = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
                _nativeOwner = nil,
                _queuedScan = nil,
                _nextPingSample = 0,
                _networkLead = 0,
                _nextTick = 0,
                _nextDiscovery = 0,
                _manualUntil = 0,
                _retryAt = 0,
                _candidates = {},
                _cachedCharacter = nil,
                _stats = {
                    scans = 0,
                    threats = 0,
                    proximityTriggers = 0,
                    tackleTriggers = 0,
                    kickTriggers = 0,
                    attempts = 0,
                    started = 0,
                    nativeBlocked = 0,
                    observerErrors = 0,
                    errors = 0,
                    maxTickMs = 0,
                    readyTicks = 0,
                    blockedTicks = 0,
                    motionAligned = 0,
                    kickStateWithoutPresentation = 0,
                    earlyAttackSamples = 0,
                    pickupReadinessWakes = 0,
                    pickupInputResets = 0,
                },
            }, DmcAutoDribble)

            self._focusLostConn = UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased:Connect(function()
                self._focused = false
            end)
            self._focusGainConn = UserInputService.WindowFocused:Connect(function()
                self._focused = true
            end)

            self._storeConn = self._store:OnChanged("Striker.AutoDribble", function(val)
                self:SetEnabled(val == true)
            end)

            if self._store:Get("Striker.AutoDribble") == true then
                task.defer(function()
                    if self._alive and self._store:Get("Striker.AutoDribble") == true then
                        self:SetEnabled(true)
                    end
                end)
            end

            return self
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:SetIgnoreInputFn(fn)
            self._ignoreInputFn = fn
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:GetStatus()
            return self._status
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:GetStats()
            return self._stats
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_loadModules()
            local P = GameModules.Paths
            local N = self._N or {}
            local needed = {
                Controls = P.Controls,
                Input = P.LibrariesInput,
                Tackle = P.SlideTackle,
                Sprint = P.ClientSprint,
                Shoot = P.ClientShoot,
                ChargeState = P.ActionChargeState,
                Effects = P.ClientTackleEffects,
                KickCore = P.KickCore,
                Power = P.KickPowerups,
                Dodge = P.Dodge,
                ClientDodge = P.ClientDodge,
                Renderer = P.ClientBallRenderer,
                Motion = P.CharacterMotion,
                Actors = P.Actors,
                Teams = P.ClientActorTeams,
                Match = P.MatchSettings,
                Controllers = P.CharacterControllers,
                Freeze = P.ClientKickoffFreeze,
                Replay = P.ClientGoalReplay,
                Hitboxes = P.HitboxSettings,
            }
            for k, path in pairs(needed) do
                if not N[k] then
                    N[k] = GameModules.Get(path, true)
                end
            end
            self._N = N

            for _, item in ipairs({
                { N.Controls, "CreateInput" },
                { N.Controls, "IsInput" },
                { N.Controls, "IsAvailable" },
                { N.Controls, "IsOnCooldown" },
                { N.Controls, "IsActive" },
                { N.Input, "ShouldIgnoreKeybind" },
                { N.Tackle, "GetSlidingUntil" },
                { N.Tackle, "GetMaximumTravelBetween" },
                { N.Sprint, "CanSpendStamina" },
                { N.Shoot, "IsCharging" },
                { N.ChargeState, "IsCharging" },
                { N.Effects, "GetObservedChargeFill" },
                { N.KickCore, "GetMinimumReleaseDelaySeconds" },
                { N.Power, "GetChargeConstants" },
                { N.ClientDodge, "HandleInputBegan" },
                { N.ClientDodge, "IsDribbling" },
                { N.ClientDodge, "UpdateControlState" },
                { N.ClientDodge, "SetOwnerUserId" },
            }) do
                assert(item[1] and type(item[1][item[2]]) == "function", "Missing native dribble API: " .. item[2])
            end
            assert(N.Dodge and finite(N.Dodge.Constants.StaminaCost), "Missing dribble stamina cost")
            local box = N.Hitboxes and N.Hitboxes.Kick
            assert(box and finiteVector(box.Size) and typeof(box.CFrameOffset) == "CFrame", "Missing native kick hitbox")
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_currentBallId()
            local N = self._N
            if N and N.Renderer and type(N.Renderer.GetMatchBallId) == "function" then
                return N.Renderer.GetMatchBallId()
            end
            return nil
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_ownsBall(ch)
            local N = self._N
            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
            self._localPlayer = lp
            if not (N and lp) then
                return false
            end
            local owner = self._nativeOwner
            if owner and owner.character == ch and owner.ballId == self:_currentBallId()
                and owner.context == N.Motion.GetContext(ch) then
                return owner.userId == lp.UserId
            end
            return N.Renderer.GetOwnedUserId() == lp.UserId
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_stopWatch()
            self._queuedScan = nil
            self._nextPingSample = 0
            self._networkLead = 0
            if self._heartbeat then
                self._heartbeat:Disconnect()
                self._heartbeat = nil
            end
            if self._inputConnection then
                self._inputConnection:Disconnect()
                self._inputConnection = nil
            end
            if self._controlConnection then
                self._controlConnection:Disconnect()
                self._controlConnection = nil
            end
            self._pickupWindow = nil
            for _, conn in ipairs(self._attackConnections) do
                conn:Disconnect()
            end
            table.clear(self._attackConnections)
            for _, conn in ipairs(self._characterConnections) do
                conn:Disconnect()
            end
            table.clear(self._characterConnections)
            table.clear(self._characterIndex)
            self._characterIndexReady = false
            table.clear(self._attacks)
            if self._hookGuard then
                self._hookGuard:RestoreTag("DmcDribble")
            end
            self._nativeOwner = nil
            self._candidates = {}
            self._cachedCharacter = nil
            self._nextDiscovery = 0
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_stopWithError(err)
            self._enabled = false
            self._generation = self._generation + 1
            self:_stopWatch()
            self._status = "ERROR"
            self._lastError = tostring(err)
            self._stats.errors = self._stats.errors + 1
            warn("[LouisHub/DmcDribble] " .. self._lastError)
            if self._store:Get("Striker.AutoDribble") == true then
                self._store:Set("Striker.AutoDribble", false)
            end
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_requestScan()
            if self._queuedScan or not (self._alive and self._enabled) then
                return
            end
            local ticket = {}
            self._queuedScan = ticket
            task.defer(function()
                if self._queuedScan ~= ticket or not (self._alive and self._enabled) then
                    return
                end
                self._nextTick = os.clock() + C.Interval
                self:_runScan()
            end)
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_watchNativeOwner()
            local N = self._N
            if not (self._hookGuard and N and N.ClientDodge) then
                return
            end
            self._hookGuard:WrapTagged("DmcDribble", N.ClientDodge, "SetOwnerUserId", function(original)
                return function(userId, ...)
                    local previous = self._nativeOwner
                    if self._alive and self._enabled then
                        self._nativeOwner = nil
                    end
                    local result = table.pack(original(userId, ...))
                    if self._alive and self._enabled then
                        local ok = pcall(function()
                            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
                            local ch = lp and lp.Character
                            self._nativeOwner = ch and {
                                character = ch,
                                ballId = self:_currentBallId(),
                                context = N.Motion.GetContext(ch),
                                userId = userId,
                            } or nil
                            if self._nativeOwner and lp and userId == lp.UserId and (not previous
                                or previous.userId ~= userId or previous.character ~= ch
                                or previous.ballId ~= self._nativeOwner.ballId or previous.context ~= self._nativeOwner.context) then
                                self._pickupWindow = {
                                    character = ch,
                                    ballId = self._nativeOwner.ballId,
                                    context = self._nativeOwner.context,
                                    expires = os.clock() + C.PickupReadySeconds,
                                }
                                if not self._manualHadBall and self._manualUntil > os.clock() then
                                    self._manualUntil = 0
                                    self._stats.pickupInputResets = self._stats.pickupInputResets + 1
                                end
                                self._nextTick = 0
                                self._nextDiscovery = 0
                                self._retryAt = 0
                                self:_requestScan()
                            elseif not lp or userId ~= lp.UserId then
                                self._pickupWindow = nil
                            end
                        end)
                        if not ok then
                            self._nativeOwner = nil
                            self._stats.observerErrors = self._stats.observerErrors + 1
                        end
                    end
                    return table.unpack(result, 1, result.n)
                end
            end)
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_watchPickupReadiness()
            local N = self._N
            if type(N.Controls.OnStateChanged) ~= "function" then
                return
            end
            local function changed(name, field, active, available, _, cooldown)
                if self._scanning or not (self._alive and self._enabled) then
                    return
                end
                local pickup = self._pickupWindow
                if not pickup then
                    return
                end
                local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
                local ch = lp and lp.Character
                if os.clock() > pickup.expires or ch ~= pickup.character or not ch or not ch.Parent
                    or self:_currentBallId() ~= pickup.ballId or N.Motion.GetContext(ch) ~= pickup.context then
                    self._pickupWindow = nil
                    return
                end
                local becameReady = name == "Dribble" and available == true and cooldown ~= true
                    and (field == "Available" or field == "Cooldown")
                local strikeEnded = field == "Active" and active == false
                    and (name == "Kick" or name == "Pass" or name == "Lob" or name == "RainbowFlick")
                if not (becameReady or strikeEnded) or not self:_ownsBall(ch) or N.ClientDodge.IsDribbling() then
                    return
                end
                self._stats.pickupReadinessWakes = self._stats.pickupReadinessWakes + 1
                self._nextTick = 0
                self:_requestScan()
            end

            self._controlConnection = N.Controls.OnStateChanged(function(...)
                local ok = pcall(changed, ...)
                if not ok then
                    self._stats.observerErrors = self._stats.observerErrors + 1
                end
            end)
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_watchCharacters()
            local function added(node)
                local model
                if node:IsA("Model") then
                    if node:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or node:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        model = node
                    end
                elseif node:IsA("Humanoid") or (node:IsA("BasePart") and node.Name == "HumanoidRootPart") then
                    model = node.Parent
                end
                if model and model:IsA("Model") then
                    self._characterIndex[model] = true
                    self._nextDiscovery = 0
                    self._nextTick = 0
                end
            end

            table.insert(self._characterConnections, workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(added))
            table.insert(self._characterConnections, workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(node)
                if self._characterIndex[node] then
                    self._characterIndex[node] = nil
                    self._nextDiscovery = 0
                    self._nextTick = 0
                end
            end))
