            for _, node in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                added(node)
            end
            self._characterIndexReady = true
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_resolveCharacter(character)
            local N = self._N
            return character and type(N.Motion.ResolveCharacter) == "function"
                and N.Motion.ResolveCharacter(character) or character
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_actorFor(character, actor)
            local N = self._N
            if actor and actor ~= character and self:_resolveCharacter(actor.Character) == character then
                return actor
            end
            if type(N.Actors.GetFromCharacter) == "function" then
                actor = N.Actors.GetFromCharacter(character)
            else
                local userId = character:GetAttribute("UserId")
                actor = finite(userId) and N.Actors.GetByUserId(userId) or nil
            end
            return actor and self:_resolveCharacter(actor.Character) == character and actor or nil
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_characterTeam(character, actor)
            local N = self._N
            if actor then
                return N.Teams.GetActorTeamName(actor)
            end
            if type(N.Teams.GetCharacterTeamName) == "function" then
                return N.Teams.GetCharacterTeamName(character)
            end
            return N.Teams.GetActorTeamName(character)
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_observeAttack(kind, userId, ...)
            local N = self._N
            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
            if kind ~= "TackleStarted" and kind ~= "KickChargeStarted" and kind ~= "KickChargeUpdated"
                and kind ~= "KickReleased" and kind ~= "KickChargeCanceled" then
                return
            end
            if not finite(userId) or (lp and userId == lp.UserId) then
                return
            end
            local actor = N.Actors.GetByUserId(userId)
            local character = self:_resolveCharacter(actor and actor.Character)
            if not character or not character.Parent then
                return
            end

            local now = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
            local context = N.Motion.GetContext(character)
            local state = self._attacks[character]
            if not state or state.context ~= context then
                state = { context = context }
                self._attacks[character] = state
            end

            local started = select(7, ...)
            if kind == "TackleStarted" then
                if not finite(started) then return end
                if started > now or now - started > N.Tackle.Constants.HitboxDelaySeconds + N.Tackle.Constants.HitboxSeconds then
                    return
                end
                if state.slideStarted and started <= state.slideStarted then return end
                state.slideStarted = started
                state.slideDirection = attackDirection(select(2, ...))
            else
                local actionId, presentationId, sequence = select(9, ...), select(11, ...), select(13, ...)
                local previous = state.kick
                if previous then
                    if finite(presentationId) and finite(previous.presentationId) then
                        if presentationId < previous.presentationId then return end
                    elseif actionId ~= nil and previous.actionId ~= nil and actionId ~= previous.actionId
                        and kind ~= "KickChargeStarted" then
                        return
                    end
                end
                local same = previous and ((presentationId ~= nil and presentationId == previous.presentationId)
                    or (presentationId == nil and actionId ~= nil and actionId == previous.actionId))
                local terminal = kind == "KickReleased" or kind == "KickChargeCanceled"
                if same and not terminal then
                    if previous.ended then return end
                    if finite(sequence) and finite(previous.sequence) and sequence <= previous.sequence then return end
                end
                if terminal then
                    state.kick = { ended = true, actionId = actionId, presentationId = presentationId }
                else
                    local passType, charge = select(1, ...), select(3, ...)
                    if not finite(charge) or charge < 0 or not finite(started) or started > now then return end
                    if previous and finite(previous.started) and started < previous.started then return end
                    if passType ~= "Shot" then
                        state.kick = { ended = true, actionId = actionId, presentationId = presentationId }
                        return
                    end
                    local constants = N.Power.GetChargeConstants(actor, N.KickCore.Constants)
                    if not constants or not finite(constants.MaximumChargeSeconds) then return end
                    if now - started > constants.MaximumChargeSeconds + 0.25 then return end
                    state.kick = {
                        started = started,
                        charge = charge,
                        at = now,
                        actionId = actionId,
                        presentationId = presentationId,
                        sequence = sequence,
                        direction = attackDirection(select(4, ...)) or attackDirection(select(15, ...)),
                        sawCharging = N.ChargeState.IsCharging(actor) or (same and previous.sawCharging) or false,
                        expires = started + constants.MaximumChargeSeconds + 0.25,
                    }
                end
            end

            self._stats.earlyAttackSamples = self._stats.earlyAttackSamples + 1
            self._nextTick = 0
            self._nextDiscovery = 0
            self:_requestScan()
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_watchAttacks()
            local remotes = GameModules.GetBallCommandRemotes()
            for _, remote in ipairs(remotes) do
                table.insert(self._attackConnections, remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
                    if not (self._alive and self._enabled) then return end
                    local ok = pcall(self._observeAttack, self, ...)
                    if not ok then
                        self._stats.observerErrors = self._stats.observerErrors + 1
                    end
                end))
            end
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_discover(ch)
            local seen, list = {}, {}
            local function add(character, actor)
                character = self:_resolveCharacter(character)
                if not character or character == ch or seen[character] then return end
                seen[character] = true
                list[#list + 1] = { character = character, actor = self:_actorFor(character, actor) }
            end
            if Players then
                for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    add(player.Character, player)
                end
            end
            for character in pairs(self._characterIndex) do
                if character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
                    add(character)
                end
            end
            for character in pairs(self._attacks) do
                if character.Parent then
                    add(character)
                end
            end
            if not self._characterIndexReady then
                local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
                local function children(container)
                    if not container then return end
                    for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                        if child:IsA("Model") then add(child) end
                    end
                end
                children(folder)
                children(folder and folder:FindFirstChild("NPCs"))
            end
            self._candidates = list
            self._cachedCharacter = ch
            self._nextDiscovery = os.clock() + C.DiscoverySeconds
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_alignDisplayedMotion(character, cf, velocity, slideAge, slideDirection)
            local N = self._N
            local motion = N.Motion
            if type(motion.GetDisplayDelaySeconds) ~= "function"
                or (type(motion.GetSimulationRoot) == "function" and motion.GetSimulationRoot(character)) then
                return cf, 0
            end
            local delay = motion.GetDisplayDelaySeconds(character)
            if not finite(delay) or delay <= 0 then
                return cf, 0
            end
            delay = math.min(delay, C.MaximumDisplayLead)
            local displacement = velocity * delay
            if slideAge then
                local direction = slideDirection or flatVec(velocity)
                if not slideDirection and direction.Magnitude < 1 then
                    direction = flatVec(cf.LookVector)
                end
                if direction.Magnitude > 1e-5 then
                    local constants = N.Tackle.Constants
                    local motionSeconds = math.min(delay, slideAge)
                    local travel = N.Tackle.GetMaximumTravelBetween(
                        slideAge - motionSeconds,
                        slideAge,
                        constants.StartupDashDistance,
                        constants.Distance
                    )
                    displacement = flatVec(velocity) * (delay - motionSeconds)
                        + direction.Unit * travel
                        + Vector3.new(0, velocity.Y * delay, 0)
                end
            end
            return cf + displacement, delay
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_contactEnvelope(rootCF, box, observedPosition, ownCF, ownSize)
            local extent = Vector3.new(C.Padding, C.Padding, C.Padding)
            if ownCF and finiteVector(ownSize) and ownSize.X > 0 and ownSize.Y > 0 and ownSize.Z > 0 then
                local attackCF = rootCF * box.CFrameOffset
                local right = attackCF:VectorToObjectSpace(ownCF.RightVector) * (ownSize.X * 0.5)
                local up = attackCF:VectorToObjectSpace(ownCF.UpVector) * (ownSize.Y * 0.5)
                local forward = attackCF:VectorToObjectSpace(ownCF.LookVector) * (ownSize.Z * 0.5)
                extent = Vector3.new(
                    math.max(C.Padding, math.abs(right.X) + math.abs(up.X) + math.abs(forward.X)),
                    math.max(C.Padding, math.abs(right.Y) + math.abs(up.Y) + math.abs(forward.Y)),
                    math.max(C.Padding, math.abs(right.Z) + math.abs(up.Z) + math.abs(forward.Z))
                )
            end
            local half = box.Size * 0.5 + extent
            if observedPosition then
                local delta = observedPosition - rootCF.Position
                local offset = (rootCF * box.CFrameOffset):VectorToObjectSpace(delta)
                half = half + Vector3.new(math.abs(offset.X), math.abs(offset.Y), math.abs(offset.Z)) * 0.5
                rootCF = rootCF + delta * 0.5
            end
            return rootCF, half
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_reactionLead()
            local clock = os.clock()
            if clock >= self._nextPingSample then
                self._nextPingSample = clock + C.PingSampleSeconds
                local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
                local ok, ping = pcall(function()
                    return lp:GetNetworkPing()
                end)
                self._networkLead = (ok and finite(ping) and ping >= 0) and math.min(ping * 0.5, C.MaximumNetworkLead) or 0
            end
            return self._networkLead
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_contactTime(ownCF, ownVelocity, otherCF, otherVelocity, age, slideDirection, observedPosition, ownSize, horizon)
            local N = self._N
            local constants = N.Tackle.Constants
            local first = math.max(0, constants.HitboxDelaySeconds - age)
            local last = math.min(horizon or C.Lookahead, constants.HitboxDelaySeconds + constants.HitboxSeconds - age)
            if last < first then return nil end

            local direction = slideDirection or flatVec(otherVelocity)
            if not slideDirection and direction.Magnitude < 1 then
                direction = flatVec(otherCF.LookVector)
            end
            if direction.Magnitude < 1e-5 then return nil end
            direction = direction.Unit

            local box = N.Hitboxes.SlideTackle
            local facing = CFrame.lookAt(otherCF.Position, otherCF.Position + direction)
            local half
            facing, half = self:_contactEnvelope(facing, box, observedPosition, ownCF, ownSize)

            local function point(t)
                local travel = N.Tackle.GetMaximumTravelBetween(age, age + t, constants.StartupDashDistance, constants.Distance)
                local rootCF = facing + direction * travel
                return (rootCF * box.CFrameOffset):PointToObjectSpace(ownCF.Position + ownVelocity * t)
            end

            local previous = point(first)
            if segmentBox(previous, previous, half) then return first end
            local steps = math.max(1, math.ceil((last - first) / C.Step))
            for i = 1, steps do
                local t = first + (last - first) * i / steps
                local current = point(t)
                if segmentBox(previous, current, half) then return t end
                previous = current
            end
            return nil
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_kickWindow(candidate, horizon)
            local N = self._N
            local other = candidate.character
            local actor = self:_actorFor(other, candidate.actor)
            if not actor or not finite(actor.UserId) or N.Match.IsGoalkeeperCharacter(other) then
                return nil
            end
            local charging = N.ChargeState.IsCharging(actor)
            local fill, compact = N.Effects.GetObservedChargeFill(actor.UserId)
            local attack = self._attacks[other]
            local early = attack and attack.context == N.Motion.GetContext(other) and attack.kick
            if early then
                local now = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
                if early.ended or now > early.expires then return nil end
                if charging then
                    early.sawCharging = true
                elseif early.sawCharging and now - early.at > C.ChargeStateGrace then
                    return nil
                end
                local constants = N.Power.GetChargeConstants(actor, N.KickCore.Constants)
                local maximum = constants and constants.MaximumChargeSeconds
                if not finite(maximum) or maximum <= 0 then return nil end
                fill = math.clamp(math.max(now - early.started, early.charge + now - early.at) / maximum, 0, 1)
                compact = false
            elseif not charging then
                return nil
            end

            if not finite(fill) or fill < 0 or fill > 1 or compact ~= false then
                if fill == nil then
                    self._stats.kickStateWithoutPresentation = self._stats.kickStateWithoutPresentation + 1
                end
                return nil
            end

            local constants = N.Power.GetChargeConstants(actor, N.KickCore.Constants)
            local maximum = constants and constants.MaximumChargeSeconds
            if not finite(maximum) or maximum <= 0 then return nil end
            local first = N.KickCore.GetMinimumReleaseDelaySeconds(fill * maximum, constants)
            if not finite(first) or first < 0 or first > (horizon or C.KickLookahead) then return nil end
            return { first = first, fill = fill, direction = early and early.direction or nil }
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_kickContactTime(ownCF, ownVelocity, otherCF, otherVelocity, first, direction, observedPosition, ownSize, horizon)
            local N = self._N
            local box = N.Hitboxes.Kick
            local last = horizon or C.KickLookahead
            if last < first then return nil end

            if direction then
                otherCF = CFrame.lookAt(otherCF.Position, otherCF.Position + direction)
            end
            local half
            otherCF, half = self:_contactEnvelope(otherCF, box, observedPosition, ownCF, ownSize)

            local function point(t)
                local rootCF = otherCF + otherVelocity * t
                return (rootCF * box.CFrameOffset):PointToObjectSpace(ownCF.Position + ownVelocity * t)
            end

            local previous = point(first)
            if segmentBox(previous, previous, half) then return first end
            local steps = math.max(1, math.ceil((last - first) / C.Step))
            for i = 1, steps do
                local t = first + (last - first) * i / steps
                local current = point(t)
                if segmentBox(previous, current, half) then return t end
                previous = current
            end
            return nil
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_eligible()
            local N = self._N
            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
            self._localPlayer = lp
            if not (self._alive and self._enabled) then return nil, "OFF" end
            if not self._focused then return nil, "UNFOCUSED" end
            if GuiService and GuiService.MenuIsOpen then return nil, "MENU OPEN" end
            if N.Input.ShouldIgnoreKeybind(false) then return nil, "TEXT / KEYBIND" end
            if os.clock() < self._manualUntil then return nil, "MANUAL INPUT" end

            local ch = lp and lp.Character
            local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not ch or not ch.Parent or not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return nil, "WAITING" end
            if N.Match.IsGoalkeeperCharacter(ch) then return nil, "GK ROLE" end
            if N.Freeze and N.Freeze.IsFrozen and N.Freeze.IsFrozen() then return nil, "FROZEN" end
            if N.Replay and N.Replay.IsActive and N.Replay.IsActive() then return nil, "REPLAY" end
            if N.Controllers.IsSuspended(ch) then return nil, "RECOVERING" end
            if not self:_ownsBall(ch) then return nil, "NO BALL" end
            if N.Shoot.IsCharging() then return nil, "SHOOTING" end
            for _, name in ipairs({ "Kick", "Pass", "Lob", "RainbowFlick" }) do
                if N.Controls.IsActive(name) then return nil, "STRIKING" end
            end
            if N.ClientDodge.IsDribbling() then return nil, "DRIBBLING" end
            if not N.Controllers.IsLanded(ch, hum) then return nil, "MOVING" end
            if not N.Controls.IsAvailable("Dribble") or N.Controls.IsOnCooldown("Dribble") then return nil, "COOLDOWN / BLOCKED" end
            if not N.Sprint.CanSpendStamina(N.Dodge.Constants.StaminaCost, true) then return nil, "LOW STAMINA" end
            if os.clock() < self._retryAt then return nil, "RETRY WAIT" end
            return ch, root
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_tick()
            local N = self._N
            local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
            local character = lp and lp.Character
            if character and character.Parent and self:_ownsBall(character) and not N.ClientDodge.IsDribbling() then
                N.ClientDodge.UpdateControlState()
            end

            local ch, rootOrReason = self:_eligible()
            if not ch then
                self._stats.blockedTicks = self._stats.blockedTicks + 1
                if rootOrReason == "COOLDOWN / BLOCKED" then
                    if N.Controls.IsOnCooldown("Dribble") then
                        local remaining = type(N.Controls.GetCooldownRemaining) == "function"
                            and N.Controls.GetCooldownRemaining("Dribble") or nil
                        self._status = finite(remaining) and string.format("COOLDOWN %.1fs", math.max(0, remaining)) or "COOLDOWN"
                    else
                        self._status = "NOT READY"
                    end
                else
                    self._status = rootOrReason
                end
                return
            end

            self._stats.readyTicks = self._stats.readyTicks + 1
            local root = rootOrReason
            local team = N.Teams.GetActorTeamName(lp)
            local opposing = N.Match.Constants.TeamDisplayNames[team] and N.Match.GetOpposingTeamName(team)
            if not opposing then
                self._status = "WAITING FOR OPPONENT"
                return
            end

            local context = N.Motion.GetContext(ch)
            local ownCF = N.Motion.GetCFrame(ch) or root.CFrame
            local velocity = N.Motion.GetVelocity(ch) or root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            if not finiteVector(ownCF.Position) or not finiteVector(velocity) then return end
            if self._cachedCharacter ~= ch or os.clock() >= self._nextDiscovery then
                self:_discover(ch)
            end

            self._stats.scans = self._stats.scans + 1
            self._status = "WATCHING"

            local lead = self:_reactionLead()
            local tackleHorizon, kickHorizon = C.Lookahead + lead, C.KickLookahead + lead
            local now, best = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(), nil

            for _, candidate in ipairs(self._candidates) do
                local other = candidate.character
                candidate.actor = self:_actorFor(other, candidate.actor)
                if other.Parent and N.Motion.GetContext(other) == context
                    and self:_characterTeam(other, candidate.actor) == opposing then

                    local untilTime = N.Tackle.GetSlidingUntil(other)
                    local constants = N.Tackle.Constants
                    local attack = self._attacks[other]
                    if attack and attack.context == context and attack.slideStarted then
                        untilTime = math.max(finite(untilTime) and untilTime or 0, attack.slideStarted + constants.TotalMotionSeconds)
                    end
                    local age = finite(untilTime) and (now - (untilTime - constants.TotalMotionSeconds)) or nil
                    local hitboxEndsAt = constants.HitboxDelaySeconds + constants.HitboxSeconds
                    local sliding = age ~= nil and age >= 0 and age <= hitboxEndsAt
                    local slideDirection = sliding and attack and attack.context == context and attack.slideStarted
                        and math.abs(untilTime - (attack.slideStarted + constants.TotalMotionSeconds)) < 1e-4
                        and attack.slideDirection or nil

                    local kick = self:_kickWindow(candidate, kickHorizon)
                    if sliding or kick then
                        local hum = other:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        local otherRoot = other:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if hum and hum.Health > 0 and otherRoot then
                            local otherCF = N.Motion.GetCFrame(other) or otherRoot.CFrame
                            local otherVelocity = N.Motion.GetVelocity(other) or otherRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                            if finiteVector(otherCF.Position) and finiteVector(otherVelocity)
                                and (otherCF.Position - ownCF.Position).Magnitude <= C.Range then
                                local observedPosition = otherCF.Position
                                local displayLead
                                otherCF, displayLead = self:_alignDisplayedMotion(other, otherCF, otherVelocity, sliding and age or nil, slideDirection)
                                if displayLead > 0 then
                                    self._stats.motionAligned = self._stats.motionAligned + 1
                                end
                                local uncertainPosition = displayLead > 0 and observedPosition or nil
                                local eta = sliding and self:_contactTime(ownCF, velocity, otherCF, otherVelocity, age, slideDirection, uncertainPosition, root.Size, tackleHorizon) or nil
                                local kickEta = kick and self:_kickContactTime(ownCF, velocity, otherCF, otherVelocity, kick.first, kick.direction, uncertainPosition, root.Size, kickHorizon) or nil
                                local reason = "tackle"
                                if kickEta and (not eta or kickEta < eta) then
                                    eta, reason = kickEta, "kick"
                                end
                                local distance = flatVec(otherCF.Position - ownCF.Position).Magnitude
                                if eta and (not best or eta < best.eta or (eta == best.eta and distance < (best.distance or math.huge))) then
                                    best = { character = other, eta = eta, distance = distance, reason = reason }
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            if not best or not self:_eligible() then return end

            self._stats.threats = self._stats.threats + 1
            local input = N.Controls.CreateInput("Dribble")
            assert(input and N.Controls.IsInput("Dribble", input), "Native dribble binding unavailable")
            self._retryAt = os.clock() + C.RetrySeconds
            self._stats.attempts = self._stats.attempts + 1

            N.ClientDodge.HandleInputBegan(input, false)
            local started = N.ClientDodge.IsDribbling() == true
            if started then
                self._pickupWindow = nil
                self._stats.started = self._stats.started + 1
                if best.reason == "kick" then
                    self._stats.kickTriggers = self._stats.kickTriggers + 1
                else
                    self._stats.tackleTriggers = self._stats.tackleTriggers + 1
                end
            else
                self._stats.nativeBlocked = self._stats.nativeBlocked + 1
            end
            self._status = started and "DRIBBLING" or "NATIVE BLOCKED"
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:_runScan()
            self._queuedScan = nil
            local start = os.clock()
            self._scanning = true
            local ok, err = pcall(self._tick, self)
            self._scanning = false
            self._stats.maxTickMs = math.max(self._stats.maxTickMs, (os.clock() - start) * 1000)
            if not ok then
                self:_stopWithError(err)
            end
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:SetEnabled(value)
            value = value == true
            self._generation = self._generation + 1
            local request = self._generation

            if not value then
                self._enabled = false
                self:_stopWatch()
                self._status = "OFF"
                return true
            end

            if not self._alive or self._loading then return false end
            if self._enabled then return true end

            self._loading = true
            local ok, err = pcall(self._loadModules, self)
            self._loading = false
            if request ~= self._generation or not self._alive then return false end
            if not ok then
                self:_stopWithError(err)
                return false
            end

            self._enabled = true
            self._status = "WATCHING"
            self._nextTick = 0

            local connected, connErr = pcall(function()
                self:_watchNativeOwner()
                self:_watchPickupReadiness()
                self:_watchCharacters()
                self:_watchAttacks()

                local N = self._N
                self._inputConnection = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
                    if not self._enabled then return end
                    local checked, inputErr = pcall(function()
                        if N.Input.ShouldIgnoreKeybind(processed)
                            or (self._ignoreInputFn and self._ignoreInputFn(input)) then
                            return
                        end
                        for _, name in ipairs({ "Kick", "Pass", "Lob", "RainbowFlick", "Dribble", "Tackle", "Jump" }) do
                            if N.Controls.IsInput(name, input) then
                                self._manualUntil = os.clock() + C.ManualGrace
                                local lp = self._localPlayer or (Players and Players.LocalPlayer)
                                local ch = lp and lp.Character
                                self._manualHadBall = ch ~= nil and self:_ownsBall(ch)
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                    if not checked then
                        self:_stopWithError(inputErr)
                    end
                end)

                self._heartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    local start = os.clock()
                    if not (self._alive and self._enabled) or start < self._nextTick then
                        return
                    end
                    if self._nextTick == 0 then
                        self._nextTick = start + C.Interval
                    else
                        self._nextTick = self._nextTick + (math.floor((start - self._nextTick) / C.Interval) + 1) * C.Interval
                    end
                    self:_runScan()
                end)
            end)

            if not connected then
                self:_stopWithError(connErr)
                return false
            end
            return true
        end

        function DmcAutoDribble:Destroy()
            if not self._alive then
                return
            end
            self._alive = false
            self:SetEnabled(false)
            if self._storeConn then self._storeConn:Disconnect(); self._storeConn = nil end
            if self._focusLostConn then self._focusLostConn:Disconnect(); self._focusLostConn = nil end
            if self._focusGainConn then self._focusGainConn:Disconnect(); self._focusGainConn = nil end
        end

        return DmcAutoDribble
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Automation/StrikerController (Bicycle Range, Multi-Def & Fake Trick)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Automation/StrikerController", function()
        local StrikerController = {}
        StrikerController.__index = StrikerController

        function StrikerController.new(store, matchBridge, ballBridge, playerBridge, engines, dispatcher, dmcDribble)
            return setmetatable({
                _store = store,
                _match = matchBridge,
                _ball = ballBridge,
                _player = playerBridge,
                _engines = engines,
                _dispatcher = dispatcher,
                _dmcDribble = dmcDribble,
                _flickComboActive = false,
                _flickComboExpiresAt = 0,
                _flickTriggeredAt = 0,
                _fakeTrickStage = nil,
                _fakeTrickUntil = 0,
                _fakeTrickCooldownUntil = 0,
                _fakeTrickEvadeDir = nil,
                _lastStatus = "Idle",
                _lastPassScanAt = 0,
                _lastDefenderScanAt = 0,
                _cachedDefThreat = nil,
            }, StrikerController)
        end

        function StrikerController:TriggerFlickCombo()
            if self._match:IsGoalkeeper() then
                return false
            end
            if not self._ball:IsLocalOwner() then
                return false
            end
            if not self._player:CanUseRainbowFlick() then
                return false
            end
            local ok = self._dispatcher:TriggerRainbowFlick()
            if ok then
                self._flickComboActive = true
                self._flickTriggeredAt = os.clock()
                self._flickComboExpiresAt = os.clock() + 1.8
                self._lastStatus = "FlickCombo: Popped"
                return true
            end
            return false
        end

        function StrikerController:IsFlickComboActive()
            return self._flickComboActive and os.clock() < self._flickComboExpiresAt
        end

        -- Lightweight Multi-Defender Threat Scanner (Potato-Friendly)
        function StrikerController:_scanDefendersAhead(cfg, actorsSnapshot, aimDir)
            local now = os.clock()
            local scanInterval = cfg.DefenderScanInterval or 0.08
            if self._cachedDefThreat and (now - self._lastDefenderScanAt) < scanInterval then
                return self._cachedDefThreat
            end
            self._lastDefenderScanAt = now

            local root = self._player:GetRoot()
            if not (root and actorsSnapshot and actorsSnapshot.Enemies) then
                self._cachedDefThreat = { Count = 0, ClosestDist = math.huge, EvadeDir = nil }
                return self._cachedDefThreat
            end

            local myPos = root.Position
            local fwd = flatUnit(aimDir or root.CFrame.LookVector, Vector3.new(0, 0, -1))
            local right = Vector3.new(-fwd.Z, 0, fwd.X)
            local radiusCap = math.max(10, cfg.FakeTrickDefenderRadius or 14) + 6
            local maxCheck = math.clamp(cfg.MultiDefenderMaxCheck or 4, 1, 8)

            local count = 0
            local checked = 0
            local closestDist = math.huge
            local lateralBias = 0

            for _, enemy in ipairs(actorsSnapshot.Enemies) do
                if not enemy.IsRagdolled and not enemy.IsStunned and not enemy.IsGoalkeeper then
                    local rel = flatVec(enemy.Position - myPos)
                    local d = rel.Magnitude
                    if d <= radiusCap and d > 0.5 then
                        checked = checked + 1
                        local dotFwd = rel.Unit:Dot(fwd)
                        if dotFwd > 0.45 then
                            count = count + 1
                            local lat = rel:Dot(right)
                            lateralBias = lateralBias + (lat >= 0 and 1 or -1)
                            if d < closestDist then
                                closestDist = d
                            end
                        end
                        if checked >= maxCheck then
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            local evadeDir = nil
            if count > 0 then
                evadeDir = (lateralBias >= 0) and (-right + fwd * 0.35).Unit or (right + fwd * 0.35).Unit
            end

            self._cachedDefThreat = {
                Count = count,
                ClosestDist = closestDist,
                EvadeDir = evadeDir,
            }
            return self._cachedDefThreat
        end

        function StrikerController:TriggerFakeTrickCombo(actorsSnapshot)
            local now = os.clock()
            if self._match:IsGoalkeeper() or not self._ball:IsLocalOwner() then
                return false
            end
            if self._fakeTrickStage or now < self._fakeTrickCooldownUntil then
                return false
            end

            local cfg = self._store:Get("Striker")
            local snap = actorsSnapshot or self._player:GetAllActorsSnapshot()
            local sol = self._engines.GoalSolver:GetLastSolution()
            local threat = self:_scanDefendersAhead(cfg, snap, sol and sol.AimDirection)

            local mode = cfg.FakeTrickMode or "SmartAuto"
            if mode == "FlickFake" or (mode == "SmartAuto" and threat.Count >= 2 and self._player:CanUseRainbowFlick()) then
                self._fakeTrickCooldownUntil = now + 1.4
                return self:TriggerFlickCombo()
            end

            local started = self._dispatcher:TriggerFakeShotBait(0.09)
            if started then
                self._fakeTrickStage = "Baiting"
                self._fakeTrickUntil = now + 0.14
                self._fakeTrickEvadeDir = threat.EvadeDir
                self._fakeTrickCooldownUntil = now + 1.25
                self._lastStatus = "FakeTrick: Shot Bait"
                return true
            elseif threat.EvadeDir and self._dispatcher:TriggerLateralDodgeFeint(threat.EvadeDir) then
                self._fakeTrickStage = "Feinting"
                self._fakeTrickUntil = now + 0.24
                self._fakeTrickCooldownUntil = now + 1.15
                self._lastStatus = "FakeTrick: Dodge Feint"
                return true
            end

            return false
        end

        function StrikerController:GetStatus()
            if self._store:Get("Striker.AutoDribble") == true and self._dmcDribble then
                local ds = self._dmcDribble:GetStatus()
                if ds == "DRIBBLING" or ds == "NATIVE BLOCKED" then
                    return "Dribble: " .. ds
                end
            end
            return self._lastStatus
        end

        function StrikerController:Step(actorsSnapshot)
            if self._match:IsGoalkeeper() or self._match:IsKickoffFrozen() then
                self._flickComboActive = false
                self._fakeTrickStage = nil
                self._lastStatus = self._match:IsGoalkeeper() and "GK Role" or "Kickoff"
                return
            end

            if self._player:IsRagdolled() or self._player:IsStunned() then
                self._flickComboActive = false
                self._fakeTrickStage = nil
                self._lastStatus = "Ragdolled/Stunned"
                return
            end

            local cfg = self._store:Get("Striker")
            local visCfg = self._store:Get("Visuals")
            local perfOn = self._store:Get("Meta.PerformanceMode") == true
            local ballSnap = self._ball:GetSnapshot()
            local now = os.clock()

            if cfg.PassLaneRadar and ballSnap.IsLocalOwner and (now - self._lastPassScanAt > 0.10) then
                self._engines.PassLane:Evaluate(actorsSnapshot)
                self._lastPassScanAt = now
            end

            local isCharging = self._player:IsChargingKickOrPass()

            -- Aimbot Goal (TargetTrackingCorrection) saat charge manual
            if cfg.TargetTrackingCorrection and isCharging and ballSnap.IsLocalOwner and not self._dispatcher:IsShooting() then
                local trackPocket = cfg.TrackingPlacement or "Smart"
                local targetData = self._engines.GoalSolver:SolveCustomTargetPlacement(trackPocket)
                if targetData and targetData.TargetPosition then
                    self._dispatcher:AimCameraAtPoint(targetData.TargetPosition, cfg.TrackingSmoothness or 0.20)
                end
            end

            -- Proses lanjutan Fake Trick State Machine
            if self._fakeTrickStage then
                if not ballSnap.IsLocalOwner then
                    self._fakeTrickStage = nil
                elseif now >= self._fakeTrickUntil then
                    if self._fakeTrickStage == "Baiting" then
                        local mode = cfg.FakeTrickMode or "SmartAuto"
                        if (mode == "SmartAuto" or mode == "LateralDodge") and self._fakeTrickEvadeDir then
                            if self._dispatcher:TriggerLateralDodgeFeint(self._fakeTrickEvadeDir) then
                                self._fakeTrickStage = "Feinting"
                                self._fakeTrickUntil = now + 0.22
                                self._lastStatus = "FakeTrick: Side Feint"
                                return
                            end
                        end
                        self._fakeTrickStage = "ReadyStrike"
                    elseif self._fakeTrickStage == "Feinting" then
                        if not self._player:IsDribbling() then
                            self._fakeTrickStage = "ReadyStrike"
                        else
                            return
                        end
                    end

                    if self._fakeTrickStage == "ReadyStrike" then
                        self._fakeTrickStage = nil
                        local freshSol = self._engines.GoalSolver:SolveBestShot(cfg, actorsSnapshot, { Force = true })
                        if freshSol and not freshSol.Blocked and self._dispatcher:ExecuteShot(freshSol, false) then
                            self._lastStatus = "FakeTrick -> Strike!"
                            return
                        end
                    end
                else
                    return
                end
            end

            local volleyEval = nil
            if (cfg.AutoVolley or self._flickComboActive) and not ballSnap.IsLocalOwner then
                volleyEval = self._engines.Volley:Evaluate(cfg)
            end

            local needOffBallMarker = (not perfOn) and visCfg and visCfg.ShowGoalTargetMarker
            local monkeyPatchOn = self._store:Get("Meta.MonkeyPatchMode") == true
            local trajCorrectionOn = cfg.TargetTrajectoryCorrection == true
            local shouldSolvePreview = ballSnap.IsLocalOwner
                or (self._flickComboActive and volleyEval ~= nil)
                or (cfg.AutoVolley and volleyEval ~= nil and volleyEval.IsAvailable)
                or needOffBallMarker
                or (trajCorrectionOn and isCharging)

            local shotSolution = nil
            if shouldSolvePreview then
                local skipCal = not (ballSnap.IsLocalOwner or monkeyPatchOn or trajCorrectionOn)
                if perfOn and not cfg.AutoShoot and not monkeyPatchOn and not trajCorrectionOn then
                    skipCal = true
                end
                shotSolution = self._engines.GoalSolver:SolveBestShot(cfg, actorsSnapshot, {
                    MaxAge = ballSnap.IsLocalOwner and (perfOn and 0.10 or 0.08) or 0.18,
                    SkipCalibration = skipCal,
                })
            end

            if self._flickComboActive then
                if now > self._flickComboExpiresAt or (ballSnap.OwnerUserId and not ballSnap.IsLocalOwner) then
                    self._flickComboActive = false
                elseif not ballSnap.IsLocalOwner and (now - self._flickTriggeredAt >= 0.14) then
                    if volleyEval then
                        if volleyEval.IsLanded and cfg.AllowBicycleShot then
                            self._dispatcher:TriggerJump()
                            self._lastStatus = "FlickCombo: Jump"
                        end
                        if not isCharging and not self._dispatcher:IsShooting() then
                            local vSol = self._engines.GoalSolver:SolveBestShot(cfg, actorsSnapshot, { Force = true })
                            if vSol and self._dispatcher:ExecuteShot(vSol, true) then
                                self._flickComboActive = false
                                self._lastStatus = "FlickCombo: BicycleShot!"
                                return
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            if cfg.AutoVolley and not ballSnap.IsLocalOwner then
                if volleyEval and volleyEval.IsAvailable then
                    local distOk = true
                    if shotSolution and shotSolution.GoalMouth then
                        local maxVDist = math.min(cfg.VolleyMaxDistance or 85, 100)
                        distOk = shotSolution.Zone.Distance <= maxVDist
                    end

                    if distOk and not isCharging and not self._dispatcher:IsShooting() then
                        if volleyEval.RecommendedAction == "JumpForBicycle" then
                            self._dispatcher:TriggerJump()
                            self._lastStatus = "AutoVolley: Jumping"
                            return
                        elseif volleyEval.RecommendedAction == "Headbutt" then
                            local headSol = self._engines.GoalSolver:SolveBestShot({
                                ShotPlacement = cfg.VolleyPlacement or cfg.ShotPlacement,
                                PowerMode = "Adaptive",
                                AutoCurveBypass = false,
                                HardCapDistance = 100,
                                MinShootDistance = 0,
                                MaxShootDistance = 100,
                                RangeMode = "Custom",
                            }, actorsSnapshot, { Force = true })
                            if headSol then
                                self._dispatcher:AimCameraAtPoint(headSol.TargetPosition, 0)
                            end
                            if self._dispatcher:TriggerHeadbutt() then
                                self._lastStatus = "AutoHeadbutt!"
                                return
                            end
                        else
                            local vSol = self._engines.GoalSolver:SolveBestShot({
                                ShotPlacement = cfg.VolleyPlacement or cfg.ShotPlacement,
                                PowerMode = cfg.PowerMode,
                                ManualPowerPercent = cfg.ManualPowerPercent,
                                AutoCurveBypass = cfg.AutoCurveBypass,
                                AntiGKMinMarginStuds = cfg.AntiGKMinMarginStuds,
                                HardCapDistance = 100,
                                MinShootDistance = 0,
                                MaxShootDistance = cfg.VolleyMaxDistance or 85,
                                RangeMode = "Custom",
                            }, actorsSnapshot, { Force = true })
                            if vSol and self._dispatcher:ExecuteShot(vSol, true) then
                                self._lastStatus = "AutoVolley: " .. volleyEval.RecommendedAction
                                return
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            -- Auto Shoot dengan Slider Jarak Khusus Bicycle Kick + Multi-Defender Check + Fake Trick
            if cfg.AutoShoot and ballSnap.IsLocalOwner and shotSolution and not self._flickComboActive then
                if not self._player:IsDribbling() and not isCharging and not self._dispatcher:IsShooting() then
                    local mode = cfg.ShotMode or "SmartAuto"
                    local dist = shotSolution.Zone and shotSolution.Zone.Distance or 999

                    -- Pengecekan Slider Jarak Khusus jika Mode = FlickBicycle (Salto Otomatis)
                    if mode == "FlickBicycle" then
                        local minBike = cfg.BicycleAutoShootMinDist or 16
                        local maxBike = cfg.BicycleAutoShootMaxDist or 55
                        if dist < minBike then
                            self._lastStatus = string.format("Bike Hold (< %dst)", minBike)
                            return
                        elseif dist > maxBike then
                            self._lastStatus = string.format("Bike Hold (> %dst)", maxBike)
                            return
                        end
                    end

                    if shotSolution.Zone.InRange then
                        local defThreat = self:_scanDefendersAhead(cfg, actorsSnapshot, shotSolution.AimDirection)

                        -- Jika ada bek dekat atau multi-defender muncul di jalur tembak
                        if defThreat.Count > 0 and defThreat.ClosestDist <= (cfg.FakeTrickDefenderRadius or 13) then
                            if cfg.FakeTrickEnabled and now >= self._fakeTrickCooldownUntil then
                                if self:TriggerFakeTrickCombo(actorsSnapshot) then
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        if cfg.AntiBlockGate and (shotSolution.Blocked or defThreat.Count >= 2) then
                            if shotSolution.BlockKind ~= "Barricade" then
                                if cfg.FakeTrickEnabled and now >= self._fakeTrickCooldownUntil then
                                    if self:TriggerFakeTrickCombo(actorsSnapshot) then
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                                self._lastStatus = defThreat.Count >= 2
                                    and "Held (Multi-Def Blocked)"
                                    or ("Held (" .. tostring(shotSolution.BlockKind) .. " blocking)")
                                return
                            end
                        end

                        if cfg.AntiGKReachCheck and shotSolution.GKSaveMargin and shotSolution.GKSaveMargin < 1.5 and dist > 20 then
                            self._lastStatus = string.format("Held (GK Covering %.1fst)", shotSolution.GKSaveMargin)
                            return
                        end

                        if mode == "FlickBicycle" then
                            if self:TriggerFlickCombo() then
                                return
                            end
                        elseif mode == "SmartAuto" then
                            local defenderAhead = shotSolution.BlockKind == "Defender" or defThreat.Count >= 1
                            if defenderAhead and dist > (cfg.QuickSnapDistance or 18) and self._player:CanUseRainbowFlick() then
                                if self:TriggerFlickCombo() then
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        local freshSol = self._engines.GoalSolver:SolveBestShot(cfg, actorsSnapshot, { Force = true })
                        if freshSol then
                            if cfg.AntiBlockGate and freshSol.Blocked and freshSol.BlockKind ~= "Barricade" then
                                self._lastStatus = "Held (Path Changed)"
                                return
                            end
                            if self._dispatcher:ExecuteShot(freshSol, false) then
                                self._lastStatus = string.format("Shoot %s (%.0fst | +%.1f)",
                                    tostring(freshSol.TargetLabel or cfg.ShotPlacement),
                                    freshSol.Zone.Distance,
                                    freshSol.GKSaveMargin or 0
                                )
                                return
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        self._lastStatus = shotSolution.Zone.Reason or "OutOfRange"
                    end
                end
            elseif shotSolution then
                if not shotSolution.Zone.InRange then
                    self._lastStatus = shotSolution.Zone.Zone
                elseif shotSolution.Blocked then
                    self._lastStatus = "Lane Blocked"
                elseif shotSolution.IsGKReachable then
                    self._lastStatus = string.format("Tight Corner (%.0fst)", shotSolution.Zone.Distance)
                else
                    self._lastStatus = string.format("Open Corner (%.0fst)", shotSolution.Zone.Distance)
                end
            elseif cfg.AutoDribble and self._dmcDribble then
                self._lastStatus = "Dribble: " .. tostring(self._dmcDribble:GetStatus())
            else
                self._lastStatus = "Idle"
            end
        end

        return StrikerController
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Visuals/TrajectoryOverlay3D (Object-Pooled & Potato-Friendly)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Visuals/TrajectoryOverlay3D", function(Require)
        local Janitor = Require("Core/Janitor")

        local TrajectoryOverlay3D = {}
        TrajectoryOverlay3D.__index = TrajectoryOverlay3D

        local MAX_SEGMENTS = 32
        local MAX_BOUNCES = 3

        function TrajectoryOverlay3D.new(store, matchBridge, ballBridge, playerBridge, engines)
            local self = setmetatable({
                _janitor = Janitor.new(),
                _store = store,
                _match = matchBridge,
                _ball = ballBridge,
                _player = playerBridge,
                _engines = engines,
                _folder = nil,
                _segments = {},
                _bounceRings = {},
                _landingRing = nil,
                _targetMarker = nil,
                _gkThreatMarker = nil,
                _gkDiveBeam = nil,
                _usedSegments = 0,
                _usedBounces = 0,
                _allHidden = true,
                _lastArcRef = nil,
                _lastArcColor = nil,
            }, TrajectoryOverlay3D)
            self:_initPool()
            return self
        end

        function TrajectoryOverlay3D:_makePart(name, shape, size, color)
            local p = Instance.new("Part")
            p.Name = name
            p.Shape = shape
            p.Size = size
            p.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
            p.Color = color
            p.Transparency = 1
            p.Anchored = true
            p.CanCollide = false
            p.CanQuery = false
            p.CanTouch = false
            p.CastShadow = false
            p.Parent = self._folder
            return p
        end

        function TrajectoryOverlay3D:_initPool()
            local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
            folder.Name = "LouisHub_3DOverlay"
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            folder.Parent = cam or workspace
            self._folder = folder
            self._janitor:Add(folder, "Destroy")

            for i = 1, MAX_SEGMENTS do
                self._segments[i] = self:_makePart("ArcSeg_" .. i, Enum.PartType.Block, Vector3.new(0.14, 0.14, 1), Color3.fromRGB(64, 196, 255))
            end
            for i = 1, MAX_BOUNCES do
                local ring = self:_makePart("Bounce_" .. i, Enum.PartType.Cylinder, Vector3.new(0.1, 2.2, 2.2), Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 64))
                self._bounceRings[i] = ring
            end
            self._landingRing = self:_makePart("LandingRing", Enum.PartType.Cylinder, Vector3.new(0.12, 3.4, 3.4), Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 140))
            self._targetMarker = self:_makePart("GoalTarget", Enum.PartType.Ball, Vector3.new(1.5, 1.5, 1.5), Color3.fromRGB(64, 255, 160))
            self._gkThreatMarker = self:_makePart("GkThreat", Enum.PartType.Ball, Vector3.new(1.8, 1.8, 1.8), Color3.fromRGB(255, 72, 72))
            self._gkDiveBeam = self:_makePart("GkDiveDir", Enum.PartType.Block, Vector3.new(0.22, 0.14, 8), Color3.fromRGB(72, 240, 160))
        end

        function TrajectoryOverlay3D:_hideUnusedArc(usedSegs, usedBounces)
            for i = usedSegs + 1, self._usedSegments do
                self._segments[i].Transparency = 1
            end
            self._usedSegments = usedSegs

            for i = usedBounces + 1, self._usedBounces do
                self._bounceRings[i].Transparency = 1
            end
            self._usedBounces = usedBounces
        end

        function TrajectoryOverlay3D:_hideAll()
            if self._allHidden then
                return
            end
            self._allHidden = true
            self._lastArcRef = nil
            self:_hideUnusedArc(0, 0)
            if self._landingRing.Transparency ~= 1 then
                self._landingRing.Transparency = 1
            end
            if self._targetMarker.Transparency ~= 1 then
                self._targetMarker.Transparency = 1
            end
            if self._gkThreatMarker.Transparency ~= 1 then
                self._gkThreatMarker.Transparency = 1
            end
            if self._gkDiveBeam.Transparency ~= 1 then
                self._gkDiveBeam.Transparency = 1
            end
        end

        function TrajectoryOverlay3D:_renderArcPoints(trajRef, points, color)
            if not points or #points < 2 then
                self._lastArcRef = nil
                self:_hideUnusedArc(0, self._usedBounces)
                return
            end
            if self._lastArcRef == trajRef and self._lastArcColor == color then
                return
            end
            self._lastArcRef = trajRef
            self._lastArcColor = color

            local used = 0
            local stride = math.max(1, math.floor((#points - 1) / MAX_SEGMENTS) + ((#points - 1) % MAX_SEGMENTS > 0 and 1 or 0))
            local idx = 1
            while idx < #points and used < MAX_SEGMENTS do
                local nextIdx = math.min(#points, idx + stride)
                local a = points[idx]
                local b = points[nextIdx]
                local dist = (b - a).Magnitude
                if dist > 0.05 then
                    used = used + 1
                    local seg = self._segments[used]
                    seg.Color = color
                    seg.Size = Vector3.new(0.13, 0.13, dist)
                    seg.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt((a + b) * 0.5, b)
                    seg.Transparency = 0.25
                end
                idx = nextIdx
            end
            self:_hideUnusedArc(used, self._usedBounces)
        end

        function TrajectoryOverlay3D:Render()
            local visCfg = self._store:Get("Visuals")
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if self._folder and cam and self._folder.Parent ~= cam then
                self._folder.Parent = cam
            end

            if not visCfg or not visCfg.Trajectory3DEnabled then
                self:_hideAll()
                return
            end

            local perfOn = self._store:Get("Meta.PerformanceMode") == true
            self._allHidden = false

            local ac = visCfg.AccentColor or { R = 64, G = 196, B = 255 }
            local accentColor = Color3.fromRGB(ac.R, ac.G, ac.B)
            local isGk = self._match:IsGoalkeeper()
            local ballSnap = self._ball:GetSnapshot()

            if isGk then
                if self._targetMarker.Transparency ~= 1 then
                    self._targetMarker.Transparency = 1
                end
                local analysis = self._engines.Goalkeeper:GetLastAnalysis()
                local showThreat = false
                local showBeam = false

                if analysis then
                    local threat = analysis.IncomingThreat or analysis.PreShotThreat
                    if threat and threat.CrossingPosition then
                        self._gkThreatMarker.Position = threat.CrossingPosition
                        self._gkThreatMarker.Transparency = 0.2
                        showThreat = true
                    end
                    local root = self._player:GetRoot()
                    if not perfOn and visCfg.ShowDebugOverlays and root and analysis.DiveAdvice and analysis.DiveAdvice.WorldDirection then
                        local dir = analysis.DiveAdvice.WorldDirection
                        local len = math.clamp(analysis.DiveAdvice.MissDistance or 10, 4, 14)
                        self._gkDiveBeam.Size = Vector3.new(0.22, 0.12, len)
                        self._gkDiveBeam.Color = analysis.DiveAdvice.AssistActive and Color3.fromRGB(70, 255, 160) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 75)
                        local pStart = root.Position + Vector3.new(0, -1.5, 0)
                        self._gkDiveBeam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(pStart + dir * (len * 0.5), pStart + dir * len)
                        self._gkDiveBeam.Transparency = 0.35
                        showBeam = true
                    end
                end

                if not showThreat and self._gkThreatMarker.Transparency ~= 1 then
                    self._gkThreatMarker.Transparency = 1
                end
                if not showBeam and self._gkDiveBeam.Transparency ~= 1 then
                    self._gkDiveBeam.Transparency = 1
                end

                if not perfOn and ballSnap.State and (ballSnap.Mode == "Airborne" or ballSnap.Mode == "Rolling") then
                    local traj = self._engines.Physics:SampleTrajectory(
                        ballSnap.State,
                        ballSnap.BoundaryWorld,
                        1.8,
                        0.05,
                        visCfg.TrajectoryMaxBounces or 2
                    )
                    if traj then
                        local bCount = math.min(#traj.Bounces, MAX_BOUNCES)
                        for i = 1, bCount do
                            local b = traj.Bounces[i]
                            local ring = self._bounceRings[i]
                            ring.CFrame = CFrame.new(b.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi * 0.5)
                            ring.Transparency = 0.3
                        end
                        self:_hideUnusedArc(self._usedSegments, bCount)
                        self:_renderArcPoints(traj, traj.Points, Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 90))
                        if visCfg.ShowReboundMarker and traj.Landing then
                            self._landingRing.CFrame = CFrame.new(traj.Landing.EffectPosition) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi * 0.5)
                            self._landingRing.Transparency = 0.25
                        elseif self._landingRing.Transparency ~= 1 then
                            self._landingRing.Transparency = 1
                        end
                        return
                    end
                end

                self._lastArcRef = nil
                self:_hideUnusedArc(0, 0)
                if self._landingRing.Transparency ~= 1 then
                    self._landingRing.Transparency = 1
                end
                return
            end

            -- Striker mode
            if self._gkThreatMarker.Transparency ~= 1 then
                self._gkThreatMarker.Transparency = 1
            end
            if self._gkDiveBeam.Transparency ~= 1 then
                self._gkDiveBeam.Transparency = 1
            end

            local shotSol = self._engines.GoalSolver:GetLastSolution()
            local showTarget = visCfg.ShowGoalTargetMarker and shotSol and shotSol.TargetPosition and (not perfOn or ballSnap.IsLocalOwner)
            if showTarget then
                local markerPos = shotSol.CrossingPosition or shotSol.TargetPosition
                self._targetMarker.Position = markerPos
                if shotSol.Blocked then
                    self._targetMarker.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90)
                elseif not shotSol.Zone.InRange then
                    self._targetMarker.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 75)
                elseif shotSol.IsGKReachable then
                    self._targetMarker.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 175, 65)
                else
                    self._targetMarker.Color = Color3.fromRGB(70, 255, 150)
                end
                self._targetMarker.Transparency = 0.22
            elseif self._targetMarker.Transparency ~= 1 then
                self._targetMarker.Transparency = 1
            end

            if perfOn then
                self._lastArcRef = nil
                self:_hideUnusedArc(0, 0)
                if self._landingRing.Transparency ~= 1 then
                    self._landingRing.Transparency = 1
                end
                return
            end

            if ballSnap.IsLocalOwner and self._store:Get("Striker.CurveGuide") and shotSol and shotSol.VirtualState then
                local traj = self._engines.Physics:SampleTrajectory(
                    shotSol.VirtualState,
                    ballSnap.BoundaryWorld,
                    1.6,
                    0.05,
                    visCfg.TrajectoryMaxBounces or 2
                )
                if traj then
                    local arcColor = (shotSol.Curve and shotSol.Curve ~= 0) and Color3.fromRGB(180, 120, 255) or accentColor
                    self:_hideUnusedArc(self._usedSegments, 0)
                    self:_renderArcPoints(traj, traj.Points, arcColor)
                    if self._landingRing.Transparency ~= 1 then
                        self._landingRing.Transparency = 1
                    end
                    return
                end
            elseif not ballSnap.IsLocalOwner and ballSnap.State and (ballSnap.Mode == "Airborne" or ballSnap.Mode == "Rolling") then
                local traj = self._engines.Physics:SampleTrajectory(
                    ballSnap.State,
                    ballSnap.BoundaryWorld,
                    1.8,
                    0.05,
                    visCfg.TrajectoryMaxBounces or 2
                )
                if traj then
                    self:_hideUnusedArc(self._usedSegments, 0)
                    self:_renderArcPoints(traj, traj.Points, accentColor)
                    if visCfg.ShowReboundMarker and traj.Landing then
                        self._landingRing.CFrame = CFrame.new(traj.Landing.EffectPosition) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi * 0.5)
                        self._landingRing.Transparency = 0.3
                    elseif self._landingRing.Transparency ~= 1 then
                        self._landingRing.Transparency = 1
                    end
                    return
                end
            end

            self._lastArcRef = nil
            self:_hideUnusedArc(0, 0)
            if self._landingRing.Transparency ~= 1 then
                self._landingRing.Transparency = 1
            end
        end

        function TrajectoryOverlay3D:Destroy()
            self._janitor:Destroy()
        end

        return TrajectoryOverlay3D
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Visuals/WorldIndicators
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Visuals/WorldIndicators", function(Require)
        local Janitor = Require("Core/Janitor")

        local WorldIndicators = {}
        WorldIndicators.__index = WorldIndicators

        function WorldIndicators.new(store, matchBridge, ballBridge, playerBridge, engines, rootGui)
            local self = setmetatable({
                _janitor = Janitor.new(),
                _store = store,
                _match = matchBridge,
                _ball = ballBridge,
                _player = playerBridge,
                _engines = engines,
                _rootGui = rootGui,
                _passBillboard = nil,
                _passLabel = nil,
                _lastPassText = "",
                _tackleRing = nil,
                _gkBanner = nil,
                _gkBannerText = nil,
                _lastBannerText = "",
            }, WorldIndicators)
            self:_initVisuals()
            return self
        end

        function WorldIndicators:_initVisuals()
            local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            bb.Name = "LouisHub_PassMarker"
            bb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(116, 28)
            bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 4.4, 0)
            bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
            bb.Enabled = false
            bb.Parent = self._rootGui

            local pill = Instance.new("Frame")
            pill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            pill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 22, 32)
            pill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
            pill.Parent = bb

            local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
            corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            corner.Parent = pill

            local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(70, 235, 160)
            stroke.Thickness = 1.5
            stroke.Parent = pill

            local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            lbl.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            lbl.TextSize = 11
            lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 255, 240)
            lbl.Text = "PASS"
            lbl.Parent = pill

            self._passBillboard = bb
            self._passLabel = lbl
            self._janitor:Add(bb, "Destroy")

            local ring = Instance.new("Part")
            ring.Name = "LouisHub_TackleRing"
            ring.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
            ring.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
            ring.Color = Color3.fromRGB(64, 196, 255)
            ring.Transparency = 1
            ring.Anchored = true
            ring.CanCollide = false
            ring.CanQuery = false
            ring.CanTouch = false
            ring.CastShadow = false
            ring.Parent = workspace.CurrentCamera or workspace
            self._tackleRing = ring
            self._janitor:Add(ring, "Destroy")

            local gkBanner = Instance.new("Frame")
            gkBanner.Name = "LouisHub_GKAdvisor"
            gkBanner.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
            gkBanner.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.16, 0)
            gkBanner.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(310, 32)
            gkBanner.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 16, 20)
            gkBanner.BackgroundTransparency = 0.16
            gkBanner.Visible = false
            gkBanner.Parent = self._rootGui

            local gkCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
            gkCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
            gkCorner.Parent = gkBanner

            local gkStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            gkStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 90)
            gkStroke.Thickness = 1.4
            gkStroke.Parent = gkBanner

            local gkTxt = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            gkTxt.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            gkTxt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            gkTxt.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            gkTxt.TextSize = 11
            gkTxt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 235, 235)
            gkTxt.Text = ""
            gkTxt.Parent = gkBanner

            self._gkBanner = gkBanner
            self._gkBannerText = gkTxt
            self._janitor:Add(gkBanner, "Destroy")
        end

        function WorldIndicators:Render()
            local isGk = self._match:IsGoalkeeper()
            local root = self._player:GetRoot()
            local perfOn = self._store:Get("Meta.PerformanceMode") == true

            if not isGk and self._store:Get("Striker.PassLaneRadar") and self._ball:IsLocalOwner() then
                local candidates = self._engines.PassLane:GetLastCandidates()
                local best = candidates and candidates[1]
                if best and best.Character and best.Character.Parent then
                    local targetRoot = best.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if targetRoot then
                        if self._passBillboard.Adornee ~= targetRoot then
                            self._passBillboard.Adornee = targetRoot
                        end
                        local passTxt = string.format("%s • %.0fst", best.RecommendedPassType == "LobPass" and "LOB" or "PASS", best.Distance)
                        if self._lastPassText ~= passTxt then
                            self._lastPassText = passTxt
                            self._passLabel.Text = passTxt
                        end
                        if not self._passBillboard.Enabled then
                            self._passBillboard.Enabled = true
                        end
                    elseif self._passBillboard.Enabled then
                        self._passBillboard.Enabled = false
                    end
                elseif self._passBillboard.Enabled then
                    self._passBillboard.Enabled = false
                end
            elseif self._passBillboard.Enabled then
                self._passBillboard.Enabled = false
            end

            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if cam and self._tackleRing.Parent ~= cam then
                self._tackleRing.Parent = cam
            end
            if not perfOn and not isGk and root and self._store:Get("Striker.TackleReachRing") and not self._ball:IsLocalOwner() then
                local _, maxR = self._engines.Tackle:GetReachLimits()
                local ballPos = self._ball:GetPosition()
                local inRange = ballPos and ((ballPos - root.Position) * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)).Magnitude <= maxR
                local targetSize = Vector3.new(0.08, maxR * 2, maxR * 2)
                if self._tackleRing.Size ~= targetSize then
                    self._tackleRing.Size = targetSize
                end
                self._tackleRing.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position.X, root.Position.Y - 2.6, root.Position.Z) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi * 0.5)
                self._tackleRing.Color = inRange and Color3.fromRGB(70, 255, 150) or Color3.fromRGB(64, 196, 255)
                self._tackleRing.Transparency = inRange and 0.68 or 0.86
            elseif self._tackleRing.Transparency ~= 1 then
                self._tackleRing.Transparency = 1
            end

            if isGk and self._store:Get("Goalkeeper.DiveAdvisor") then
                local analysis = self._engines.Goalkeeper:GetLastAnalysis()
                local dmcInfo = self._engines.DmcGK:GetTelemetryInfo()
                local bannerTxt = nil
                if analysis and analysis.IncomingThreat and analysis.DiveAdvice then
                    local adv = analysis.DiveAdvice
                    local tag = adv.NeedsJump and "JUMP" or ("DIVE " .. tostring(adv.DirectionName))
                    local assistTag = adv.AssistActive and "ASSIST LOCK" or "3D SWEPT"
                    bannerTxt = string.format("INBOUND %.2fs  |  %s  |  %s",
                        adv.OffsetSeconds or analysis.IncomingThreat.ETA,
                        tag,
                        assistTag
                    )
                elseif analysis and analysis.PreShotThreat and self._store:Get("Goalkeeper.PreShotThreatAlert") then
                    bannerTxt = string.format("CHARGING SHOT  |  ETA %.2fs  |  %.0f st/s",
                        analysis.PreShotThreat.ETA,
                        analysis.PreShotThreat.ShotSpeed
                    )
                elseif dmcInfo and (dmcInfo.DisplayStatus == "CLOSE-RANGE RUSH" or dmcInfo.DisplayStatus == "COVERING ANGLE" or dmcInfo.DisplayStatus == "CATCH OBSERVED") then
                    bannerTxt = string.format("DMC GK  |  %s", tostring(dmcInfo.DisplayStatus))
                end

                if bannerTxt then
                    if self._lastBannerText ~= bannerTxt then
                        self._lastBannerText = bannerTxt
                        self._gkBannerText.Text = bannerTxt
                    end
                    if not self._gkBanner.Visible then
                        self._gkBanner.Visible = true
                    end
                elseif self._gkBanner.Visible then
                    self._gkBanner.Visible = false
                end
            elseif self._gkBanner.Visible then
                self._gkBanner.Visible = false
            end
        end

        function WorldIndicators:Destroy()
            self._janitor:Destroy()
        end

        return WorldIndicators
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: Visuals/PitchRadar2D (Object-Pooled Tactical Radar)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("Visuals/PitchRadar2D", function(Require)
        local Janitor = Require("Core/Janitor")

        local PitchRadar2D = {}
        PitchRadar2D.__index = PitchRadar2D

        local MAX_PLAYER_DOTS = 24
        local MAX_BARRICADES = 12

        function PitchRadar2D.new(store, locale, matchBridge, ballBridge, playerBridge, engines, rootGui)
            local self = setmetatable({
                _janitor = Janitor.new(),
                _store = store,
                _locale = locale,
                _match = matchBridge,
                _ball = ballBridge,
                _player = playerBridge,
                _engines = engines,
                _rootGui = rootGui,
                _container = nil,
                _uiScale = nil,
                _titleLabel = nil,
                _pitchArea = nil,
                _dots = {},
                _barricades = {},
                _localDot = nil,
                _ballDot = nil,
                _landingDot = nil,
                _usedDots = 0,
                _usedBarricades = 0,
                _lastSize = -1,
                _lastOpacity = -1,
                _lastVpY = -1,
                _cachedTransform = nil,
                _transformAt = 0,
            }, PitchRadar2D)
            self:_buildUI()
            return self
        end

        function PitchRadar2D:_updateScale()
            if not self._uiScale then
                return
            end
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local vpY = cam and cam.ViewportSize.Y or 720
            if vpY == self._lastVpY then
                return
            end
            self._lastVpY = vpY
            local autoFit = (vpY < 480) and math.clamp(vpY / 480, 0.65, 1.0) or 1.0
            self._uiScale.Scale = autoFit
        end

        function PitchRadar2D:_buildUI()
            local size = self._store:Get("Visuals.RadarSize") or 156
            local savedPos = self._store:Get("UI.RadarPos")
            local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
            frame.Name = "LouisHub_PitchRadar"
            if type(savedPos) == "table" then
                frame.Position = UDim2.new(savedPos.XS or 0, savedPos.XO or 14, savedPos.YS or 0.5, savedPos.YO or -105)
            else
                frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, -size * 0.65)
            end
            frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, math.floor(size * 1.35))
            frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 16, 22)
            frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
            frame.Parent = self._rootGui

            local uiScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
            uiScale.Parent = frame
            self._uiScale = uiScale

            local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
            corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
            corner.Parent = frame

            local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(55, 72, 95)
            stroke.Thickness = 1.2
            stroke.Parent = frame

            local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
            title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            title.TextSize = 10
            title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(165, 200, 235)
            title.Text = self._locale and self._locale:T("RADAR_TITLE") or "LOUIS HUB RADAR"
            title.Parent = frame
            self._titleLabel = title

            self._janitor:Add(self._store:OnChanged("Meta.Language", function()
                if self._titleLabel and self._locale then
