                    self._titleLabel.Text = self._locale:T("RADAR_TITLE")
                end
            end))

            local pitch = Instance.new("Frame")
            pitch.Name = "Pitch"
            pitch.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 20)
            pitch.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -28)
            pitch.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 34, 28)
            pitch.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
            pitch.ClipsDescendants = true
            pitch.Parent = frame

            local pitchStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            pitchStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(70, 120, 95)
            pitchStroke.Thickness = 1
            pitchStroke.Parent = pitch

            local halfLine = Instance.new("Frame")
            halfLine.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
            halfLine.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5)
            halfLine.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
            halfLine.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 135, 105)
            halfLine.BorderSizePixel = 0
            halfLine.Parent = pitch

            local function makeDot(dotName, pxSize, color, zIdx)
                local d = Instance.new("Frame")
                d.Name = dotName
                d.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                d.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(pxSize, pxSize)
                d.BackgroundColor3 = color
                d.BorderSizePixel = 0
                d.Visible = false
                d.ZIndex = zIdx or 2
                d.Parent = pitch
                local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
                c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
                c.Parent = d
                return d
            end

            for i = 1, MAX_PLAYER_DOTS do
                self._dots[i] = makeDot("Actor_" .. i, 7, Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90), 3)
            end
            for i = 1, MAX_BARRICADES do
                local b = Instance.new("Frame")
                b.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                b.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 4)
                b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 160, 50)
                b.BorderSizePixel = 0
                b.Visible = false
                b.ZIndex = 2
                b.Parent = pitch
                self._barricades[i] = b
            end

            self._localDot = makeDot("Local", 9, Color3.fromRGB(64, 225, 255), 5)
            self._ballDot = makeDot("Ball", 8, Color3.fromRGB(255, 235, 70), 6)
            self._landingDot = makeDot("Landing", 6, Color3.fromRGB(90, 255, 150), 4)

            local dragging = false
            local dragInput, dragStart, startPos
            self._janitor:Connect(frame.InputBegan, function(input)
                if self._store:Get("UI.HudLocked") then
                    return
                end
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragging = true
                    dragInput = input
                    dragStart = input.Position
                    startPos = frame.Position
                end
            end)
            self._janitor:Connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
                if not dragging then
                    return
                end
                if input == dragInput or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                    local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                    frame.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
                end
            end)
            self._janitor:Connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
                if dragging and (input == dragInput or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    dragging = false
                    dragInput = nil
                    local p = frame.Position
                    self._store:Set("UI.RadarPos", {
                        XS = p.X.Scale,
                        XO = p.X.Offset,
                        YS = p.Y.Scale,
                        YO = p.Y.Offset,
                    })
                end
            end)

            self._container = frame
            self._pitchArea = pitch
            self._janitor:Add(frame, "Destroy")
            self:_updateScale()
        end

        function PitchRadar2D:_getPitchTransform()
            local now = os.clock()
            local myTeam = self._match:GetLocalTeamName()
            if self._cachedTransform and (now - self._transformAt) < 2.0 and self._cachedTransform.Team == myTeam then
                local t = self._cachedTransform
                return t.Center, t.Right, t.Fwd, t.HalfW, t.HalfL
            end

            local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
            local data = map and map:FindFirstChild("Data")
            local t1Goal = data and data:FindFirstChild("Team1") and data.Team1:FindFirstChild("Goal")
            local t2Goal = data and data:FindFirstChild("Team2") and data.Team2:FindFirstChild("Goal")

            local center, right, fwd, halfW, halfL
            if t1Goal and t2Goal then
                local p1 = t1Goal.Position
                local p2 = t2Goal.Position
                center = (p1 + p2) * 0.5
                local axisZ = Vector3.new(p2.X - p1.X, 0, p2.Z - p1.Z)
                halfL = math.max(80, axisZ.Magnitude * 0.52)
                fwd = axisZ.Magnitude > 1e-4 and axisZ.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
                right = Vector3.new(-fwd.Z, 0, fwd.X)
                if myTeam == "Team2" then
                    fwd = -fwd
                    right = -right
                end
                halfW = halfL * 0.72
            else
                center = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                right = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
                fwd = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
                halfW = 130
                halfL = 190
            end

            self._cachedTransform = {
                Team = myTeam,
                Center = center,
                Right = right,
                Fwd = fwd,
                HalfW = halfW,
                HalfL = halfL,
            }
            self._transformAt = now
            return center, right, fwd, halfW, halfL
        end

        function PitchRadar2D:_project(worldPos, center, right, fwd, halfW, halfL)
            local rel = Vector3.new(worldPos.X - center.X, 0, worldPos.Z - center.Z)
            local nx = math.clamp(0.5 + (rel:Dot(right) / (halfW * 2)), 0.03, 0.97)
            local ny = math.clamp(0.5 - (rel:Dot(fwd) / (halfL * 2)), 0.03, 0.97)
            return UDim2.fromScale(nx, ny)
        end

        function PitchRadar2D:Render(actorsSnapshot)
            local enabled = self._store:Get("Visuals.Radar2DEnabled") == true
            if self._container.Visible ~= enabled then
                self._container.Visible = enabled
            end
            if not enabled then
                return
            end

            self:_updateScale()
            local size = self._store:Get("Visuals.RadarSize") or 156
            if self._lastSize ~= size then
                self._lastSize = size
                self._container.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, math.floor(size * 1.35))
            end

            local opacity = math.clamp(self._store:Get("Visuals.RadarOpacity") or 0.85, 0.2, 1)
            if self._lastOpacity ~= opacity then
                self._lastOpacity = opacity
                self._container.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - opacity
            end

            local perfOn = self._store:Get("Meta.PerformanceMode") == true
            local center, right, fwd, halfW, halfL = self:_getPitchTransform()

            local dotIdx = 0
            if actorsSnapshot then
                for _, mate in ipairs(actorsSnapshot.Teammates or {}) do
                    dotIdx = dotIdx + 1
                    if dotIdx <= MAX_PLAYER_DOTS then
                        local d = self._dots[dotIdx]
                        d.Position = self:_project(mate.Position, center, right, fwd, halfW, halfL)
                        d.BackgroundColor3 = mate.IsGoalkeeper and Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 75) or Color3.fromRGB(70, 210, 255)
                        if not d.Visible then d.Visible = true end
                    end
                end
                for _, enemy in ipairs(actorsSnapshot.Enemies or {}) do
                    dotIdx = dotIdx + 1
                    if dotIdx <= MAX_PLAYER_DOTS then
                        local d = self._dots[dotIdx]
                        d.Position = self:_project(enemy.Position, center, right, fwd, halfW, halfL)
                        d.BackgroundColor3 = enemy.IsSliding and Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 220)
                            or (enemy.IsGoalkeeper and Color3.fromRGB(255, 165, 50) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 85, 85))
                        if not d.Visible then d.Visible = true end
                    end
                end
            end

            local clampedDots = math.min(dotIdx, MAX_PLAYER_DOTS)
            for i = clampedDots + 1, self._usedDots do
                self._dots[i].Visible = false
            end
            self._usedDots = clampedDots

            local root = self._player:GetRoot()
            if root then
                self._localDot.Position = self:_project(root.Position, center, right, fwd, halfW, halfL)
                if not self._localDot.Visible then self._localDot.Visible = true end
            elseif self._localDot.Visible then
                self._localDot.Visible = false
            end

            local ballSnap = self._ball:GetSnapshot()
            if ballSnap.Position then
                self._ballDot.Position = self:_project(ballSnap.Position, center, right, fwd, halfW, halfL)
                if not self._ballDot.Visible then self._ballDot.Visible = true end
            elseif self._ballDot.Visible then
                self._ballDot.Visible = false
            end

            local showLanding = false
            if not perfOn and ballSnap.State and not ballSnap.IsLocalOwner and (ballSnap.Mode == "Airborne" or ballSnap.Mode == "Rolling") then
                local traj = self._engines.Physics:SampleTrajectory(ballSnap.State, ballSnap.BoundaryWorld, 1.5, 0.08, 1)
                if traj and traj.Landing then
                    self._landingDot.Position = self:_project(traj.Landing.Position, center, right, fwd, halfW, halfL)
                    showLanding = true
                end
            end
            if self._landingDot.Visible ~= showLanding then
                self._landingDot.Visible = showLanding
            end

            local bUsed = 0
            if not perfOn then
                local barricades = self._engines.GoalSolver:GetActiveBarricadeParts()
                bUsed = math.min(#barricades, MAX_BARRICADES)
                for i = 1, bUsed do
                    local part = barricades[i]
                    if part and part.Parent then
                        local b = self._barricades[i]
                        b.Position = self:_project(part.Position, center, right, fwd, halfW, halfL)
                        if not b.Visible then b.Visible = true end
                    end
                end
            end
            for i = bUsed + 1, self._usedBarricades do
                self._barricades[i].Visible = false
            end
            self._usedBarricades = bUsed
        end

        function PitchRadar2D:Destroy()
            self._janitor:Destroy()
        end

        return PitchRadar2D
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- BOOTSTRAP PART 9
    --========================================================================
    local ctx = LouisHub.Context
    local rootGui = createExecutorGui("LouisHub_Root")
    ctx.RootGui = rootGui

    local DmcAutoDribbleClass = LouisHub.Require("Automation/DmcAutoDribble")
    local StrikerControllerClass = LouisHub.Require("Automation/StrikerController")
    local TrajectoryOverlay3DClass = LouisHub.Require("Visuals/TrajectoryOverlay3D")
    local WorldIndicatorsClass = LouisHub.Require("Visuals/WorldIndicators")
    local PitchRadar2DClass = LouisHub.Require("Visuals/PitchRadar2D")

    local dmcDribble = DmcAutoDribbleClass.new(ctx.Store, ctx.HookGuard)
    local strikerController = StrikerControllerClass.new(
        ctx.Store,
        ctx.Match,
        ctx.Ball,
        ctx.Player,
        ctx.Engines,
        ctx.Automation.Dispatcher,
        dmcDribble
    )

    local trajectoryOverlay3D = TrajectoryOverlay3DClass.new(ctx.Store, ctx.Match, ctx.Ball, ctx.Player, ctx.Engines)
    local worldIndicators = WorldIndicatorsClass.new(ctx.Store, ctx.Match, ctx.Ball, ctx.Player, ctx.Engines, rootGui)
    local pitchRadar2D = PitchRadar2DClass.new(ctx.Store, ctx.Locale, ctx.Match, ctx.Ball, ctx.Player, ctx.Engines, rootGui)

    ctx.Automation.Dribble = dmcDribble
    ctx.Automation.Striker = strikerController

    ctx.Visuals = {
        Trajectory3D = trajectoryOverlay3D,
        Indicators = worldIndicators,
        Radar2D = pitchRadar2D,
    }

    local latestActorsSnapshot = { Teammates = {}, Enemies = {}, EnemyGoalkeeper = nil }
    local lastActorScan = 0

    ctx.Scheduler:AddHeartbeat("LouisHub_Automation", 0, function(dt)
        local isGk = ctx.Match:IsGoalkeeper()
        local now = os.clock()
        local perfOn = ctx.Store:Get("Meta.PerformanceMode") == true
        local scanInterval = perfOn and 0.08 or 0.03

        if not isGk and (now - lastActorScan >= scanInterval) then
            latestActorsSnapshot = ctx.Player:GetAllActorsSnapshot()
            lastActorScan = now
        end

        ctx.Automation.Sprint:Step()
        ctx.Automation.Goalkeeper:Step(dt)
        if not isGk then
            strikerController:Step(latestActorsSnapshot)
        end
    end)

    ctx.Scheduler:AddRenderStep("LouisHub_Visuals", 0.016, function()
        trajectoryOverlay3D:Render()
        worldIndicators:Render()
        if ctx.Store:Get("Visuals.Radar2DEnabled") == true then
            local radarActors = ctx.Match:IsGoalkeeper()
                and ctx.Player:GetAllActorsSnapshot(0.12)
                or latestActorsSnapshot
            pitchRadar2D:Render(radarActors)
        else
            pitchRadar2D:Render(nil)
        end
    end)

    if ctx.Performance and type(ctx.Performance.Apply) == "function" then
        ctx.Performance:Apply()
    end

    local hotkeyConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
        if gameProcessed then
            return
        end
        local flickKeyName = ctx.Store:Get("Striker.FlickComboKey") or "F"
        local fakeKeyName = ctx.Store:Get("Striker.FakeTrickKey") or "G"
        local gkDiveKeyName = ctx.Store:Get("Goalkeeper.SmartDiveKey") or "E"

        local flickKey = Enum.KeyCode[flickKeyName]
        local fakeKey = Enum.KeyCode[fakeKeyName]
        local gkKey = Enum.KeyCode[gkDiveKeyName]

        if flickKey and input.KeyCode == flickKey and not ctx.Match:IsGoalkeeper() then
            strikerController:TriggerFlickCombo()
        elseif fakeKey and input.KeyCode == fakeKey and not ctx.Match:IsGoalkeeper() then
            strikerController:TriggerFakeTrickCombo(latestActorsSnapshot)
        elseif gkKey and input.KeyCode == gkKey and ctx.Match:IsGoalkeeper() then
            ctx.Automation.Goalkeeper:TriggerSmartDive()
        end
    end)

    LouisHub.OnDestroy(function()
        if hotkeyConn and hotkeyConn.Connected then
            hotkeyConn:Disconnect()
        end
        pcall(function() dmcDribble:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() trajectoryOverlay3D:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() worldIndicators:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() pitchRadar2D:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function()
            if rootGui and rootGui.Parent then
                rootGui:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end)

    LouisHub.PartLoaded[9] = true
    print(string.format("[LouisHub v%s] Part 9/10 initialized (DmcAutoDribble, Upgraded StrikerController & Visuals Ready)", LouisHub.Version))
end

--============================================================================
-- LOUIS HUB // ILLEGAL SOCCER SUITE v3.2.0 (ULTRA PRECISION & ANTI-LAG EDITION)
-- PART 10/10: BILINGUAL DASHBOARD, TOASTS, QUICK HUD & PERSISTENT SESSION IO
-- Requires Parts 1/10 through 9/10 loaded in getgenv().LouisHub
--============================================================================

do
    local GlobalEnv = (type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or _G
    local LouisHub = GlobalEnv.LouisHub

    if type(LouisHub) ~= "table"
        or not (LouisHub.PartLoaded
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[1]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[2]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[3]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[4]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[5]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[6]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[7]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[8]
            and LouisHub.PartLoaded[9])
    then
        error("[LouisHub] Parts 1/10 through 9/10 must be executed before Part 10/10!", 2)
    end

    local UserInputService = LouisHub.SafeService("UserInputService")
    local TweenService = LouisHub.SafeService("TweenService")

    local function tr(ctx, key, ...)
        if ctx and ctx.Locale and type(ctx.Locale.T) == "function" then
            return ctx.Locale:T(key, ...)
        end
        if select("#", ...) > 0 then
            local ok, res = pcall(string.format, tostring(key), ...)
            if ok then
                return res
            end
        end
        return tostring(key)
    end

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: UI/Theme
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("UI/Theme", function()
        return {
            Colors = {
                BgMain = Color3.fromRGB(13, 17, 24),
                BgCard = Color3.fromRGB(20, 26, 36),
                BgControl = Color3.fromRGB(28, 36, 49),
                BgHover = Color3.fromRGB(36, 46, 62),
                BgDanger = Color3.fromRGB(46, 16, 22),
                Stroke = Color3.fromRGB(52, 66, 88),
                Accent = Color3.fromRGB(64, 196, 255),
                AccentDark = Color3.fromRGB(26, 86, 120),
                Success = Color3.fromRGB(72, 232, 148),
                Warning = Color3.fromRGB(255, 196, 68),
                Danger = Color3.fromRGB(255, 88, 96),
                TextPrimary = Color3.fromRGB(238, 244, 252),
                TextMuted = Color3.fromRGB(145, 162, 186),
            },
            Fonts = {
                Bold = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
                Medium = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
                Regular = Enum.Font.Gotham,
            },
        }
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: UI/Components (Clean Bilingual Live-Updating Controls)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("UI/Components", function(Require)
        local Theme = Require("UI/Theme")
        local C = Theme.Colors
        local F = Theme.Fonts

        local Components = {}

        function Components.Round(inst, radius)
            local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
            c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 8)
            c.Parent = inst
            return c
        end

        function Components.Stroke(inst, color, thickness, transparency)
            local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            s.Color = color or C.Stroke
            s.Thickness = thickness or 1
            s.Transparency = transparency or 0
            s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
            s.Parent = inst
            return s
        end

        function Components.Tween(inst, duration, props)
            if not TweenService then
                for k, v in pairs(props) do
                    inst[k] = v
                end
                return nil
            end
            local info = TweenInfo.new(duration or 0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            local tw = TweenService:Create(inst, info, props)
            tw:Play()
            return tw
        end

        function Components.BindLocaleText(inst, store, janitor, locale, key, transformFn)
            local function apply()
                local txt = (locale and locale:T(key)) or tostring(key)
                if transformFn then
                    txt = transformFn(txt)
                end
                inst.Text = txt
            end
            apply()
            janitor:Add(store:OnChanged("Meta.Language", apply))
        end

        function Components.MakeDraggable(dragHandle, moveTarget, janitor, isLockedFn, onMoved, onTapped, onDragStateChanged)
            local dragging = false
            local moved = false
            local dragInput = nil
            local dragStart = nil
            local startPos = nil

            janitor:Connect(dragHandle.InputBegan, function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragging = true
                    moved = false
                    dragInput = input
                    dragStart = input.Position
                    startPos = moveTarget.Position
                    if onDragStateChanged then
                        onDragStateChanged(true)
                    end
                end
            end)

            janitor:Connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
                if not dragging then
                    return
                end
                if input == dragInput or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                    local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                    if delta.Magnitude > 6 then
                        moved = true
                    end
                    if isLockedFn and isLockedFn() then
                        return
                    end
                    if moved then
                        moveTarget.Position = UDim2.new(
                            startPos.X.Scale,
                            startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                            startPos.Y.Scale,
                            startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                        )
                    end
                end
            end)

            janitor:Connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
                if dragging and (input == dragInput or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    dragging = false
                    dragInput = nil
                    if onDragStateChanged then
                        onDragStateChanged(false)
                    end
                    if not moved then
                        if onTapped then
                            onTapped()
                        end
                    elseif onMoved and not (isLockedFn and isLockedFn()) then
                        onMoved(moveTarget.Position)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        function Components.SectionHeader(parent, store, janitor, locale, titleKey, order)
            local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            lbl.Name = "Section_" .. tostring(titleKey)
            lbl.LayoutOrder = order
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.Font = F.Bold
            lbl.TextSize = 11
            lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            lbl.TextColor3 = C.Accent
            lbl.Parent = parent
            Components.BindLocaleText(lbl, store, janitor, locale, titleKey, string.upper)
            return lbl
        end

        function Components.WarningBanner(parent, store, janitor, locale, titleKey, bodyKey, watchPath, order)
            local box = Instance.new("Frame")
            box.Name = "WarningBanner"
            box.LayoutOrder = order
            box.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 68)
            box.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgDanger
            box.BorderSizePixel = 0
            box.Parent = parent
            Components.Round(box, 8)
            local stroke = Components.Stroke(box, C.Danger, 1.4, 0.15)

            local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 5)
            title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 16)
            title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            title.Font = F.Bold
            title.TextSize = 11
            title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            title.TextColor3 = C.Danger
            title.Parent = box
            Components.BindLocaleText(title, store, janitor, locale, titleKey)

            local body = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            body.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 22)
            body.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -26)
            body.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            body.Font = F.Regular
            body.TextSize = 10
            body.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            body.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
            body.TextWrapped = true
            body.TextColor3 = C.TextPrimary
            body.Parent = box
            Components.BindLocaleText(body, store, janitor, locale, bodyKey)

            if watchPath then
                local function refreshActive(val)
                    local active = val == true
                    stroke.Thickness = active and 2.0 or 1.2
                    stroke.Transparency = active and 0 or 0.35
                    box.BackgroundColor3 = active and Color3.fromRGB(64, 18, 24) or C.BgDanger
                end
                refreshActive(store:Get(watchPath))
                janitor:Add(store:OnChanged(watchPath, refreshActive))
            end

            return box
        end

        function Components.Toggle(parent, store, janitor, locale, labelKey, configPath, order, subtitleKey, dangerAccent)
            local hasSub = subtitleKey ~= nil
            local row = Instance.new("Frame")
            row.Name = "Toggle_" .. configPath
            row.LayoutOrder = order
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, hasSub and 48 or 36)
            row.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgCard
            row.BorderSizePixel = 0
            row.Parent = parent
            Components.Round(row, 8)
            local rowStroke = Components.Stroke(row, dangerAccent and C.Danger or C.Stroke, 1, dangerAccent and 0.25 or 0.35)

            local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, hasSub and 5 or 0)
            title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, hasSub and 18 or 36)
            title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            title.Font = F.Medium
            title.TextSize = 12
            title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            title.TextColor3 = dangerAccent and C.Danger or C.TextPrimary
            title.Parent = row
            Components.BindLocaleText(title, store, janitor, locale, labelKey)

            if hasSub then
                local sub = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                sub.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 23)
                sub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 20)
                sub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                sub.Font = F.Regular
                sub.TextSize = 10
                sub.TextWrapped = true
                sub.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                sub.TextColor3 = C.TextMuted
                sub.Parent = row
                Components.BindLocaleText(sub, store, janitor, locale, subtitleKey)
            end

            local switch = Instance.new("TextButton")
            switch.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
            switch.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0)
            switch.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42, 22)
            switch.AutoButtonColor = false
            switch.Text = ""
            switch.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgControl
            switch.Parent = row
            Components.Round(switch, 11)

            local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
            knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
            knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, 0)
            knob.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16)
            knob.BackgroundColor3 = C.TextMuted
            knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
            knob.Parent = switch
            Components.Round(knob, 8)

            local onTrackCol = dangerAccent and Color3.fromRGB(115, 28, 36) or C.AccentDark
            local onKnobCol = dangerAccent and C.Danger or C.Accent

            local function refresh(val)
                local on = val == true
                Components.Tween(switch, 0.14, {
                    BackgroundColor3 = on and onTrackCol or C.BgControl,
                })
                Components.Tween(knob, 0.14, {
                    Position = on and UDim2.new(0, 23, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, 0),
                    BackgroundColor3 = on and onKnobCol or C.TextMuted,
                })
                if dangerAccent then
                    rowStroke.Transparency = on and 0 or 0.35
                end
            end

            refresh(store:Get(configPath))
            janitor:Add(store:OnChanged(configPath, refresh))

            janitor:Connect(switch.MouseButton1Click, function()
                store:Set(configPath, not (store:Get(configPath) == true))
            end)

            return row
        end

        function Components.Slider(parent, store, janitor, locale, scrollFrame, labelKey, configPath, minVal, maxVal, stepVal, suffix, order, onSlidingChanged)
            local row = Instance.new("Frame")
            row.Name = "Slider_" .. configPath
            row.LayoutOrder = order
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 50)
            row.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgCard
            row.BorderSizePixel = 0
            row.Parent = parent
            Components.Round(row, 8)
            Components.Stroke(row, C.Stroke, 1, 0.35)

            local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 5)
            title.Size = UDim2.new(0.65, -12, 0, 18)
            title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            title.Font = F.Medium
            title.TextSize = 12
            title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            title.TextColor3 = C.TextPrimary
            title.Parent = row
            Components.BindLocaleText(title, store, janitor, locale, labelKey)

            local valueLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            valueLbl.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
            valueLbl.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 5)
            valueLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0.35, -12, 0, 18)
            valueLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            valueLbl.Font = F.Bold
            valueLbl.TextSize = 11
            valueLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
            valueLbl.TextColor3 = C.Accent
            valueLbl.Text = ""
            valueLbl.Parent = row

            local track = Instance.new("Frame")
            track.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 31)
            track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 8)
            track.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgControl
            track.BorderSizePixel = 0
            track.Parent = row
            Components.Round(track, 4)

            local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
            fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 1)
            fill.BackgroundColor3 = C.Accent
            fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
            fill.Parent = track
            Components.Round(fill, 4)

            local thumb = Instance.new("Frame")
            thumb.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            thumb.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            thumb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14)
            thumb.BackgroundColor3 = C.TextPrimary
            thumb.BorderSizePixel = 0
            thumb.Parent = track
            Components.Round(thumb, 7)

            local step = stepVal or 1
            local function formatNum(n)
                if step < 1 then
                    return string.format("%.2f%s", n, suffix or "")
                end
                return string.format("%d%s", math.round(n), suffix or "")
            end

            local function refresh(val)
                local num = tonumber(val) or minVal
                num = math.clamp(num, minVal, maxVal)
                local alpha = (maxVal > minVal) and ((num - minVal) / (maxVal - minVal)) or 0
                fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(alpha, 1)
                thumb.Position = UDim2.fromScale(alpha, 0.5)
                valueLbl.Text = formatNum(num)
            end

            local function applyFromInputX(screenX)
                local absPos = track.AbsolutePosition.X
                local absSize = math.max(1, track.AbsoluteSize.X)
                local alpha = math.clamp((screenX - absPos) / absSize, 0, 1)
                local raw = minVal + (maxVal - minVal) * alpha
                local snapped = math.round(raw / step) * step
                snapped = math.clamp(snapped, minVal, maxVal)
                if step >= 1 then
                    snapped = math.round(snapped)
                else
                    snapped = math.round(snapped * 100) / 100
                end
                store:Set(configPath, snapped)
            end

            local sliding = false
            local activeInput = nil

            local hitZone = Instance.new("TextButton")
            hitZone.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            hitZone.Text = ""
            hitZone.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, -8)
            hitZone.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 16)
            hitZone.Parent = track

            janitor:Connect(hitZone.InputBegan, function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    sliding = true
                    activeInput = input
                    if scrollFrame then
                        scrollFrame.ScrollingEnabled = false
                    end
                    if onSlidingChanged then
                        onSlidingChanged(true)
                    end
                    applyFromInputX(input.Position.X)
                end
            end)

            janitor:Connect(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
                if not sliding then
                    return
                end
                if input == activeInput or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                    applyFromInputX(input.Position.X)
                end
            end)

            janitor:Connect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
                if sliding and (input == activeInput or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    sliding = false
                    activeInput = nil
                    if scrollFrame then
                        scrollFrame.ScrollingEnabled = true
                    end
                    if onSlidingChanged then
                        onSlidingChanged(false)
                    end
                end
            end)

            refresh(store:Get(configPath))
            janitor:Add(store:OnChanged(configPath, refresh))
            return row
        end

        function Components.Selector(parent, store, janitor, locale, labelKey, configPath, options, order, displayMap)
            local row = Instance.new("Frame")
            row.Name = "Selector_" .. configPath
            row.LayoutOrder = order
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
            row.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgCard
            row.BorderSizePixel = 0
            row.Parent = parent
            Components.Round(row, 8)
            Components.Stroke(row, C.Stroke, 1, 0.35)

            local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0)
            title.Size = UDim2.new(0.48, -12, 1, 0)
            title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            title.Font = F.Medium
            title.TextSize = 12
            title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            title.TextColor3 = C.TextPrimary
            title.Parent = row
            Components.BindLocaleText(title, store, janitor, locale, labelKey)

            local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
            btn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
            btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0)
            btn.Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 0, 24)
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgControl
            btn.AutoButtonColor = false
            btn.Font = F.Bold
            btn.TextSize = 11
            btn.TextColor3 = C.Accent
            btn.Text = ""
            btn.Parent = row
            Components.Round(btn, 6)
            Components.Stroke(btn, C.Stroke, 1, 0.2)

            local function refresh(val)
                local raw = val or options[1]
                local shown = (displayMap and displayMap[raw]) or raw
                btn.Text = "< " .. tostring(shown) .. " >"
            end

            refresh(store:Get(configPath))
            janitor:Add(store:OnChanged(configPath, refresh))

            janitor:Connect(btn.MouseButton1Click, function()
                local cur = store:Get(configPath)
                local curIdx = 1
                for i, opt in ipairs(options) do
                    if opt == cur then
                        curIdx = i
                        break
                    end
                end
                local nextIdx = (curIdx % #options) + 1
                store:Set(configPath, options[nextIdx])
            end)

            return row
        end

        function Components.Keybind(parent, store, janitor, locale, labelKey, configPath, order)
            local row = Instance.new("Frame")
            row.Name = "Keybind_" .. configPath
            row.LayoutOrder = order
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
            row.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgCard
            row.BorderSizePixel = 0
            row.Parent = parent
            Components.Round(row, 8)
            Components.Stroke(row, C.Stroke, 1, 0.35)

            local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0)
            title.Size = UDim2.new(0.55, -12, 1, 0)
            title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            title.Font = F.Medium
            title.TextSize = 12
            title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            title.TextColor3 = C.TextPrimary
            title.Parent = row
            Components.BindLocaleText(title, store, janitor, locale, labelKey)

            local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
            btn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
            btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0)
            btn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(110, 24)
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgControl
            btn.AutoButtonColor = false
            btn.Font = F.Bold
            btn.TextSize = 11
            btn.TextColor3 = C.TextPrimary
            btn.Text = tostring(store:Get(configPath) or "None")
            btn.Parent = row
            Components.Round(btn, 6)
            Components.Stroke(btn, C.Stroke, 1, 0.2)

            local listening = false
            local function refresh(val)
                if not listening then
                    btn.Text = tostring(val or "None")
                    btn.TextColor3 = C.TextPrimary
                end
            end

            janitor:Add(store:OnChanged(configPath, refresh))

            janitor:Connect(btn.MouseButton1Click, function()
                if listening then
                    return
                end
                listening = true
                btn.Text = (locale and locale:T("PRESS_KEY")) or "Press Key..."
                btn.TextColor3 = C.Warning

                local capConn
                capConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
                        listening = false
                        capConn:Disconnect()
                        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
                            refresh(store:Get(configPath))
                            return
                        end
                        store:Set(configPath, input.KeyCode.Name)
                        refresh(input.KeyCode.Name)
                    end
                end)
                janitor:Add(capConn, "Disconnect")
            end)

            return row
        end

        function Components.Button(parent, store, janitor, locale, textKey, bgColor, textColor, order, onClick)
            local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
            btn.Name = "Btn_" .. tostring(textKey)
            btn.LayoutOrder = order
            btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34)
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = bgColor or C.BgControl
            btn.AutoButtonColor = false
            btn.Font = F.Bold
            btn.TextSize = 12
            btn.TextColor3 = textColor or C.TextPrimary
            btn.Parent = parent
            Components.Round(btn, 8)
            Components.Stroke(btn, C.Stroke, 1, 0.25)
            Components.BindLocaleText(btn, store, janitor, locale, textKey)

            janitor:Connect(btn.MouseButton1Click, function()
                Components.Tween(btn, 0.08, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.25 })
                task.delay(0.08, function()
                    if btn and btn.Parent then
                        Components.Tween(btn, 0.12, { BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
                    end
                end)
                if onClick then
                    onClick()
                end
            end)

            return btn
        end

        return Components
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: UI/ToastManager
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("UI/ToastManager", function(Require)
        local Janitor = Require("Core/Janitor")
        local Theme = Require("UI/Theme")
        local Components = Require("UI/Components")

        local C = Theme.Colors
        local F = Theme.Fonts

        local ToastManager = {}
        ToastManager.__index = ToastManager

        function ToastManager.new(rootGui)
            local self = setmetatable({
                _janitor = Janitor.new(),
                _container = nil,
            }, ToastManager)

            local container = Instance.new("Frame")
            container.Name = "LouisHub_Toasts"
            container.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
            container.Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 48)
            container.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(272, 220)
            container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            container.ZIndex = 30
            container.Parent = rootGui

            local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
            layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
            layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
            layout.Parent = container

            self._container = container
            self._janitor:Add(container, "Destroy")
            return self
        end

        function ToastManager:Push(message, accentColor, duration)
            if not (self._container and self._container.Parent) then
                return
            end
            local card = Instance.new("Frame")
            card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
            card.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgMain
            card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.06
            card.ZIndex = 31
            card.Parent = self._container
            Components.Round(card, 8)
            Components.Stroke(card, accentColor or C.Accent, 1.4, 0.1)

            local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            lbl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0)
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0)
            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.Font = F.Medium
            lbl.TextSize = 11
            lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            lbl.TextColor3 = C.TextPrimary
            lbl.TextWrapped = true
            lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            lbl.Text = "Louis Hub: " .. tostring(message)
            lbl.ZIndex = 32
            lbl.Parent = card

            task.delay(duration or 2.8, function()
                if card and card.Parent then
                    Components.Tween(card, 0.2, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
                    Components.Tween(lbl, 0.2, { TextTransparency = 1 })
                    task.delay(0.22, function()
                        if card and card.Parent then
                            card:Destroy()
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end

        function ToastManager:Destroy()
            self._janitor:Destroy()
        end

        return ToastManager
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: UI/QuickHud
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("UI/QuickHud", function(Require)
        local Janitor = Require("Core/Janitor")
        local Theme = Require("UI/Theme")
        local Components = Require("UI/Components")

        local C = Theme.Colors
        local F = Theme.Fonts

        local QuickHud = {}
        QuickHud.__index = QuickHud

        function QuickHud.new(ctx, rootGui, onToggleDashboard)
            local self = setmetatable({
                _janitor = Janitor.new(),
                _ctx = ctx,
                _rootGui = rootGui,
                _onToggleDashboard = onToggleDashboard,
                _floatBtn = nil,
                _floatStroke = nil,
                _floatStatusDot = nil,
                _floatScale = nil,
                _telemetryPill = nil,
                _telemetryLabel = nil,
                _telemetryScale = nil,
                _quickBar = nil,
                _quickBarScale = nil,
                _strikerRow = nil,
                _gkRow = nil,
                _strikerToggles = {},
                _gkToggles = {},
                _lastVpX = -1,
                _lastVpY = -1,
                _lastTelemetryText = "",
                _lastIsGk = nil,
            }, QuickHud)
            self:_build()
            return self
        end

        function QuickHud:_savePos(configKey, udim2)
            self._ctx.Store:Set(configKey, {
                XS = udim2.X.Scale,
                XO = udim2.X.Offset,
                YS = udim2.Y.Scale,
                YO = udim2.Y.Offset,
            })
        end

        function QuickHud:_loadPos(configKey, defaultUDim2)
            local p = self._ctx.Store:Get(configKey)
            if type(p) == "table" then
                return UDim2.new(p.XS or 0, p.XO or 0, p.YS or 0, p.YO or 0)
            end
            return defaultUDim2
        end

        function QuickHud:_updateHudScale()
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local vp = cam and cam.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
            if vp.X == self._lastVpX and vp.Y == self._lastVpY then
                return
            end
            self._lastVpX = vp.X
            self._lastVpY = vp.Y

            local scale = 1.0
            if vp.X < 700 or vp.Y < 480 then
                scale = math.clamp(math.min(vp.X / 540, vp.Y / 430), 0.65, 0.95)
            end
            if self._telemetryScale then
                self._telemetryScale.Scale = scale
            end
            if self._quickBarScale then
                self._quickBarScale.Scale = scale
            end
            if self._floatScale then
                self._floatScale.Scale = math.max(0.85, scale)
            end
        end

        function QuickHud:_makeQuickToggle(parent, textKey, configPath, order, registryTable, customOnColor)
            local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
            btn.Name = "Q_" .. tostring(textKey)
            btn.LayoutOrder = order
            btn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(72, 28)
            btn.AutoButtonColor = false
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgMain
            btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
            btn.Font = F.Bold
            btn.TextSize = 10
            btn.TextColor3 = C.TextMuted
            btn.Parent = parent
            Components.Round(btn, 7)
            local stroke = Components.Stroke(btn, C.Stroke, 1.2, 0.2)
            Components.BindLocaleText(btn, self._ctx.Store, self._janitor, self._ctx.Locale, textKey)

            local onStroke = customOnColor or C.Accent
            local onBg = customOnColor and Color3.fromRGB(20, 78, 52) or C.AccentDark

            local function refresh(val)
                local on = val == true
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = on and onBg or C.BgMain
                btn.TextColor3 = on and C.TextPrimary or C.TextMuted
                stroke.Color = on and onStroke or C.Stroke
            end

            refresh(self._ctx.Store:Get(configPath))
            self._janitor:Add(self._ctx.Store:OnChanged(configPath, refresh))

            self._janitor:Connect(btn.MouseButton1Click, function()
                self._ctx.Store:Set(configPath, not (self._ctx.Store:Get(configPath) == true))
            end)

            registryTable[configPath] = btn
            return btn
        end

        function QuickHud:_makeActionBtn(parent, textKey, accentColor, order, onClick)
            local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
            btn.Name = "A_" .. tostring(textKey)
            btn.LayoutOrder = order
            btn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(76, 28)
            btn.AutoButtonColor = false
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = accentColor
            btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
            btn.Font = F.Bold
            btn.TextSize = 10
            btn.TextColor3 = C.TextPrimary
            btn.Parent = parent
            Components.Round(btn, 7)
            Components.Stroke(btn, accentColor, 1.2, 0.1)
            Components.BindLocaleText(btn, self._ctx.Store, self._janitor, self._ctx.Locale, textKey)

            self._janitor:Connect(btn.MouseButton1Click, function()
                if onClick then
                    onClick()
                end
            end)
            return btn
        end

        function QuickHud:_build()
            local store = self._ctx.Store

            local floatBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
            floatBtn.Name = "LouisHub_FloatingToggle"
            floatBtn.Position = self:_loadPos("UI.FloatBtnPos", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0.14, 0))
            floatBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(92, 32)
            floatBtn.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgMain
            floatBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
            floatBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
            floatBtn.Text = ""
            floatBtn.ZIndex = 20
            floatBtn.Parent = self._rootGui
            Components.Round(floatBtn, 16)
            local floatStroke = Components.Stroke(floatBtn, C.Accent, 1.5, 0.2)

            local fScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
            fScale.Parent = floatBtn
            self._floatScale = fScale

            local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
            dot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
            dot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, 0)
            dot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8)
            dot.BackgroundColor3 = C.Success
            dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
            dot.ZIndex = 21
            dot.Parent = floatBtn
            Components.Round(dot, 4)

            local floatTxt = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            floatTxt.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 0)
            floatTxt.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, 0)
            floatTxt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            floatTxt.Font = F.Bold
            floatTxt.TextSize = 11
            floatTxt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            floatTxt.TextColor3 = C.TextPrimary
            floatTxt.Text = "LOUIS"
            floatTxt.ZIndex = 21
            floatTxt.Parent = floatBtn

            Components.MakeDraggable(
                floatBtn,
                floatBtn,
                self._janitor,
                function()
                    return store:Get("UI.HudLocked") == true
                end,
                function(newPos)
                    self:_savePos("UI.FloatBtnPos", newPos)
                end,
                function()
                    self._onToggleDashboard()
                end
            )

            self._floatBtn = floatBtn
            self._floatStroke = floatStroke
            self._floatStatusDot = dot
            self._janitor:Add(floatBtn, "Destroy")

            local pill = Instance.new("Frame")
            pill.Name = "LouisHub_TelemetryPill"
            pill.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
            pill.Position = self:_loadPos("UI.TelemetryPos", UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 10))
            pill.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(412, 30)
            pill.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgMain
            pill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.14
            pill.Parent = self._rootGui
            Components.Round(pill, 15)
            Components.Stroke(pill, C.Accent, 1.2, 0.35)

            local tScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
            tScale.Parent = pill
            self._telemetryScale = tScale

            local pillText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            pillText.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0)
            pillText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -82, 1, 0)
            pillText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            pillText.Font = F.Bold
            pillText.TextSize = 11
            pillText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            pillText.TextColor3 = C.TextPrimary
            pillText.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            pillText.Text = "LOUIS HUB | Loading..."
            pillText.Parent = pill

            local menuBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
            menuBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
            menuBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -5, 0.5, 0)
            menuBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 22)
            menuBtn.BackgroundColor3 = C.AccentDark
            menuBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
            menuBtn.Font = F.Bold
            menuBtn.TextSize = 10
            menuBtn.TextColor3 = C.TextPrimary
            menuBtn.Text = "MENU"
            menuBtn.Parent = pill
            Components.Round(menuBtn, 11)
            Components.Stroke(menuBtn, C.Accent, 1, 0.25)

            self._janitor:Connect(menuBtn.MouseButton1Click, function()
                self._onToggleDashboard()
            end)

            Components.MakeDraggable(
                pill,
                pill,
                self._janitor,
                function()
                    return store:Get("UI.HudLocked") == true
                end,
                function(newPos)
                    self:_savePos("UI.TelemetryPos", newPos)
                end
            )

            self._telemetryPill = pill
            self._telemetryLabel = pillText
            self._janitor:Add(pill, "Destroy")

            local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
            bar.Name = "LouisHub_QuickBar"
            bar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
            bar.Position = self:_loadPos("UI.QuickBarPos", UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -76))
            bar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(476, 36)
            bar.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgMain
            bar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.22
            bar.Parent = self._rootGui
            Components.Round(bar, 10)
            Components.Stroke(bar, C.Stroke, 1.2, 0.25)

            local qScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
            qScale.Parent = bar
            self._quickBarScale = qScale

            Components.MakeDraggable(
                bar,
                bar,
                self._janitor,
                function()
                    return store:Get("UI.HudLocked") == true
                end,
                function(newPos)
                    self:_savePos("UI.QuickBarPos", newPos)
                end
            )

            local function createRoleRow(rowName)
                local r = Instance.new("Frame")
                r.Name = rowName
                r.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                r.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                r.Parent = bar

                local l = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                l.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
                l.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
                l.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
                l.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                l.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
                l.Parent = r
                return r
            end

            local strikerRow = createRoleRow("StrikerQuickRow")
            self:_makeQuickToggle(strikerRow, "Q_SHOOT", "Striker.AutoShoot", 1, self._strikerToggles)
            self:_makeQuickToggle(strikerRow, "Q_SILENT", "Striker.TargetTrajectoryCorrection", 2, self._strikerToggles)
            self:_makeQuickToggle(strikerRow, "Q_VOLLEY", "Striker.AutoVolley", 3, self._strikerToggles)
            self:_makeQuickToggle(strikerRow, "Q_DRIBBLE", "Striker.AutoDribble", 4, self._strikerToggles)
            self:_makeActionBtn(strikerRow, "Q_FAKETRICK", Color3.fromRGB(165, 92, 235), 5, function()
                self._ctx.Automation.Striker:TriggerFakeTrickCombo()
            end)
            self:_makeQuickToggle(strikerRow, "Q_PERF", "Meta.PerformanceMode", 6, self._strikerToggles, C.Success)

            local gkRow = createRoleRow("GoalkeeperQuickRow")
            self:_makeQuickToggle(gkRow, "Q_AUTODIVE", "Goalkeeper.AutoDive", 1, self._gkToggles)
            self:_makeActionBtn(gkRow, "Q_SMARTDIVE", Color3.fromRGB(220, 92, 72), 2, function()
                self._ctx.Automation.Goalkeeper:TriggerSmartDive()
            end)
            self:_makeQuickToggle(gkRow, "Q_RUSH", "Goalkeeper.CloseRangeRush", 3, self._gkToggles)
            self:_makeQuickToggle(gkRow, "Q_COVER", "Goalkeeper.PreShotCoverage", 4, self._gkToggles)
            self:_makeQuickToggle(gkRow, "Q_POS", "Goalkeeper.PositionAssist", 5, self._gkToggles)
            self:_makeQuickToggle(gkRow, "Q_PERF", "Meta.PerformanceMode", 6, self._gkToggles, C.Success)

            self._quickBar = bar
            self._strikerRow = strikerRow
            self._gkRow = gkRow
            self._janitor:Add(bar, "Destroy")
            self:_updateHudScale()
        end

        function QuickHud:Render()
            self:_updateHudScale()
            local uiCfg = self._ctx.Store:Get("UI")
            local dashOpen = uiCfg.DashboardOpen == true
            local bypassOn = self._ctx.Store:Get("Meta.BypassMode") == true
            local monkeyOn = self._ctx.Store:Get("Meta.MonkeyPatchMode") == true
            local perfOn = self._ctx.Store:Get("Meta.PerformanceMode") == true

            local showFloat = uiCfg.ShowFloatingButton ~= false
            if self._floatBtn.Visible ~= showFloat then
                self._floatBtn.Visible = showFloat
            end
            self._floatStroke.Color = monkeyOn and C.Danger or (perfOn and C.Success or (dashOpen and C.Accent or C.Stroke))
            self._floatStatusDot.BackgroundColor3 = monkeyOn and C.Danger or (bypassOn and C.Success or C.Warning)

            local showTel = uiCfg.ShowTelemetryStrip == true
            if self._telemetryPill.Visible ~= showTel then
                self._telemetryPill.Visible = showTel
            end

            local showBar = uiCfg.ShowQuickBar == true
            if self._quickBar.Visible ~= showBar then
                self._quickBar.Visible = showBar
            end

            local isGk = self._ctx.Match:IsGoalkeeper()
            if self._lastIsGk ~= isGk then
                self._lastIsGk = isGk
                self._strikerRow.Visible = not isGk
                self._gkRow.Visible = isGk
            end

            if not showTel then
                return
            end

            local ballSnap = self._ctx.Ball:GetSnapshot()
            local stamPct = math.round(self._ctx.Player:GetStamina() * 100)
            local roleTag = isGk and "GK" or "STRIKER"
            local statusTxt = isGk
                and self._ctx.Automation.Goalkeeper:GetStatus()
                or self._ctx.Automation.Striker:GetStatus()

            local txt = string.format(
                "LOUIS v%s [%s%s] • %.0f st/s • Stam %d%% • %s",
                LouisHub.Version,
                roleTag,
                perfOn and " | FPS+" or "",
                ballSnap.Speed or 0,
                stamPct,
                tostring(statusTxt)
            )
            if self._lastTelemetryText ~= txt then
                self._lastTelemetryText = txt
                self._telemetryLabel.Text = txt
            end
        end

        function QuickHud:Destroy()
            self._janitor:Destroy()
        end

        return QuickHud
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- MODULE: UI/Dashboard (Upgraded Full v3.2.0 Menu Suite)
    --========================================================================
    LouisHub.Define("UI/Dashboard", function(Require)
        local Janitor = Require("Core/Janitor")
        local Theme = Require("UI/Theme")
        local Components = Require("UI/Components")

        local C = Theme.Colors
        local F = Theme.Fonts

        local Dashboard = {}
        Dashboard.__index = Dashboard

        local FULL_HEIGHT = 428
        local MINI_HEIGHT = 42

        function Dashboard.new(ctx, rootGui, toastManager)
            local self = setmetatable({
                _janitor = Janitor.new(),
                _ctx = ctx,
                _rootGui = rootGui,
                _toast = toastManager,
                _window = nil,
                _uiScale = nil,
                _tabBar = nil,
                _content = nil,
                _minBtn = nil,
                _tabs = {},
                _tabButtons = {},
                _activeTab = "STRIKER",
                _telemetryCardLbl = nil,
                _lastTelemetryCardTxt = "",
                _profileListLbl = nil,
                _isDraggingOrSliding = false,
            }, Dashboard)
            self:_build()
            return self
        end

        function Dashboard:IsInteracting()
            return self._isDraggingOrSliding == true
        end

        function Dashboard:_getTargetScale()
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local vp = cam and cam.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
            local userScale = math.clamp(self._ctx.Store:Get("UI.HudScale") or 1.0, 0.7, 1.35)
            local autoFit = 1.0
            if vp.Y < 500 or vp.X < 680 then
                autoFit = math.clamp(math.min(vp.Y / 470, vp.X / 540), 0.65, 1.0)
            end
            return userScale * autoFit
        end

        function Dashboard:_updateViewportScale()
            if not self._uiScale then
                return
            end
            self._uiScale.Scale = self:_getTargetScale()
        end

        function Dashboard:_switchTab(tabName)
            self._activeTab = tabName
            for name, page in pairs(self._tabs) do
                page.Visible = (name == tabName)
            end
            for name, btn in pairs(self._tabButtons) do
                local active = (name == tabName)
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = active and C.AccentDark or C.BgControl
                btn.TextColor3 = active and C.TextPrimary or C.TextMuted
            end
            if tabName == "CONFIG" then
                self:RenderTelemetry()
            end
        end

        function Dashboard:_createPage(container, tabName)
            local page = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
            page.Name = "Page_" .. tabName
            page.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            page.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            page.BorderSizePixel = 0
            page.ScrollBarThickness = 4
            page.ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.Accent
            page.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
            page.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
            page.Visible = false
            page.Parent = container

            local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
            pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)
            pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 12)
            pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8)
            pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 14)
            pad.Parent = page

            local list = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
            list.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            list.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
            list.Parent = page

            local function syncCanvas()
                page.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, list.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 24)
            end
            self._janitor:Connect(list:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"), syncCanvas)
            task.defer(syncCanvas)

            self._tabs[tabName] = page
            return page
        end

        function Dashboard:_applyMinimizedState(minimized, instant)
            local isMin = minimized == true
            if self._minBtn then
                self._minBtn.Text = isMin and "+" or "-"
            end
            if self._tabBar then
                self._tabBar.Visible = not isMin
            end
            if self._content then
                self._content.Visible = not isMin
            end
            local targetSize = UDim2.fromOffset(476, isMin and MINI_HEIGHT or FULL_HEIGHT)
            if instant then
                self._window.Size = targetSize
            else
                Components.Tween(self._window, 0.16, { Size = targetSize })
            end
        end

        function Dashboard:_build()
            local store = self._ctx.Store
            local locale = self._ctx.Locale

            local function setInteracting(active)
                self._isDraggingOrSliding = active == true
            end

            local win = Instance.new("Frame")
            win.Name = "LouisHub_Dashboard"
            win.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            win.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
            win.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(476, FULL_HEIGHT)
            win.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgMain
            win.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - math.clamp(store:Get("UI.HudOpacity") or 0.94, 0.4, 1)
            win.ClipsDescendants = true
            win.Visible = store:Get("UI.DashboardOpen") == true
            win.Parent = self._rootGui
            Components.Round(win, 12)
            Components.Stroke(win, C.Accent, 1.5, 0.25)

            local uiScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
            uiScale.Parent = win
            self._uiScale = uiScale
            self._window = win
            self._janitor:Add(win, "Destroy")

            local header = Instance.new("Frame")
            header.Name = "Header"
            header.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 42)
            header.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgCard
            header.BorderSizePixel = 0
            header.Parent = win
            Components.Round(header, 12)

            local headerFix = Instance.new("Frame")
            headerFix.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -10)
            headerFix.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10)
            headerFix.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgCard
            headerFix.BorderSizePixel = 0
            headerFix.Parent = header

            local brandLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            brandLbl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 4)
            brandLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 18)
            brandLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            brandLbl.Font = F.Bold
            brandLbl.TextSize = 14
            brandLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            brandLbl.TextColor3 = C.TextPrimary
            brandLbl.Text = "LOUIS HUB"
            brandLbl.Parent = header

            local subBrand = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            subBrand.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 21)
            subBrand.Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 14)
            subBrand.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            subBrand.Font = F.Regular
            subBrand.TextSize = 10
            subBrand.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            subBrand.TextColor3 = C.Accent
            subBrand.Text = "Illegal Soccer Suite  •  v" .. LouisHub.Version
            subBrand.Parent = header

            local minBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
            minBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
            minBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -42, 0.5, 0)
            minBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26)
            minBtn.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgControl
            minBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
            minBtn.Font = F.Bold
            minBtn.TextSize = 14
            minBtn.TextColor3 = C.TextPrimary
            minBtn.Text = "-"
            minBtn.Parent = header
            Components.Round(minBtn, 7)
            self._minBtn = minBtn

            self._janitor:Connect(minBtn.MouseButton1Click, function()
                store:Set("UI.DashboardMinimized", not (store:Get("UI.DashboardMinimized") == true))
            end)

            local closeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
            closeBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
            closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0)
            closeBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26)
            closeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgControl
            closeBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
            closeBtn.Font = F.Bold
            closeBtn.TextSize = 12
            closeBtn.TextColor3 = C.TextPrimary
            closeBtn.Text = "X"
            closeBtn.Parent = header
            Components.Round(closeBtn, 7)

            self._janitor:Connect(closeBtn.MouseButton1Click, function()
                self:SetVisible(false)
            end)

            Components.MakeDraggable(header, win, self._janitor, nil, nil, nil, setInteracting)

            local tabBar = Instance.new("Frame")
            tabBar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 48)
            tabBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 30)
            tabBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            tabBar.Parent = win
            self._tabBar = tabBar

            local tabLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
            tabLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
            tabLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            tabLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
            tabLayout.Parent = tabBar

            local tabDefs = {
                { Id = "STRIKER", Key = "TAB_STRIKER" },
                { Id = "GOALKEEPER", Key = "TAB_GK" },
                { Id = "RADAR & 3D", Key = "TAB_VISUALS" },
                { Id = "CONFIG", Key = "TAB_CONFIG" },
            }
            for idx, def in ipairs(tabDefs) do
                local tb = Instance.new("TextButton")
                tb.Name = "Tab_" .. def.Id
                tb.LayoutOrder = idx
                tb.Size = UDim2.new(0.25, -5, 1, 0)
                tb.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgControl
                tb.AutoButtonColor = false
                tb.Font = F.Bold
                tb.TextSize = 11
                tb.TextColor3 = C.TextMuted
                tb.Parent = tabBar
                Components.Round(tb, 7)
                Components.Stroke(tb, C.Stroke, 1, 0.3)
                Components.BindLocaleText(tb, store, self._janitor, locale, def.Key)

                self._tabButtons[def.Id] = tb
                self._janitor:Connect(tb.MouseButton1Click, function()
                    self:_switchTab(def.Id)
                end)
            end

            local content = Instance.new("Frame")
            content.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 84)
            content.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -90)
            content.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 21, 30)
            content.BorderSizePixel = 0
            content.Parent = win
            Components.Round(content, 9)
            Components.Stroke(content, C.Stroke, 1, 0.4)
            self._content = content

            --================================================================
            -- TAB 1: STRIKER (Aimbot Goal, Silent Aim Pro, Bicycle, Fake Trick)
            --================================================================
            local pStriker = self:_createPage(content, "STRIKER")
            local o = 1
            local function nextOrder()
                o = o + 1
                return o
            end

            Components.SectionHeader(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_SHOOT", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_AUTOSHOOT", "Striker.AutoShoot", nextOrder(), "SUB_AUTOSHOOT")
            Components.Selector(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_SHOTMODE", "Striker.ShotMode", { "SmartAuto", "Direct", "FlickBicycle" }, nextOrder())
            Components.Selector(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_PLACEMENT", "Striker.ShotPlacement", { "AntiGK", "SmartCorner", "TopLeft", "TopRight", "BottomLeft", "BottomRight", "CenterLow" }, nextOrder())
            Components.Slider(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, pStriker, "LBL_BIKE_MIN_DIST", "Striker.BicycleMinDistance", 8, 30, 1, " st", nextOrder(), setInteracting)
            Components.Slider(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, pStriker, "LBL_BIKE_MAX_DIST", "Striker.BicycleMaxDistance", 25, 75, 1, " st", nextOrder(), setInteracting)

            Components.SectionHeader(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_AIMBOT_GOAL", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_AIMBOT_GOAL", "Striker.TargetTrackingCorrection", nextOrder(), "SUB_AIMBOT_GOAL")
            Components.Selector(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_AIMBOT_PLACE", "Striker.TrackingPlacement", { "Smart", "TopLeft", "TopRight", "BottomLeft", "BottomRight", "CenterLow" }, nextOrder())
            Components.Slider(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, pStriker, "LBL_AIMBOT_SMOOTH", "Striker.TrackingSmoothness", 0.05, 0.50, 0.01, "", nextOrder(), setInteracting)

            Components.SectionHeader(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_SILENT_GOAL", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_SILENT_GOAL", "Striker.TargetTrajectoryCorrection", nextOrder(), "SUB_SILENT_GOAL")
            Components.Selector(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_SILENT_PLACE", "Striker.TrajectoryPlacement", { "Smart", "TopLeft", "TopRight", "BottomLeft", "BottomRight", "CenterLow" }, nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_SILENT_ARC", "Striker.SilentAimArcCompensation", nextOrder(), "SUB_SILENT_ARC")
            Components.Slider(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, pStriker, "LBL_SILENT_PITCH", "Striker.SilentAimMaxPitchDeg", 10, 35, 1, "°", nextOrder(), setInteracting)
            Components.Slider(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, pStriker, "LBL_SILENT_CURVE", "Striker.SilentAimCurveStrength", 0.2, 1.0, 0.05, "", nextOrder(), setInteracting)

            Components.SectionHeader(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_FAKE_TRICK", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_FAKE_TRICK", "Striker.FakeTrickEnabled", nextOrder(), "SUB_FAKE_TRICK")
            Components.Slider(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, pStriker, "LBL_FAKE_TRICK_DIST", "Striker.FakeTrickDefenderRadius", 8, 24, 1, " st", nextOrder(), setInteracting)
            Components.Slider(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, pStriker, "LBL_FAKE_TRICK_DELAY", "Striker.FakeTrickDelaySeconds", 0.05, 0.25, 0.01, "s", nextOrder(), setInteracting)

            Components.SectionHeader(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_MULTI_DEF", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_MULTI_DEF", "Striker.MultiDefenderCheck", nextOrder(), "SUB_MULTI_DEF")
            Components.Slider(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, pStriker, "LBL_DEF_RADIUS", "Striker.DefenderScanRadius", 12, 35, 1, " st", nextOrder(), setInteracting)
            Components.Slider(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, pStriker, "LBL_DEF_ANGLE", "Striker.DefenderBlockAngleDeg", 15, 60, 1, "°", nextOrder(), setInteracting)

            Components.SectionHeader(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_SHOOT_GATES", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_ANTIGK_GATE", "Striker.AntiGKReachCheck", nextOrder(), "SUB_ANTIGK_GATE")
            Components.Slider(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, pStriker, "LBL_ANTIGK_MARGIN", "Striker.AntiGKMinMarginStuds", 2.0, 12.0, 0.5, " st", nextOrder(), setInteracting)
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_LOCK_AIM", "Striker.LockAimDuringShot", nextOrder(), "SUB_LOCK_AIM")
            Components.Selector(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_RANGE_MODE", "Striker.RangeMode", { "Smart", "Custom" }, nextOrder())
            Components.Slider(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, pStriker, "LBL_MIN_SHOOT", "Striker.MinShootDistance", 0, 50, 1, " st", nextOrder(), setInteracting)
            Components.Slider(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, pStriker, "LBL_MAX_SHOOT", "Striker.MaxShootDistance", 20, 100, 1, " st", nextOrder(), setInteracting)
            Components.Slider(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, pStriker, "LBL_QUICKSNAP", "Striker.QuickSnapDistance", 5, 25, 1, " st", nextOrder(), setInteracting)
            Components.Selector(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_POWER_MODE", "Striker.PowerMode", { "Adaptive", "Full", "Manual" }, nextOrder())
            Components.Slider(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, pStriker, "LBL_MANUAL_POWER", "Striker.ManualPowerPercent", 30, 100, 1, "%", nextOrder(), setInteracting)
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_AUTO_CURVE", "Striker.AutoCurveBypass", nextOrder(), "SUB_AUTO_CURVE")
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_ANTI_BLOCK", "Striker.AntiBlockGate", nextOrder(), "SUB_ANTI_BLOCK")

            Components.SectionHeader(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_VOLLEY", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_AUTO_VOLLEY", "Striker.AutoVolley", nextOrder(), "SUB_AUTO_VOLLEY")
            Components.Selector(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_VOLLEY_PLACE", "Striker.VolleyPlacement", { "AntiGK", "SmartCorner", "TopLeft", "TopRight", "BottomLeft", "BottomRight", "CenterLow" }, nextOrder())
            Components.Slider(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, pStriker, "LBL_VOLLEY_DIST", "Striker.VolleyMaxDistance", 20, 100, 1, " st", nextOrder(), setInteracting)
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_ALLOW_BICYCLE", "Striker.AllowBicycleShot", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_AUTO_JUMP_BIKE", "Striker.AutoJumpForBicycle", nextOrder(), "SUB_AUTO_JUMP_BIKE")
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_ALLOW_HEADBUTT", "Striker.AllowHeadbutt", nextOrder())

            Components.SectionHeader(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_DRIBBLE", nextOrder())
