            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_AUTO_DRIBBLE", "Striker.AutoDribble", nextOrder(), "SUB_AUTO_DRIBBLE")
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_PASS_RADAR", "Striker.PassLaneRadar", nextOrder(), "SUB_PASS_RADAR")
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_CURVE_GUIDE", "Striker.CurveGuide", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_STAMINA_GUARD", "Striker.StaminaGuard", nextOrder(), "SUB_STAMINA_GUARD")
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_UNLIMITED_STAMINA", "Striker.UnlimitedStamina", nextOrder(), "SUB_UNLIMITED_STAMINA")
            Components.Slider(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, pStriker, "LBL_STAMINA_FLOOR", "Striker.StaminaFloorPercent", 3, 35, 1, "%", nextOrder(), setInteracting)
            Components.Toggle(pStriker, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_TACKLE_RING", "Striker.TackleReachRing", nextOrder())

            --================================================================
            -- TAB 2: GOALKEEPER
            --================================================================
            local pGk = self:_createPage(content, "GOALKEEPER")
            o = 1
            Components.SectionHeader(pGk, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_GK_SAVE", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pGk, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_AUTO_DIVE", "Goalkeeper.AutoDive", nextOrder(), "SUB_AUTO_DIVE")
            Components.Toggle(pGk, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_CLOSE_RUSH", "Goalkeeper.CloseRangeRush", nextOrder(), "SUB_CLOSE_RUSH")
            Components.Toggle(pGk, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_HIGH_JUMPS", "Goalkeeper.HighBallJumps", nextOrder(), "SUB_HIGH_JUMPS")
            Components.Toggle(pGk, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_JUMP_THEN_DIVE", "Goalkeeper.JumpThenDive", nextOrder(), "SUB_JUMP_THEN_DIVE")
            Components.Selector(
                pGk,
                store,
                self._janitor,
                locale,
                "LBL_DIVE_DIRECTION",
                "Goalkeeper.DiveFilter",
                { "SMART", "FORWARD", "LEFT", "RIGHT", "SIDES" },
                nextOrder(),
                { SMART = "Smart", FORWARD = "Forward", LEFT = "Left", RIGHT = "Right", SIDES = "Sides" }
            )
            Components.Toggle(pGk, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_BACK_RECOVERY", "Goalkeeper.BackwardRecovery", nextOrder(), "SUB_BACK_RECOVERY")

            Components.SectionHeader(pGk, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_GK_POS", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pGk, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_POS_ASSIST", "Goalkeeper.PositionAssist", nextOrder(), "SUB_POS_ASSIST")
            Components.Toggle(pGk, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_PRESHOT_COVERAGE", "Goalkeeper.PreShotCoverage", nextOrder(), "SUB_PRESHOT_COVERAGE")
            Components.Selector(
                pGk,
                store,
                self._janitor,
                locale,
                "LBL_POS_MODE",
                "Goalkeeper.PositionMode",
                { "THREATS", "HOME_AND_THREATS" },
                nextOrder(),
                { THREATS = "Threats Only", HOME_AND_THREATS = "Home + Threats" }
            )
            Components.Toggle(pGk, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_RESPECT_MANUAL", "Goalkeeper.RespectManualMovement", nextOrder(), "SUB_RESPECT_MANUAL")

            Components.SectionHeader(pGk, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_GK_DIST", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pGk, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_PRESHOT_ALERT", "Goalkeeper.PreShotThreatAlert", nextOrder(), "SUB_PRESHOT_ALERT")
            Components.Toggle(pGk, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_DIVE_ADVISOR", "Goalkeeper.DiveAdvisor", nextOrder(), "SUB_DIVE_ADVISOR")
            Components.Toggle(pGk, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_CLEAR_GUARD", "Goalkeeper.ClearGuard", nextOrder(), "SUB_CLEAR_GUARD")
            Components.Toggle(pGk, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_GK_BADGE", "Goalkeeper.GKZoneBadge", nextOrder())
            Components.Button(pGk, store, self._janitor, locale, "BTN_SMART_DIVE", C.AccentDark, C.TextPrimary, nextOrder(), function()
                self._ctx.Automation.Goalkeeper:TriggerSmartDive()
            end)

            --================================================================
            -- TAB 3: RADAR & 3D (Anti-Lag & FPS Booster)
            --================================================================
            local pVis = self:_createPage(content, "RADAR & 3D")
            o = 1
            Components.SectionHeader(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_PERFORMANCE", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_PERF_MODE", "Meta.PerformanceMode", nextOrder(), "SUB_PERF_MODE")
            Components.Toggle(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_PERF_GFX", "Meta.LowGraphicsBoost", nextOrder(), "SUB_PERF_GFX")

            Components.SectionHeader(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_RADAR2D", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_ENABLE_RADAR", "Visuals.Radar2DEnabled", nextOrder())
            Components.Slider(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, pVis, "LBL_RADAR_SIZE", "Visuals.RadarSize", 120, 250, 4, " px", nextOrder(), setInteracting)
            Components.Slider(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, pVis, "LBL_RADAR_OPACITY", "Visuals.RadarOpacity", 0.3, 1.0, 0.05, "", nextOrder(), setInteracting)

            Components.SectionHeader(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_TRAJ3D", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_TRAJ_3D", "Visuals.Trajectory3DEnabled", nextOrder())
            Components.Slider(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, pVis, "LBL_MAX_BOUNCE", "Visuals.TrajectoryMaxBounces", 0, 3, 1, "", nextOrder(), setInteracting)
            Components.Toggle(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_REBOUND_MARKER", "Visuals.ShowReboundMarker", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_GOAL_MARKER", "Visuals.ShowGoalTargetMarker", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_DEBUG_OVERLAYS", "Visuals.ShowDebugOverlays", nextOrder())

            Components.SectionHeader(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_HUD_LAYOUT", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_SHOW_FLOAT", "UI.ShowFloatingButton", nextOrder(), "SUB_SHOW_FLOAT")
            Components.Toggle(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_SHOW_QUICKBAR", "UI.ShowQuickBar", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_SHOW_TELEMETRY", "UI.ShowTelemetryStrip", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_LOCK_HUD", "UI.HudLocked", nextOrder())
            Components.Slider(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, pVis, "LBL_DASH_SCALE", "UI.HudScale", 0.7, 1.35, 0.05, "x", nextOrder(), setInteracting)
            Components.Slider(pVis, store, self._janitor, locale, pVis, "LBL_DASH_OPACITY", "UI.HudOpacity", 0.5, 1.0, 0.02, "", nextOrder(), setInteracting)

            --================================================================
            -- TAB 4: CONFIG, MONKEY PATCH & LANGUAGE
            --================================================================
            local pCfg = self:_createPage(content, "CONFIG")
            o = 1
            Components.SectionHeader(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_TELEMETRY", nextOrder())

            local telBox = Instance.new("Frame")
            telBox.LayoutOrder = nextOrder()
            telBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 96)
            telBox.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgCard
            telBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
            telBox.Parent = pCfg
            Components.Round(telBox, 8)
            Components.Stroke(telBox, C.Stroke, 1, 0.3)

            local telLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            telLbl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 6)
            telLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -12)
            telLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            telLbl.Font = F.Regular
            telLbl.TextSize = 11
            telLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            telLbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
            telLbl.TextWrapped = true
            telLbl.TextColor3 = C.TextPrimary
            telLbl.Text = "Initializing telemetry..."
            telLbl.Parent = telBox
            self._telemetryCardLbl = telLbl

            Components.SectionHeader(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_LANGUAGE", nextOrder())
            Components.Selector(
                pCfg,
                store,
                self._janitor,
                locale,
                "LBL_LANGUAGE",
                "Meta.Language",
                { "EN", "ID" },
                nextOrder(),
                { EN = "English", ID = "Bahasa Indonesia" }
            )

            Components.SectionHeader(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_ENGINE", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_PERF_MODE", "Meta.PerformanceMode", nextOrder(), "SUB_PERF_MODE")
            Components.Toggle(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_BYPASS_MODE", "Meta.BypassMode", nextOrder(), "SUB_BYPASS_MODE")

            Components.WarningBanner(
                pCfg,
                store,
                self._janitor,
                locale,
                "WARN_MONKEY_TITLE",
                "WARN_MONKEY_BODY",
                "Meta.MonkeyPatchMode",
                nextOrder()
            )
            Components.Toggle(
                pCfg,
                store,
                self._janitor,
                locale,
                "LBL_MONKEY_PATCH",
                "Meta.MonkeyPatchMode",
                nextOrder(),
                "SUB_MONKEY_PATCH",
                true
            )

            Components.SectionHeader(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_CONFIG_IO", nextOrder())

            local profBox = Instance.new("Frame")
            profBox.LayoutOrder = nextOrder()
            profBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
            profBox.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgCard
            profBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
            profBox.Parent = pCfg
            Components.Round(profBox, 8)
            Components.Stroke(profBox, C.Stroke, 1, 0.35)

            local profInput = Instance.new("TextBox")
            profInput.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 4)
            profInput.Size = UDim2.new(0.62, -10, 1, -8)
            profInput.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgControl
            profInput.ClearTextOnFocus = false
            profInput.Font = F.Bold
            profInput.TextSize = 11
            profInput.TextColor3 = C.TextPrimary
            profInput.PlaceholderText = tr(self._ctx, "LBL_PROFILE_PH")
            profInput.Text = tostring(store:Get("Meta.ActiveProfile") or "Default")
            profInput.Parent = profBox
            Components.Round(profInput, 6)

            self._janitor:Add(store:OnChanged("Meta.Language", function()
                if profInput and profInput.Parent then
                    profInput.PlaceholderText = tr(self._ctx, "LBL_PROFILE_PH")
                end
            end))

            local cycleBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
            cycleBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
            cycleBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0)
            cycleBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.36, -6, 0, 26)
            cycleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = C.BgControl
            cycleBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
            cycleBtn.Font = F.Bold
            cycleBtn.TextSize = 10
            cycleBtn.TextColor3 = C.Accent
            cycleBtn.Parent = profBox
            Components.Round(cycleBtn, 6)
            Components.BindLocaleText(cycleBtn, store, self._janitor, locale, "BTN_LIST_PROFILES")

            local profListLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            profListLbl.LayoutOrder = nextOrder()
            profListLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
            profListLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            profListLbl.Font = F.Regular
            profListLbl.TextSize = 10
            profListLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            profListLbl.TextColor3 = C.TextMuted
            profListLbl.Parent = pCfg
            self._profileListLbl = profListLbl

            local function updateProfileLabel()
                local prefix = tr(self._ctx, "LBL_SAVED_PREFIX")
                profListLbl.Text = prefix .. " " .. table.concat(store:ListProfiles(), ", ")
            end
            updateProfileLabel()
            self._janitor:Add(store:OnChanged("Meta.Language", updateProfileLabel))

            self._janitor:Connect(cycleBtn.MouseButton1Click, function()
                local list = store:ListProfiles()
                updateProfileLabel()
                local cur = profInput.Text
                local idx = 1
                for i, name in ipairs(list) do
                    if name == cur then
                        idx = i
                        break
                    end
                end
                profInput.Text = list[(idx % #list) + 1] or "Default"
            end)

            Components.Button(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "BTN_SAVE_CFG", C.AccentDark, C.TextPrimary, nextOrder(), function()
                local okSave, savedName = store:SaveConfig(profInput.Text)
                profInput.Text = savedName
                updateProfileLabel()
                self._toast:Push(tr(self._ctx, okSave and "TOAST_SAVED" or "TOAST_SAVED_MEM", savedName), C.Success)
            end)

            Components.Button(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "BTN_LOAD_CFG", C.BgControl, C.TextPrimary, nextOrder(), function()
                local okLoad, res = store:LoadConfig(profInput.Text)
                if okLoad then
                    profInput.Text = res
                    updateProfileLabel()
                    self._toast:Push(tr(self._ctx, "TOAST_LOADED", res), C.Accent)
                else
                    self._toast:Push(tr(self._ctx, "TOAST_LOAD_FAIL", tostring(res)), C.Danger)
                end
            end)

            Components.Button(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "BTN_DEL_PROFILE", C.BgControl, C.Warning, nextOrder(), function()
                local okDel, err = store:DeleteProfile(profInput.Text)
                updateProfileLabel()
                if okDel then
                    profInput.Text = store:Get("Meta.ActiveProfile") or "Default"
                    self._toast:Push(tr(self._ctx, "TOAST_DELETED"), C.Warning)
                else
                    self._toast:Push(tostring(err), C.Danger)
                end
            end)

            Components.Button(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "BTN_RESET_DEF", C.BgControl, C.TextPrimary, nextOrder(), function()
                store:ResetToDefaults()
                updateProfileLabel()
                self._toast:Push(tr(self._ctx, "TOAST_RESET"), C.Warning)
            end)

            Components.Toggle(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_AUTOSAVE", "Meta.AutoSave", nextOrder())
            Components.Toggle(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_AUTOLOAD", "Meta.AutoLoad", nextOrder())

            Components.SectionHeader(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_KEYBINDS", nextOrder())
            Components.Keybind(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_KEY_MENU", "UI.ToggleKey", nextOrder())
            Components.Keybind(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_KEY_FLICK", "Striker.FlickComboKey", nextOrder())
            Components.Keybind(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "LBL_KEY_GKDIVE", "Goalkeeper.SmartDiveKey", nextOrder())

            Components.SectionHeader(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "SEC_SESSION", nextOrder())
            Components.Button(pCfg, store, self._janitor, locale, "BTN_UNLOAD", Color3.fromRGB(130, 32, 38), C.TextPrimary, nextOrder(), function()
                LouisHub.Destroy()
            end)

            self:_switchTab("STRIKER")
            self:_updateViewportScale()
            self:_applyMinimizedState(store:Get("UI.DashboardMinimized") == true, true)

            self._janitor:Add(store:OnChanged("UI.HudScale", function()
                self:_updateViewportScale()
            end))
            self._janitor:Add(store:OnChanged("UI.DashboardMinimized", function(minState)
                self:_applyMinimizedState(minState == true, false)
            end))
            self._janitor:Add(store:OnChanged("UI.DashboardOpen", function(open)
                local show = open == true
                if show then
                    local targetScale = self:_getTargetScale()
                    self._uiScale.Scale = targetScale * 0.92
                    self._window.Visible = true
                    Components.Tween(self._uiScale, 0.14, { Scale = targetScale })
                else
                    self._window.Visible = false
                end
            end))
            self._janitor:Add(store:OnChanged("UI.HudOpacity", function(alpha)
                local a = math.clamp(alpha or 0.94, 0.4, 1)
                self._window.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - a
            end))
            self._janitor:Add(store:OnChanged("Meta.BypassMode", function(on)
                self._toast:Push(tr(self._ctx, on and "TOAST_BYPASS_ON" or "TOAST_BYPASS_OFF"), on and C.Success or C.Warning)
            end))
            self._janitor:Add(store:OnChanged("Meta.PerformanceMode", function(on)
                self._toast:Push(tr(self._ctx, on and "TOAST_PERF_ON" or "TOAST_PERF_OFF"), on and C.Success or C.Accent)
            end))
            self._janitor:Add(store:OnChanged("Meta.MonkeyPatchMode", function(on)
                self._toast:Push(
                    tr(self._ctx, on and "TOAST_MONKEY_ON" or "TOAST_MONKEY_OFF"),
                    on and C.Danger or C.Success,
                    on and 4.2 or 2.4
                )
            end))
            self._janitor:Add(store:OnChanged("Meta.Language", function()
                self._lastTelemetryCardTxt = ""
                self:RenderTelemetry()
            end))

            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if cam then
                self._janitor:Connect(cam:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"), function()
                    self:_updateViewportScale()
                end)
            end
        end

        function Dashboard:SetVisible(visible)
            self._ctx.Store:Set("UI.DashboardOpen", visible == true)
        end

        function Dashboard:Toggle()
            self:SetVisible(not (self._ctx.Store:Get("UI.DashboardOpen") == true))
        end

        function Dashboard:RenderTelemetry()
            if self._activeTab ~= "CONFIG" then
                return
            end
            if not (self._window and self._window.Visible and self._telemetryCardLbl) then
                return
            end
            if self._ctx.Store:Get("UI.DashboardMinimized") == true then
                return
            end

            local stats = self._ctx.Scheduler:GetStats()
            local ballSnap = self._ctx.Ball:GetSnapshot()
            local role = self._ctx.Match:GetRoleName()
            local zoneTxt = "-"
            local sol = self._ctx.Engines.GoalSolver:GetLastSolution()
            if sol and sol.Zone then
                zoneTxt = string.format("%s (%.0fst | +%.1f)", tostring(sol.TargetLabel or sol.Zone.Zone), sol.Zone.Distance or 0, sol.GKSaveMargin or 0)
            end

            local bypassOn = self._ctx.Store:Get("Meta.BypassMode") == true
            local monkeyOn = self._ctx.Store:Get("Meta.MonkeyPatchMode") == true
            local perfOn = self._ctx.Store:Get("Meta.PerformanceMode") == true
            local hookCount = self._ctx.HookGuard and self._ctx.HookGuard:GetWrappedCount() or 0
            local engineTxt = string.format("Scoped(%s) | MP(%s) [%d] | AntiLag(%s)",
                bypassOn and "ON" or "OFF",
                monkeyOn and "ON" or "OFF",
                hookCount,
                perfOn and "ON" or "OFF"
            )
            local fileMode = self._ctx.Store:IsFilePersistenceAvailable() and "Disk JSON" or "Memory"
            local dribbleStatus = self._ctx.Automation.Dribble and self._ctx.Automation.Dribble:GetStatus() or "OFF"
            local gkStatus = self._ctx.Automation.Goalkeeper and self._ctx.Automation.Goalkeeper:GetStatus() or "OFF"

            local txt = string.format(
                "Role: %s  |  Team: %s  |  Save: %s\nEngine: %s\nBall: %s (%.1f st/s)  |  Shot Target: %s\nDmc GK: %s  |  Dmc Dribble: %s\nHeartbeat: %.2f ms  |  Render: %.2f ms",
                role,
                tostring(self._ctx.Match:GetLocalTeamName() or "None"),
                fileMode,
                engineTxt,
                tostring(ballSnap.Mode),
                ballSnap.Speed or 0,
                zoneTxt,
                tostring(gkStatus),
                tostring(dribbleStatus),
                stats.LastHeartbeatMs or 0,
                stats.LastRenderMs or 0
            )
            if self._lastTelemetryCardTxt ~= txt then
                self._lastTelemetryCardTxt = txt
                self._telemetryCardLbl.Text = txt
            end
        end

        function Dashboard:Destroy()
            self._janitor:Destroy()
        end

        return Dashboard
    end)

    --========================================================================
    -- BOOTSTRAP PART 10 (FINAL INTEGRATION)
    --========================================================================
    local ctx = LouisHub.Context
    local ToastManagerClass = LouisHub.Require("UI/ToastManager")
    local QuickHudClass = LouisHub.Require("UI/QuickHud")
    local DashboardClass = LouisHub.Require("UI/Dashboard")

    local toastManager = ToastManagerClass.new(ctx.RootGui)
    local dashboard = nil
    local quickHud = QuickHudClass.new(ctx, ctx.RootGui, function()
        if dashboard then
            dashboard:Toggle()
        end
    end)
    dashboard = DashboardClass.new(ctx, ctx.RootGui, toastManager)

    ctx.UI = {
        Toast = toastManager,
        QuickHud = quickHud,
        Dashboard = dashboard,
    }

    local function isInputOverLouisUI(input)
        if not (ctx.RootGui and ctx.RootGui.Parent and input) then
            return false
        end
        local kind = input.UserInputType
        if kind ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and kind ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2
            and kind ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3 and kind ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            return false
        end
        local pt = input.Position
        for _, child in ipairs(ctx.RootGui:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("GuiObject") and child.Visible and child.Name ~= "LouisHub_Toasts" and child.Name ~= "LouisHub_GKAdvisor" then
                local p = child.AbsolutePosition
                local s = child.AbsoluteSize
                if pt.X >= p.X and pt.X <= p.X + s.X and pt.Y >= p.Y and pt.Y <= p.Y + s.Y then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    if ctx.Automation and ctx.Automation.Dribble then
        ctx.Automation.Dribble:SetIgnoreInputFn(isInputOverLouisUI)
    end
    if ctx.Engines and ctx.Engines.DmcGK then
        ctx.Engines.DmcGK:SetUiBusyChecker(function()
            return dashboard and dashboard:IsInteracting()
        end)
    end

    ctx.Scheduler:AddRenderStep("LouisHub_UI", 0.05, function()
        quickHud:Render()
        dashboard:RenderTelemetry()
    end)

    if ctx.Performance and type(ctx.Performance.Apply) == "function" then
        ctx.Performance:Apply()
    end

    local uiKeyConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
        if gameProcessed then
            return
        end
        local toggleKeyName = ctx.Store:Get("UI.ToggleKey") or "RightShift"
        local keyEnum = Enum.KeyCode[toggleKeyName]
        if keyEnum and input.KeyCode == keyEnum then
            dashboard:Toggle()
        end
    end)

    LouisHub.OnDestroy(function()
        if uiKeyConn and uiKeyConn.Connected then
            uiKeyConn:Disconnect()
        end
        pcall(function() quickHud:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() dashboard:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() toastManager:Destroy() end)
    end)

    LouisHub.PartLoaded[10] = true

    toastManager:Push(
        tr(ctx, "TOAST_LOADED_BOOT", LouisHub.Version, ctx.Match:GetRoleName(), ctx.Store:Get("Meta.BypassMode") and "ON" or "OFF"),
        Color3.fromRGB(64, 196, 255),
        3.4
    )
    print(string.format("[LouisHub v%s] Part 10/10 initialized — Suite Complete! Press %s or tap LOUIS / MENU.",
        LouisHub.Version,
        tostring(ctx.Store:Get("UI.ToggleKey"))
    ))
end
